.github/workflows/build-macos.yml
================================================================================
name: Build GRAILD for macOS

on:
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  build:
    name: macOS ${{ matrix.arch }}
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.runner }}
    timeout-minutes: 45
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        include:
          - runner: macos-15-intel
            arch: x86_64
            python-arch: x64
          - runner: macos-15
            arch: arm64
            python-arch: arm64
    env:
      GRAILD_BUILD_ARCH: ${{ matrix.arch }}
      PIP_DISABLE_PIP_VERSION_CHECK: '1'
    steps:
      - name: Check out application source
        uses: actions/checkout@v6
        with:
          persist-credentials: false

      - name: Set up native Python
        uses: actions/setup-python@v6
        with:
          python-version: '3.13'
          architecture: ${{ matrix.python-arch }}
          cache: pip
          cache-dependency-path: requirements.txt

      - name: Install build dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
          python -m pip check

      - name: Run platform tests
        run: python -m unittest test_mac_support test_mac_updater test_mac_build_assets test_brand_assets test_tls_support test_oauth_config test_account_tags test_achievements test_audio_files test_game_modes test_round_resume -v

      - name: Build and verify app bundle
        run: python build_macos.py --expected-arch "$GRAILD_BUILD_ARCH"

      - name: Verify packaged OAuth TLS without developer CA paths
        env:
          SSL_CERT_FILE: /tmp/graild-nonexistent-ca.pem
          SSL_CERT_DIR: /tmp/graild-nonexistent-ca-directory
        run: |
          VERSION=$(python -c 'from app_version import APP_VERSION; print(APP_VERSION)')
          FOLDER="dist/releases/v$VERSION/macOS-$GRAILD_BUILD_ARCH"
          "$FOLDER/GRAILD.app/Contents/MacOS/GRAILD" --tls-smoke-test "$FOLDER/tls-smoke.json"
          cat "$FOLDER/tls-smoke.json"

      - name: Record ZIP checksum and build information
        run: |
          python - <<'PY'
          import hashlib, json, os, platform
          from pathlib import Path
          from app_version import APP_VERSION
          arch = os.environ['GRAILD_BUILD_ARCH']
          folder = Path('dist/releases') / ('v' + APP_VERSION) / ('macOS-' + arch)
          archive = folder / ('GRAILD-' + APP_VERSION + '-macOS-' + arch + '.zip')
          digest = hashlib.sha256(archive.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
          (folder / 'SHA256SUMS.txt').write_text(digest + '  ' + archive.name + '\n')
          info = {'version': APP_VERSION, 'architecture': arch,
                  'macos_build_host': platform.mac_ver()[0],
                  'commit': os.environ['GITHUB_SHA'], 'sha256': digest,
                  'signing': 'ad-hoc test build; not notarized',
                  'interactive_runtime_tests': 'pending on a real tester Mac'}
          (folder / 'build-info.json').write_text(json.dumps(info, indent=2))
          with open(os.environ['GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY'], 'a') as summary:
              summary.write(f'### GRAILD {APP_VERSION} macOS {arch}\n\n')
              summary.write(f'Built `{archive.name}`. Download the artifact below.\n\n')
              summary.write('This is an ad-hoc signed test build. Developer ID notarization and interactive audio/login tests are still pending. No GitHub Release was published.\n')
          PY

      # Upload the ditto ZIP, not the raw .app: it preserves permissions and symlinks.
      - name: Upload Mac test build
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v6
        with:
          name: GRAILD-macOS-${{ matrix.arch }}
          path: |
            dist/releases/*/macOS-${{ matrix.arch }}/*.zip
            dist/releases/*/macOS-${{ matrix.arch }}/SHA256SUMS.txt
            dist/releases/*/macOS-${{ matrix.arch }}/build-info.json
            dist/releases/*/macOS-${{ matrix.arch }}/tls-smoke.json
          if-no-files-found: error
          retention-days: 14
          compression-level: 0

achievements.py
================================================================================
"""Achievement catalog and persistent per-account progress."""

ACHIEVEMENTS = []

PROFILE_TAGS = {
    "owner": {"id": "owner", "name": "Owner / Creator", "kind": "assigned",
              "description": "Verified owner / creator of GRAIL'D."},
    "beta_tester": {
        "id": "beta_tester", "name": "Beta Tester", "kind": "assigned",
        "description": "Awarded by the owner for helping beta test GRAIL'D.",
    },
    "top_earner": {
        "id": "top_earner", "name": "Top earner", "kind": "earned",
        "description": "Unlocked all 50 achievements.",
    },
}

def milestones(category, metric, targets, names, requirement):
    for target, name in zip(targets, names):
        ACHIEVEMENTS.append({"id": f"{metric}_{target}", "name": name,
                             "category": category, "metric": metric,
                             "target": target, "description": requirement.format(n=f"{target:,}")})

milestones("Recognition", "correct", [1, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250, 500, 999],
           ["First Hit", "Finding Your Sound", "On the Wavelength", "Fifty Shades of Juice",
            "Century Club", "Deep Cut Detective", "Human Shazam", "999 Forever"],
           "Make {n} correct song guesses in total.")
milestones("Points", "points", [100, 500, 1000, 2500, 5000, 10000, 25000, 99999],
           ["On the Board", "Pocket Change", "Four Figures", "Stacking Up", "High Roller",
            "Five Figures", "Vault Builder", "999 Fortune"],
           "Earn {n} lifetime points.")
milestones("Streaks", "streak", [3, 5, 10, 15, 25, 50, 99],
           ["Three-Peat", "Heating Up", "Unstoppable", "In the Zone", "Purple Flame",
            "Never Miss a Beat", "Streak Legend"],
           "Win {n} rounds in a row without losing a round. Wrong guesses within a winning round are allowed.")
milestones("Collection", "grails", [1, 5, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250],
           ["First Grail", "Crate Digger", "Hidden Gems", "Grail Hunter", "Private Vault",
            "Archive Keeper", "Museum of Juice"],
           "Correctly identify {n} different songs to collect their grails.")
milestones("Dedication", "rounds", [10, 50, 100, 250, 500, 1000],
           ["Settling In", "Regular Listener", "On Repeat", "Long Session", "No Skips in Spirit",
            "Thousand-Round Tour"],
           "Finish {n} rounds, won or lost. Changing or rerolling a song does not count.")
milestones("Quick Ears", "first_try", [1, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250],
           ["Instant Recognition", "One-Shot Wonder", "Quick Draw", "Split-Second Ear",
            "First-Try Specialist", "One Second Legend"],
           "Win {n} rounds on the first attempt, without a wrong guess or skipped attempt. Tracked since the achievements update.")
milestones("Hard Mode", "hard", [1, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250],
           ["Hard Debut", "Pressure Tested", "Hard-Hitting", "Against the Odds", "Expert Ear",
            "Hard Mode Royalty"],
           "Win {n} rounds on Hard difficulty. Tracked since the achievements update.")
for provider, name in [("google", "Google Connected"), ("discord", "Discord Connected")]:
    ACHIEVEMENTS.append({"id": f"linked_{provider}", "name": name,
                         "category": "Connections", "metric": provider, "target": 1,
                         "description": f"Link a verified {provider.title()} account to your profile."})


def account_achievements(connection, user_id, now):
    account = connection.execute("SELECT * FROM accounts WHERE user_id = ?", (user_id,)).fetchone()
    if not account:
        return {"entries": [], "unlocked": 0, "total": len(ACHIEVEMENTS)}
    counters = {row["metric"]: row["value"] for row in connection.execute(
        "SELECT metric, value FROM achievement_counters WHERE user_id = ?", (user_id,))}
    providers = {row["provider"] for row in connection.execute(
        "SELECT provider FROM external_identities WHERE user_id = ?", (user_id,))}
    metrics = {"correct": account["total_correct"], "points": account["total_points"],
               "streak": account["best_streak"], "rounds": account["songs_played"],
               "grails": connection.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM grails WHERE user_id = ?", (user_id,)).fetchone()[0],
               "first_try": counters.get("first_try", 0), "hard": counters.get("hard", 0),
               "google": int("google" in providers), "discord": int("discord" in providers)}
    earned = {row["achievement_id"]: row["unlocked_at"] for row in connection.execute(
        "SELECT achievement_id, unlocked_at FROM account_achievements WHERE user_id = ?", (user_id,))}
    entries = []
    for item in ACHIEVEMENTS:
        progress = min(metrics[item["metric"]], item["target"])
        if progress >= item["target"] and item["id"] not in earned:
            connection.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO account_achievements (user_id, achievement_id, unlocked_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                               (user_id, item["id"], now))
            earned[item["id"]] = now
        unlocked = item["id"] in earned
        entries.append({**item, "progress": item["target"] if unlocked else progress,
                        "unlocked": unlocked, "unlocked_at": earned.get(item["id"])})
    return {"entries": entries, "unlocked": sum(item["unlocked"] for item in entries), "total": len(ACHIEVEMENTS)}

audio_files.py

"""Recognize audio files and normalize them with the bundled FFmpeg decoder."""
import hashlib
import mimetypes
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import threading

import imageio_ffmpeg

AUDIO_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".mp3", ".wav", ".wave", ".ogg", ".oga", ".opus", ".flac", ".m4a",
    ".aac", ".wma", ".aif", ".aiff", ".aifc", ".alac", ".ape", ".wv",
    ".amr", ".awb", ".au", ".snd", ".caf", ".mka", ".m4b", ".m4r",
    ".mp2", ".mp1", ".ac3", ".eac3", ".dts", ".tta", ".tak", ".spx",
    ".dsf", ".dff", ".webm", ".weba", ".mp4", ".3gp", ".3g2",
}
_DECODE_LOCK = threading.Lock()


def is_audio_file(filename, *, entry=None):
    path = Path(filename)
    # Known audio extensions do not need the Windows MIME registry. DirEntry
    # reuses directory metadata instead of issuing a stat for every song.
    if not (entry.is_file() if entry is not None else path.is_file()):
        return False
    if path.suffix.lower() in AUDIO_EXTENSIONS:
        return True
    mime, _ = mimetypes.guess_type(str(path))
    return bool(mime and mime.startswith("audio/"))


def get_playback_path(filename, cache_dir=None):
    source = Path(filename).resolve()
    stat = source.stat()
    fingerprint = f"{source}:{stat.st_size}:{stat.st_mtime_ns}:pcm-v1"
    key = hashlib.sha256(fingerprint.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    cache = Path(cache_dir) if cache_dir else Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "JuiceSpot-audio-cache"
    cache.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target = cache / (key + ".wav")
    with _DECODE_LOCK:
        if target.is_file() and target.stat().st_size > 44:
            return str(target)
        handle, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".wav", dir=cache)
        os.close(handle)
        temporary = Path(temporary_name)
        try:
            command = [imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe(), "-nostdin", "-hide_banner", "-loglevel", "error", "-y",
                       "-protocol_whitelist", "file,pipe", "-i", str(source), "-map", "0:a:0", "-vn",
                       "-ac", "2", "-ar", "44100", "-c:a", "pcm_s16le", str(temporary)]
            try:
                result = subprocess.run(command, capture_output=True, timeout=120,
                                        creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                raise ValueError("Audio decoding timed out. Try a shorter file.") from None
            if result.returncode or temporary.stat().st_size <= 44:
                raise ValueError("This file could not be decoded into playable audio.")
            os.replace(temporary, target)
        finally:
            if temporary.exists():
                temporary.unlink()
    return str(target)

================================================================================
auto_update.py
================================================================================
"""Background GitHub beta updates; only signed release payloads are eligible."""
import json
import os
import platform
from pathlib import Path
import secrets
import shutil
import sys
import threading
import time
from urllib.parse import urlsplit, unquote
import uuid

import requests

from app_version import APP_VERSION
from update_trust import DEFAULT_REPOSITORY, DEFAULT_CHANNEL, TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS
from update_security import (UpdateError, SemVer, strict_json, repository, verify_manifest,
                             verify_file, sha256_file, atomic_json, update_root)


class Cancelled(UpdateError):
    pass


def validate_https(url, hosts):
    parsed = urlsplit(str(url))
    if parsed.scheme != "https" or parsed.hostname not in hosts or parsed.username or parsed.password or parsed.port not in (None, 443) or parsed.fragment:
        raise UpdateError("Untrusted update download location.")
    return str(url)


def validate_asset_url(url, repo, tag, asset):
    validate_https(url, {"github.com"})
    actual = unquote(urlsplit(url).path)
    prefix = "/" + repo + "/releases/download/"
    if not actual[:len(prefix)].casefold() == prefix.casefold() or actual[len(prefix):] != tag + "/" + asset or urlsplit(url).query:
        raise UpdateError("Update asset does not belong to this GitHub release.")
    return url


class UpdateManager:
    def __init__(self, root=None, keys=None, session=None):
        self.platform = 'macos' if sys.platform == 'darwin' else 'windows'
        self.architecture = platform.machine() if self.platform == 'macos' else None
        self.root = Path(root) if root else update_root()
        self.root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (self.root / "jobs").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self.keys = TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS if keys is None else keys
        self.session = session or requests.Session()
        self.session.headers.update({"User-Agent": "GRAILD/" + APP_VERSION, "Accept": "application/vnd.github+json"})
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        self.cancel = threading.Event()
        self.candidate = None
        self.ready_job = None
        self.config = {"repository": DEFAULT_REPOSITORY, "channel": DEFAULT_CHANNEL, "automatic": True}
        try:
            saved = strict_json((self.root / "settings.json").read_bytes())
            repo = saved.get("repository") or DEFAULT_REPOSITORY
            if repo:
                repository(repo)
            if saved.get("channel", "beta") not in ("stable", "beta"):
                raise UpdateError("Invalid saved update channel.")
            self.config.update({"repository": repo, "channel": saved.get("channel", "beta"), "automatic": saved.get("automatic", True) is True})
        except (OSError, UpdateError, AttributeError):
            pass
        self.last_result = None
        results = sorted((self.root / "jobs").glob("*/result.json"), key=lambda path: path.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)
        if results:
            try:
                self.last_result = strict_json(results[0].read_bytes())
            except (OSError, UpdateError):
                pass
        self.state = {"status": "idle", "message": "Ready to check for updates.", "version": APP_VERSION, "downloaded": 0, "total": 0}
        # Interrupted downloads are never considered installable or resumed blindly.
        for job in list((self.root / "jobs").iterdir())[:200]:
            if job.is_dir() and len(job.name) == 32:
                partial = job / "download.part"
                if partial.is_file() and not partial.is_symlink() and partial.resolve().is_relative_to(self.root.resolve()):
                    try:
                        partial.unlink()
                    except OSError:
                        pass

    def snapshot(self):
        with self.lock:
            return {**self.state, "current_version": APP_VERSION, "repository": self.config["repository"],
                    "channel": self.config["channel"], "automatic": self.config["automatic"], "signing_configured": bool(self.keys), "last_result": self.last_result, "can_download": self.candidate is not None}

    def _state(self, status, message, **values):
        with self.lock:
            self.state.update({"status": status, "message": message, **values})

    def save_settings(self, repo, channel, automatic=True):
        with self.lock:
            if self.state["status"] in ("checking", "downloading", "verifying", "installing"):
                raise UpdateError("Wait for the current update operation or cancel its download first.")
            repo = str(repo or "").strip()
            if repo:
                repository(repo)
            if channel not in ("stable", "beta"):
                raise UpdateError("Choose the beta or stable update channel.")
            self.config = {"repository": repo, "channel": channel, "automatic": bool(automatic)}
            atomic_json(self.root / "settings.json", self.config)
            self.candidate = None
            self.ready_job = None
            self.state = {"status": "idle", "message": "Update settings saved.", "downloaded": 0, "total": 0}
        return self.snapshot()

    def _response(self, url, stream=False):
        hosts = {"api.github.com"} if urlsplit(url).hostname == "api.github.com" else {"github.com", "release-assets.githubusercontent.com", "objects.githubusercontent.com", "github-releases.githubusercontent.com"}
        for _ in range(6):
            validate_https(url, hosts)
            response = self.session.get(url, timeout=(5, 20), stream=stream, allow_redirects=False)
            if response.status_code in (301, 302, 303, 307, 308):
                redirect = response.headers.get("Location", "")
                response.close()
                validate_https(redirect, hosts)
                url = redirect
                continue
            if response.status_code != 200:
                code = response.status_code
                response.close()
                messages = {403: "GitHub denied the request or its API rate limit was reached.",
                            404: "The GitHub repository or release was not found. Use a public repository.",
                            429: "GitHub is rate limiting update checks. Try again later.",
                            503: "GitHub updates are temporarily unavailable."}
                raise UpdateError(messages.get(code, "The update server returned HTTP " + str(code) + "."))
            return response
        raise UpdateError("Too many update download redirects.")

    def _json(self, url, limit=8 * 1024 * 1024):
        response = self._response(url, stream=True)
        try:
            content = bytearray()
            for chunk in response.iter_content(65536):
                content.extend(chunk)
                if len(content) > limit:
                    raise UpdateError("Update metadata exceeded the size limit.")
            # GitHub release lists can be larger than a small signed manifest.
            return json.loads(bytes(content)) if limit > 131072 else strict_json(bytes(content))
        finally:
            response.close()

    def check(self, automatic=False):
        if self.platform == 'macos' and self.architecture not in ('arm64', 'x86_64'):
            self._state('unsupported', 'This Mac architecture is not supported for automatic updates.')
            return self.snapshot()
        with self.lock:
            if automatic and not self.config["automatic"]:
                return self.snapshot()
            if self.state["status"] in ("checking", "downloading", "verifying", "installing", "ready"):
                return self.snapshot()
            if not self.config["repository"]:
                self._state("unconfigured", "Set a GitHub repository in Settings to enable updates.")
                return self.snapshot()
            if not self.keys:
                self._state("unconfigured", "This build has no trusted release-signing key. The publisher must configure one before distributing updates.")
                return self.snapshot()
            self._state("checking", "Checking GitHub for signed updates...")
        threading.Thread(target=self._check_worker, name="GRAILD-update-check", daemon=True).start()
        return self.snapshot()

    def _check_worker(self):
        with self.lock:
            self.candidate = None
        try:
            repo, channel = self.config["repository"], self.config["channel"]
            releases = []
            for page in range(1, 4):
                batch = self._json("https://api.github.com/repos/" + repo + "/releases?per_page=100&page=" + str(page))
                if not isinstance(batch, list):
                    raise UpdateError("GitHub returned an invalid release list.")
                releases.extend(batch)
                if len(batch) < 100:
                    break
            candidates = []
            for release in releases:
                try:
                    if release.get("draft"):
                        continue
                    tag = release["tag_name"]
                    version = SemVer.parse(tag[1:] if tag.startswith("v") else tag)
                    if version <= SemVer.parse(APP_VERSION) or (channel == "stable" and (release.get("prerelease") or version.prerelease)):
                        continue
                    candidates.append((version, release))
                except (UpdateError, KeyError, TypeError, AttributeError):
                    continue
            candidates.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)
            rejected = False
            for _, release in candidates[:20]:
                try:
                    assets = {asset["name"]: asset for asset in release.get("assets", [])}
                    metadata_name = f'graild-update-macos-{self.architecture}.json' if self.platform == 'macos' else 'graild-update.json'
                    if metadata_name not in assets:
                        continue
                    metadata_asset = assets[metadata_name]
                    metadata_url = validate_asset_url(metadata_asset["browser_download_url"], repo, release["tag_name"], metadata_name)
                    envelope = self._json(metadata_url, limit=131072)
                    manifest = self._verify_manifest(envelope, repo, channel, release['tag_name'])
                    asset = assets[manifest["asset"]]
                    url = validate_asset_url(asset["browser_download_url"], repo, manifest["tag"], manifest["asset"])
                    with self.lock:
                        self.candidate = {"envelope": envelope, "manifest": manifest, "url": url, "repository": repo, "channel": channel}
                    self._state("available", "A verified update is available.", version=manifest["version"], notes=manifest.get("notes", ""), downloaded=0, total=manifest["size"])
                    return
                except (UpdateError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError):
                    rejected = True
            if rejected:
                raise UpdateError("Newer releases were found, but none had a valid trusted signed manifest. Nothing was downloaded or installed.")
            self._state("up_to_date", "You are up to date on the " + channel + " channel.")
        except Exception as error:
            message = str(error) if isinstance(error, UpdateError) else "Could not check for updates. Check your connection and try again."
            self._state("error", message)

    def download(self):
        with self.lock:
            if self.state["status"] in ("downloading", "verifying", "ready", "installing"):
                return self.snapshot()
            if not self.candidate:
                raise UpdateError("Check for a verified update first.")
            candidate = dict(self.candidate)
            self.cancel.clear()
            self._state("downloading", "Downloading verified release...", downloaded=0, total=candidate["manifest"]["size"])
        threading.Thread(target=self._download_worker, args=(candidate,), name="GRAILD-update-download", daemon=True).start()
        return self.snapshot()

    def _verify_manifest(self, envelope, repo, channel, tag=None):
        return verify_manifest(envelope, self.keys, repo, channel, APP_VERSION, tag,
                               platform=self.platform, architecture=self.architecture)

    def _download_worker(self, candidate):
        job = self.root / "jobs" / uuid.uuid4().hex
        partial = job / "download.part"
        response = None
        try:
            job.mkdir(mode=0o700)
            manifest = self._verify_manifest(candidate['envelope'], candidate['repository'], candidate['channel'])
            atomic_json(job / "manifest.json", candidate["envelope"])
            response = self._response(candidate["url"], stream=True)
            received = 0
            with open(partial, "xb") as file:
                for chunk in response.iter_content(256 * 1024):
                    if self.cancel.is_set():
                        raise Cancelled("Update download cancelled. Your current app is unchanged.")
                    received += len(chunk)
                    if received > manifest["size"]:
                        raise UpdateError("Downloaded update exceeded its signed size.")
                    file.write(chunk)
                    self._state("downloading", "Downloading update...", downloaded=received)
                file.flush()
                os.fsync(file.fileno())
            self._state("verifying", "Verifying the downloaded update...")
            verify_file(partial, manifest)
            if self.cancel.is_set():
                raise Cancelled("Update download cancelled. Your current app is unchanged.")
            if self.platform == 'macos':
                from mac_update_security import archive_bundle_digest
                if archive_bundle_digest(partial) != manifest['bundle_sha256']:
                    raise UpdateError('The Mac archive does not match its signed bundle checksum.')
            if self.cancel.is_set():
                raise Cancelled('Update download cancelled. Your current app is unchanged.')
            os.replace(partial, job / ('payload.zip' if self.platform == 'macos' else 'payload.exe'))
            with self.lock:
                self.ready_job = job
            self._state("ready", "Download verified. Ready to restart and install.", downloaded=received)
        except Exception as error:
            message = str(error) if isinstance(error, UpdateError) else "Download interrupted. Your current app is unchanged; retry when connected."
            self._state("cancelled" if isinstance(error, Cancelled) else "error", message)
        finally:
            if response:
                response.close()
            if partial.exists():
                partial.unlink()

    def cancel_download(self):
        self.cancel.set()
        return self.snapshot()

    def install(self, helper_source, target=None):
        if self.platform == 'macos':
            from mac_updater import prepare_install
            try:
                return prepare_install(self)
            except Exception as error:
                message = str(error) if isinstance(error, UpdateError) else 'The Mac updater could not prepare safely. The current app was left open.'
                self._state('error', message)
                raise UpdateError(message) from error
        from graild_updater import launch
        if os.name != "nt" or not getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
            raise UpdateError("Installation is available in the packaged Windows app. Development mode can check and verify downloads.")
        with self.lock:
            if self.state["status"] != "ready" or not self.ready_job or not self.candidate:
                raise UpdateError("No verified update is ready to install.")
            job = self.ready_job
            manifest = verify_manifest(strict_json((job / "manifest.json").read_bytes()), self.keys,
                                       self.config["repository"], self.config["channel"], APP_VERSION)
            verify_file(job / "payload.exe", manifest)
            target = Path(target or sys.executable).resolve()
            # Fail before closing the app if its installation directory is not writable.
            probe = target.parent / (".graild-write-test-" + uuid.uuid4().hex)
            try:
                probe.write_bytes(b"test")
            finally:
                if probe.exists():
                    probe.unlink()
            helper = job / "updater.exe"
            shutil.copyfile(helper_source, helper)
            if sha256_file(helper) != sha256_file(helper_source):
                raise UpdateError("Bundled updater copy failed verification.")
            atomic_json(job / "request.json", {"target": str(target), "parent_pid": os.getpid(),
                        "old_sha256": sha256_file(target), "repository": self.config["repository"],
                        "channel": self.config["channel"], "current_version": APP_VERSION,
                        "health_token": secrets.token_hex(32)})
            self._state("installing", "Preparing the updater. GRAIL'D will restart shortly...")
        process = launch([str(helper), "--job", str(job)])
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 40
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            if (job / "ready.json").is_file():
                return self.snapshot()
            result = job / "result.json"
            if result.is_file():
                self._state("error", strict_json(result.read_bytes()).get("message", "Updater preparation failed."))
                raise UpdateError(self.snapshot()["message"])
            if process.poll() is not None:
                break
            time.sleep(0.1)
        self._state("error", "The updater did not become ready. Your app was left open; please retry.")
        raise UpdateError(self.snapshot()["message"])


class UnavailableUpdateManager:
    """Keep gameplay available when Windows update storage is inaccessible."""
    def __init__(self, reason):
        self.message = "Update storage is unavailable. GRAIL'D can still be used. " + str(reason)

    def snapshot(self):
        return {"status": "error", "message": self.message, "current_version": APP_VERSION,
                "version": APP_VERSION, "repository": DEFAULT_REPOSITORY, "channel": DEFAULT_CHANNEL,
                "automatic": False, "signing_configured": bool(TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS),
                "can_download": False, "downloaded": 0, "total": 0, "last_result": None}

    def check(self, automatic=False):
        return self.snapshot()

    def cancel_download(self):
        return self.snapshot()

    def save_settings(self, *args, **kwargs):
        raise UpdateError(self.message)

    def download(self):
        raise UpdateError(self.message)

    def install(self, *args, **kwargs):
        raise UpdateError(self.message)

    def _state(self, status, message, **values):
        self.message = message

================================================================================
beta_update_smoke.py
================================================================================
"""Opt-in isolated beta update diagnostic; never changes a normal installation.

No signing keys are loaded here. Local fixtures must pass the real updater's
compiled publisher signature, repository, version and checksum validation.
"""
import base64
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sqlite3
import threading
import time
from urllib.parse import urlsplit


def write_json(path, value):
    from update_security import atomic_json
    atomic_json(path, value)


def read_context(filename):
    path = Path(filename).resolve()
    data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    root = path.parent
    if root.parent.name != 'GRAILD-BetaValidation' or not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{32}', root.name):
        raise RuntimeError('This diagnostic requires an isolated beta-validation directory.')
    if (root/'ISOLATED_TEST_ONLY').read_text(encoding='utf-8') != data['nonce']:
        raise RuntimeError('The isolated test marker is missing.')
    expected = root/'install'/'GRAILD.exe'
    import sys
    if Path(sys.executable).resolve() != expected.resolve():
        raise RuntimeError('Diagnostic refused: this is not the isolated test installation.')
    return root,data


def profile_snapshot(directory):
    directory = Path(directory)
    tables = {}
    for filename in ('accounts.sqlite3','canary_api.sqlite3','canary_matches.sqlite3'):
        path=directory/filename
        if not path.exists(): continue
        connection=sqlite3.connect(path.as_uri()+'?mode=ro',uri=True)
        try:
            for (table,) in connection.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' ORDER BY name"):
                digest=hashlib.sha256()
                rows=connection.execute('SELECT * FROM "'+table.replace('"','""')+'"').fetchall()
                normalized=[]
                for row in rows:
                    normalized.append(json.dumps([{'bytes':base64.b64encode(v).decode()} if isinstance(v,bytes) else v for v in row],sort_keys=True,ensure_ascii=False))
                for row in sorted(normalized):digest.update(row.encode())
                tables[filename+':'+table]=digest.hexdigest()
        finally:connection.close()
    files={}
    for name in ('session.dat','juicespot_config.json','song_stats.json'):
        path=directory/name
        if path.exists():files[name]=hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    return {'tables':tables,'files':files}


class LocalReleaseResponse:
    status_code=200
    headers={}
    def __init__(self, content=None, filename=None):self.content=content;self.filename=filename
    def iter_content(self,size):
        if self.filename:
            with open(self.filename,'rb') as file:
                while True:
                    chunk=file.read(size)
                    if not chunk:break
                    time.sleep(.006)
                    yield chunk
        else:yield self.content
    def close(self):pass


class LocalReleaseTransport:
    def __init__(self,context):self.context=context;self.headers={}
    def get(self,url,**kwargs):
        from update_trust import DEFAULT_REPOSITORY
        context=self.context
        manifest=json.loads(Path(context['manifest']).read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['manifest']
        prefix='https://github.com/'+DEFAULT_REPOSITORY+'/releases/download/'+manifest['tag']+'/'
        if url.startswith('https://api.github.com/repos/'+DEFAULT_REPOSITORY+'/releases?'):
            release={'tag_name':manifest['tag'],'draft':False,'prerelease':True,
                     'assets':[{'name':name,'browser_download_url':prefix+name} for name in ('graild-update.json',manifest['asset'])]}
            return LocalReleaseResponse(json.dumps([release]).encode())
        if url==prefix+'graild-update.json':return LocalReleaseResponse(Path(context['manifest']).read_bytes())
        if url==prefix+manifest['asset']:return LocalReleaseResponse(filename=context['asset'])
        raise RuntimeError('Unexpected URL in isolated release-feed simulation.')


def run(filename, post_update=False):
    root,context=read_context(filename)
    os.environ['APPDATA']=str(root/'roaming')
    os.environ['LOCALAPPDATA']=str(root/'local')
    os.environ['SDL_AUDIODRIVER']='dummy'
    os.environ['GRAILD_BETA_TEST_CONTEXT']=str(root/'context.json')
    import webview
    from app_version import APP_VERSION
    from web_app import JuiceSpotApp,resource_path
    from canary_integration import CanaryIntegration
    # Keep this updater-only diagnostic from changing caches or scanning music.
    original_scan=CanaryIntegration.start_era_scan
    original_load=JuiceSpotApp.load_tracks
    CanaryIntegration.start_era_scan=lambda *args,**kwargs:{'status':'idle'}
    def no_music_scan(self):self.tracks=[];self.current_track=None;self.library_error=None
    JuiceSpotApp.load_tracks=no_music_scan
    try:app=JuiceSpotApp()
    finally:
        CanaryIntegration.start_era_scan=original_scan
        JuiceSpotApp.load_tracks=original_load
    app._canary.start=lambda *args,**kwargs:{'status':'failed','message':'Canary network disabled in isolated updater test.'}
    app._updates.session=LocalReleaseTransport(context)
    app._updates.save_settings('[REDACTED_GITHUB_OWNER]/GRAILD-Releases','beta',True)
    account=app.get_account_state()
    if not account.get('authenticated'):
        raise RuntimeError('The copied persistent session did not restore; no update test was started.')
    auth={'authenticated':True,'discord_linked':'discord' in account['profile']['linked_providers']}
    profile=Path(os.environ['APPDATA'])/'JuiceSpot'
    baseline_file=root/'baseline.json'
    rollback=(root/'update-started.json').exists() and not post_update
    role='post-update' if post_update else 'rollback' if rollback else 'initial'
    window=webview.create_window('GRAILD isolated beta update validation',resource_path('index.html'),js_api=app,hidden=True)
    app._window=window
    window.events.loaded+=app._on_window_loaded
    def loaded():
        try:
            if role=='initial':
                baseline={'profile':profile_snapshot(profile),'auth':auth}
                write_json(baseline_file,baseline)
                deadline=time.monotonic()+50
                while time.monotonic()<deadline:
                    state=app.get_update_state()
                    if state['status']=='available':break
                    if state['status']=='error':raise RuntimeError(state['message'])
                    time.sleep(.1)
                else:raise RuntimeError('v0.8.1 was not detected in the simulated release feed.')
                window.evaluate_js('renderUpdateState('+json.dumps(state)+')')
                notification=window.evaluate_js("!document.getElementById('update-notification').classList.contains('hidden') && document.getElementById('update-version').textContent === '0.8.1' && document.getElementById('update-notes').textContent.length > 0")
                if context.get('expected_failure'):
                    notification=window.evaluate_js("!document.getElementById('update-notification').classList.contains('hidden')")
                if not notification:raise RuntimeError('Native update notification did not display correctly.')
                write_json(root/'update-started.json',{'started':True})
                window.evaluate_js('updateNow(); true')
                progress=False
                deadline=time.monotonic()+100
                while time.monotonic()<deadline:
                    state=app.get_update_state()
                    if state['status']=='downloading' and state.get('downloaded',0)>0:progress=True
                    if state['status']=='installing':
                        write_json(root/'initial-report.json',{'passed':True,'version':APP_VERSION,'notification':True,'progress_observed':progress,'verified_download':True,'job':str(app._updates.ready_job),'auth':auth})
                        return
                    if state['status']=='error':raise RuntimeError(state['message'])
                    time.sleep(.1)
                raise RuntimeError('The updater did not reach installation.')
            baseline=json.loads(baseline_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            after=profile_snapshot(profile)
            if after!=baseline['profile']:raise RuntimeError('The copied profile/settings/cache changed during update.')
            if auth!=baseline['auth']:raise RuntimeError('Persistent authentication did not survive update.')
            result={'passed':True,'role':role,'version':APP_VERSION,'discord_session_retained':auth['discord_linked'],
                    'persistent_login_retained':True,'settings_and_cache_retained':True}
            write_json(root/(role+'-report.json'),result)
            # Give the real helper time to observe the native startup health signal.
            threading.Timer(2,window.destroy).start()
        except Exception as error:
            write_json(root/'diagnostic-error.json',{'error':str(error),'role':role,'version':APP_VERSION})
            window.destroy()
    window.events.loaded+=loaded
    watchdog=threading.Timer(150,window.destroy);watchdog.daemon=True;watchdog.start()
    try:webview.start()
    finally:watchdog.cancel()
    return 1 if (root/'diagnostic-error.json').exists() else 0

branding/generate_icons.py

"""Convert the supplied logo into app assets without changing its design.

Requires Pillow only when regenerating icons; the app uses the generated files.
"""
from pathlib import Path
from PIL import Image


def main():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    with Image.open(root / 'branding' / 'logo-source.png') as source:
        image = source.convert('RGBA')
        if image.width != image.height:
            raise SystemExit('Use a square logo; automatic cropping is disabled.')
        assets = root / 'assets'
        assets.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        image.resize((256, 256), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS).save(assets / 'graild-logo.png', optimize=True)
        image.save(assets / 'graild.ico', format='ICO', sizes=[(n, n) for n in (16, 24, 32, 48, 64, 128, 256)])
        image.resize((1024, 1024), Image.Resampling.LANCZOS).save(assets / 'graild.icns', format='ICNS')
    print('Generated header PNG, multi-size Windows ICO, and macOS ICNS.')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

mkdir "%BUILD_TEMP%\spec"

echo Building the standalone signed-update installer...
py -3.13 -m PyInstaller --noconfirm --clean --noconsole --onefile --name GRAILDUpdater --icon "%~dp0assets\graild.ico" --distpath "%BUILD_TEMP%\dist" --workpath "%BUILD_TEMP%\work" --specpath "%BUILD_TEMP%\spec" graild_updater.py
if errorlevel 1 goto build_failed

if errorlevel 1 goto build_failed

if errorlevel 1 goto build_failed

echo.
py -3.13 "%~dp0publish_build.py" "%BUILD_TEMP%\dist\JuiceGuesserMain.exe" "%~dp0dist\JuiceGuesserMain.exe"
if errorlevel 1 goto build_failed
goto finish

:build_failed
set "BUILD_RESULT=1"
echo.
echo Build failed. Check the error messages above.

:finish
if /i "%~1"=="--no-pause" goto done
pause
:done
exit /b %BUILD_RESULT%

================================================================================
build_macos.py

"""Build a native macOS app; run separately on Apple Silicon and Intel Macs."""
import argparse
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import uuid


UI_ASSET_FILES = (
    'tailwind.css', 'fonts.css', 'inter-400.ttf', 'inter-600.ttf',
    'inter-700.ttf', 'inter-900.ttf', 'Inter-OFL.txt', 'Tailwind-LICENSE.txt',
    'graild-logo.png', 'graild.ico', 'graild.icns',
)


def ensure_ui_assets(root):
    """Repair browser uploads that flattened assets/ into the repository root.

    Only the known UI resources are copied, and existing assets are preserved.
    Validate the whole list before creating anything so incomplete uploads fail
    with an actionable message rather than a PyInstaller traceback.
    """
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    assets = root / 'assets'
    missing = []
    repairs = []
    for name in UI_ASSET_FILES:
        target = assets / name
        if target.is_file():
            continue
        source = root / name
        if source.is_file():
            repairs.append((source, target))
        else:
            missing.append('assets/' + name)
    if missing:
        raise SystemExit('Missing UI resources: ' + ', '.join(missing) +
                         '. Upload the assets folder, or its files to the repository root; the builder can restore the folder.')
    if repairs:
        assets.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        for source, target in repairs:
            shutil.copyfile(source, target)
            print('Restored uploaded resource: assets/' + target.name, flush=True)
    return assets


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--output-dir', type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--expected-arch', choices=['arm64', 'x86_64'])
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if sys.platform != 'darwin':
        raise SystemExit('Build this app on a Mac. PyInstaller cannot produce a macOS app from this Windows installation.')
    from app_version import APP_VERSION
    import imageio_ffmpeg
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    arch = platform.machine()
    if arch not in ('arm64', 'x86_64'):
        raise SystemExit('Use native arm64 or x86_64 Python.')
    if args.expected_arch and arch != args.expected_arch:
        raise SystemExit('This build kit requires native ' + args.expected_arch + ' Python.')
    ffmpeg_arch = subprocess.check_output(['lipo', '-archs', imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()], text=True)
    if arch not in ffmpeg_arch.split():
        raise SystemExit('The installed FFmpeg binary does not match this Python architecture.')
    output = args.output_dir.resolve() if args.output_dir else root / 'dist' / 'releases' / ('v' + APP_VERSION) / ('macOS-' + arch)
    if output.exists():
        raise SystemExit('Output already exists; archive it before rebuilding. Nothing was overwritten.')
    ensure_ui_assets(root)
    stage = root / 'build' / ('mac-' + uuid.uuid4().hex)
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-m', 'PyInstaller', '--noconfirm', '--clean',
                    '--distpath', str(stage / 'dist'), '--workpath', str(stage / 'work'),
                    str(root / 'GRAILD-mac.spec')], cwd=root, check=True)
    output.mkdir(parents=True)
    app = output / 'GRAILD.app'
    shutil.move(str(stage / 'dist' / 'GRAILD.app'), app)
    subprocess.run(['codesign', '--verify', '--deep', '--strict', str(app)], check=True)
    archive = output / ('GRAILD-' + APP_VERSION + '-macOS-' + arch + '.zip')
    subprocess.run(['ditto', '-c', '-k', '--sequesterRsrc', '--keepParent', str(app), str(archive)], check=True)
    from mac_update_security import archive_bundle_digest, bundle_digest, verify_bundle
    digest = archive_bundle_digest(archive)
    if digest != bundle_digest(app):
        raise SystemExit('The archived Mac app does not match the native bundle. Do not publish it.')
    verify_bundle(app, {'bundle_sha256':digest,'version':APP_VERSION,'architecture':arch})
    print('Built locally (not published):', archive)
    print('Distribution still requires Developer ID signing/notarization and testing on this Mac.')


if __name__ == '__main__': main()

================================================================================
canary_client.py
================================================================================
"""Public, metadata-only Canary API v1 client with an offline SQLite cache."""
import base64
from contextlib import contextmanager
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import random
import sqlite3
import threading
import time
from urllib.parse import quote

import requests

BASE_URL = "https://canary.best/api/v1"
SONG_FIELDS = ("id", "title", "artist", "era", "category", "duration_seconds", "duration_str", "page_url", "cover_url")
TRACKER_FIELDS = ("alt_names", "producers", "engineers", "record_date", "dates", "first_previewed", "additional_info")


class CanaryError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, message, status=None, retry_after=0):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status = status
        self.retry_after = retry_after


def public_song(song):
    result = {key: song[key] for key in SONG_FIELDS if key in song}
    tracker = song.get("tracker") or {}
    result["tracker"] = {key: tracker[key] for key in TRACKER_FIELDS if key in tracker} if isinstance(tracker, dict) else {}
    return result


class CanaryClient:
    def __init__(self, cache_path, session=None, interval=0.8, timeout=(5, 15)):
        self.cache_path = str(cache_path)
        Path(self.cache_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.session = session or requests.Session()
        self.session.headers.update({"User-Agent": "GRAILD/1.0 CanaryMetadataIntegration", "Accept": "application/json"})
        self.interval, self.timeout = interval, timeout
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        self.last_request = 0.0
        self.cooldown = 0.0
        with self._db() as db:
            db.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_cache (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, saved REAL NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL)")

    @contextmanager
    def _db(self):
        connection = sqlite3.connect(self.cache_path, timeout=10)
        try:
            with connection:
                yield connection
        finally:
            connection.close()

    @staticmethod
    def _id(song_id):
        value = str(song_id).strip()
        if not value or value in (".", "..") or len(value) > 256 or any(char in value for char in "/?#\\"):
            raise CanaryError("Invalid Canary song identifier.", 400)
        return quote(value, safe="")

    def _read(self, key):
        with self._db() as db:
            row = db.execute("SELECT saved, payload FROM api_cache WHERE key = ?", (key,)).fetchone()
        if row:
            try:
                payload = json.loads(row[1])
                return (row[0], payload) if isinstance(payload, dict) else None
            except (ValueError, TypeError):
                pass
        return None

    def _write(self, key, payload):
        with self._db() as db:
            db.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO api_cache VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (key, time.time(), json.dumps(payload)))

    def _get(self, path, params=None, image=False, ttl=3600):
        key = json.dumps([path, params or {}, image], sort_keys=True)
        with self.lock:
            cached = self._read(key)
            if cached and time.time() - cached[0] < ttl:
                return {**cached[1], "_cached": True, "_stale": False}
            try:
                if time.monotonic() < self.cooldown:
                    raise CanaryError("Canary is rate limited. Please wait before trying again.", 429,
                                      max(1, int(self.cooldown - time.monotonic())))
                delay = self.interval - (time.monotonic() - self.last_request)
                if delay > 0:
                    time.sleep(delay)
                self.last_request = time.monotonic()
                response = self.session.get(BASE_URL + path, params=params, timeout=self.timeout, stream=image)
                try:
                    status = response.status_code
                    if status != 200:
                        messages = {400: "Canary could not process those search filters.",
                                    404: "That song or artwork was not found on Canary.",
                                    429: "Canary is rate limited. Please wait before trying again.",
                                    503: "Canary's catalogue is temporarily unavailable."}
                        retry = 60
                        if status == 429:
                            try:
                                retry = max(1, min(600, int(response.headers.get("Retry-After", "60"))))
                            except ValueError:
                                pass
                            self.cooldown = time.monotonic() + retry
                        raise CanaryError(messages.get(status, "Canary is temporarily unavailable."), status, retry if status == 429 else 0)
                    if image:
                        mime = response.headers.get("Content-Type", "").split(";")[0].lower()
                        if mime not in ("image/jpeg", "image/png", "image/webp", "image/gif", "image/avif"):
                            raise CanaryError("Canary did not return a supported cover image.")
                        content = bytearray()
                        for chunk in response.iter_content(65536):
                            content.extend(chunk)
                            if len(content) > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
                                raise CanaryError("This Canary artwork is too large to display.")
                        payload = {"image": "data:" + mime + ";base64," + base64.b64encode(content).decode("ascii")}
                    else:
                        payload = response.json()
                        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
                            raise CanaryError("Canary returned an unexpected metadata response.")
                    self._write(key, payload)
                    return {**payload, "_cached": False, "_stale": False}
                finally:
                    response.close()
            except (requests.Timeout, requests.ConnectionError) as error:
                failure = CanaryError("Canary is offline or the request timed out. Local playback is still available.")
            except requests.RequestException:
                failure = CanaryError("Could not contact Canary. Local playback is still available.")
            except ValueError:
                failure = CanaryError("Canary returned unreadable metadata. Please try again later.")
            except CanaryError as error:
                if error.status in (400, 404):
                    raise
                failure = error
            if cached:
                return {**cached[1], "_cached": True, "_stale": True,
                        "_notice": str(failure) + " Showing saved metadata."}
            raise failure

    def get_catalog(self):
        return self._get("/catalog", ttl=86400)

    def search_songs(self, query="", era="", category="", artist="", page=0, limit=20):
        if not isinstance(page, int) or not isinstance(limit, int) or page < 0 or not 1 <= limit <= 100:
            raise CanaryError("Invalid catalogue page or page size.", 400)
        params = {"page": page, "limit": limit}
        for key, value in (("q", query), ("era", era), ("category", category), ("artist", artist)):
            if str(value).strip():
                params[key] = str(value).strip()
        data = self._get("/songs", params)
        if not isinstance(data.get("songs"), list):
            raise CanaryError("Canary returned an unexpected song list.")
        return {**data, "songs": [public_song(song) for song in data["songs"] if isinstance(song, dict)]}

    def random_song(self, era=""):
        first = self.search_songs(era=era, limit=1)
        total = int(first.get("total", 0))
        if not total:
            raise CanaryError("Canary has no songs in the selected recording era.")
        page = random.randrange(total)
        result = first if page == 0 else self.search_songs(era=era, page=page, limit=1)
        if not result.get("songs"):
            raise CanaryError("Canary's catalogue changed. Please choose another random song.")
        song = result["songs"][0]
        return {"song": song, "_notice": result.get("_notice", "")}

    def cached_recording_eras(self):
        saved = self._read("recording-eras-v1")
        return saved[1].get("eras", []) if saved else []

    def get_song_index(self):
        saved = self._read("catalog-song-index-v1")
        if saved and time.time() - saved[0] < 86400:
            return {**saved[1], "_cached": True}
        records = {}
        notice = ""
        try:
            for page in range(100):
                result = self.search_songs(page=page, limit=100)
                notice = notice or result.get("_notice", "")
                for song in result["songs"]:
                    if song.get("id"):
                        records[song["id"]] = song
                total = int(result.get("total", 0))
                if (page + 1) * 100 >= total:
                    if len(records) < total:
                        raise CanaryError("Canary's catalogue is changing. Automatic era checks will retry later.")
                    break
                if not result["songs"]:
                    raise CanaryError("Canary returned an incomplete catalogue. Please retry later.")
            else:
                raise CanaryError("The catalogue is too large to check safely. Please retry later.")
        except CanaryError as error:
            if saved:
                return {**saved[1], "_cached": True, "_stale": True,
                        "_notice": str(error) + " Using the saved Canary catalogue."}
            raise
        data = {"songs": list(records.values()), "_notice": notice}
        self._write("catalog-song-index-v1", data)
        self._write("recording-eras-v1", {"eras": sorted({song["era"] for song in records.values() if song.get("era")}), "_notice": notice})
        return data

    def get_recording_eras(self):
        cached = self._read("recording-eras-v1")
        if cached and time.time() - cached[0] < 86400:
            return {**cached[1], "_cached": True}
        index = self.get_song_index()
        return {"eras": sorted({song["era"] for song in index["songs"] if song.get("era")}),
                "_notice": index.get("_notice", "")}

    def get_song(self, song_id):
        data = self._get("/songs/" + self._id(song_id), ttl=86400)
        if not data.get("id") or not data.get("title") or str(data["id"]) != str(song_id):
            raise CanaryError("Canary returned incomplete song details.")
        return {**public_song(data), **{key: value for key, value in data.items() if key.startswith("_")}}

    def get_cover(self, song_id):
        return self._get("/songs/" + self._id(song_id) + "/cover", image=True, ttl=7 * 86400)

    def get_covers(self, song_id):
        return self._get("/songs/" + self._id(song_id) + "/covers", ttl=86400)

    def search_covers(self, query, limit=10):
        if not str(query).strip():
            raise CanaryError("Enter a song title to search covers.", 400)
        return self._get("/covers/search", {"q": str(query).strip(), "limit": max(1, min(100, int(limit)))}, ttl=86400)

================================================================================
canary_integration.py
================================================================================
"""Local-file matching and bounded background jobs for Canary metadata."""
from contextlib import contextmanager
from difflib import SequenceMatcher
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import sqlite3
import threading
import time
import unicodedata
import uuid

from canary_client import CanaryClient, CanaryError


def normalized_title(value):
    value = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", str(value)).casefold().replace("_", " ")
    value = re.sub(r"^juice\s*wrld\s*[-:]+\s*", "", value)
    value = re.sub(r"^\d{1,3}[. _-]+", "", value)
    return " ".join(re.findall(r"[^\W_]+", value, re.UNICODE))


def search_title(filename):
    value = Path(filename).stem.replace("_", " ")
    value = re.sub(r"^juice\s*wrld\s*[-:]+\s*", "", value, flags=re.I)
    value = re.sub(r"^\d{1,3}[. _-]+", "", value)
    value = re.sub(r"\s*[-(\[]?\s*(?:session(?:s| edit)?|alternate(?: version)?|alt(?: version)?|demo|original(?: version)?|normal(?: version)?|studio(?: version)?|album version|v\d+|version \d+)[)\]]?\s*$", "", value, flags=re.I)
    return value.strip(" -_()[]")


def rank_candidates(filename, songs):
    local = normalized_title(Path(filename).stem)
    base = normalized_title(search_title(filename))
    candidates = []
    for song in songs:
        title = normalized_title(song.get("title", ""))
        aliases = (song.get("tracker") or {}).get("alt_names") or []
        if isinstance(aliases, str):
            aliases = [aliases]
        aliases = [normalized_title(alias) for alias in aliases]
        if local == title:
            score, reason = 100, "Exact title. Confirm this is the same recording."
        elif local in aliases:
            score, reason = 95, "Exact alternate name. Confirm the recording version."
        elif base == title or base in aliases:
            score, reason = 75, "Related title; the local filename indicates a different version or session edit."
        else:
            score = int(SequenceMatcher(None, base, title).ratio() * 60)
            reason = "Possible title match. Verify the era, category, and recording before linking."
        candidates.append({**song, "match_score": score, "match_reason": reason})
    return sorted(candidates, key=lambda song: (-song["match_score"], song.get("title", "")))


def era_title_key(value):
    # These are catalogue naming variants of a session, not a licence to strip
    # arbitrary demo/master/version markers or link recordings automatically.
    value = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", str(value)).casefold().replace("_", " ")
    value = re.sub(r"^juice\s*wrld\s*[-:]+\s*", "", value)
    # Bare leading numbers can be part of the real title (for example 10 Feet).
    # Strip only explicit track numbering such as "01 - " or "01. ".
    value = re.sub(r"^\d{1,3}(?:\s+-\s+|\.\s+)", "", value)
    value = " ".join(re.findall(r"[^\W_]+", value, re.UNICODE))
    return re.sub(r"\b(?:sessions|session edits?)$", "session", value)


def automatic_era(filename, title_index):
    # Version markers remain part of the key; similarity alone is never enough.
    title = era_title_key(Path(filename).stem)
    candidates = title_index.get(title, [])
    if not candidates:
        return {"era": "", "status": "not_found"}
    eras = {song.get("era", "") for song in candidates}
    if len(eras) == 1 and "" not in eras:
        return {"era": next(iter(eras)), "status": "checked"}
    return {"era": "", "status": "needs_review"}


def build_title_index(songs):
    index = {}
    for song in songs:
        aliases = (song.get("tracker") or {}).get("alt_names") or []
        if isinstance(aliases, str):
            aliases = [aliases]
        for name in [song.get("title", ""), *aliases]:
            key = era_title_key(name)
            if key:
                index.setdefault(key, []).append(song)
    return index


class CanaryIntegration:
    def __init__(self, directory, client=None):
        directory = Path(directory)
        directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.client = client or CanaryClient(directory / "canary_api.sqlite3")
        self.path = str(directory / "canary_matches.sqlite3")
        self.jobs = {}
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self._scan_generation = 0
        self._scan_pending = None
        self._scan_thread = None
        self._scan_state = {"status": "idle", "total": 0, "processed": 0, "resolved": 0, "review": 0}
        with self.db() as db:
            db.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS local_matches (path TEXT PRIMARY KEY, size INTEGER, modified INTEGER, song_id TEXT, metadata TEXT, artwork TEXT)")
            db.execute("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS era_checks (path TEXT PRIMARY KEY, size INTEGER NOT NULL, modified INTEGER NOT NULL, era TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, checked REAL NOT NULL)")

    @contextmanager
    def db(self):
        connection = sqlite3.connect(self.path, timeout=10)
        connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        try:
            with connection:
                yield connection
        finally:
            connection.close()

    @staticmethod
    def identity(filename):
        path = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(filename))
        stat = os.stat(path)
        return path, stat.st_size, stat.st_mtime_ns

    def local_match(self, filename):
        try:
            path, size, modified = self.identity(filename)
            with self.db() as db:
                row = db.execute("SELECT * FROM local_matches WHERE path = ? AND size = ? AND modified = ?", (path, size, modified)).fetchone()
            if row:
                return {"song": json.loads(row["metadata"]), "artwork": row["artwork"] or ""}
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            pass
        return None

    def library_matches(self, directory, filenames):
        with self.db() as db:
            rows = {row["path"]: row for row in db.execute("SELECT path, size, modified, metadata FROM local_matches")}
        matches = {}
        for filename in filenames:
            try:
                path, size, modified = self.identity(os.path.join(directory, filename))
                row = rows.get(path)
                if row and row["size"] == size and row["modified"] == modified:
                    matches[filename] = json.loads(row["metadata"])
            except (OSError, ValueError):
                continue
        return matches

    def library_era_checks(self, directory, filenames):
        with self.db() as db:
            rows = {row["path"]: row for row in db.execute("SELECT * FROM era_checks")}
        result = {}
        for filename in filenames:
            try:
                path, size, modified = self.identity(os.path.join(directory, filename))
                row = rows.get(path)
                if row and row["size"] == size and row["modified"] == modified:
                    result[filename] = {"era": row["era"], "status": row["status"], "checked": row["checked"]}
            except OSError:
                pass
        # An explicit recording confirmation always takes precedence.
        for filename, song in self.library_matches(directory, filenames).items():
            result[filename] = {"era": song.get("era", ""), "status": "confirmed", "checked": time.time()}
        return result

    def start_era_scan(self, directory, filenames, force=False):
        directory = os.path.abspath(directory)
        with self.lock:
            self._scan_generation += 1
            self._scan_pending = (directory, list(filenames), bool(force), self._scan_generation)
            self._scan_state = {"status": "queued", "directory": directory, "total": len(filenames),
                                "processed": 0, "resolved": 0, "review": 0}
            if self._scan_thread is None or not self._scan_thread.is_alive():
                self._scan_thread = threading.Thread(target=self._era_scan_worker, name="Canary-era-scan", daemon=True)
                self._scan_thread.start()
        return self.era_scan_status(directory)

    def era_scan_status(self, directory):
        with self.lock:
            if self._scan_state.get("directory") != os.path.abspath(directory):
                return {"status": "idle", "total": 0, "processed": 0, "resolved": 0, "review": 0}
            return {key: value for key, value in self._scan_state.items() if key != "directory"}

    def _era_scan_worker(self):
        while True:
            with self.lock:
                if self._scan_pending is None:
                    self._scan_thread = None
                    return
                directory, filenames, force, generation = self._scan_pending
                self._scan_pending = None
                self._scan_state["status"] = "running"
            try:
                existing = self.library_era_checks(directory, filenames)
                pending = [name for name in filenames if name not in existing or
                           (existing[name]["status"] != "confirmed" and (force or time.time() - existing[name]["checked"] >= 86400))]
                title_index = {}
                notice = ""
                if pending:
                    snapshot = self.client.get_song_index()
                    title_index = build_title_index(snapshot["songs"])
                    notice = snapshot.get("_notice", "")
                for filename in pending:
                    with self.lock:
                        if generation != self._scan_generation:
                            break
                    full_path = os.path.join(directory, filename)
                    try:
                        identity = self.identity(full_path)
                        outcome = automatic_era(filename, title_index)
                        if self.identity(full_path) != identity:
                            continue
                        with self.db() as db:
                            db.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO era_checks VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                                       (*identity, outcome["era"], outcome["status"], time.time()))
                        existing[filename] = outcome
                    except OSError:
                        continue
                    with self.lock:
                        if generation == self._scan_generation:
                            self._scan_state["processed"] = len(existing)
                with self.lock:
                    if generation == self._scan_generation:
                        self._scan_state.update({"status": "complete", "processed": len(filenames),
                            "resolved": sum(bool(value.get("era")) for value in existing.values()),
                            "review": sum(not value.get("era") for value in existing.values()), "notice": notice})
            except Exception as error:
                with self.lock:
                    if generation == self._scan_generation:
                        self._scan_state.update({"status": "failed", "message": str(error) if isinstance(error, CanaryError) else
                                                "Automatic Canary era check failed. Local playback is still available."})

    def forget(self, filename):
        path = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(filename))
        with self.db() as db:
            db.execute("DELETE FROM local_matches WHERE path = ?", (path,))

    def confirm(self, filename, song_id):
        identity = self.identity(filename)
        info = self.song_info(song_id)
        if self.identity(filename) != identity:
            raise CanaryError("The local file changed. Please search for its metadata again.")
        with self.db() as db:
            db.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO local_matches VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                       (*identity, info["song"]["id"], json.dumps(info["song"]), info["artwork"]))
        return {**info, "confirmed": True, "filename": Path(filename).name}

    def song_info(self, song_id):
        song = self.client.get_song(song_id)
        notice = song.get("_notice", "")
        artwork = ""
        try:
            cover = self.client.get_cover(song_id)
            artwork = cover.get("image", "")
            notice = notice or cover.get("_notice", "")
        except CanaryError as error:
            notice = (notice + " " + str(error)).strip()
        return {"song": song, "artwork": artwork, "notice": notice}

    def local_info(self, filename, query=None):
        saved = self.local_match(filename)
        if saved and query is None:
            try:
                info = self.confirm(filename, saved["song"]["id"])
                return info
            except CanaryError as error:
                return {**saved, "confirmed": True, "filename": Path(filename).name,
                        "notice": str(error) + " Showing confirmed local metadata."}
        query = search_title(filename) if query is None else str(query).strip()
        if not query:
            raise CanaryError("Enter a title to find a Canary recording.", 400)
        result = self.client.search_songs(query, limit=20)
        return {"confirmed": False, "filename": Path(filename).name, "query": query,
                "candidates": rank_candidates(filename, result["songs"]),
                "notice": result.get("_notice", ""),
                "message": "Choose a recording explicitly. Similar titles and session edits are never merged automatically."}

    def start(self, task, **arguments):
        with self.lock:
            # Completed results expire; active work is bounded to protect the API.
            expired = [key for key, job in self.jobs.items() if job["status"] != "running" and time.monotonic() - job["created"] > 300]
            for key in expired:
                del self.jobs[key]
            if sum(job["status"] == "running" for job in self.jobs.values()) >= 4:
                return {"status": "failed", "message": "Please wait for the current Canary requests to finish."}
            if len(self.jobs) >= 40:
                finished = next((key for key, job in self.jobs.items() if job["status"] != "running"), None)
                if finished:
                    del self.jobs[finished]
            job_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
            self.jobs[job_id] = {"status": "running", "created": time.monotonic()}
        def work():
            try:
                if task == "search":
                    result = self.client.search_songs(**arguments)
                elif task == "catalog":
                    result = self.client.get_catalog()
                elif task == "random":
                    result = self.client.random_song(**arguments)
                elif task == "eras":
                    result = self.client.get_recording_eras()
                elif task == "info":
                    result = self.song_info(**arguments)
                elif task == "local":
                    result = self.local_info(**arguments)
                elif task == "confirm":
                    result = self.confirm(**arguments)
                else:
                    raise CanaryError("Unknown Canary operation.")
                outcome = {"status": "complete", "result": result}
            except CanaryError as error:
                outcome = {"status": "failed", "message": str(error), "http_status": error.status, "retry_after": error.retry_after}
            except Exception:
                outcome = {"status": "failed", "message": "Canary metadata could not be loaded. Your local audio and game progress are unchanged."}
            with self.lock:
                self.jobs[job_id].update(outcome)
        threading.Thread(target=work, name="Canary-metadata", daemon=True).start()
        return {"job_id": job_id, "status": "running"}

    def poll(self, job_id):
        with self.lock:
            job = self.jobs.get(str(job_id))
            return {key: value for key, value in job.items() if key != "created"} if job else {"status": "failed", "message": "This metadata request expired. Please retry."}

================================================================================
canary_smoke.py
