"""Opt-in isolated live integration check for development and packaged Windows builds."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import traceback
import wave
import subprocess


def run(output_filename):
    result = {"passed": False, "checks": []}
    output = Path(output_filename).resolve()
    try:
        if not os.environ.get("GRAILD_SMOKE_REAL_AUDIO"):
            os.environ["SDL_AUDIODRIVER"] = "dummy"
        import pygame
        import requests
        import webview
        from web_app import JuiceSpotApp, resource_path
        from app_version import APP_VERSION
        from audio_files import get_playback_path
        import mutagen, cryptography, imageio_ffmpeg, sqlite3, ssl
        assert Path(resource_path("GRAILDUpdater.exe")).is_file()
        for asset in ("index.html", "canary_ui.js", "update_ui.js", "assets/tailwind.css", "assets/fonts.css", "assets/inter-400.ttf", "assets/inter-600.ttf", "assets/inter-700.ttf", "assets/inter-900.ttf"):
            assert Path(resource_path(asset)).is_file(), asset
        result["checks"].append("bundled Python dependencies, UI assets, fonts and updater helper")
        result["frozen"] = bool(getattr(__import__("sys"), "frozen", False))
        result["audio_driver"] = pygame.mixer.get_init()
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="GRAILD-Canary-check-") as temporary:
            root = Path(temporary)
            os.environ["APPDATA"] = str(root / "profile")
            os.environ["LOCALAPPDATA"] = str(root / "local")
            music = root / "music"
            music.mkdir()
            with wave.open(str(music / "Rental.wav"), "wb") as sound:
                sound.setnchannels(1)
                sound.setsampwidth(2)
                sound.setframerate(44100)
                sound.writeframes(b"\0\0" * 44100)
            original_loader = JuiceSpotApp.load_config_folder
            JuiceSpotApp.load_config_folder = lambda self: str(music)
            try:
                app = JuiceSpotApp()
            finally:
                JuiceSpotApp.load_config_folder = original_loader
            registration = app.register_account("canary_test", "isolated-smoke-password")
            assert registration["success"], registration
            result["checks"].append("isolated profile and existing account/achievement flow")
            assert not app.audio_error, app.audio_error
            leaderboard = app.get_global_leaderboard()
            result["live_leaderboard"] = {"success": leaderboard.get("success"), "message": leaderboard.get("message", "")}
            try:
                health = requests.get(app.get_discord_broker_url() + "/healthz", timeout=25)
                result["discord_broker_health_status"] = health.status_code
            except requests.RequestException as error:
                result["discord_broker_health_status"] = type(error).__name__
            result["checks"].append("packaged broker connectivity and public leaderboard request (no score upload)")
            updates = app.check_for_updates()
            assert updates["current_version"] == APP_VERSION
            if not updates["repository"]:
                assert updates["status"] == "unconfigured"
            result["checks"].append("configured app version and update settings")
            scan_deadline = time.monotonic() + 70
            while time.monotonic() < scan_deadline:
                scan = app.get_canary_era_scan()
                if scan["status"] == "complete":
                    break
                if scan["status"] == "failed":
                    raise AssertionError(scan)
                time.sleep(0.1)
            else:
                raise AssertionError("Automatic era scan timed out")
            checked = app._canary.library_era_checks(str(music), ["Rental.wav"])
            assert checked["Rental.wav"]["era"] == "DRFL", checked
            assert app._canary.local_match(music / "Rental.wav") is None
            result["checks"].append("automatic startup Canary era check without linking recordings")
            def wait(operation, options=None):
                started = app.start_canary_request(operation, options)
                assert started.get("job_id"), started
                deadline = time.monotonic() + 70
                while time.monotonic() < deadline:
                    job = app.get_canary_request(started["job_id"])
                    if job["status"] == "complete":
                        return job["result"]
                    if job["status"] == "failed":
                        raise AssertionError(job)
                    time.sleep(0.1)
                raise AssertionError("Background request timed out")
            search = wait("search", {"query": "Rental"})
            assert any(song["id"] == "rental" for song in search["songs"])
            result["checks"].append("live metadata search in background worker")
            candidates = wait("local", {"filename": "Rental.wav"})
            assert not candidates["confirmed"]
            assert app._canary.local_match(music / "Rental.wav") is None
            info = wait("confirm", {"filename": "Rental.wav", "song_id": "rental"})
            assert info["confirmed"] and info["song"]["id"] == "rental"
            assert info["artwork"].startswith("data:image/"), info.get("notice")
            result["checks"].append("explicit local-file confirmation, live details and artwork")
            era = app.set_recording_era(info["song"]["era"])
            assert era["success"] and era["state"]["eligible_track_count"] == 1
            assert app.get_catalog()[0]["canary"]["id"] == "rental"
            for mode in ("classic", "random"):
                app.set_game_mode(mode)
                assert app.play_snippet()["ok"]
                app.stop_audio()
            assert app.play_correct_answer() is None
            app.stop_audio()
            pygame.mixer.music.unload()
            result["checks"].append("era eligibility and both local-only audio modes with bundled decoder")
            choice = wait("random", {"era": info["song"]["era"]})
            assert choice["song"]["era"] == info["song"]["era"]
            assert choice["song"]["page_url"].startswith("https://canary.best/song/")
            assert app.set_library_source("canary")["success"]
            app.load_game_preferences()
            assert app.library_source == "canary" and app.recording_era == info["song"]["era"]
            assert app.set_library_source("local")["success"]
            result["checks"].append("per-account era/source preferences and era-filtered random Canary page selection")
            client = app._canary.client
            with client._db() as db:
                db.execute("UPDATE api_cache SET saved = 0")
            original_get = client.session.get
            def offline(*args, **kwargs):
                raise requests.ConnectionError("isolated offline check")
            client.session.get = offline
            try:
                assert client.search_songs("Rental")["_stale"]
            finally:
                client.session.get = original_get
            result["checks"].append("offline metadata cache fallback")
            # Exercise the actual bundled HTML, JavaScript, and native WebView2 bridge.
            window = webview.create_window("GRAILD Canary integration check", resource_path("index.html"), js_api=app, hidden=True)
            app._window = window
            ui_result = {"passed": False}
            def loaded():
                try:
                    ready = window.evaluate_js("typeof canaryRequest === 'function' && !!document.getElementById('recording-era-select') && !!document.getElementById('song-information')")
                    assert ready, "Canary UI assets did not load"
                    styling = window.evaluate_js("(() => { const node = document.createElement('div'); node.className = 'hidden bg-purple-600'; document.body.append(node); const style = getComputedStyle(node); const ok = style.display === 'none' && style.backgroundColor === 'rgb(147, 51, 234)'; node.remove(); return ok && !document.querySelector('script[src^=https],link[href^=https]'); })()")
                    assert styling, "Local stylesheet did not load or the UI still depends on a remote script/font"
                    deadline = time.monotonic() + 15
                    while not window.evaluate_js("document.fonts.check('400 16px Inter') && document.fonts.status === 'loaded'"):
                        assert time.monotonic() < deadline, "Bundled font did not load"
                        time.sleep(0.1)
                    result["checks"].append("native WebView2 renders local CSS and bundled Inter font without CDN assets")
                    updater_ui = window.evaluate_js("(() => { renderUpdateState(" + json.dumps(app.get_update_state()) + "); return typeof updateNow === 'function' && !!document.getElementById('manual-update-check') && document.getElementById('settings-current-version').textContent === " + json.dumps(APP_VERSION) + "; })()")
                    assert updater_ui, "Packaged update UI did not load"
                    payload = json.dumps(info)
                    ui_result["passed"] = bool(window.evaluate_js("(() => { beginCanaryInfo('Rental.wav'); renderCanarySongInfo(" + payload + ", 'Rental.wav', true); return document.getElementById('song-info-content').textContent.includes('Rental') && document.querySelectorAll('#song-info-content img').length === 1; })()"))
                except Exception:
                    ui_result["error"] = traceback.format_exc()
                finally:
                    window.destroy()
            window.events.loaded += loaded
            watchdog = threading.Timer(45, window.destroy)
            watchdog.daemon = True
            watchdog.start()
            try:
                webview.start()
            finally:
                watchdog.cancel()
            assert ui_result["passed"], ui_result
            result["checks"].append("native hidden WebView2 loads Canary script, Song Information and real artwork")
            app.stop_audio()
            pygame.mixer.music.unload()
            pygame.mixer.quit()
        result["passed"] = True
    except Exception:
        result["error"] = traceback.format_exc()
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return 0 if result["passed"] else 1

================================================================================
canary_ui.js

// Metadata requests run in Python workers; polling keeps the desktop UI responsive.
let canaryPage = 0;
let automaticEraWatchToken = 0;
let canaryBrowserToken = 0;
let canaryInfoToken = 0;
let canaryManifestLoaded = false;
let canaryLocalFilename = null;
let canaryErasLoading = false;
let canaryErasRequested = false;
let canaryRandomToken = 0;
const knownCanaryEras = new Set(['JUTE', 'WOD', 'DRFL']);

function canaryText(tag, text, className = '') {
    const element = document.createElement(tag);
    element.textContent = text;
    element.className = className;
    return element;
}

function canaryButton(text, action) {
    const button = canaryText('button', text, 'rounded-lg border border-purple-500/40 bg-purple-500/10 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold hover:bg-purple-500/20 disabled:opacity-40');
    button.type = 'button';
    button.addEventListener('click', action);
    return button;
}

async function canaryRequest(operation, options = {}) {
    const job = await window.pywebview.api.start_canary_request(operation, options);
    if (job.status === 'failed') throw new Error(job.message);
    const deadline = Date.now() + 90000;
    while (Date.now() < deadline) {
        const status = await window.pywebview.api.get_canary_request(job.job_id);
        if (status.status === 'complete') return status.result;
        if (status.status === 'failed') throw new Error(status.message);
        await new Promise(resolve => window.setTimeout(resolve, 300));
    }
    throw new Error('Canary is taking longer than expected. Please try again later.');
}

function switchCatalogView(view) {
    const remote = view === 'canary';
    document.getElementById('catalog-grid').classList.toggle('hidden', remote);
    document.getElementById('local-format-control').classList.toggle('hidden', remote);
    document.getElementById('canary-browser').classList.toggle('hidden', !remote);
    for (const name of ['local', 'canary']) {
        const button = document.getElementById(`${name}-catalog-tab`);
        const active = name === view;
        button.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(active));
        button.classList.toggle('bg-purple-600', active);
        button.classList.toggle('bg-[#1e1e2f]', !active);
    }
    if (remote && !canaryManifestLoaded) {
        canaryManifestLoaded = true;
        loadCanaryManifest();
        searchCanary(0);
    }
}

async function loadCanaryManifest() {
    const select = document.getElementById('canary-category');
    const defaults = ['Songs', 'Remasters', 'Sessions', 'Masters', 'Freestyle'];
    function populate(categories) {
        const previous = select.value;
        select.replaceChildren();
        for (const category of ['', ...categories]) {
            const option = canaryText('option', category || 'All categories');
            option.value = category;
            select.append(option);
        }
        select.value = previous;
    }
    populate(defaults);
    try {
        const metadata = await canaryRequest('catalog');
        if (Array.isArray(metadata.categories)) populate(metadata.categories);
    } catch (error) {
        // Search remains usable if the manifest is temporarily unavailable.
        document.getElementById('canary-browser-status').textContent = error.message;
    }
}

async function searchCanary(page = 0) {
    const token = ++canaryBrowserToken;
    const status = document.getElementById('canary-browser-status');
    const button = document.getElementById('canary-search-button');
    status.textContent = 'Searching Canary...';
    button.disabled = true;
    document.getElementById('canary-previous').disabled = true;
    document.getElementById('canary-next').disabled = true;
    const query = {query: document.getElementById('canary-search').value.trim(),
                   era: document.getElementById('canary-era').value.trim(),
                   category: document.getElementById('canary-category').value, page};
    try {
        const result = await canaryRequest('search', query);
        if (token !== canaryBrowserToken) return;
        canaryPage = page;
        const songs = result.songs || [];
        const grid = document.getElementById('canary-results');
        grid.replaceChildren();
        for (const song of songs) {
            if (song.era) knownCanaryEras.add(song.era);
            const card = canaryText('article', '', 'glass-card rounded-2xl p-5 space-y-3');
            card.append(canaryText('h3', song.title, 'font-bold text-white'));
            card.append(canaryText('p', `${song.artist || 'Unknown artist'} | ${song.era || 'Era unknown'} | ${song.category || 'Uncategorized'} | ${song.duration_str || 'Duration unknown'}`, 'text-xs text-slate-400'));
            card.append(canaryButton('Song information', () => openCanarySong(song.id)));
            grid.append(card);
        }
        updateCanaryEraSuggestions();
        const total = Number(result.total || 0);
        status.textContent = result._notice || `${total.toLocaleString()} matching songs. Metadata only; audio comes from your local library.`;
        if (!songs.length) grid.append(canaryText('p', 'No matching songs. Try another title, era, or category.', 'text-sm text-slate-400'));
        document.getElementById('canary-pagination').textContent = `Page ${page + 1} of ${Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / 20))}`;
        document.getElementById('canary-previous').disabled = page === 0;
        document.getElementById('canary-next').disabled = (page + 1) * 20 >= total;
    } catch (error) {
        if (token === canaryBrowserToken) status.textContent = error.message;
    } finally {
        if (token === canaryBrowserToken) button.disabled = false;
    }
}

function updateCanaryEraSuggestions() {
    const list = document.getElementById('canary-era-options');
    list.replaceChildren();
    for (const era of [...knownCanaryEras].sort()) {
        const option = document.createElement('option');
        option.value = era;
        list.append(option);
    }
}

function updateRecordingEraOptions(state) {
    const eras = [...new Set([...(state.recording_eras || []), ...(state.recording_era ? [state.recording_era] : [])])];
    for (const id of ['recording-era-select', 'hub-recording-era-select', 'account-recording-era-select']) {
        const select = document.getElementById(id);
        if (!select) continue;
        select.replaceChildren();
        for (const era of ['', ...eras]) {
            const option = canaryText('option', era || 'All eras (no era filter)');
            option.value = era;
            select.append(option);
            if (era) knownCanaryEras.add(era);
        }
        select.value = state.recording_era || '';
        select.disabled = !state.authenticated;
    }
    const eligible = state.eligible_track_count ?? state.track_count;
    const website = state.library_source === 'canary';
    for (const id of ['hub-library-source', 'account-library-source']) {
        const source = document.getElementById(id);
        if (source) { source.value = website ? 'canary' : 'local'; source.disabled = !state.authenticated; }
    }
    document.getElementById('local-game-choices')?.classList.toggle('hidden', website);
    document.getElementById('canary-website-choice')?.classList.toggle('hidden', !website);
    if (website && !canaryErasRequested && !canaryErasLoading) refreshCanaryEraList();
    const summary = website
        ? `Canary website shuffle: ${state.recording_era || 'all recording eras'}. Saved for your account.`
        : state.recording_era
        ? `${eligible} local song(s) in Canary era ${state.recording_era}. Saved for your account.`
        : eras.length
            ? 'All local songs are eligible. Select an era to use Canary-checked songs in that era.'
            : 'All eras selected. Canary checks your songs automatically; uncertain matches can be reviewed in Catalog.';
    for (const id of ['hub-era-summary', 'account-era-summary']) {
        const node = document.getElementById(id);
        if (node) node.textContent = summary;
    }
    updateCanaryEraSuggestions();
}

async function changeRecordingEra(era) {
    ++canaryRandomToken;
    const selects = ['recording-era-select', 'hub-recording-era-select', 'account-recording-era-select'].map(id => document.getElementById(id)).filter(Boolean);
    selects.forEach(select => { select.disabled = true; });
    try {
        const result = await window.pywebview.api.set_recording_era(era);
        renderState(result.state);
        document.getElementById('guess-input').value = '';
        document.getElementById('suggestions').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('cover-container').classList.add('hidden');
        showFeedback(result.state.error || result.message, result.success && result.state.has_track);
        for (const id of ['hub-era-summary', 'account-era-summary']) {
            const node = document.getElementById(id);
            if (node && !result.success) node.textContent = result.message;
        }
    } catch (error) {
        showFeedback('Could not change the recording era. Please try again.');
        selects.forEach(select => { select.disabled = !appState?.authenticated; });
    }
}

function addLocalCanaryControls(card, song) {
    const metadata = song.canary;
    card.append(canaryText('p', metadata
        ? `Canary: ${metadata.title} | ${metadata.era || 'Era unknown'} | ${metadata.category || 'Uncategorized'}`
        : song.canary_era ? `Canary era: ${song.canary_era} (automatically checked)`
        : song.era_status === 'needs_review' ? 'Conflicting Canary eras - review recording'
        : song.era_status === 'not_found' ? 'No exact Canary match - review recording'
        : 'Canary era check pending', 'text-xs text-slate-400'));
    card.append(canaryButton(metadata ? 'Song information' : 'Match on Canary', () => openLocalCanary(song.filename)));
}

function beginCanaryInfo(filename = null) {
    canaryLocalFilename = filename;
    const dialog = document.getElementById('song-information');
    if (!dialog.open) dialog.showModal();
    document.getElementById('song-info-status').textContent = 'Loading song information...';
    document.getElementById('song-info-content').replaceChildren();
    document.getElementById('song-match-search-form').classList.toggle('hidden', !filename);
    if (filename) document.getElementById('song-match-query').value = filename.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '');
    return ++canaryInfoToken;
}

async function openLocalCanary(filename, query) {
    const token = beginCanaryInfo(filename);
    try {
        const options = {filename};
        if (query !== undefined) options.query = query;
        const result = await canaryRequest('local', options);
        if (token !== canaryInfoToken) return;
        if (result.confirmed) renderCanarySongInfo(result, filename, true);
        else renderCanaryCandidates(result, filename);
    } catch (error) {
        if (token === canaryInfoToken) document.getElementById('song-info-status').textContent = error.message;
    }
}

function searchLocalCanary() {
    if (canaryLocalFilename) openLocalCanary(canaryLocalFilename, document.getElementById('song-match-query').value.trim());
}

function renderCanaryCandidates(result, filename) {
    const content = document.getElementById('song-info-content');
    content.replaceChildren();
    document.getElementById('song-info-status').textContent = result.notice || result.message;
    content.append(canaryText('p', `Local file: ${filename}`, 'text-sm font-bold break-words'));
    for (const song of result.candidates || []) {
        const row = canaryText('article', '', 'rounded-xl border border-slate-700 p-4 space-y-2');
        row.append(canaryText('h3', song.title, 'font-bold'));
        row.append(canaryText('p', `${song.era || 'Era unknown'} | ${song.category || 'Uncategorized'} | ${song.duration_str || 'Duration unknown'}`, 'text-xs text-slate-400'));
        row.append(canaryText('p', song.match_reason, 'text-xs text-amber-300'));
        row.append(canaryButton('Review this recording', () => openCanarySong(song.id, filename)));
        content.append(row);
    }
    if (!(result.candidates || []).length) content.append(canaryText('p', 'No candidates found. Search another title or alias below.', 'text-sm text-slate-400'));
}

async function openCanarySong(songId, filename = null) {
    const token = beginCanaryInfo(filename);
    try {
        const info = await canaryRequest('info', {song_id: songId});
        if (token === canaryInfoToken) renderCanarySongInfo(info, filename, false);
    } catch (error) {
        if (token === canaryInfoToken) document.getElementById('song-info-status').textContent = error.message;
    }
}

function renderCanarySongInfo(info, filename, confirmed) {
    const song = info.song;
    const tracker = song.tracker || {};
    const content = document.getElementById('song-info-content');
    content.replaceChildren();
    document.getElementById('song-info-status').textContent = info.notice || (confirmed ? 'Confirmed metadata for this local file.' : 'Canary song metadata');
    if (filename) content.append(canaryText('p', `Local file: ${filename}`, 'text-xs text-slate-400 break-words'));
    if (info.artwork && info.artwork.startsWith('data:image/')) {
        const image = document.createElement('img');
        image.src = info.artwork;
        image.alt = `Cover art for ${song.title}`;
        image.className = 'w-40 h-40 rounded-xl object-cover';
        content.append(image);
    }
    const fields = [['Title', song.title], ['Artist', song.artist], ['Recording era', song.era],
                    ['Category', song.category], ['Duration', song.duration_str || (song.duration_seconds ? `${song.duration_seconds} seconds` : '')],
                    ['Producer', tracker.producers], ['Recording dates', tracker.record_date],
                    ['Other dates', tracker.dates], ['Alternate names', tracker.alt_names]];
    const details = canaryText('dl', '', 'grid grid-cols-[8rem_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-x-4 gap-y-3 text-sm');
    for (const [label, value] of fields) {
        details.append(canaryText('dt', label, 'text-slate-400'));
        details.append(canaryText('dd', Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(', ') : String(value || 'Not provided'), 'whitespace-pre-line break-words text-white'));
    }
    content.append(details);
    if (song.page_url) content.append(canaryButton('Open song on Canary', async () => {
        try {
            const opened = await window.pywebview.api.open_canary_page(song.page_url);
            if (!opened.success) document.getElementById('song-info-status').textContent = 'Could not open the Canary song page.';
        }
        catch (error) { document.getElementById('song-info-status').textContent = 'Could not open your browser.'; }
    }));
    if (filename && !confirmed) {
        content.append(canaryText('p', 'Confirm only if this is the same recording. Linking metadata does not change or merge your local audio.', 'text-xs text-amber-300'));
        content.append(canaryButton('Confirm this recording', event => confirmLocalCanary(event.currentTarget, filename, song.id)));
    }
    if (filename && confirmed) content.append(canaryButton('Remove metadata match', async event => {
        event.currentTarget.disabled = true;
        try {
            const removed = await window.pywebview.api.forget_canary_match(filename);
            if (!removed.success) throw new Error(removed.message);
            await refreshCatalog();
            renderState(await window.pywebview.api.get_state());
            await openLocalCanary(filename, filename.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ''));
        } catch (error) { document.getElementById('song-info-status').textContent = error.message; }
    }));
}

async function confirmLocalCanary(button, filename, songId) {
    const token = canaryInfoToken;
    button.disabled = true;
    document.getElementById('song-info-status').textContent = 'Saving your confirmed match...';
    try {
        const result = await canaryRequest('confirm', {filename, song_id: songId});
        await refreshCatalog();
        renderState(await window.pywebview.api.get_state());
        if (token === canaryInfoToken) renderCanarySongInfo(result, filename, true);
    } catch (error) {
        if (token === canaryInfoToken) document.getElementById('song-info-status').textContent = error.message;
        button.disabled = false;
    }
}

document.getElementById('song-information').addEventListener('close', () => { ++canaryInfoToken; });


async function changeLibrarySource(source) {
    ++canaryRandomToken;
    try {
        const result = await window.pywebview.api.set_library_source(source);
        renderState(result.state);
        if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.message);
        switchTab('hub');
    } catch (error) {
        const status = document.getElementById('hub-era-summary');
        if (status) status.textContent = error.message;
    }
}

async function refreshCanaryEraList() {
    if (canaryErasLoading) return;
    canaryErasRequested = true;
    canaryErasLoading = true;
    const status = document.getElementById('canary-era-load-status');
    const button = document.getElementById('canary-refresh-eras');
    if (button) button.disabled = true;
    if (status) status.textContent = 'Loading recording eras from Canary metadata...';
    try {
        const result = await canaryRequest('eras');
        for (const era of result.eras || []) knownCanaryEras.add(era);
        renderState(await window.pywebview.api.get_state());
        if (status) status.textContent = result._notice || 'Canary recording eras are ready.';
    } catch (error) {
        if (status) status.textContent = error.message;
    } finally {
        canaryErasLoading = false;
        if (button) button.disabled = false;
    }
}

async function playRandomCanarySong() {
    if (!appState?.authenticated) { switchTab('account'); return; }
    const token = ++canaryRandomToken;
    const button = document.getElementById('canary-random-play');
    const status = document.getElementById('canary-random-status');
    button.disabled = true;
    status.textContent = 'Choosing a random Canary song...';
    try {
        const result = await canaryRequest('random', {era: appState.recording_era || ''});
        if (token !== canaryRandomToken || !appState.authenticated || appState.library_source !== 'canary') return;
        const opened = await window.pywebview.api.open_canary_page(result.song.page_url);
        if (!opened.success) throw new Error('Could not open the Canary player in your browser.');
        status.textContent = `Opened ${result.song.title} on Canary. Press Play there to listen.`;
    } catch (error) {
        if (token === canaryRandomToken) status.textContent = error.message;
    } finally { button.disabled = false; }
}


async function watchAutomaticEraScan() {
    const token = ++automaticEraWatchToken;
    const status = document.getElementById('automatic-era-status');
    const button = document.getElementById('automatic-era-recheck');
    if (!status) return;
    try {
        while (token === automaticEraWatchToken) {
            const progress = await window.pywebview.api.get_canary_era_scan();
            if (token !== automaticEraWatchToken) return;
            const running = progress.status === 'running' || progress.status === 'queued';
            button.disabled = running;
            if (running) {
                status.textContent = `Checking Canary eras: ${progress.processed || 0} / ${progress.total || 0} songs. You can keep using the app.`;
                await new Promise(resolve => window.setTimeout(resolve, 1000));
                continue;
            }
            if (progress.status === 'complete') {
                status.textContent = `${progress.resolved || 0} / ${progress.total || 0} songs have Canary eras; ${progress.review || 0} need review or have no exact match.` + (progress.notice ? ` ${progress.notice}` : '');
                await refreshCatalog();
                renderState(await window.pywebview.api.get_state());
            } else if (progress.status === 'failed') {
                status.textContent = progress.message;
            } else {
                status.textContent = 'Choose a music folder to automatically check song eras on Canary.';
            }
            return;
        }
    } catch (error) {
        if (token === automaticEraWatchToken) {
            status.textContent = 'Could not check Canary eras. Local playback is still available; use Recheck to retry.';
            button.disabled = false;
        }
    }
}

async function recheckAutomaticEras() {
    try {
        await window.pywebview.api.start_canary_era_scan();
        watchAutomaticEraScan();
    } catch (error) {
        document.getElementById('automatic-era-status').textContent = 'Could not start the Canary era check. Please retry.';
    }
}

================================================================================
discord_oauth_broker/app.py
================================================================================
"""Discord OAuth broker for the GRAIL'D Windows desktop client."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import secrets
import time
import urllib.parse
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import httpx
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, RedirectResponse, Response
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from tag_rules import ACHIEVEMENT_IDS, TAGS

DISCORD_ISSUER = "https://discord.com"
DISCORD_AUTHORIZE_URL = "https://discord.com/oauth2/authorize"
DISCORD_TOKEN_URL = "https://discord.com/api/v10/oauth2/token"
DISCORD_USER_URL = "https://discord.com/api/v10/users/@me"
CALLBACK_PATH = "/oauth/callback"
STATE_TTL_SECONDS = 600
TICKET_TTL_SECONDS = 120
REDIS_PREFIX = "graild-discord-oauth:v1:"
GLOBAL_SESSION_TTL_SECONDS = 90 * 24 * 60 * 60
GLOBAL_SESSION_PREFIX = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}global-session:"
GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_SCORES = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}leaderboard:scores"
GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_PROFILES = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}leaderboard:profiles"
GLOBAL_PLAYERS = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}players"
GLOBAL_TAG_PREFIX = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}tag:"
GLOBAL_CUSTOM_TAGS = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}custom-tags"
STATE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32,128}$")

CONSUME_TICKET_SCRIPT = """
local raw = redis.call('GET', KEYS[1])
if not raw then
  return 0
end
local ticket = cjson.decode(raw)
if ticket.desktop_state ~= ARGV[1] or ticket.redirect_uri ~= ARGV[2] then
  return -1
end
redis.call('DEL', KEYS[1])
return raw
"""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
  discord_client_id: str
  discord_client_secret: str
  discord_redirect_uri: str
  redis_url: str
  owner_discord_ids: str = "[REDACTED_DISCORD_ACCOUNT_ID]"

  @classmethod
  def from_env(cls) -> "Settings":
    return cls(
        discord_client_id=os.environ.get("DISCORD_CLIENT_ID", "").strip(),
        discord_client_secret=os.environ.get("DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET", ""),
        discord_redirect_uri=os.environ.get("DISCORD_REDIRECT_URI", "").strip(),
        redis_url=os.environ.get("REDIS_URL", "redis://redis:6379/0").strip(),
        owner_discord_ids=os.environ.get("GRAILD_OWNER_DISCORD_IDS", "[REDACTED_DISCORD_ACCOUNT_ID]").strip(),
    )

  def validate(self) -> None:
    if not self.discord_client_id or not self.discord_client_secret:
      raise RuntimeError("DISCORD_CLIENT_ID and DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET must be set on the broker.")
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(self.discord_redirect_uri)
    if (
        parsed.scheme != "https"
        or not parsed.hostname
        or parsed.path != CALLBACK_PATH
        or parsed.query
        or parsed.fragment
        or parsed.username
        or parsed.password
    ):
      raise RuntimeError(
          "DISCORD_REDIRECT_URI must be the public HTTPS callback ending in /oauth/callback."
      )
    if not self.redis_url:
      raise RuntimeError("REDIS_URL must point to a Redis 7 service.")
    if any(not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]{15,22}', value.strip()) for value in self.owner_discord_ids.split(',') if value.strip()):
      raise RuntimeError('GRAILD_OWNER_DISCORD_IDS must contain numeric Discord user IDs separated by commas.')


def desktop_redirect_is_valid(value: str) -> bool:
  """Only permit the exact temporary IPv4 loopback redirect used by web_app.py."""
  try:
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(value)
    port = parsed.port
  except ValueError:
    return False
  return bool(
      parsed.scheme == "http"
      and parsed.hostname == "127.0.0.1"
      and port is not None
      and 1 <= port <= 65535
      and parsed.path == "/"
      and not parsed.query
      and not parsed.fragment
      and not parsed.username
      and not parsed.password
  )


def parse_form(body: bytes, content_type: str, required: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict[str, str]:
  if content_type.split(";", 1)[0].strip().lower() != "application/x-www-form-urlencoded":
    raise HTTPException(status_code=415, detail="Expected application/x-www-form-urlencoded.")
  if len(body) > 8192:
    raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail="Request is too large.")
  try:
    values = urllib.parse.parse_qs(body.decode("utf-8"), keep_blank_values=True, strict_parsing=True)
  except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
    raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Malformed form data.") from None
  result: dict[str, str] = {}
  for name in required:
    items = values.get(name, [])
    if len(items) != 1 or not items[0]:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Missing or repeated field: {name}.")
    result[name] = items[0]
  return result


async def issue_global_session(redis: Any, identity: dict[str, str | None]) -> str:
  token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
  session_key = GLOBAL_SESSION_PREFIX + hashlib.sha256(token.encode("ascii")).hexdigest()
  session_value = json.dumps({
      "provider": identity["provider"],
      "subject": identity["subject"],
      "display_name": identity.get("display_name") or "",
  }, separators=(",", ":"))
  stored = await redis.set(
      session_key,
      session_value,
      ex=GLOBAL_SESSION_TTL_SECONDS,
      nx=True,
  )
  if not stored:
    raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Could not create leaderboard session.")
  await register_global_player(redis, identity)
  return token


def global_player_id(identity):
  return hashlib.sha256(f"{identity['provider']}:{identity['subject']}".encode()).hexdigest()


async def register_global_player(redis, identity):
  player_id = global_player_id(identity)
  await redis.hset(GLOBAL_PLAYERS, player_id, json.dumps({
      'provider': identity['provider'], 'subject': identity['subject'],
      'display_name': identity.get('display_name') or identity['provider'].title() + ' player',
  }))
  return player_id


async def authenticated_global_identity(request: Request) -> dict[str, str]:
  authorization = request.headers.get("authorization", "")
  scheme, separator, token = authorization.partition(" ")
  if not separator or scheme.casefold() != "bearer" or not token:
    raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="A valid leaderboard bearer token is required.")
  key = GLOBAL_SESSION_PREFIX + hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
  value = await request.app.state.redis.get(key)
  if not value:
    raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Leaderboard session expired or was revoked.")
  try:
    identity = json.loads(value)
  except (TypeError, ValueError):
    await request.app.state.redis.delete(key)
    raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Leaderboard session is invalid.") from None
  if identity.get("provider") not in ("google", "discord") or not identity.get("subject"):
    await request.app.state.redis.delete(key)
    raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Leaderboard session is invalid.")
  return identity


def make_app(
    settings: Settings | None = None,
    redis_client: Any | None = None,
    http_client: Any | None = None,
) -> FastAPI:
  config = settings or Settings.from_env()
  owns_redis = redis_client is None
  owns_http = http_client is None

  @asynccontextmanager
  async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    config.validate()
    if owns_redis:
      app.state.redis = Redis.from_url(config.redis_url, decode_responses=True)
    else:
      app.state.redis = redis_client
    if owns_http:
      app.state.http = httpx.AsyncClient(
          timeout=httpx.Timeout(15.0, connect=5.0),
          follow_redirects=False,
          headers={"User-Agent": "GRAILD-Discord-OAuth-Broker/1.0"},
      )
    else:
      app.state.http = http_client
    await app.state.redis.ping()
    try:
      yield
    finally:
      if owns_http:
        await app.state.http.aclose()
      if owns_redis:
        await app.state.redis.aclose()

  app = FastAPI(
      title="GRAIL'D Discord OAuth Broker",
      docs_url=None,
      redoc_url=None,
      openapi_url=None,
      lifespan=lifespan,
  )
  owner_ids = {value.strip() for value in config.owner_discord_ids.split(',') if value.strip()}

  def is_owner(identity):
    return identity['provider'] == 'discord' and identity['subject'] in owner_ids

  async def tag_definitions():
    custom = await app.state.redis.hgetall(GLOBAL_CUSTOM_TAGS)
    return {**TAGS, **{tag_id: json.loads(raw) for tag_id, raw in custom.items()}}

  async def global_tags(identity, definitions=None):
    player_id = global_player_id(identity)
    tags = [{**TAGS['owner'], 'awarded_at': None}] if is_owner(identity) else []
    definitions = definitions if definitions is not None else await tag_definitions()
    for tag_id, definition in definitions.items():
      if tag_id == 'owner':
        continue
      raw = await app.state.redis.get(GLOBAL_TAG_PREFIX + player_id + ':' + tag_id)
      if raw:
        tags.append({**definition, 'awarded_at': json.loads(raw)['awarded_at']})
    return {'player_id': player_id, 'provider': identity['provider'], 'tags': tags,
            'can_manage_tags': is_owner(identity)}

  @app.post('/account/tags/sync')
  async def sync_account_tags(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get('content-type', ''), ('achievement_ids',))
    try:
      ids = json.loads(form['achievement_ids'])
      if not isinstance(ids, list) or len(ids) > 50 or any(not isinstance(item, str) for item in ids):
        raise ValueError()
      if not set(ids) <= ACHIEVEMENT_IDS or len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
        raise ValueError()
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail='Expected up to 50 distinct, recognized achievement IDs.') from None
    player_id = await register_global_player(app.state.redis, identity)
    if set(ids) == ACHIEVEMENT_IDS:
      await app.state.redis.set(GLOBAL_TAG_PREFIX + player_id + ':top_earner',
                                json.dumps({'awarded_at': int(time.time())}), nx=True)
    return {'success': True, **await global_tags(identity)}

  @app.get('/account/tags')
  async def get_account_tags(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    await register_global_player(app.state.redis, identity)
    return {'success': True, **await global_tags(identity)}

  @app.get('/account/tag-players')
  async def tag_players(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    if not is_owner(identity):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='Only the verified owner can manage global tags.')
    players = await app.state.redis.hgetall(GLOBAL_PLAYERS)
    accounts = []
    definitions = await tag_definitions()
    for player_id, raw in players.items():
      player = json.loads(raw)
      tags = await global_tags(player, definitions)
      accounts.append({'player_id': player_id, 'display_name': player['display_name'],
                       'provider': player['provider'], 'tag_ids': [tag['id'] for tag in tags['tags']],
                       'beta_tester': any(t['id'] == 'beta_tester' for t in tags['tags'])})
    return {'success': True, 'accounts': sorted(accounts, key=lambda p: (p['display_name'].casefold(), p['player_id']))}

  @app.post('/account/tags/assign')
  async def assign_account_tag(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    if not is_owner(identity):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='Only the verified owner can manage global tags.')
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get('content-type', ''), ('player_id', 'tag_id', 'assigned'))
    await check_assignable_tag(form['tag_id'], form['assigned'])
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[a-f0-9]{64}', form['player_id']):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail='Invalid global player ID.')
    if not await app.state.redis.hget(GLOBAL_PLAYERS, form['player_id']):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail='This player must sign in before a tag can be assigned.')
    return await store_assigned_tag(identity, form['player_id'], form['assigned'] == 'true', form['tag_id'])

  async def check_assignable_tag(tag_id, assigned):
    definition = (await tag_definitions()).get(tag_id)
    if not definition or tag_id == 'owner' or definition['kind'] != 'assigned' or assigned not in ('true', 'false'):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail='Choose an assignable tag. Owner and earned tags cannot be assigned manually.')
    return definition

  async def store_assigned_tag(identity, player_id, assigned, tag_id):
    key = GLOBAL_TAG_PREFIX + player_id + ':' + tag_id
    if assigned:
      await app.state.redis.set(key, json.dumps({'awarded_at': int(time.time()),
                                               'assigned_by': global_player_id(identity)}), nx=True)
    else:
      await app.state.redis.delete(key)
    definition = (await tag_definitions())[tag_id]
    return {'success': True, 'message': definition['name'] + ' tag ' + ('assigned.' if assigned else 'removed.')}

  @app.get('/account/tags/catalog')
  async def custom_tag_catalog(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    if not is_owner(identity):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='Only the verified owner can manage custom tags.')
    return {'success': True, 'entries': [tag for tag_id, tag in (await tag_definitions()).items()
                                       if tag_id != 'owner' and tag['kind'] == 'assigned']}

  @app.post('/account/tags/create')
  async def create_custom_tag(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    if not is_owner(identity):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='Only the verified owner can create custom tags.')
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get('content-type', ''), ('name', 'description'))
    name, description = ' '.join(form['name'].split()), form['description'].strip()
    reserved = {'owner', 'creator', 'owner / creator', 'owner/creator'} | {tag['name'].casefold() for tag in TAGS.values()}
    if not 1 <= len(name) <= 32 or not 1 <= len(description) <= 240 or name.casefold() in reserved or any(ord(c) < 32 or ord(c) == 127 for c in name + description):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail='Use a unique tag name (1–32 characters) and hover description (1–240 characters). Built-in names are reserved.')
    tag_id = 'custom_' + hashlib.sha256(name.casefold().encode()).hexdigest()[:24]
    definition = {'id': tag_id, 'name': name, 'description': description, 'kind': 'assigned'}
    if not await app.state.redis.hsetnx(GLOBAL_CUSTOM_TAGS, tag_id, json.dumps(definition)):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail='A tag with that name already exists. Choose it from the tag list.')
    return {'success': True, 'message': 'Custom tag created.', 'tag': definition}

  @app.post('/account/tags/discord')
  async def assign_discord_tag(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    if not is_owner(identity):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='Only the verified owner can manage global tags.')
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get('content-type', ''), ('discord_id', 'tag_id', 'assigned'))
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]{15,22}', form['discord_id']):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail='Enter a numeric Discord user ID.')
    await check_assignable_tag(form['tag_id'], form['assigned'])
    player_id = global_player_id({'provider': 'discord', 'subject': form['discord_id']})
    result = await store_assigned_tag(identity, player_id, form['assigned'] == 'true', form['tag_id'])
    result['message'] += ' The change applies when that Discord account signs in on any device.'
    return result

  @app.middleware("http")
  async def no_store_and_security_headers(request: Request, call_next):
    response = await call_next(request)
    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    response.headers["Pragma"] = "no-cache"
    response.headers["X-Content-Type-Options"] = "nosniff"
    response.headers["Referrer-Policy"] = "no-referrer"
    response.headers["X-Frame-Options"] = "DENY"
    return response

  @app.get("/healthz")
  async def health(request: Request):
    try:
      await request.app.state.redis.ping()
    except Exception:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Broker storage unavailable.") from None
    return {"status": "ok"}

  @app.post("/oauth/google/verify")
  async def google_verify(request: Request):
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get("content-type", ""),
                      ("access_token",))
    if len(form["access_token"]) > 8192:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid Google access token.")
    try:
      response = await request.app.state.http.get(
          "https://openidconnect.googleapis.com/v1/userinfo",
          headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {form['access_token']}", "Accept": "application/json"},
      )
      response.raise_for_status()
      user = response.json()
    except (httpx.HTTPError, ValueError) as error:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Google identity could not be verified.") from error
    subject = str(user.get("sub") or "")
    if not subject or len(subject) > 256 or user.get("email_verified") is not True:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Google account email is not verified.")
    identity = {
        "provider": "google",
        "subject": subject,
        "display_name": str(user.get("name") or user.get("email") or "")[:100],
    }
    global_token = await issue_global_session(request.app.state.redis, identity)
    return {
        **identity,
        "email": user.get("email"),
        "global_token": global_token,
    }

  @app.post("/oauth/start")
  async def oauth_start(request: Request):
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get("content-type", ""),
                      ("provider", "state", "redirect_uri"))
    if form["provider"] != "discord":
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unsupported provider.")
    if not STATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(form["state"]):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid desktop state value.")
    if not desktop_redirect_is_valid(form["redirect_uri"]):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Desktop redirect must be an IPv4 loopback URI.")

    broker_state = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    verifier = secrets.token_urlsafe(64)
    challenge = __import__("base64").urlsafe_b64encode(
        hashlib.sha256(verifier.encode("ascii")).digest()
    ).rstrip(b"=").decode("ascii")
    state_record = json.dumps({
        "desktop_state": form["state"],
        "redirect_uri": form["redirect_uri"],
        "verifier": verifier,
        "created_at": int(time.time()),
    }, separators=(",", ":"))
    stored = await request.app.state.redis.set(
        f"{REDIS_PREFIX}state:{hashlib.sha256(broker_state.encode()).hexdigest()}",
        state_record,
        ex=STATE_TTL_SECONDS,
        nx=True,
    )
    if not stored:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Could not create an OAuth state record.")

    authorization_url = DISCORD_AUTHORIZE_URL + "?" + urllib.parse.urlencode({
        "client_id": config.discord_client_id,
        "response_type": "code",
        "redirect_uri": config.discord_redirect_uri,
        "scope": "identify email",
        "state": broker_state,
        "code_challenge": challenge,
        "code_challenge_method": "S256",
    })
    return {"authorization_url": authorization_url}

  @app.get(CALLBACK_PATH, include_in_schema=False)
  async def discord_callback(request: Request):
    query = request.query_params
    provider_state = query.get("state", "")
    if not STATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(provider_state):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid OAuth state.")
    if query.get("iss") != DISCORD_ISSUER:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unexpected OAuth issuer.")

    state_key = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}state:{hashlib.sha256(provider_state.encode()).hexdigest()}"
    raw_state = await request.app.state.redis.getdel(state_key)
    if not raw_state:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="OAuth state expired or was already used.")
    state_record = json.loads(raw_state)

    error = query.get("error")
    if error:
      redirect_query = urllib.parse.urlencode({
          "error": "access_denied",
          "state": state_record["desktop_state"],
          "iss": DISCORD_ISSUER,
      })
      return RedirectResponse(
          f"{state_record['redirect_uri']}?{redirect_query}",
          status_code=303,
          headers={"Cache-Control": "no-store", "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer"},
      )

    code = query.get("code", "")
    if not code or len(code) > 4096:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Missing or invalid authorization code.")

    try:
      token_response = await request.app.state.http.post(
          DISCORD_TOKEN_URL,
          data={
              "grant_type": "authorization_code",
              "code": code,
              "redirect_uri": config.discord_redirect_uri,
              "code_verifier": state_record["verifier"],
          },
          auth=(config.discord_client_id, config.discord_client_secret),
          headers={"Accept": "application/json"},
      )
      token_response.raise_for_status()
      tokens = token_response.json()
      access_token = tokens.get("access_token")
      scopes = set(str(tokens.get("scope", "")).split())
      if not access_token or "identify" not in scopes:
        raise ValueError("Discord did not grant the identify scope.")
      user_response = await request.app.state.http.get(
          DISCORD_USER_URL,
          headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {access_token}", "Accept": "application/json"},
      )
      user_response.raise_for_status()
      user = user_response.json()
    except (httpx.HTTPError, ValueError, KeyError) as error:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Discord authorization could not be verified.") from error

    subject = str(user.get("id", ""))
    if not subject.isdigit() or len(subject) > 24:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Discord returned an invalid user identity.")
    display_name = user.get("global_name") or user.get("username") or ""
    ticket = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    ticket_record = json.dumps({
        "desktop_state": state_record["desktop_state"],
        "redirect_uri": state_record["redirect_uri"],
        "subject": subject,
        "email": user.get("email") if user.get("verified") else None,
        "display_name": str(display_name)[:100],
        "username": str(user.get("username", ""))[:64],
        "issuer": DISCORD_ISSUER,
        "issued_at": int(time.time()),
    }, separators=(",", ":"))
    stored = await request.app.state.redis.set(
        f"{REDIS_PREFIX}ticket:{hashlib.sha256(ticket.encode()).hexdigest()}",
        ticket_record,
        ex=TICKET_TTL_SECONDS,
        nx=True,
    )
    if not stored:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Could not issue a sign-in ticket.")

    redirect_query = urllib.parse.urlencode({
        "ticket": ticket,
        "state": state_record["desktop_state"],
        "iss": DISCORD_ISSUER,
    })
    return RedirectResponse(
        f"{state_record['redirect_uri']}?{redirect_query}",
        status_code=303,
        headers={"Cache-Control": "no-store", "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer"},
    )

  @app.post("/oauth/complete")
  async def oauth_complete(request: Request):
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get("content-type", ""),
                      ("provider", "state", "ticket", "redirect_uri"))
    if form["provider"] != "discord":
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unsupported provider.")
    if not STATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(form["state"]):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid desktop state value.")
    if not desktop_redirect_is_valid(form["redirect_uri"]):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Desktop redirect must be an IPv4 loopback URI.")
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32,128}", form["ticket"]):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid sign-in ticket.")

    ticket_key = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}ticket:{hashlib.sha256(form['ticket'].encode()).hexdigest()}"
    result = await request.app.state.redis.eval(
        CONSUME_TICKET_SCRIPT,
        1,
        ticket_key,
        form["state"],
        form["redirect_uri"],
    )
    if result == 0:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=410, detail="Ticket expired or already used.")
    if result == -1:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Ticket does not match this authorization.")
    record = json.loads(result)
    if record.get("issuer") != DISCORD_ISSUER or not record.get("subject", "").isdigit():
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Ticket identity validation failed.")
    identity = {
        "provider": "discord",
        "subject": record["subject"],
        "email": record.get("email"),
        "display_name": record.get("display_name", ""),
        "username": record.get("username", ""),
    }
    global_token = await issue_global_session(request.app.state.redis, identity)
    return {**identity, "global_token": global_token}

  @app.post("/leaderboard/update")
  async def leaderboard_update(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get("content-type", ""),
                      ("display_name", "points", "correct", "wrong"))
    display_name = form["display_name"].strip()
    if not display_name or len(display_name) > 64:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Display name must be 1–64 characters.")
    try:
      points = int(form["points"])
      correct = int(form["correct"])
      wrong = int(form["wrong"])
    except ValueError:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Leaderboard totals must be integers.") from None
    if min(points, correct, wrong) < 0 or max(points, correct, wrong) > 2_000_000_000:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Leaderboard totals are out of range.")
    member = f"{identity['provider']}:{identity['subject']}"
    script = """
    local old_raw = redis.call('HGET', KEYS[1], ARGV[1])
    local old = old_raw and cjson.decode(old_raw) or {}
    local points = math.max(tonumber(old.points or 0), tonumber(ARGV[4]))
    local correct = math.max(tonumber(old.correct or 0), tonumber(ARGV[5]))
    local wrong = math.max(tonumber(old.wrong or 0), tonumber(ARGV[6]))
    local record = cjson.encode({
      provider = ARGV[2], display_name = ARGV[3],
      points = points, correct = correct, wrong = wrong
    })
    redis.call('HSET', KEYS[1], ARGV[1], record)
    redis.call('ZADD', KEYS[2], points, ARGV[1])
    return record
    """
    raw_record = await request.app.state.redis.eval(
        script,
        2,
        GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_PROFILES,
        GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_SCORES,
        member,
        identity["provider"],
        display_name,
        points,
        correct,
        wrong,
    )
    return {"success": True, "entry": json.loads(raw_record)}

  @app.get("/leaderboard")
  async def global_leaderboard(request: Request):
    members = await request.app.state.redis.zrevrange(
        GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_SCORES, 0, 49
    )
    entries = []
    definitions = await tag_definitions()
    for member in members:
      raw = await request.app.state.redis.hget(GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_PROFILES, member)
      if raw:
        entry = json.loads(raw)
        provider, subject = member.split(':', 1)
        await register_global_player(app.state.redis, {'provider': provider, 'subject': subject,
                                                      'display_name': entry['display_name']})
        entry.update({key: value for key, value in (await global_tags({'provider': provider, 'subject': subject}, definitions)).items()
                      if key in ('tags', 'player_id')})
        entry["rank"] = len(entries) + 1
        entries.append(entry)
    return {"entries": entries}

  @app.post("/leaderboard/revoke")
  async def revoke_global_session(request: Request):
    authorization = request.headers.get("authorization", "")
    token = authorization.partition(" ")[2]
    if not token:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="A leaderboard bearer token is required.")
    key = GLOBAL_SESSION_PREFIX + hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    removed = await request.app.state.redis.delete(key)
    return {"revoked": bool(removed)}

  return app


app = make_app()

"""Global profile-tag rules; keep achievement IDs aligned with the desktop catalog."""
TARGETS = {
    'correct': [1, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250, 500, 999],
    'points': [100, 500, 1000, 2500, 5000, 10000, 25000, 99999],
    'streak': [3, 5, 10, 15, 25, 50, 99],
    'grails': [1, 5, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250],
    'rounds': [10, 50, 100, 250, 500, 1000],
    'first_try': [1, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250],
    'hard': [1, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250],
}
ACHIEVEMENT_IDS = {f'{metric}_{target}' for metric, targets in TARGETS.items() for target in targets}
ACHIEVEMENT_IDS |= {'linked_google', 'linked_discord'}
TAGS = {
    'owner': {'id': 'owner', 'name': 'Owner / Creator', 'kind': 'assigned',
              'description': "Verified owner / creator of GRAIL'D."},
    'beta_tester': {'id': 'beta_tester', 'name': 'Beta Tester', 'kind': 'assigned',

import hashlib
import json
import time
import urllib.parse

import httpx
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app import Settings, make_app


class MemoryRedis:
  def __init__(self):
    self.values = {}
    self.hashes = {}
    self.sorted_sets = {}

  def _live(self, key):
    record = self.values.get(key)
    if record and record[1] is not None and record[1] <= time.time():
      self.values.pop(key, None)
      return None
    return record

  async def ping(self):
    return True

  async def aclose(self):
    return None

  async def set(self, key, value, ex=None, nx=False):
    if self._live(key) and nx:
      return None
    expires = time.time() + ex if ex else None
    self.values[key] = (value, expires)
    return True

  async def get(self, key):
    record = self._live(key)
    return record[0] if record else None

  async def getdel(self, key):
    record = self._live(key)
    self.values.pop(key, None)
    return record[0] if record else None

  def expire_now(self, key):
    value, _expires = self.values[key]
    self.values[key] = (value, time.time() - 1)

  async def eval(self, script, _number_of_keys, *args):
    if "local old_raw" in script:
      profiles_key, scores_key, member, provider, display_name, points, correct, wrong = args
      previous = json.loads(self.hashes[profiles_key][member]) if member in self.hashes.get(profiles_key, {}) else {}
      entry = {
          "provider": provider,
          "display_name": display_name,
          "points": max(previous.get("points", 0), int(points)),
          "correct": max(previous.get("correct", 0), int(correct)),
          "wrong": max(previous.get("wrong", 0), int(wrong)),
      }
      encoded = json.dumps(entry)
      self.hashes.setdefault(profiles_key, {})[member] = encoded
      self.sorted_sets.setdefault(scores_key, {})[member] = entry["points"]
      return encoded
    key, desktop_state, redirect_uri = args
    record = await self.get(key)
    if record is None:
      return 0
    ticket = json.loads(record)
    if ticket["desktop_state"] != desktop_state or ticket["redirect_uri"] != redirect_uri:
      return -1
    self.values.pop(key, None)
    return record

  async def hget(self, key, field):
    return self.hashes.get(key, {}).get(field)

  async def hset(self, key, field, value):
    self.hashes.setdefault(key, {})[field] = value
    return 1

  async def hgetall(self, key):
    return dict(self.hashes.get(key, {}))

  async def hsetnx(self, key, field, value):
    records = self.hashes.setdefault(key, {})
    if field in records:
      return 0
    records[field] = value
    return 1

  async def zrevrange(self, key, start, stop):
    members = sorted(
        self.sorted_sets.get(key, {}),
        key=lambda member: (-self.sorted_sets[key][member], member),
    )
    return members[start:stop + 1]

  async def delete(self, key):
    return int(self.values.pop(key, None) is not None)


def make_test_client():
  redis = MemoryRedis()

  async def discord_mock(request):
    if request.url.path.endswith("/oauth2/token"):
      form = urllib.parse.parse_qs(request.content.decode())
      assert form["grant_type"] == ["authorization_code"]
      assert form["code_verifier"]
      assert form["redirect_uri"] == ["https://broker.example.test/oauth/callback"]
      assert request.headers.get("authorization", "").startswith("Basic ")
      return httpx.Response(200, json={
          "access_token": "[REDACTED_TEST_ACCESS_TOKEN]",
          "scope": "identify email",
      })
    if request.url.path.endswith("/users/@me"):
      assert request.headers["authorization"] == "Bearer [REDACTED_TEST_ACCESS_TOKEN]"
      return httpx.Response(200, json={
          "id": "123456789012345678",
          "username": "test_user",
          "global_name": "Test User",
          "email": "test@example.test",
          "verified": True,
      })
    if request.url.path.endswith("/v1/userinfo"):
      assert request.headers["authorization"] == "Bearer [REDACTED_TEST_ACCESS_TOKEN]"
      return httpx.Response(200, json={
          "sub": "google-subject-987",
          "name": "Google User",
          "email": "google@example.test",
          "email_verified": True,
      })
    return httpx.Response(404)

  http_client = httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(discord_mock))
  settings = Settings(
      discord_client_id="test-client-id",
      discord_client_secret="[REDACTED_TEST_CLIENT_SECRET]",
      discord_redirect_uri="https://broker.example.test/oauth/callback",
      redis_url="redis://unused",
  )
  app = make_app(settings, redis_client=redis, http_client=http_client)
  app.state.redis = redis
  app.state.http = http_client
  return TestClient(app), redis


def test_desktop_redirect_accepts_only_exact_loopback_root():
  from app import desktop_redirect_is_valid

  assert desktop_redirect_is_valid("http://127.0.0.1:49123/")
  assert not desktop_redirect_is_valid("https://127.0.0.1:49123/")
  assert not desktop_redirect_is_valid("http://localhost:49123/")
  assert not desktop_redirect_is_valid("http://127.0.0.1:49123.evil.example/")
  assert not desktop_redirect_is_valid("http://127.0.0.1:49123/other")
  assert not desktop_redirect_is_valid("http://127.0.0.1:49123/?next=https://evil.example")


def test_discord_flow_contract_pkce_and_single_use_ticket():
  client, _redis = make_test_client()
  desktop_state = "d" * 43
  desktop_redirect = "http://127.0.0.1:49123/"
  start = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord",
      "state": desktop_state,
      "redirect_uri": desktop_redirect,
  })
  assert start.status_code == 200
  authorization = urllib.parse.urlsplit(start.json()["authorization_url"])
  assert authorization.hostname == "discord.com"
  query = urllib.parse.parse_qs(authorization.query)
  assert query["scope"] == ["identify email"]
  assert query["code_challenge_method"] == ["S256"]
  assert query["redirect_uri"] == ["https://broker.example.test/oauth/callback"]

  callback = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "one-time-code",
      "state": query["state"][0],
      "iss": "https://discord.com",
  }, follow_redirects=False)
  assert callback.status_code == 303
  returned = urllib.parse.urlsplit(callback.headers["location"])
  assert returned.netloc == "127.0.0.1:49123"
  callback_values = urllib.parse.parse_qs(returned.query)
  assert callback_values["state"] == [desktop_state]
  assert callback_values["iss"] == ["https://discord.com"]
  ticket = callback_values["ticket"][0]

  complete = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord",
      "state": desktop_state,
      "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": desktop_redirect,
  })
  assert complete.status_code == 200
  assert complete.json() == {
      "provider": "discord",
      "subject": "123456789012345678",
      "email": "test@example.test",
      "display_name": "Test User",
      "username": "test_user",
      "global_token": complete.json()["global_token"],
  }
  global_token = complete.json()["global_token"]
  replay = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord",
      "state": desktop_state,
      "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": desktop_redirect,
  })
  assert replay.status_code == 410
  assert global_token


def test_mismatched_ticket_binding_does_not_consume_ticket():
  client, _redis = make_test_client()
  state = "s" * 43
  redirect_uri = "http://127.0.0.1:49000/"
  start = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": state, "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
  })
  broker_state = urllib.parse.parse_qs(
      urllib.parse.urlsplit(start.json()["authorization_url"]).query
  )["state"][0]
  callback = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://discord.com",
  }, follow_redirects=False)
  ticket = urllib.parse.parse_qs(urllib.parse.urlsplit(callback.headers["location"]).query)["ticket"][0]
  mismatch = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": "x" * 43, "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
  })
  assert mismatch.status_code == 403
  redirect_mismatch = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": state, "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": "http://127.0.0.1:49001/",
  })
  assert redirect_mismatch.status_code == 403
  valid = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": state, "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
  })
  assert valid.status_code == 200


def test_expired_ticket_cannot_be_redeemed():
  client, redis = make_test_client()
  state = "e" * 43
  redirect_uri = "http://127.0.0.1:49200/"
  start = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": state, "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
  })
  broker_state = urllib.parse.parse_qs(
      urllib.parse.urlsplit(start.json()["authorization_url"]).query
  )["state"][0]
  callback = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://discord.com",
  }, follow_redirects=False)
  ticket = urllib.parse.parse_qs(urllib.parse.urlsplit(callback.headers["location"]).query)["ticket"][0]
  key = "graild-discord-oauth:v1:ticket:" + hashlib.sha256(ticket.encode()).hexdigest()
  redis.expire_now(key)
  complete = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": state, "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
  })
  assert complete.status_code == 410


def test_expired_oauth_state_cannot_complete_callback():
  client, redis = make_test_client()
  start = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord",
      "state": "t" * 43,
      "redirect_uri": "http://127.0.0.1:49201/",
  })
  broker_state = urllib.parse.parse_qs(
      urllib.parse.urlsplit(start.json()["authorization_url"]).query
  )["state"][0]
  state_key = "graild-discord-oauth:v1:state:" + hashlib.sha256(broker_state.encode()).hexdigest()
  redis.expire_now(state_key)
  callback = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://discord.com",
  })
  assert callback.status_code == 400


def test_google_verification_issues_a_global_session():

  assert response.status_code == 200
  assert response.json()["provider"] == "google"
  assert response.json()["subject"] == "google-subject-987"
  assert response.json()["global_token"]


def test_global_leaderboard_requires_token_and_ranks_verified_users():
  client, _redis = make_test_client()
  unauthorized = client.post("/leaderboard/update", data={
    "display_name": "Player", "points": 100, "correct": 3, "wrong": 1,
  })
  assert unauthorized.status_code == 401

  verified = client.post("/oauth/google/verify", data={"access_token": "[REDACTED_TEST_ACCESS_TOKEN]"})
  token = verified.json()["global_token"]
  headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}
  update = client.post("/leaderboard/update", headers=headers, data={
    "display_name": "Player", "points": 100, "correct": 3, "wrong": 1,
  })
  assert update.status_code == 200
  stale_update = client.post("/leaderboard/update", headers=headers, data={
    "display_name": "Player", "points": 50, "correct": 1, "wrong": 1,
  })
  assert stale_update.json()["entry"]["points"] == 100
  board = client.get("/leaderboard")
  assert board.json()["entries"][0]["display_name"] == "Player"
  assert board.json()["entries"][0]["provider"] == "google"
  assert client.post("/leaderboard/revoke", headers=headers).json() == {"revoked": True}
  expired_session = client.post("/leaderboard/update", headers=headers, data={
    "display_name": "Player", "points": 110, "correct": 4, "wrong": 1,
  })
  assert expired_session.status_code == 401

  short_state = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": "short",
      "redirect_uri": "http://127.0.0.1:49123/",
  })

def test_callback_rejects_wrong_issuer_and_replayed_state():
  client, _redis = make_test_client()
  start = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": "q" * 43,
      "redirect_uri": "http://127.0.0.1:49123/",
  })
  broker_state = urllib.parse.parse_qs(
      urllib.parse.urlsplit(start.json()["authorization_url"]).query
  )["state"][0]
  wrong_issuer = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://attacker.example",
  })
  assert wrong_issuer.status_code == 400
  valid = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://discord.com",
  }, follow_redirects=False)
  assert valid.status_code == 303
  replay = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://discord.com",
  })
  assert replay.status_code == 400

import asyncio
import json
import runpy
from pathlib import Path

from app import issue_global_session, global_player_id
from tag_rules import ACHIEVEMENT_IDS
from tests.test_app import make_test_client

OWNER = '[REDACTED_DISCORD_ACCOUNT_ID]'


def session(redis, subject, provider='discord'):
    identity = {'provider': provider, 'subject': subject, 'display_name': 'Player ' + subject[-3:]}
    token = asyncio.run(issue_global_session(redis, identity))
    return {'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token}, global_player_id(identity)


def test_catalog_matches_desktop():
    desktop_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'achievements.py'
    if desktop_path.is_file():
        desktop = runpy.run_path(str(desktop_path))
        assert ACHIEVEMENT_IDS == {item['id'] for item in desktop['ACHIEVEMENTS']}
    assert len(ACHIEVEMENT_IDS) == 50


def test_owner_assigns_and_second_installation_recognizes_same_identity():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    owner, _ = session(redis, OWNER)
    tester, player_id = session(redis, '123456789012345678')
    assert client.post('/account/tags/assign', headers=owner, data={
        'player_id': player_id, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'true'}).status_code == 200
    before = client.get('/account/tags', headers=tester).json()
    second_installation, second_id = session(redis, '123456789012345678')
    after = client.get('/account/tags', headers=second_installation).json()
    assert player_id == second_id == after['player_id']
    assert before['tags'] == after['tags']
    assert after['tags'][0]['id'] == 'beta_tester'
    players = client.get('/account/tag-players', headers=owner).json()['accounts']
    assert next(p for p in players if p['player_id'] == player_id)['beta_tester']
    assert '123456789012345678' not in json.dumps(players)
    client.post('/account/tags/assign', headers=owner, data={
        'player_id': player_id, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'false'})
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=second_installation).json()['tags'] == []

    client, redis = make_test_client()
    tester, player_id = session(redis, '123456789012345678')
    google, _ = session(redis, OWNER, 'google')
    for headers in ({}, tester, google):

            'player_id': player_id, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'true', 'is_creator': '1'}).status_code in (401, 403)
        assert client.get('/account/tag-players', headers=headers).status_code in (401, 403)
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=google).json()['can_manage_tags'] is False
    owner, _ = session(redis, OWNER)
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=owner).json()['tags'][0]['id'] == 'owner'


def test_top_earner_requires_all_real_ids_and_is_permanent():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    player, _ = session(redis, '123456789012345678')
    ids = sorted(ACHIEVEMENT_IDS)
    assert client.post('/account/tags/sync', headers=player, data={'achievement_ids': json.dumps(ids[:-1])}).json()['tags'] == []
    for invalid in (ids[:-1] + ['invented'], ids[:-1] + [ids[0]], 'all', [None]):
        assert client.post('/account/tags/sync', headers=player, data={'achievement_ids': json.dumps(invalid)}).status_code == 400
    earned = client.post('/account/tags/sync', headers=player, data={'achievement_ids': json.dumps(ids)}).json()
    assert earned['tags'][0]['id'] == 'top_earner'
    assert client.post('/account/tags/sync', headers=player, data={'achievement_ids': '[]'}).json()['tags'] == earned['tags']
    other, _ = session(redis, '987654321012345678')
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=other).json()['tags'] == []

================================================================================
graild_updater.py
