"""Separate, network-free updater. Only installs publisher-signed app binaries."""
import argparse
from contextlib import contextmanager
import ctypes
from ctypes import wintypes
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time

from app_version import APP_VERSION
from update_trust import TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS
from update_security import (UpdateError, atomic_json, strict_json, validated_job,
                             verify_manifest, verify_file, sha256_file, update_root)


def kernel():
    api = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True)
    api.OpenProcess.argtypes = [wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.BOOL, wintypes.DWORD]
    api.OpenProcess.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
    api.CloseHandle.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
    api.WaitForSingleObject.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE, wintypes.DWORD]
    api.QueryFullProcessImageNameW.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE, wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.LPWSTR, ctypes.POINTER(wintypes.DWORD)]
    api.CreateMutexW.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, wintypes.BOOL, wintypes.LPCWSTR]
    api.CreateMutexW.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
    api.ReleaseMutex.argtypes = [wintypes.HANDLE]
    return api


@contextmanager
def install_lock(target):
    if os.name != "nt":
        raise UpdateError("The installer is supported on Windows only.")
    api = kernel()
    name = "Local\\GRAILD-Update-" + hashlib.sha256(str(target).casefold().encode()).hexdigest()
    handle = api.CreateMutexW(None, False, name)
    if not handle:
        raise UpdateError("Could not lock the installation.")
    acquired = api.WaitForSingleObject(handle, 0) in (0, 128)
    try:
        if not acquired:
            raise UpdateError("Another GRAIL'D update is already running.")
        yield
    finally:
        if acquired:
            api.ReleaseMutex(handle)
        api.CloseHandle(handle)


class ParentProcess:
    def __init__(self, pid, target):
        self.api = kernel()
        self.handle = self.api.OpenProcess(0x00100000 | 0x1000, False, int(pid))
        if not self.handle:
            raise UpdateError("GRAIL'D exited before the updater was ready. Reopen it and retry.")
        length = wintypes.DWORD(32768)
        buffer = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(length.value)
        if not self.api.QueryFullProcessImageNameW(self.handle, 0, buffer, ctypes.byref(length)) or os.path.normcase(os.path.realpath(buffer.value)) != os.path.normcase(str(target)):
            self.close()
            raise UpdateError("Updater parent process does not match the target executable.")

    def wait(self):
        if self.api.WaitForSingleObject(self.handle, 180000) != 0:
            raise UpdateError("GRAIL'D is still running. No files were replaced.")

    def close(self):
        if self.handle:
            self.api.CloseHandle(self.handle)
            self.handle = None


def independent_environment():
    environment = {key: value for key, value in os.environ.items() if not key.startswith("_PYI_")}
    environment["PYINSTALLER_RESET_ENVIRONMENT"] = "1"
    return environment


def launch(command):
    # PyInstaller changes the DLL search path; do not leak its bundle into a new EXE.
    if os.name == "nt":
        ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetDllDirectoryW(None)
    try:
        return subprocess.Popen(command, env=independent_environment(),
                                creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0), close_fds=True)
    finally:
        if os.name == "nt" and hasattr(sys, "_MEIPASS"):
            ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetDllDirectoryW(sys._MEIPASS)


def replace_when_unlocked(source, target, seconds=30):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + seconds
    while True:
        try:
            os.replace(source, target)
            return
        except PermissionError:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                raise UpdateError("The executable is still locked. Close other copies of GRAIL'D and retry.") from None
            time.sleep(0.25)


def copy_new(source, destination):
    with open(source, "rb") as incoming, open(destination, "xb") as outgoing:
        shutil.copyfileobj(incoming, outgoing, length=1024 * 1024)
        outgoing.flush()
        os.fsync(outgoing.fileno())


def stop_launched_process(process):
    if process.poll() is not None:
        return
    if os.name == "nt":
        taskkill = Path(os.environ.get("SystemRoot", "C:/Windows")) / "System32" / "taskkill.exe"
        subprocess.run([str(taskkill), "/PID", str(process.pid), "/T", "/F"], capture_output=True,
                       creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0), timeout=30)
    else:
        process.terminate()
    process.wait(timeout=30)


@contextmanager
def executable_handle(path, writable=False, timeout=30):
    if os.name != "nt":
        yield
        return
    api = kernel()
    api.CreateFileW.argtypes = [wintypes.LPCWSTR, wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.DWORD, ctypes.c_void_p,
                               wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.DWORD, wintypes.HANDLE]
    api.CreateFileW.restype = wintypes.HANDLE
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    # A write lease cannot coexist with a running executable image. It also
    # prevents another process from starting the old image during backup.
    access = 0x40000000 if writable else 0x80000000
    sharing = (0x1 | 0x4) if writable else 0x1
    handle = None
    while True:
        handle = api.CreateFileW(str(path), access, sharing, None, 3, 0x80, None)
        if handle and handle != ctypes.c_void_p(-1).value:
            break
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            raise UpdateError("The executable is in use or cannot be locked safely. Close other app copies and retry.")
        time.sleep(0.25)
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        api.CloseHandle(handle)


def monitor_startup(target, job, token, version):
    # Hold the verified target read-only across process creation, preventing
    # modification between the final hash check and Windows loading the image.
    request = strict_json((job / "request.json").read_bytes())
    manifest = verify_manifest(strict_json((job / "manifest.json").read_bytes()), TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS,
                               request["repository"], request["channel"], request["current_version"])
    with executable_handle(target):
        verify_file(target, manifest)
        process = launch([str(target), "--post-update", str(job), token])
    try:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 90
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            health = job / "health.json"
            if health.is_file():
                try:
                    data = strict_json(health.read_bytes())
                    if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get("token") == token:
                        if data.get("version") != version:
                            raise UpdateError("The new executable reported the wrong application version.")
                        if process.poll() in (None, 0):
                            return
                except OSError:
                    pass
            if process.poll() is not None:
                raise UpdateError("The new build exited before its startup health check passed.")
            time.sleep(0.25)
        raise UpdateError("The new build did not pass its startup health check in time.")
    except Exception:
        stop_launched_process(process)
        raise


def read_request(job, keys):
    request = strict_json((job / "request.json").read_bytes())
    if request.get("current_version") != APP_VERSION:
        raise UpdateError("This updater does not match the running app version.")
    manifest = verify_manifest(strict_json((job / "manifest.json").read_bytes()), keys,
                               request["repository"], request["channel"], request["current_version"])
    raw_target = Path(request["target"])
    target = raw_target.resolve()
    data = Path(os.environ.get("APPDATA", Path.home() / "AppData" / "Roaming")) / "JuiceSpot"
    if not raw_target.is_absolute() or raw_target.is_symlink() or target.suffix.lower() != ".exe" or target.is_relative_to(data.resolve()) or target.is_relative_to(update_root().resolve()):
        raise UpdateError("Invalid installation target. User data cannot be an installation directory.")
    if not isinstance(request.get("health_token"), str) or len(request["health_token"]) != 64:
        raise UpdateError("Invalid update health token.")
    if not isinstance(request.get("old_sha256"), str) or len(request["old_sha256"]) != 64:
        raise UpdateError("Missing original executable checksum.")
    return request, manifest, target


def rollback(target, backup, old_digest):
    if not backup.is_file() or sha256_file(backup) != old_digest:
        raise UpdateError("The recovery backup failed verification. It was not executed.")
    replace_when_unlocked(backup, target)
    if sha256_file(target) != old_digest:
        raise UpdateError("The restored executable failed verification.")


def install_job(directory, keys=TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS, parent_factory=ParentProcess, startup=monitor_startup):
    job = validated_job(directory)
    request, manifest, target = read_request(job, keys)
    payload = job / "payload.exe"
    verify_file(payload, manifest)
    backup = target.parent / (".graild-backup-" + job.name + ".exe")
    incoming = target.parent / (".graild-new-" + job.name + ".exe")
    committed = False
    parent = None
    with install_lock(target):
        try:
            if not target.is_file() or sha256_file(target) != request["old_sha256"]:
                raise UpdateError("The installed executable changed. No update was installed.")
            parent = parent_factory(request["parent_pid"], target)
            atomic_json(job / "ready.json", {"ready": True})
            parent.wait()
            parent.close()
            parent = None
            # Verify independently again after the original app has exited.
            verify_file(payload, manifest)
            if sha256_file(target) != request["old_sha256"]:
                raise UpdateError("The original executable changed during shutdown.")
            atomic_json(job / "journal.json", {"phase": "preparing", "version": manifest["version"]})
            copy_new(payload, incoming)
            verify_file(incoming, manifest)
            with executable_handle(target, writable=True):
                copy_new(target, backup)
                if sha256_file(backup) != request["old_sha256"]:
                    raise UpdateError("Could not verify the recovery backup.")
                atomic_json(job / "journal.json", {"phase": "prepared", "version": manifest["version"]})
            # MoveFileEx cannot replace a destination while our own write handle
            # is open. Release it here; Windows still refuses a running image.
            replace_when_unlocked(incoming, target)
            committed = True
            atomic_json(job / "journal.json", {"phase": "installed", "version": manifest["version"]})
            verify_file(target, manifest)
            startup(target, job, request["health_token"], manifest["version"])
            atomic_json(job / "journal.json", {"phase": "healthy", "version": manifest["version"]})
            atomic_json(job / "result.json", {"status": "installed", "version": manifest["version"], "message": "Update installed and startup verified."})
        except Exception as error:
            if committed:
                try:
                    rollback(target, backup, request["old_sha256"])
                    atomic_json(job / "journal.json", {"phase": "rolled_back", "version": manifest["version"]})
                    atomic_json(job / "result.json", {"status": "rolled_back", "message": str(error) + " The previous build was restored."})
                    launch([str(target)])
                except Exception as recovery_error:
                    atomic_json(job / "result.json", {"status": "recovery_required", "message": str(recovery_error), "backup": str(backup)})
            else:
                atomic_json(job / "result.json", {"status": "failed", "message": str(error)})
            raise
        finally:
            if parent:
                parent.close()
            if incoming.exists():
                incoming.unlink()


def recover_job(directory, keys=TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS):
    job = validated_job(directory)
    request, manifest, target = read_request(job, keys)
    backup = target.parent / (".graild-backup-" + job.name + ".exe")
    with install_lock(target):
        current = sha256_file(target) if target.is_file() else ""
        if current == request["old_sha256"]:
            atomic_json(job / "result.json", {"status": "rolled_back", "message": "The previous build is already installed."})
            return
        if current and current != manifest["sha256"]:
            raise UpdateError("The installation changed independently; recovery will not overwrite it.")
        rollback(target, backup, request["old_sha256"])
        atomic_json(job / "journal.json", {"phase": "rolled_back", "version": manifest["version"]})
        atomic_json(job / "result.json", {"status": "rolled_back", "message": "Interrupted update recovered. The previous build is restored."})


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--job", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--recover", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        if args.recover:
            recover_job(args.job)
        else:
            install_job(args.job)
        return 0
    except Exception as error:
        try:
            job = validated_job(args.job)
            if not (job / "result.json").exists():
                atomic_json(job / "result.json", {"status": "failed", "message": str(error)})
        except Exception:
            pass
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

GRAILD-Discord-Bot/bot.py

"""Standalone GRAIL'D community bot. No desktop imports or privileged intents."""
import asyncio
import logging
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass,  field
from pathlib import Path

import aiohttp
import discord
from discord import app_commands
from dotenv import load_dotenv

from graild_api import GraildAPI, DEFAULT_BACKEND, ProfileUnavailable
from github_releases import GitHubReleases, RELEASES_URL, release_date
from http_client import APIError, JSONClient, https_origin

PURPLE = 0x8B5CF6
log = logging.getLogger('graild.bot')


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
    token: str = field(repr=False)
    scope: str
    guild_id: int | None
    application_id: int | None
    backend: str

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls):
        token = os.environ.get('DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN', '').strip()
        if not token or token.startswith('replace_'):
            raise ValueError('Set DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN in your environment or local .env file.')
        scope = os.environ.get('DISCORD_COMMAND_SCOPE', 'global').strip().lower()
        if scope not in ('guild', 'global'):
            raise ValueError('DISCORD_COMMAND_SCOPE must be guild or global.')
        def snowflake(name, required=False):
            value = os.environ.get(name, '').strip()
            if not value and not required: return None
            if not value.isascii() or not value.isdigit() or not 1 <= int(value) < 2**64:
                raise ValueError(name + ' must be a valid Discord ID.')
            return int(value)
        guild = snowflake('DISCORD_GUILD_ID')
        app = snowflake('DISCORD_APPLICATION_ID')
        backend = https_origin(os.environ.get('GRAILD_BACKEND_URL', DEFAULT_BACKEND).strip())
        return cls(token, scope, guild, app, backend)


def embed(title, description=''):
    result = discord.Embed(title=title[:256], description=description[:3900], colour=PURPLE)
    result.set_footer(text="GRAIL'D • Juice WRLD song guessing")
    return result


def safe_name(value):
    return discord.utils.escape_markdown(discord.utils.escape_mentions(str(value)))


class GraildCommands(app_commands.Group):
    def __init__(self, bot):
        super().__init__(name='graild', description="GRAIL'D downloads, updates and public stats")
        self.bot = bot

    @app_commands.command(name='download', description="Get published GRAIL'D beta downloads")
    @app_commands.checks.cooldown(1, 5, key=lambda i: i.user.id)
    async def download(self, interaction: discord.Interaction):
        await interaction.response.defer(thinking=True)
        downloads = await self.bot.releases.downloads()
        response = embed("Download GRAIL'D beta", 'Only published app assets are listed. Platform versions may differ.')
        for platform, asset in downloads.items():
            response.add_field(name=platform, value=safe_name(asset['version'])[:80] + '\n[Download](' + asset['url'] + ')', inline=False)
        if not downloads:
            response.description = 'Beta releases exist, but no recognised Windows or macOS app downloads are attached. Source/build-kit ZIPs are not playable apps.'
        response.url = RELEASES_URL
        await interaction.followup.send(embed=response, allowed_mentions=discord.AllowedMentions.none())

    @app_commands.command(name='updates', description="Read the latest published GRAIL'D beta release notes")
    @app_commands.checks.cooldown(1, 5, key=lambda i: i.user.id)
    async def updates(self, interaction: discord.Interaction):
        await interaction.response.defer(thinking=True)
        release = await self.bot.releases.latest()
        notes = release.get('body') or 'No release notes were published.'
        if not isinstance(notes, str): notes = 'No readable release notes were published.'
        notes = discord.utils.escape_mentions(notes)
        if len(notes) > 3500: notes = notes[:3500] + '\n\n… Read the full notes on GitHub.'
        response = embed("GRAIL'D beta updates", notes)
        response.url = self.bot.releases.page_url(release)
        response.add_field(name='Version', value=safe_name(release['tag_name'])[:200], inline=True)
        response.add_field(name='Published (UTC)', value=release_date(release).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC'), inline=True)
        await interaction.followup.send(embed=response, allowed_mentions=discord.AllowedMentions.none())

    @app_commands.command(name='leaderboard', description="Show the top 10 public global GRAIL'D players")
    @app_commands.checks.cooldown(1, 5, key=lambda i: i.user.id)
    async def leaderboard(self, interaction: discord.Interaction):
        await interaction.response.defer(thinking=True)
        entries = await self.bot.api.leaderboard()
        lines = [f"**{entry['rank']}. {safe_name(entry['display_name'])}** — {entry['points']:,} points\n"
                 f"{entry['correct']:,} correct · {entry['wrong']:,} incorrect" for entry in entries]
        response = embed('Global leaderboard — Top 10', '\n\n'.join(lines) or 'No players have been published to the global leaderboard yet.')
        response.set_footer(text='Public backend totals • cached for up to 60 seconds • scores are reported by game clients')
        await interaction.followup.send(embed=response, allowed_mentions=discord.AllowedMentions.none())

    @app_commands.command(name='profile', description="Check your linked GRAIL'D profile availability")
    @app_commands.checks.cooldown(1, 10, key=lambda i: i.user.id)
    async def profile(self, interaction: discord.Interaction):
        await interaction.response.defer(ephemeral=True, thinking=True)
        try:
            await self.bot.api.profile(interaction.user.id)
        except ProfileUnavailable as error:
            await interaction.followup.send(embed=embed('Linked profile', str(error)), ephemeral=True)

    @app_commands.command(name='help', description="Show GRAIL'D bot commands")
    async def help(self, interaction: discord.Interaction):
        response = embed("GRAIL'D commands", '\n'.join([
            '**/graild download** — Published Windows, Intel Mac and Apple Silicon app downloads.',
            '**/graild updates** — Latest published beta version, date and notes.',
            '**/graild leaderboard** — Top 10 players from the public global backend.',
            '**/graild profile** — Private profile availability check; backend integration pending.',
            '**/graild help** — This command list.',
        ]))
        await interaction.response.send_message(embed=response, ephemeral=True)

    async def on_error(self, interaction, error):
        cause = getattr(error, 'original', error)
        if isinstance(cause, app_commands.CommandOnCooldown):
            message = f'Please wait {cause.retry_after:.0f} seconds before trying that command again.'
        elif isinstance(cause, APIError):
            message = str(cause)
        else:
            # Log the class, not raw exceptions/headers/URLs that might contain credentials.
            log.error('Command failed (%s).', type(cause).__name__)
            message = 'The command could not be completed. Please try again later.'
        response = embed('GRAIL\'D — request unavailable', message)
        if interaction.response.is_done():
            await interaction.followup.send(embed=response, ephemeral=True)
        else:
            await interaction.response.send_message(embed=response, ephemeral=True)


class GraildBot(discord.Client):
    def __init__(self, settings):
        intents = discord.Intents.none()
        intents.guilds = True  # Non-privileged; no members, presence or message content.
        super().__init__(intents=intents, application_id=settings.application_id,
                         allowed_mentions=discord.AllowedMentions.none())
        self.settings = settings
        self.tree = app_commands.CommandTree(self)
        # Load every public command before the one startup synchronization.
        self.tree.add_command(GraildCommands(self))
        self._commands_synced = False
        self.http_session = None

    async def setup_hook(self):
        self.http_session = aiohttp.ClientSession(
            timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=15, connect=5),
            headers={'User-Agent': 'GRAILD-Discord-Bot/0.1', 'Accept': 'application/json'})
        self.releases = GitHubReleases(JSONClient(self.http_session, ttl=300))
        self.api = GraildAPI(JSONClient(self.http_session, ttl=60), self.settings.backend)
        await self.sync_public_commands()

    async def sync_public_commands(self):
        if self._commands_synced:
            return
        if self.settings.scope == 'guild':
            log.info('Legacy guild scope setting overridden: public commands now register globally.')
        try:
            synced = await self.tree.sync()
        except (discord.HTTPException, app_commands.AppCommandError) as error:
            log.error('Global command synchronization failed (%s, HTTP %s, Discord code %s). '
                      'Check application ID, installation scopes and connectivity; restart after fixing.',
                      type(error).__name__, getattr(error, 'status', 'n/a'), getattr(error, 'code', 'n/a'))
            raise RuntimeError('Global command synchronization failed; startup stopped.') from None
        self._commands_synced = True
        names = []
        def collect(command, prefix=''):
            name = f'{prefix} {command.name}'.strip()
            children = getattr(command, 'options', [])
            subcommands = [child for child in children if child.type in (
                discord.AppCommandOptionType.subcommand, discord.AppCommandOptionType.subcommand_group)]
            if subcommands:
                for child in subcommands:
                    collect(child, name)
            else:
                names.append('/' + name)
        for command in synced:
            collect(command)
        log.info('Successfully synchronized %d global command group(s), %d commands: %s',
                 len(synced), len(names), ', '.join(names))
        # Remove only our legacy test-guild slash group, after global sync succeeds.
        # Global commands also serve the test server; unrelated commands are untouched.
        if self.settings.guild_id:
            try:
                guild = discord.Object(id=self.settings.guild_id)
                for command in await self.tree.fetch_commands(guild=guild):
                    if command.name == 'graild' and command.type == discord.AppCommandType.chat_input:
                        await command.delete()
                        log.info('Removed legacy test-server /graild registration; global commands serve this server.')
            except discord.HTTPException as error:
                log.warning('Global sync succeeded, but test-server command cleanup failed '
                            '(%s, HTTP %s, Discord code %s). Cleanup will retry next startup.',
                            type(error).__name__, error.status, error.code)

    async def close(self):
        try:
            if self.http_session is not None:
                await self.http_session.close()
        finally:
            await super().close()


def main():
    load_dotenv(Path(__file__).with_name(".env"), encoding="utf-8-sig")
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format='%(levelname)s %(name)s: %(message)s')
    try:
        settings = Settings.from_env()
    except ValueError as error:
        raise SystemExit(str(error)) from None
    bot = GraildBot(settings)
    async def run():
        async with bot:
            await bot.start(settings.token)
    try:
        asyncio.run(run())
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    except discord.LoginFailure:
        raise SystemExit('Discord rejected DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN. Reset it in the Developer Portal and update your environment.') from None
    except RuntimeError as error:
        raise SystemExit(str(error)) from None
    except discord.HTTPException as error:
        raise SystemExit(f'Discord API request failed (HTTP {error.status}, code {error.code}). Check connectivity and installation permissions.') from None


if __name__ == '__main__': main()

"""Read-only live checks. No Discord login, score writes or private profile reads."""
import asyncio
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path

import aiohttp
from graild_api import GraildAPI, DEFAULT_BACKEND, ProfileUnavailable
from github_releases import GitHubReleases
from http_client import JSONClient, APIError


async def main():
    report = {'checked_at': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
              'discord_connection_tested': False, 'profile_integration': 'unavailable: no authorised backend endpoint',
              'read_only': True}
    async with aiohttp.ClientSession(timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=20, connect=5),
                                    headers={'User-Agent':'GRAILD-Discord-Bot/0.1-live-check'}) as session:
        api = GraildAPI(JSONClient(session), os.environ.get('GRAILD_BACKEND_URL', DEFAULT_BACKEND))
        releases = GitHubReleases(JSONClient(session))
        try:
            entries = await api.leaderboard()
            report['leaderboard'] = {'tested': True, 'success': True, 'returned_public_entries': len(entries)}
        except APIError as error:
            report['leaderboard'] = {'tested': True, 'success': False, 'error': str(error)}
        try:
            latest = await releases.latest()
            downloads = await releases.downloads()
            report['github'] = {'tested': True, 'success': True, 'latest_beta': latest['tag_name'],
                                'published_platforms': {key: value['version'] for key,value in downloads.items()}}
        except APIError as error:
            report['github'] = {'tested': True, 'success': False, 'error': str(error)}
    path = Path(__file__).with_name('LIVE_CHECK_RESULTS.json')
    path.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))


if __name__ == '__main__': asyncio.run(main())

================================================================================
GRAILD-Discord-Bot/github_releases.py
================================================================================
"""Published beta releases and strictly recognised app assets from GitHub."""
from datetime import datetime
import re
from urllib.parse import quote

from http_client import APIError

REPOSITORY = '[REDACTED_GITHUB_OWNER]/GRAILD-Releases'
RELEASES_URL = 'https://github.com/' + REPOSITORY + '/releases'
MAC = re.compile(r'^GRAILD-(?:[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+[0-9A-Za-z.+-]*-)?macOS-(arm64|x86_64)(?:-notarized)?\.zip$', re.I)


def platform_for(name):
    if name.casefold() in ('graild.exe', 'juiceguessermain.exe'):
        return 'Windows'
    match = MAC.fullmatch(name)
    if match:
        return 'Apple Silicon Mac' if match[1].lower() == 'arm64' else 'Intel Mac'
    return None


def release_date(release):
    try:
        date = datetime.fromisoformat(release['published_at'].replace('Z', '+00:00'))
        if date.tzinfo is None: raise ValueError
        return date
    except (KeyError, TypeError, AttributeError, ValueError):
        raise APIError('GitHub returned an invalid publication date.') from None


class GitHubReleases:
    def __init__(self, client):
        self.client = client

    async def beta_releases(self):
        releases = []
        # /latest excludes prereleases: use the paginated public releases list.
        for page in range(1, 6):
            data = await self.client.get('https://api.github.com/repos/' + REPOSITORY +
                                         '/releases?per_page=100&page=' + str(page))
            if not isinstance(data, list):
                raise APIError('GitHub returned an unexpected releases response.')
            for release in data:
                if (isinstance(release, dict) and release.get('draft') is False and
                        release.get('prerelease') is True and release.get('published_at')):
                    if not isinstance(release.get('tag_name'), str):
                        raise APIError('GitHub returned an invalid release tag.')
                    releases.append(release)
            if len(data) < 100:
                break
        if not releases:
            raise APIError('No published beta releases were found. Git tags and drafts are not downloadable releases.')
        # Latest means publication time, not GitHub's stable-only Latest badge.
        return sorted(releases, key=release_date, reverse=True)

    @staticmethod
    def page_url(release):
        return RELEASES_URL + '/tag/' + quote(release['tag_name'], safe='')

    async def latest(self):
        return (await self.beta_releases())[0]

    async def downloads(self):
        platforms = {}
        for release in await self.beta_releases():
            assets = release.get('assets', [])
            if not isinstance(assets, list):
                raise APIError('GitHub returned an invalid asset list.')
            for asset in assets:
                if not isinstance(asset, dict) or not isinstance(asset.get('name'), str):
                    continue
                name = asset['name']
                platform = platform_for(name)
                if not platform or platform in platforms or asset.get('state') != 'uploaded' or type(asset.get('size')) is not int or asset['size'] <= 0:
                    continue
                url = asset.get('browser_download_url', '')
                # Only use a URL actually returned for this asset and repository.
                expected = 'https://github.com/' + REPOSITORY + '/releases/download/' + quote(release['tag_name'], safe='') + '/' + quote(name, safe='')
                if url != expected:
                    continue
                platforms[platform] = dict(url=url, version=release['tag_name'], name=name)
        return platforms

================================================================================
GRAILD-Discord-Bot/graild_api.py
================================================================================
"""Read-only adapter for the inspected GRAIL'D public leaderboard interface."""
from http_client import APIError, https_origin

DEFAULT_BACKEND = 'https://graild-discord-broker.onrender.com'


class ProfileUnavailable(APIError):
    pass


class GraildAPI:
    def __init__(self, client, base_url=DEFAULT_BACKEND):
        self.client = client
        self.base_url = https_origin(base_url)

    async def leaderboard(self):
        response = await self.client.get(self.base_url + '/leaderboard')
        if not isinstance(response, dict) or not isinstance(response.get('entries'), list):
            raise APIError('The leaderboard returned an unexpected response.')
        result = []
        for entry in response['entries'][:10]:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise APIError('The leaderboard returned an invalid player entry.')
            name = entry.get('display_name')
            rank, points = entry.get('rank'), entry.get('points')
            correct, wrong = entry.get('correct'), entry.get('wrong')
            if (not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip() or
                    any(type(n) is not int or n < 0 for n in (rank, points, correct, wrong)) or rank < 1):
                raise APIError('The leaderboard returned incomplete player totals.')
            # Allowlist public fields; never propagate tokens, emails or identities.
            result.append(dict(display_name=name[:64], rank=rank, points=points,
                               correct=correct, wrong=wrong))
        return result

    async def profile(self, discord_id):
        if type(discord_id) is not int or discord_id <= 0:
            raise APIError('A valid Discord account ID is required.')
        # The inspected broker has no authorised read-by-Discord-ID endpoint.
        # Public leaderboard entries omit IDs; display-name matching is unsafe.
        raise ProfileUnavailable(
            'Linked-profile lookup is not available yet. The current GRAIL\'D backend '
            'does not expose an authorised Discord-ID profile API. View your profile '
            'in the desktop app for now. No private account data was accessed.')

================================================================================
GRAILD-Discord-Bot/http_client.py
================================================================================
"""Bounded asynchronous JSON GET requests with short, in-memory TTL caching."""
import asyncio
import json
import time
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

import aiohttp


class APIError(Exception):
    """A safe error message suitable for displaying in Discord."""


def https_origin(value):
    try:
        parsed = urlsplit(value)
        if (parsed.scheme != 'https' or not parsed.hostname or parsed.username or
                parsed.password or parsed.port not in (None, 443) or
                parsed.path not in ('', '/') or parsed.query or parsed.fragment):
            raise ValueError
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError('Backend URL must be an HTTPS origin without credentials or a path.') from None
    return value.rstrip('/')


class JSONClient:
    def __init__(self, session, ttl=60, max_bytes=2_000_000):
        self.session, self.ttl, self.max_bytes = session, ttl, max_bytes
        self.cache = {}
        self.lock = asyncio.Lock()

    async def get(self, url):
        async with self.lock:
            saved = self.cache.get(url)
            if saved and saved[0] > time.monotonic():
                return saved[1]
            try:
                async with self.session.get(url, allow_redirects=False) as response:
                    if response.status in (403, 429):
                        raise APIError('The service is rate limiting or denying requests. Try again later.')
                    if response.status == 404:
                        raise APIError('The requested service resource is not available.')
                    if response.status != 200:
                        raise APIError('The service is temporarily unavailable. Try again shortly.')
                    data = bytearray()
                    async for chunk in response.content.iter_chunked(65536):
                        data.extend(chunk)
                        if len(data) > self.max_bytes:
                            raise APIError('The service response exceeded its allowed size.')
                    value = json.loads(data)
            except (aiohttp.ClientError, asyncio.TimeoutError):
                raise APIError('The service could not be reached or timed out. Try again shortly.') from None
            except (ValueError, UnicodeError):
                raise APIError('The service returned an invalid JSON response.') from None
            # No stale values served as if they were current after a failed refresh.
            if len(self.cache) >= 64:
                self.cache.clear()
            self.cache[url] = (time.monotonic() + self.ttl, value)
            return value

import asyncio
import os
import unittest
import discord
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock, patch

from bot import GraildBot, GraildCommands, Settings
from github_releases import GitHubReleases, platform_for, REPOSITORY
from graild_api import GraildAPI, ProfileUnavailable
from http_client import APIError, JSONClient, https_origin


def release(tag='v0.9.0', date='2026-11-01T12:00:00Z', **changes):
    value={'tag_name':tag,'published_at':date,'draft':False,'prerelease':True,'assets':[], 'body':'Notes'}
    value.update(changes);return value


def asset(name,tag='v0.9.0'):
    return {'name':name,'state':'uploaded','size':100,
            'browser_download_url':f'https://github.com/{REPOSITORY}/releases/download/{tag}/{name}'}


class ReleaseTests(unittest.IsolatedAsyncioTestCase):
    async def test_only_published_app_assets_with_platform_versions(self):
        latest=release(assets=[asset('GRAILD-0.9.0-macOS-arm64.zip'),asset('GRAILD-0.9.0-macOS-arm64-build-kit.zip'),asset('GRAILD-GitHub-Mac-Build.zip')])
        old=release('v0.8.0','2026-10-31T12:00:00Z',assets=[asset('GRAILD.exe','v0.8.0')])
        client=Mock();client.get=AsyncMock(return_value=[old,latest,release('v9.0.0',draft=True)])
        api=GitHubReleases(client)
        result=await api.downloads()
        self.assertEqual(set(result),{'Windows','Apple Silicon Mac'})
        self.assertEqual(result['Windows']['version'],'v0.8.0')
        self.assertEqual((await api.latest())['tag_name'],'v0.9.0')

    async def test_current_published_versionless_mac_packages(self):
        data=release('v0.9.0_MAC',assets=[asset('GRAILD-macOS-arm64.zip','v0.9.0_MAC'),asset('GRAILD-macOS-x86_64.zip','v0.9.0_MAC')])
        client=Mock();client.get=AsyncMock(return_value=[data])
        result=await GitHubReleases(client).downloads()
        self.assertEqual(set(result),{'Apple Silicon Mac','Intel Mac'})
        self.assertEqual(result['Intel Mac']['version'],'v0.9.0_MAC')

    async def test_wrong_origin_and_upload_state_excluded(self):
        bad=asset('GRAILD.exe');bad['browser_download_url']='https://evil.example/GRAILD.exe'
        unfinished=asset('GRAILD-0.9.0-macOS-arm64.zip');unfinished['state']='new'
        client=Mock();client.get=AsyncMock(return_value=[release(assets=[bad,unfinished])])
        self.assertEqual(await GitHubReleases(client).downloads(),{})

    async def test_no_betas_and_bad_payload_fail(self):
        for response in ([],{},[release(prerelease=False)]):
            client=Mock();client.get=AsyncMock(return_value=response)
            with self.assertRaises(APIError):await GitHubReleases(client).latest()

    def test_platform_names(self):
        self.assertEqual(platform_for('GRAILD-0.9.0-macOS-x86_64.zip'),'Intel Mac')
        for value in ['GRAILDUpdater.exe','GRAILD-GitHub-Mac-Build.zip','GRAILD-0.9.0-macOS-arm64-build-kit.zip','Source code.zip']:
            self.assertIsNone(platform_for(value))


class BackendTests(unittest.IsolatedAsyncioTestCase):
    async def test_top_ten_public_allowlist(self):
        data={'entries':[{'rank':i+1,'display_name':'name','points':12,'correct':2,'wrong':1,'email':'private','token':'secret'} for i in range(15)]}
        client=Mock();client.get=AsyncMock(return_value=data)
        api=GraildAPI(client)
        entries=await api.leaderboard()
        self.assertEqual(len(entries),10)
        self.assertNotIn('email',entries[0]);self.assertNotIn('token',entries[0])
        client.get.assert_awaited_once_with(api.base_url+'/leaderboard')

    async def test_invalid_totals_are_not_invented(self):
        client=Mock();client.get=AsyncMock(return_value={'entries':[{'display_name':'name','rank':1,'points':2}]})
        with self.assertRaises(APIError):await GraildAPI(client).leaderboard()

    async def test_profile_never_guesses_endpoint(self):
        client=Mock();client.get=AsyncMock()
        with self.assertRaises(ProfileUnavailable):await GraildAPI(client).profile(123456789)
        client.get.assert_not_called()


class Response:
    def __init__(self,status=200,data=b'{"ok":true}'):
        self.status=status;self.data=data;self.content=self
    async def __aenter__(self):return self
    async def __aexit__(self,*args):pass
    async def iter_chunked(self,size):yield self.data


class HTTPTests(unittest.IsolatedAsyncioTestCase):
    async def test_cached_and_redirects_disabled(self):
        session=Mock();session.get.return_value=Response()
        api=JSONClient(session)
        self.assertEqual(await api.get('https://example.test'),{'ok':True})
        await api.get('https://example.test')
        session.get.assert_called_once_with('https://example.test',allow_redirects=False)

    async def test_errors_and_limits(self):
        for response in [Response(429),Response(503),Response(302),Response(data=b'invalid'),Response(data=b'x'*100)]:
            session=Mock();session.get.return_value=response
            with self.assertRaises(APIError):await JSONClient(session,max_bytes=50).get('https://example.test')

    async def test_timeout_is_safe(self):
        session=Mock();session.get.side_effect=asyncio.TimeoutError
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(APIError,'timed out'):await JSONClient(session).get('https://example.test')


class ConfigTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_defaults_to_global_without_test_server(self):
        with patch.dict(os.environ,{'DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN':'fake-for-unit-test'},clear=True):
            self.assertEqual(Settings.from_env().scope, 'global')

    def test_no_privileged_intents_and_exact_group(self):
        settings=Settings('fake','guild',123,None,'https://example.test')
        bot=GraildBot(settings)
        group=GraildCommands(bot)
        self.assertEqual({c.name for c in group.commands},{'download','updates','leaderboard','profile','help'})
        self.assertFalse(bot.intents.members);self.assertFalse(bot.intents.presences);self.assertFalse(bot.intents.message_content)

    def test_invalid_origins_and_scope(self):
        for value in ['http://example.test','https://user:secret@example.test','https://example.test/path']:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):https_origin(value)
        with patch.dict(os.environ,{'DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN':'fake','DISCORD_COMMAND_SCOPE':'invalid'},clear=True):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):Settings.from_env()


class CommandTests(unittest.IsolatedAsyncioTestCase):
    async def test_defer_before_backend_and_no_mentions(self):
        sequence=[]
        async def defer(**kwargs):sequence.append('defer')
        async def request():sequence.append('request');return []
        fake=Mock();fake.api.leaderboard=AsyncMock(side_effect=request)
        interaction=Mock();interaction.response.defer=AsyncMock(side_effect=defer);interaction.followup.send=AsyncMock()
        group=GraildCommands(fake)
        await group.leaderboard.callback(group,interaction)
        self.assertEqual(sequence,['defer','request'])
        self.assertFalse(interaction.followup.send.call_args.kwargs['allowed_mentions'].everyone)

    async def test_profile_uses_only_invoker_and_ephemeral(self):
        fake=Mock();fake.api.profile=AsyncMock(side_effect=ProfileUnavailable('Pending'))
        interaction=Mock();interaction.user.id=123;interaction.response.defer=AsyncMock();interaction.followup.send=AsyncMock()
        group=GraildCommands(fake)
        await group.profile.callback(group,interaction)
        fake.api.profile.assert_awaited_once_with(123)
        self.assertTrue(interaction.response.defer.call_args.kwargs['ephemeral'])
        self.assertTrue(interaction.followup.send.call_args.kwargs['ephemeral'])

    async def test_legacy_guild_setting_syncs_global_and_removes_only_old_group(self):
        settings=Settings('fake','guild',123,None,'https://example.test')
        bot=GraildBot(settings)
        bot.tree.sync=AsyncMock(return_value=[])
        legacy=Mock(name='legacy');legacy.name='graild';legacy.type=discord.AppCommandType.chat_input
        legacy.delete=AsyncMock()
        other=Mock();other.name='other';other.delete=AsyncMock()
        bot.tree.fetch_commands=AsyncMock(return_value=[legacy,other])
        try:
            await bot.setup_hook()
            bot.tree.sync.assert_awaited_once_with()
            self.assertEqual(bot.tree.fetch_commands.call_args.kwargs['guild'].id,123)
            legacy.delete.assert_awaited_once_with()
            other.delete.assert_not_awaited()
            self.assertEqual([c.name for c in bot.tree.get_commands()],['graild'])
            await bot.sync_public_commands()
            bot.tree.sync.assert_awaited_once_with()
        finally:await bot.close()



class GlobalRegistrationTests(unittest.IsolatedAsyncioTestCase):
    async def test_explicit_global_scope(self):
        bot=GraildBot(Settings('fake','global',None,None,'https://example.test'))
        bot.tree.sync=AsyncMock(return_value=[])
        try:
            await bot.setup_hook()
            bot.tree.sync.assert_awaited_once_with()
            self.assertEqual([c.name for c in bot.tree.get_commands()],['graild'])
        finally:await bot.close()

    async def test_sync_failure_stops_startup_without_deleting_guild_commands(self):
        bot=GraildBot(Settings('fake','guild',123,None,'https://example.test'))
        bot.tree.sync=AsyncMock(side_effect=discord.Forbidden(
            Mock(status=403,reason='Forbidden'), {'code':50001,'message':'Missing Access'}))
        bot.tree.fetch_commands=AsyncMock()
        try:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError,'synchronization failed'):
                await bot.setup_hook()
            bot.tree.fetch_commands.assert_not_awaited()
            self.assertFalse(bot._commands_synced)
        finally:await bot.close()

    async def test_commands_loaded_before_sync_and_success_log_lists_subcommands(self):
        bot=GraildBot(Settings('fake','global',None,None,'https://example.test'))
        from types import SimpleNamespace
        group=bot.tree.get_command('graild')
        remote=SimpleNamespace(name='graild', options=[SimpleNamespace(
            name=c.name,type=discord.AppCommandOptionType.subcommand,options=[]) for c in group.commands])
        bot.tree.sync=AsyncMock(return_value=[remote])
        try:
            with self.assertLogs('graild.bot',level='INFO') as logs:
                await bot.setup_hook()
            for command in group.commands:
                self.assertIn('/graild '+command.name, '\n'.join(logs.output))
        finally:await bot.close()


if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

================================================================================
GRAILD-mac.spec
================================================================================
# -*- mode: python ; coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import runpy
from pathlib import Path
from PyInstaller.utils.hooks import collect_all, collect_data_files

root = Path(SPECPATH)
version = runpy.run_path(str(root / "app_version.py"))["APP_VERSION"]
datas = [(str(root / name), '.') for name in ('index.html', 'canary_ui.js', 'update_ui.js')]
datas += [(str(root / 'assets'), 'assets')]
datas += collect_data_files('certifi')
binaries = []
hiddenimports = ['keyring.backends.macOS']
for package in ('pygame', 'webview', 'imageio_ffmpeg', 'keyring'):
    data, binary, hidden = collect_all(package)
    # pywebview ships Windows SDK DLLs alongside Cocoa resources. Do not ship
    # or attempt to Mach-O-sign those PE files in a native Mac bundle.
    data = [item for item in data if not item[0].lower().endswith(('.dll', '.exe', '.pyd'))]
    binary = [item for item in binary if not item[0].lower().endswith(('.dll', '.exe', '.pyd'))]
    datas += data; binaries += binary; hiddenimports += hidden

a = Analysis([str(root / 'mac_launcher.py')], pathex=[str(root)], binaries=binaries,
             datas=datas, hiddenimports=hiddenimports,
             excludes=['tkinter', 'webview.platforms.winforms', 'webview.platforms.edgechromium',
                       'webview.platforms.mshtml', 'webview.platforms.gtk', 'webview.platforms.qt'],
             noarchive=False)
pyz = PYZ(a.pure)
identity = os.environ.get('GRAILD_MAC_SIGNING_IDENTITY') or None
exe = EXE(pyz, a.scripts, [], exclude_binaries=True, name='GRAILD', console=False,
          codesign_identity=identity, target_arch=None)
coll = COLLECT(exe, a.binaries, a.datas, name='GRAILD')
app = BUNDLE(coll, name='GRAILD.app', bundle_identifier='com.graild.desktop',
             icon=str(root / 'assets' / 'graild.icns'),
             codesign_identity=identity,
             info_plist={'CFBundleShortVersionString': version, 'CFBundleVersion': version,
                         'NSHighResolutionCapable': True,
                         'NSAppTransportSecurity': {'NSAllowsLocalNetworking': True}})

                        <p class="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-purple-400">Your trophy room</p>
                        <h2 class="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">Chase the next milestone</h2>
                        <p id="achievement-summary" class="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-300" role="status">0 of 50 unlocked</p>
                        <progress id="achievement-overall" max="50" value="0" aria-label="Achievements unlocked" class="mt-3 h-2 w-full accent-purple-500"></progress>
                        <p class="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500">Earned badges stay unlocked. Existing stats count; first-attempt and Hard-mode wins are tracked from this update.</p>
                    </div>
                    <label class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm text-slate-300">Show achievements
                        <select id="achievement-filter" onchange="renderAchievements()" class="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-[#0d0d12] px-3 py-2 text-white">
                            <option value="all">All achievements</option>
                            <option value="unlocked">Unlocked</option>
                            <option value="locked">In progress</option>
                        </select>
                    </label>
                    <div id="achievement-list" class="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2"></div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>

        <section id="tab-settings" class="hidden max-w-2xl mx-auto space-y-6">
            <div class="glass-card rounded-3xl p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-5">
                <div><h2 class="text-2xl font-black">Settings</h2><p class="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-400">GRAIL'D <span id="settings-current-version">0.8.0</span></p></div>
                <h3 class="text-lg font-bold">Beta updates</h3>
                <form onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); saveUpdateSettings()" class="space-y-4">
                    <label class="block text-sm text-slate-300">GitHub repository
                        <input id="update-repository" maxlength="140" placeholder="owner/repository" class="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-[#0d0d12] px-3 py-2 text-white">
                    </label>
                    <label class="block text-sm text-slate-300">Release channel
                        <select id="update-channel" class="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-[#0d0d12] px-3 py-2 text-white"><option value="beta">Beta (includes prereleases)</option><option value="stable">Stable</option></select>
                    </label>
                    <label class="flex gap-3 text-sm text-slate-300"><input id="update-automatic" type="checkbox" checked class="accent-purple-500">Check automatically when GRAIL'D starts</label>
                    <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-3"><button type="submit" class="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-bold">Save update settings</button><button id="manual-update-check" type="button" onclick="manualUpdateCheck()" class="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-bold disabled:opacity-40">Check for Updates</button></div>
                </form>
                <p id="update-settings-status" class="text-sm text-cyan-400" role="status"></p>
                <p id="update-last-result" class="text-xs text-slate-400"></p>
                <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Updates are installed after GRAIL'D closes. Your account, sign-in, music preferences, and Canary cache stay in your app data folder.</p>
            </div>
        </section>
    </main>
    <aside id="update-notification" class="hidden fixed bottom-4 right-4 left-4 sm:left-auto sm:w-[30rem] z-[70] rounded-2xl border border-purple-400/50 bg-[#181824] p-5 shadow-2xl" aria-labelledby="update-heading" aria-live="polite">
        <h2 id="update-heading" class="text-lg font-black">GRAIL'D update <span id="update-version"></span></h2>

        <div class="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3"><button id="update-now" type="button" onclick="updateNow()" class="rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-bold disabled:opacity-40">Update Now</button><button id="update-later" type="button" onclick="updateLater()" class="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 px-5 py-2 text-sm">Later</button><button id="update-cancel" type="button" onclick="cancelUpdateDownload()" class="hidden rounded-lg border border-rose-500/40 px-5 py-2 text-sm">Cancel download</button></div>
    </aside>

    <dialog id="song-information" aria-labelledby="song-info-heading" class="m-auto w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-3xl max-h-[85vh] overflow-y-auto rounded-3xl border border-purple-500/40 bg-[#181824] p-6 text-white shadow-2xl">
        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-4"><h2 id="song-info-heading" class="text-xl font-black">Song Information</h2><button type="button" onclick="document.getElementById('song-information').close()" class="rounded-lg border border-slate-700 px-3 py-2 text-sm">Close</button></div>
        <p id="song-info-status" class="mt-3 text-sm text-cyan-400" role="status"></p>
        <div id="song-info-content" class="mt-4 space-y-4"></div>
        <form id="song-match-search-form" class="hidden mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-2" onsubmit="event.preventDefault(); searchLocalCanary()">
            <label class="flex-1 text-xs text-slate-400">Search another title or alias<input id="song-match-query" maxlength="200" class="mt-1 w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-[#0d0d12] px-3 py-2 text-white" required></label>
            <button type="submit" class="self-end rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold">Find recordings</button>

    <!-- FOOTER -->
    <footer class="border-t border-slate-800 bg-[#0d0d12] py-6 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 space-y-1">
