    <script>
        const tabIds = ['hub', 'game', 'catalog', 'leaderboard', 'account', 'settings'];
        let appState = null;
        let catalogSongs = [];
        let catalogFilter = 'all';
        let socialPollTimer = null;
        let snippetPlaybackTimer = null;
        let achievementState = {entries: [], unlocked: 0, total: 50};

        function switchTab(tabId) {
            const nextPanel = document.getElementById('tab-' + tabId);
            if (!nextPanel || !nextPanel.classList.contains('hidden')) return;
            tabIds.forEach(id => {
                document.getElementById('tab-' + id).classList.toggle('hidden', id !== tabId);
                const navButton = document.getElementById('btn-' + id);
                if (navButton) navButton.className = id === tabId
                    ? 'px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-bold transition-all bg-purple-600 text-white shadow-lg'
                    : 'px-5 py-2 rounded-full text-xs font-bold transition-all text-slate-400 hover:text-white';
            });
            nextPanel.classList.remove('tab-panel-enter');
            void nextPanel.offsetWidth;
            nextPanel.classList.add('tab-panel-enter');
            if (tabId === 'leaderboard') refreshGlobalLeaderboard();
        }

        function showFeedback(message, success = false) {
            const banner = document.getElementById('feedback-banner');
            banner.textContent = message;
            banner.className = `text-center font-bold text-sm h-6 ${success ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}`;
        }

        function showAuthForm(mode) {
            const registering = mode === 'register';
            document.getElementById('sign-in-form').classList.toggle('hidden', registering);
            document.getElementById('register-form').classList.toggle('hidden', !registering);
            document.getElementById('signin-tab').className = registering
                ? 'rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-white'
                : 'rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-white';
            document.getElementById('register-tab').className = registering
                ? 'rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-white'
                : 'rounded-lg px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-white';
        }

        function renderAccountState(authState) {
            const globalGeneration = ++globalTagGeneration;
            tagAccountRequest += 1;
            const signedIn = authState.authenticated === true && authState.profile;
            globalTagCanManage = Boolean(signedIn && authState.profile.is_creator);
            document.getElementById('auth-card').classList.toggle('hidden', Boolean(signedIn));
            document.getElementById('profile-card').classList.toggle('hidden', !signedIn);
            if (!signedIn) {
                document.getElementById('profile-tags').replaceChildren();
                document.getElementById('tag-management').classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('tag-account-select').replaceChildren();
                showAuthForm('signin');
                switchAccountTab('profile');
                achievementState = {entries: [], unlocked: 0, total: 50};
                renderAchievements();
                return;
            }
            achievementState = authState.achievements || {entries: [], unlocked: 0, total: 50};
            renderAchievements();
            const profile = authState.profile;
            document.getElementById('profile-avatar').textContent = profile.avatar;
            document.getElementById('profile-display-name').textContent = profile.display_name;
            document.getElementById('profile-owner-tag').classList.toggle('hidden', !profile.is_creator);
            renderProfileTags(profile.tags || []);
            document.getElementById('tag-management').classList.toggle('hidden', !profile.is_creator);
            if (profile.is_creator) loadTagAccounts();
            else document.getElementById('tag-account-select').replaceChildren();
            document.getElementById('profile-username').textContent = `@${profile.username}`;
            document.getElementById('profile-created').textContent = `Member since ${new Date(profile.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}`;
            document.getElementById('profile-connected-providers').textContent = profile.linked_providers.length
                ? `Connected: ${profile.linked_providers.map(name => name[0].toUpperCase() + name.slice(1)).join(', ')}`
                : 'No connected accounts';
            document.getElementById('profile-correct').textContent = profile.total_correct;
            document.getElementById('profile-wrong').textContent = profile.total_wrong;
            document.getElementById('profile-accuracy').textContent = `${profile.accuracy}%`;
            document.getElementById('profile-current-streak').textContent = profile.current_streak;
            document.getElementById('profile-best-streak').textContent = profile.best_streak;
            document.getElementById('profile-points').textContent = profile.total_points;
            document.getElementById('profile-songs-played').textContent = profile.songs_played;
            document.getElementById('profile-grails').textContent = profile.grails_collected;
            document.getElementById('profile-edit-name').value = profile.display_name;
            document.getElementById('profile-edit-avatar').value = profile.avatar;
            document.getElementById('global-player-id').textContent = profile.global_player_id ? `Global account: ${profile.global_player_id}` : '';
            refreshGlobalTags(globalGeneration);
        }

        let tagAccountRequest = 0;
        let globalTagGeneration = 0;
        let globalTagSyncRequest = 0;
        let globalTagCanManage = false;

        async function refreshGlobalTags(generation) {
            const request = ++globalTagSyncRequest;
            const status = document.getElementById('global-tags-status');
            status.textContent = 'Syncing global tags...';
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.sync_global_tags();
                if (generation !== globalTagGeneration || request !== globalTagSyncRequest) return;
                status.textContent = result.message;
                if (!result.success) return;
                globalTagCanManage = result.can_manage_tags === true;
                renderProfileTags(result.tags || []);
                document.getElementById('global-player-id').textContent = `Global account: ${result.player_id}`;
                document.getElementById('profile-owner-tag').classList.toggle('hidden', !result.tags.some(tag => tag.id === 'owner'));
                document.getElementById('tag-management').classList.toggle('hidden', !result.can_manage_tags);
                if (result.can_manage_tags) { await loadTagCatalog(); loadTagAccounts(); }
            } catch (error) {
                if (generation === globalTagGeneration && request === globalTagSyncRequest) status.textContent = 'Global tags could not be refreshed. Your last synced tags are shown.';
            }
        }

        function renderProfileTags(tags) {
            const container = document.getElementById('profile-tags');
            container.replaceChildren();
            for (const tag of tags.filter(item => item.id !== 'owner')) {
                const badge = document.createElement('span');
                badge.className = 'profile-tag';
                badge.dataset.kind = tag.kind;
                badge.tabIndex = 0;
                badge.textContent = tag.name;
                const description = document.createElement('span');
                description.className = 'tag-tooltip';
                description.id = `profile-tag-description-${container.children.length}`;
                description.setAttribute('role', 'tooltip');
                description.textContent = tag.description;
                badge.setAttribute('aria-describedby', description.id);
                badge.append(description);
                container.append(badge);
            }
            const topEarner = tags.some(tag => tag.id === 'top_earner');
            document.getElementById('top-earner-progress').textContent = topEarner
                ? 'Top earner unlocked: you earned all 50 achievements.'
                : achievementState.unlocked === 50 ? 'All 50 achievements unlocked. Sync global tags to recognize Top earner on your account.'
                : `Top earner: unlock all 50 achievements to earn this tag (${achievementState.unlocked}/50).`;
        }

        async function loadTagAccounts() {
            const request = ++tagAccountRequest;
            const select = document.getElementById('tag-account-select');
            const previous = select.value;
            const status = document.getElementById('tag-management-status');
            select.disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('tag-assign-button').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('tag-remove-button').disabled = true;
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.get_tag_accounts();
                if (request !== tagAccountRequest) return;
                select.replaceChildren();
                if (!result.success) { status.textContent = result.message; return; }
                for (const account of result.accounts) {
                    const option = document.createElement('option');
                    option.value = account.player_id;
                    option.textContent = `${account.display_name} (${account.provider}, ${account.player_id.slice(0, 8)})${account.beta_tester ? ' — Beta Tester' : ''}`;
                    option.title = account.player_id;
                    select.append(option);
                }
                if (Array.from(select.options).some(option => option.value === previous)) select.value = previous;
                select.disabled = !select.options.length;
                document.getElementById('tag-assign-button').disabled = select.disabled;
                document.getElementById('tag-remove-button').disabled = select.disabled;
            } catch (error) {
                if (request === tagAccountRequest) status.textContent = 'Could not load accounts. Reopen your profile to retry.';
            }
        }

        async function assignTesterTag(assigned) {
            const status = document.getElementById('tag-management-status');
            const username = document.getElementById('tag-account-select').value;
            if (!username) return;
            const request = tagAccountRequest;
            document.getElementById('tag-assign-button').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('tag-remove-button').disabled = true;
            status.textContent = 'Saving tag...';
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.set_account_tag(username, selectedTagId, assigned);
                if (request !== tagAccountRequest) return;
                status.textContent = result.message;
                if (result.success) renderAccountState(await window.pywebview.api.get_account_state());
                else await loadTagAccounts();
            } catch (error) {
                if (request !== tagAccountRequest) return;
                status.textContent = 'Could not save the tag. Please retry.';
                await loadTagAccounts();
            }
        }

        async function assignDiscordTesterTag(assigned) {
            const generation = globalTagGeneration;
            const status = document.getElementById('tag-management-status');
            const input = document.getElementById('tag-discord-id');
            const buttons = [document.getElementById('discord-tag-assign'), document.getElementById('discord-tag-remove')];
            if (!input.value.trim() || buttons.some(button => button.disabled)) return;
            buttons.forEach(button => button.disabled = true);
            status.textContent = 'Saving Discord account tag...';
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.set_discord_account_tag(input.value.trim(), selectedTagId, assigned);
                if (generation !== globalTagGeneration) return;
                status.textContent = result.message;
                if (result.success) { await loadTagAccounts(); await refreshGlobalTags(generation); }
            } catch (error) {
                if (generation === globalTagGeneration) status.textContent = 'Could not save the Discord tag. Please retry.';
            } finally {
                buttons.forEach(button => button.disabled = false);
            }
        }

        async function assignLeaderboardTag(button, playerId, assigned, tagId = selectedTagId) {
            const generation = globalTagGeneration;
            const status = document.getElementById('global-status');
            button.disabled = true;
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.set_account_tag(playerId, tagId, assigned);
                if (generation !== globalTagGeneration) return;
                if (result.success) await refreshGlobalLeaderboard();
                status.textContent = result.message;
            } catch (error) {
                if (generation === globalTagGeneration) status.textContent = 'Could not save the tag. Refresh and retry.';
            } finally { button.disabled = false; }
        }

        let selectedTagId = 'beta_tester';
        let assignableTags = [{id:'beta_tester', name:'Beta Tester'}];
        let tagCatalogRequest = 0;

        async function loadTagCatalog(preferredId = selectedTagId) {
            const generation = globalTagGeneration;
            const request = ++tagCatalogRequest;
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.get_tag_catalog();
                if (generation !== globalTagGeneration || request !== tagCatalogRequest || !result.success || !Array.isArray(result.entries)) return;
                assignableTags = result.entries;
                selectedTagId = assignableTags.some(tag => tag.id === preferredId) ? preferredId : 'beta_tester';
                const select = document.getElementById('tag-definition-select');
                select.replaceChildren();
                for (const tag of assignableTags) {
                    const option = document.createElement('option');
                    option.value = tag.id; option.textContent = tag.name; option.title = tag.description || '';
                    select.append(option);
                }
                select.value = selectedTagId;
            } catch (error) { /* Existing assigned tags remain usable if the catalog is offline. */ }
        }

        async function createCustomTag() {
            const generation = globalTagGeneration;
            const button = document.getElementById('custom-tag-create');
            if (button.disabled) return;
            button.disabled = true;
            const status = document.getElementById('tag-management-status');
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.create_custom_tag(
                    document.getElementById('custom-tag-name').value.trim(),
                    document.getElementById('custom-tag-description').value.trim());
                if (generation !== globalTagGeneration) return;
                status.textContent = result.message;
                if (result.success) {
                    await loadTagCatalog(result.tag.id);
                    document.getElementById('custom-tag-name').value = '';
                    document.getElementById('custom-tag-description').value = '';
                }
            } catch (error) {
                if (generation === globalTagGeneration) status.textContent = 'Could not create the custom tag. Please retry.';
            } finally { button.disabled = false; }
        }

        function switchAccountTab(tab) {
            for (const name of ['profile', 'achievements']) {
                const active = tab === name;
                document.getElementById(`account-${name}-panel`).classList.toggle('hidden', !active);
                const button = document.getElementById(`account-${name}-tab`);
                button.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(active));
                button.className = active
                    ? 'flex-1 rounded-lg bg-purple-600 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white'
                    : 'flex-1 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-slate-400 hover:text-white';
            }
        }

        function renderAchievements() {
            const {entries, unlocked, total} = achievementState;
            document.getElementById('achievement-tab-count').textContent = `${unlocked}/${total}`;
            document.getElementById('achievement-summary').textContent = `${unlocked} of ${total} unlocked`;
            document.getElementById('achievement-overall').value = unlocked;
            const filter = document.getElementById('achievement-filter').value;
            const list = document.getElementById('achievement-list');
            list.replaceChildren();
            for (const item of entries) {
                if (filter === 'unlocked' && !item.unlocked) continue;
                if (filter === 'locked' && item.unlocked) continue;
                const card = document.createElement('article');
                card.className = item.unlocked
                    ? 'rounded-2xl border border-amber-400/40 bg-amber-400/5 p-4 space-y-2'
                    : 'rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-[#0d0d12] p-4 space-y-2';
                const category = document.createElement('p');
                category.className = 'text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-purple-400';
                category.textContent = item.category;
                const title = document.createElement('h3');
                title.className = 'font-bold text-white';
                title.textContent = `${item.unlocked ? '\u2605' : '\u25c7'} ${item.name}`;
                const description = document.createElement('p');
                description.className = 'text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-400';
                description.textContent = item.description;
                const progress = document.createElement('progress');
                progress.max = item.target;
                progress.value = item.progress;
                progress.setAttribute('aria-label', item.name);
                progress.className = 'h-1.5 w-full accent-purple-500';
                const status = document.createElement('p');
                status.className = item.unlocked ? 'text-xs text-amber-300' : 'text-xs text-slate-400';
                status.textContent = item.unlocked
                    ? `Unlocked ${new Date(item.unlocked_at).toLocaleDateString()}`
                    : `${item.progress.toLocaleString()} / ${item.target.toLocaleString()}`;
                card.append(category, title, description, progress, status);
                list.append(card);
            }
            if (!list.childElementCount) {
                const empty = document.createElement('p');
                empty.className = 'text-sm text-slate-400 sm:col-span-2';
                empty.textContent = filter === 'unlocked'
                    ? 'Your first trophy is waiting. Play a round to get started.'
                    : 'No achievements to show for this filter.';
                list.append(empty);
            }
        }

        async function connectSocial(provider) {
            const profileView = !document.getElementById('profile-card').classList.contains('hidden');
            const statusNode = document.getElementById(profileView ? 'profile-social-message' : 'auth-social-message');
            statusNode.textContent = `Starting ${provider} authorization...`;
            try {
                const started = await window.pywebview.api.start_social_auth(provider);
                if (!started.success) {
                    statusNode.textContent = started.message;
                    return;
                }
                statusNode.textContent = started.message;
                if (socialPollTimer) window.clearInterval(socialPollTimer);
                const deadline = Date.now() + 180000;
                socialPollTimer = window.setInterval(async () => {
                    try {
                        const result = await window.pywebview.api.get_social_auth_status();
                        if (result.status !== 'complete' && Date.now() < deadline) return;
                        window.clearInterval(socialPollTimer);
                        socialPollTimer = null;
                        statusNode.textContent = result.message || 'Provider sign-in timed out.';
                        if (!result.success) return;
                        if (result.registration_required) {
                            showAuthForm('register');
                            switchTab('account');
                            document.getElementById('register-username').focus();
                            document.getElementById('auth-social-message').textContent = result.email
                                ? `${result.message} Verified email: ${result.email}`
                                : result.message;
                            return;
                        }
                        if (result.authenticated) {
                            renderAccountState(result);
                            renderState(await window.pywebview.api.get_state());
                            await refreshLeaderboard();
                            document.getElementById('profile-social-message').textContent = result.message;
                            switchTab('account');
                        }
                    } catch (error) {
                        window.clearInterval(socialPollTimer);
                        socialPollTimer = null;
                        statusNode.textContent = `Provider sign-in failed: ${error.message}`;
                    }
                }, 700);
            } catch (error) {
                statusNode.textContent = `Could not connect ${provider}: ${error.message}`;
            }
        }

        async function signIn(event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            const message = document.getElementById('auth-message');
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.sign_in(
                    document.getElementById('signin-username').value,
                    document.getElementById('signin-password').value
                );
                message.textContent = result.message;
                if (!result.success) return;
                document.getElementById('signin-password').value = '';
                renderAccountState(result);
                renderState(await window.pywebview.api.get_state());
                await refreshLeaderboard();
                document.getElementById('profile-message').textContent = result.message;
            } catch (error) {
                message.textContent = `Sign-in failed: ${error.message}`;
            }
        }

        async function registerAccount(event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            const message = document.getElementById('auth-message');
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.register_account(
                    document.getElementById('register-username').value,
                    document.getElementById('register-password').value
                );
                message.textContent = result.message;
                if (!result.success) return;
                document.getElementById('register-password').value = '';
                renderAccountState(result);
                renderState(await window.pywebview.api.get_state());
                await refreshLeaderboard();
                document.getElementById('profile-message').textContent = result.message;
            } catch (error) {
                message.textContent = `Account creation failed: ${error.message}`;
            }
        }

        async function saveProfile(event) {
            event.preventDefault();
            const message = document.getElementById('profile-message');
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.update_profile(
                    document.getElementById('profile-edit-name').value,
                    document.getElementById('profile-edit-avatar').value
                );
                message.textContent = result.message;
                if (result.success) renderAccountState(result);
            } catch (error) {
                message.textContent = `Profile update failed: ${error.message}`;
            }
        }

        async function signOut() {
            const result = await window.pywebview.api.sign_out();
            renderAccountState({authenticated: false});
            renderState(await window.pywebview.api.get_state());
            await refreshLeaderboard();
            document.getElementById('auth-message').textContent = result.message;
            document.getElementById('profile-message').textContent = '';
            switchTab('account');
        }

        function renderState(state) {
            document.getElementById('game-resume-status').textContent = state.resume_error
                ? 'Could not save this round. Keep the app open and try again before closing.'
                : 'Your current round saves automatically. Return here to resume it.';
            appState = state;
            resetSnippetButton();
            const randomMode = state.game_mode === 'random';
            for (const mode of ['classic', 'random']) {
                document.getElementById('nav-mode-' + mode).setAttribute('aria-checked', String(mode === state.game_mode));
            }
            document.getElementById('game-mode-title').textContent = randomMode ? 'Random Drop' : 'Classic Snippets';
            document.getElementById('game-mode-description').textContent = randomMode
                ? 'A random starting point, with longer previews each attempt.'
                : 'Guess the song from its intro.';
            updateRecordingEraOptions(state);
            const durations = state.durations;
            const attempt = Math.min(state.attempt, durations.length - 1);
            document.getElementById('current-attempt').textContent = state.has_track ? attempt + 1 : 0;
            document.getElementById('current-attempt').parentElement.lastChild.textContent = ` of ${durations.length}`;
            document.getElementById('snippet-sec').textContent = durations[attempt] || 0;
            document.getElementById('game-score').textContent = `Score: ${state.score}`;
            document.getElementById('game-streak').textContent = state.streak;
            document.getElementById('session-score').textContent = `${state.score} pts`;
            document.getElementById('session-streak').textContent = state.streak;
            document.getElementById('session-correct-guesses').textContent = state.correct_guesses;
            document.getElementById('session-wrong-guesses').textContent = state.wrong_guesses;
            document.getElementById('next-round-btn').classList.toggle('hidden', !state.game_over);
            document.getElementById('session-tracks').textContent = state.track_count;
            document.getElementById('library-summary').textContent = `${state.track_count} audio tracks`;
            document.getElementById('folder-summary').textContent = state.folder;
            document.getElementById('catalog-summary').textContent = `${state.track_count} tracks in ${state.folder}`;
            document.getElementById('difficulty-select').value = state.difficulty;
            document.getElementById('difficulty-select').disabled =
                !state.authenticated || !state.has_track || state.game_over;
            document.getElementById('volume-control').value = state.volume;
            document.getElementById('volume-value').textContent = `${state.volume}%`;
            document.querySelectorAll('#play-btn, #guess-input, #input-area button').forEach(button => {
                button.disabled = !state.authenticated || !state.has_track || state.game_over;
            });
            document.getElementById('reroll-btn').disabled =
                !state.authenticated || !state.has_track || state.game_over || (state.eligible_track_count ?? state.track_count) < 2;
            document.querySelectorAll('#timeline-bars > div').forEach((bar, index) => {
                bar.classList.toggle('bg-purple-500', index <= attempt && state.has_track);
                bar.classList.toggle('bg-slate-700', index > attempt || !state.has_track);
                bar.classList.toggle('hidden', index >= durations.length);
            });
            if (!state.authenticated) {
                showFeedback('Sign in to play and save your profile statistics.');
            } else if (!state.has_track) {
                showFeedback(state.error || 'Choose a folder containing audio files.');
            }
        }

        async function chooseMusicFolder() {
            try {
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.select_music_folder();
                renderState(result.state);
                await refreshCatalog();
                watchAutomaticEraScan();
                await refreshLeaderboard();
                if (result.message) showFeedback(result.message, result.state.track_count > 0);
            } catch (error) {
                showFeedback(`Could not select a music folder: ${error.message}`);
            }
        }

        let changingGameMode = false;

        function setGameMenu(open) {
            document.getElementById('game-mode-menu').hidden = !open;
            document.getElementById('game-menu-toggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
        }

        function handleGameMenuKey(event) {
            if (event.key === 'Escape') {
                event.preventDefault(); setGameMenu(false);
                document.getElementById('game-menu-toggle').focus();
            } else if (event.key === 'ArrowDown' || event.key === 'ArrowUp') {
                event.preventDefault(); setGameMenu(true);
                const items = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#game-mode-menu button'));
                const index = items.indexOf(document.activeElement);
                const next = index < 0 ? (event.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 0 : items.length - 1)
                    : (index + (event.key === 'ArrowDown' ? 1 : items.length - 1)) % items.length;
                items[next].focus();
            }
        }

        async function chooseNavGameMode(mode) {
            setGameMenu(false);
            if (!appState || !appState.authenticated) { await startGameMode(mode); return; }
            await changeGameMode(mode);
            switchTab('game');
        }

        document.addEventListener('pointerdown', event => {
            if (!document.getElementById('game-nav').contains(event.target)) setGameMenu(false);
        });

        async function changeGameMode(mode) {
            if (changingGameMode || !appState || mode === appState.game_mode) return;
            changingGameMode = true;
            document.querySelectorAll('#game-mode-menu button').forEach(button => button.disabled = true);
            try {
                await startGameMode(mode);
            } finally {
                changingGameMode = false;
                document.querySelectorAll('#game-mode-menu button').forEach(button => button.disabled = false);
            }
        }

        async function startGameMode(mode) {
            if (!appState || !appState.authenticated) {
                switchTab('account');
                document.getElementById('auth-message').textContent = 'Sign in to play a game.';
                return;
            }
            const message = document.getElementById('mode-message');
            try {
                const state = await window.pywebview.api.set_game_mode(mode);
                renderState(state);
                resetSnippetButton();
                document.getElementById('guess-input').value = '';
                document.getElementById('suggestions').classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('cover-container').classList.add('hidden');
                showFeedback(state.has_track ? '' : 'Choose a music folder to start.');
                message.textContent = '';
                switchTab('game');
            } catch (error) {
                message.textContent = 'Could not start the game. Please try again.';
                showFeedback(message.textContent);
            }
        }

        async function changeDifficulty(difficulty) {
            renderState(await window.pywebview.api.set_difficulty(difficulty));
            showFeedback('New round started.', true);
        }

        async function changeVolume(value) {
            document.getElementById('volume-value').textContent = `${value}%`;
            const result = await window.pywebview.api.set_volume(Number(value) / 100);
            if (result.error) showFeedback(result.error);
        }

        function resetSnippetButton() {
            window.clearTimeout(snippetPlaybackTimer);
            document.getElementById('snippet-play-label').textContent = 'PLAY';
            document.getElementById('snippet-play-icon').textContent = '\u25b6';
            document.getElementById('play-btn').classList.remove('animate-pulse');
        }

        async function playSnippetSimulation() {
            const button = document.getElementById('play-btn');
            const result = await window.pywebview.api.play_snippet();
            if (!result.ok) {
                if (result.state) {
                    renderState(result.state);
                    await refreshCatalog();
                }
                showFeedback(result.message);
                return;
            }
            resetSnippetButton();
            document.getElementById('snippet-play-label').textContent = 'PLAYING';
            document.getElementById('snippet-play-icon').textContent = '\u266b';
            button.classList.add('animate-pulse');
            snippetPlaybackTimer = window.setTimeout(resetSnippetButton, result.duration * 1000);
        }

        function handleTyping(event) {
            const query = event.target.value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase();
            const box = document.getElementById('suggestions');
            box.replaceChildren();
            const matches = query ? catalogSongs.filter(song => song.title.toLocaleLowerCase().includes(query)).slice(0, 6) : [];
            matches.forEach(song => {
                const item = document.createElement('button');
                item.type = 'button';
                item.className = 'block w-full p-3 text-left hover:bg-purple-600 hover:text-white text-xs text-cyan-400 font-semibold border-b border-slate-800';
                item.textContent = song.title;
                item.addEventListener('click', () => selectSuggestion(song.title));
                box.append(item);
            });
            box.classList.toggle('hidden', matches.length === 0);
        }

        function selectSuggestion(title) {
            document.getElementById('guess-input').value = title;
            document.getElementById('suggestions').classList.add('hidden');
        }

        async function submitGuess() {
            const guess = document.getElementById('guess-input').value.trim();
            if (!guess) {
                showFeedback('Type a song title first.');
                return;
            }
            await applyRoundResult(await window.pywebview.api.submit_guess(guess));
        }

        async function rerollSong() {
            const result = await window.pywebview.api.reroll_song();
            renderState(result.state);
            document.getElementById('guess-input').value = '';
            document.getElementById('suggestions').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('cover-container').classList.add('hidden');
            showFeedback(result.message, result.changed);
        }

        async function skipAttempt() {
            await applyRoundResult(await window.pywebview.api.skip_attempt());
        }

        async function applyRoundResult(result) {
            renderState(result.state);
            await refreshLeaderboard();
            const profileState = await window.pywebview.api.get_account_state();
            renderAccountState(profileState);
            const feedback = result.audio_error
                ? `${result.message} Audio error: ${result.audio_error}`
                : result.message || '';
            showFeedback(feedback, result.success === true && !result.audio_error);
            if (result.state.game_over) {
                showCover(result.cover, result.state.title, result.success === true);
            }
        }

        async function startNextRound() {
            const state = await window.pywebview.api.next_round();
            renderState(state);
            document.getElementById('guess-input').value = '';
            document.getElementById('suggestions').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('cover-container').classList.add('hidden');
            showFeedback(state.has_track ? 'New round started.' : state.error || 'No songs are available.', state.has_track);
        }

        function showCover(source, title, correct = false) {
            const container = document.getElementById('cover-container');
            const art = document.getElementById('cover-art-inner');
            container.classList.remove('correct-cover-reveal');
            if (correct) void container.offsetWidth;
            art.replaceChildren();
            if (source) {
                const image = document.createElement('img');
                image.src = source;
                image.alt = `${title} cover art`;
                image.className = 'w-full h-full object-cover rounded-xl';
                art.append(image);
            } else {
                art.textContent = '💿';
            }
            document.getElementById('correct-song-title').textContent = title;
            container.classList.remove('hidden');
            if (correct) container.classList.add('correct-cover-reveal');
        }

        async function refreshCatalog() {
            catalogSongs = await window.pywebview.api.get_catalog();
            renderCatalog(catalogFilter);
        }

        async function refreshGlobalLeaderboard() {
            const button = document.getElementById('global-refresh');
            if (button.disabled) return;
            button.disabled = true;
            const status = document.getElementById('global-status');
            const list = document.getElementById('global-leaderboard');
            status.textContent = 'Loading global rankings...';
            const generation = globalTagGeneration;
            try {
                const permissions = await window.pywebview.api.sync_global_tags();
                if (generation !== globalTagGeneration) return;
                globalTagCanManage = permissions.success === true && permissions.can_manage_tags === true;
                if (globalTagCanManage) await loadTagCatalog();
                const result = await window.pywebview.api.get_global_leaderboard();
                if (generation !== globalTagGeneration) return;
                list.replaceChildren();
                status.textContent = result.message;
                for (const entry of result.entries || []) {
                    const row = document.createElement('div');
                    row.className = 'flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-xl bg-[#0d0d12] p-4 text-sm';
                    const name = document.createElement('span');
                    name.className = 'min-w-0 break-words font-bold';
                    name.textContent = `${entry.rank}. ${entry.display_name}`;
                    const score = document.createElement('span');
                    score.className = 'shrink-0 text-cyan-400';
                    score.textContent = `${Number(entry.points).toLocaleString()} pts`;
                    row.append(name, score);
                for (const tag of entry.tags || []) {
                    const badge = document.createElement('span');
                    badge.className = 'profile-tag';
                    badge.textContent = tag.name;
                    badge.title = tag.description;
                    badge.tabIndex = 0;
                    row.append(badge);
                }
                    if (globalTagCanManage && /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(entry.player_id || '')) {
                        const selector = document.createElement('select');
                        selector.className = 'rounded-lg border border-slate-700 bg-[#0d0d12] px-3 py-2 text-xs';
                        selector.setAttribute('aria-label', `Tag for ${entry.display_name}`);
                        for (const tag of assignableTags) {
                            const option = document.createElement('option');
                            option.value = tag.id; option.textContent = tag.name; selector.append(option);
                        }
                        selector.value = selectedTagId;
                        const control = document.createElement('button');
                        control.type = 'button';
                        control.className = 'rounded-lg border border-slate-700 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-cyan-300';
                        const hasTag = () => (entry.tags || []).some(tag => tag.id === selector.value);
                        const label = () => {
                            const name = assignableTags.find(tag => tag.id === selector.value)?.name || 'tag';
                            control.textContent = `${hasTag() ? 'Remove' : 'Assign'} ${name}`;
                            control.setAttribute('aria-label', `${control.textContent} for ${entry.display_name}`);
                        };
                        selector.addEventListener('change', label); label();
                        control.addEventListener('click', () => assignLeaderboardTag(control, entry.player_id, !hasTag(), selector.value));
                        row.append(selector, control);
                    }
                    list.append(row);
                }
                if (result.success && !(result.entries || []).length) {
                    const empty = document.createElement('p');
                    empty.className = 'text-sm text-slate-400';
                    empty.textContent = 'No scores yet. Connect your provider and be the first.';
                    list.append(empty);
                }
            } catch (error) {
                status.textContent = 'Could not load rankings. Refresh to retry.';
            } finally {
                button.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        async function refreshLeaderboard() {
            const leaderboard = document.getElementById('song-leaderboard');
            const songs = await window.pywebview.api.get_song_leaderboard();
            leaderboard.replaceChildren();
            if (!songs.length) {
                const empty = document.createElement('p');
                empty.className = 'px-3 py-4 text-sm text-slate-500';
                empty.textContent = 'No songs in the current folder.';
                leaderboard.append(empty);
                return;
            }
            songs.forEach(song => {
                const row = document.createElement('div');
                row.className = 'grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_4.25rem_4.25rem] items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-[#0d0d12] px-3 py-2.5 text-sm';
                const title = document.createElement('span');
                title.className = 'min-w-0 break-words text-slate-200';
                title.textContent = song.title;
                const correct = document.createElement('span');
                correct.className = 'text-right font-bold tabular-nums text-emerald-400';
                correct.textContent = song.correct;
                const wrong = document.createElement('span');
                wrong.className = 'text-right font-bold tabular-nums text-rose-400';
                wrong.textContent = song.wrong;
                row.append(title, correct, wrong);
                leaderboard.append(row);
            });
        }

        function renderCatalog(filter = 'all') {
            const formatSelect = document.getElementById('catalog-format');
            const formats = [...new Set(catalogSongs.map(song => song.format))].sort();
            if (filter !== 'all' && !formats.includes(filter)) filter = 'all';
            formatSelect.replaceChildren();
            for (const format of ['all', ...formats]) {
                const option = document.createElement('option');
                option.value = format;
                option.textContent = format === 'all' ? 'All formats' : format;
                formatSelect.append(option);
            }
            formatSelect.value = filter;
            catalogFilter = filter;
            const grid = document.getElementById('catalog-grid');
            const songs = filter === 'all' ? catalogSongs : catalogSongs.filter(song => song.format === filter);
            grid.replaceChildren();
            songs.forEach(song => {
                const card = document.createElement('article');
                card.className = 'glass-card p-4 rounded-2xl space-y-3 border border-slate-800 hover:border-purple-500/50 transition-all';
                const art = document.createElement('div');
                art.className = 'h-32 bg-[#0d0d12] rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-3xl shadow-inner text-cyan-400 font-bold border border-slate-800';
                art.textContent = '💿';
                const title = document.createElement('h4');
                title.className = 'font-bold text-sm text-white break-words';
                title.textContent = song.title;
                const format = document.createElement('p');
                format.className = 'text-[11px] text-purple-400 font-semibold';
                format.textContent = song.format;
                const details = document.createElement('div');
                details.append(title, format);
                card.append(art, details);
                addLocalCanaryControls(card, song);
                grid.append(card);
            });
            if (!songs.length) {
                const empty = document.createElement('p');
                empty.className = 'text-sm text-slate-400 col-span-full';
                empty.textContent = 'No matching audio files. Choose a folder to load your music.';
                grid.append(empty);
            }
        }

        function filterCatalog(format) {
            renderCatalog(format);
        }

        window.addEventListener('pywebviewready', async () => {
            const authState = await window.pywebview.api.get_account_state();
            renderAccountState(authState);
            renderState(await window.pywebview.api.get_state());
            await refreshCatalog();
            await refreshLeaderboard();
            watchAutomaticEraScan();
            if (!authState.authenticated) switchTab('account');
        });
    </script>
    <script src="canary_ui.js"></script>
    <script src="update_ui.js"></script>
</body>
</html>

"""Production entry point for the GRAIL'D desktop application."""

import os
import sys


def main():
    args = sys.argv[1:]
    if len(args) == 2 and args[0] == "--standalone-smoke-test":
        from standalone_smoke import run

        return run(args[1])
    if len(args) == 2 and args[0] == "--canary-smoke-test":
        from canary_smoke import run

        return run(args[1])
    if len(args) == 2 and args[0] == "--beta-update-smoke-test":
        from beta_update_smoke import run

        return run(args[1])
    if len(args) == 3 and args[0] == "--post-update":
        from update_health import set_handoff

        set_handoff(args[1], args[2])

    from web_app import main as run_app

    run_app()
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""Native macOS entry point; avoids the unused legacy Tkinter frontend."""
if __name__ == '__main__':
    import sys
    if len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == '--mac-update-helper':
        from mac_updater import main as update_main
        raise SystemExit(update_main(sys.argv[2:]))
    if len(sys.argv) == 3 and sys.argv[1] == '--tls-smoke-test':
        from tls_support import write_diagnostic_report
        raise SystemExit(write_diagnostic_report(sys.argv[2]))
    if len(sys.argv) == 4 and sys.argv[1] == '--post-mac-update':
        from update_health import set_handoff
        set_handoff(sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3])
    from web_app import main
    main()

================================================================================
mac_update_security.py
================================================================================
"""Validate signed Mac app archives and deterministic bundle contents."""
import hashlib
import os
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import plistlib
import posixpath
import stat
import struct
import subprocess
import zipfile

from update_security import UpdateError, canonical

BUNDLE_ID = 'com.graild.desktop'
MAX_UNPACKED = 2 * 1024**3


def _digest(records):
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical(sorted(records))).hexdigest()


def archive_bundle_digest(filename):
    records, links, paths = [], {}, set()
    unpacked = 0
    with zipfile.ZipFile(filename) as archive:
        if len(archive.infolist()) > 50000:
            raise UpdateError('Mac update has too many archive entries.')
        for item in archive.infolist():
            name = item.filename
            path = PurePosixPath(name)
            if '\\' in name or '\x00' in name or path.is_absolute() or str(path) != name.rstrip('/') or any(part in ('..', '.') for part in path.parts) or name.startswith('/'):
                raise UpdateError('Unsafe path in Mac update archive.')
            if not path.parts or path.parts[0] not in ('GRAILD.app', '__MACOSX'):
                raise UpdateError('The Mac update must contain only GRAILD.app and its archive metadata.')
            normalized = name.rstrip('/').casefold()
            if normalized in paths:
                raise UpdateError('Duplicate paths in Mac update archive.')
            paths.add(normalized)
            unpacked += item.file_size
            if unpacked > MAX_UNPACKED:
                raise UpdateError('Mac update exceeds the unpacked size limit.')
            mode = item.external_attr >> 16
            kind = stat.S_IFMT(mode)
            if kind not in (0, stat.S_IFREG, stat.S_IFDIR, stat.S_IFLNK):
                raise UpdateError('Unsupported special file in Mac update archive.')
            if path.parts[0] == '__MACOSX':
                if kind == stat.S_IFLNK:
                    raise UpdateError('Unsafe archive metadata link.')
                continue
            if item.is_dir():
                continue
            if len(path.parts) < 2:
                raise UpdateError('Invalid application bundle root.')
            relative = '/'.join(path.parts[1:])
            if kind == stat.S_IFLNK:
                if item.file_size > 4096:
                    raise UpdateError('Invalid Mac bundle link.')
                target = archive.read(item).decode('utf-8')
                resolved = posixpath.normpath(posixpath.join(posixpath.dirname(name), target))
                if target.startswith('/') or '\\' in target or '\x00' in target or not resolved.startswith('GRAILD.app/'):
                    raise UpdateError('Mac update links outside its app bundle.')
                links[name.rstrip('/')] = target
                records.append((relative, 'link', target))
            else:
                digest = hashlib.sha256()
                with archive.open(item) as source:
                    for chunk in iter(lambda: source.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
                        digest.update(chunk)
                records.append((relative, 'file', bool(mode & 0o111), digest.hexdigest()))
        link_targets = {name.casefold(): target for name, target in links.items()}

        def resolve_parts(parts, seen=frozenset()):
            resolved = []
            for part in parts:
                if part in ('', '.'):
                    continue
                if part == '..':
                    if len(resolved) <= 1:
                        raise UpdateError('Mac bundle link chain escapes its app.')
                    resolved.pop()
                    continue
                resolved.append(part)
                key = '/'.join(resolved).casefold()
                if key in link_targets:
                    if key in seen or len(seen) > 64:
                        raise UpdateError('Cyclic Mac bundle link chain.')
                    resolved = resolve_parts(resolved[:-1] + link_targets[key].split('/'), seen | {key})
            if not resolved or resolved[0] != 'GRAILD.app':
                raise UpdateError('Mac bundle link chain escapes its app.')
            return resolved

        for name in links:
            resolve_parts(name.split('/'))
        # Extraction must never write through a symbolic-link directory.
        for item in archive.infolist():
            for parent in PurePosixPath(item.filename).parents:
                if str(parent).casefold() in link_targets:
                    raise UpdateError('Mac archive writes through a symbolic link.')
    names = {record[0] for record in records}
    if not {'Contents/Info.plist', 'Contents/MacOS/GRAILD'} <= names:
        raise UpdateError('Mac update is missing its app metadata or executable.')
    return _digest(records)


def bundle_digest(bundle):
    bundle = Path(bundle)
    if bundle.is_symlink() or not bundle.is_dir():
        raise UpdateError('Invalid Mac app bundle.')
    root = bundle.resolve()
    records = []
    for directory, folders, files in os.walk(root, followlinks=False):
        for name in sorted(folders + files):
            path = Path(directory) / name
            relative = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
            mode = path.lstat().st_mode
            if stat.S_ISLNK(mode):
                try:
                    if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                        raise UpdateError('Mac app has a link outside its bundle.')
                except (OSError, RuntimeError) as error:
                    raise UpdateError('Invalid Mac bundle link.') from error
                records.append((relative, 'link', os.readlink(path)))
            elif stat.S_ISREG(mode):
                digest = hashlib.sha256()
                with path.open('rb') as source:
                    for chunk in iter(lambda: source.read(1024 * 1024), b''):
                        digest.update(chunk)
                records.append((relative, 'file', bool(mode & 0o111), digest.hexdigest()))
            elif not stat.S_ISDIR(mode):
                raise UpdateError('Unsupported file in Mac app bundle.')
    return _digest(records)


def verify_bundle(bundle, manifest, *, codesign=True):
    bundle = Path(bundle)
    if bundle_digest(bundle) != manifest['bundle_sha256']:
        raise UpdateError('The Mac app does not match the signed bundle checksum.')
    try:
        info = plistlib.loads((bundle / 'Contents/Info.plist').read_bytes())
        executable = bundle / 'Contents/MacOS/GRAILD'
        if info.get('CFBundleIdentifier') != BUNDLE_ID or info.get('CFBundleShortVersionString') != manifest['version'] or info.get('CFBundleExecutable') != 'GRAILD' or not executable.is_file() or executable.is_symlink() or not executable.stat().st_mode & 0o111:
            raise UpdateError('The Mac update has invalid application metadata.')
        if codesign:
            subprocess.run(['/usr/bin/codesign', '--verify', '--deep', '--strict', str(bundle)], check=True, capture_output=True, timeout=60)
            if manifest['architecture'] not in macho_architectures(executable):
                raise UpdateError('The Mac update executable has the wrong architecture.')
    except (OSError, ValueError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
        raise UpdateError('The Mac app signature or executable could not be verified.') from error


def macho_architectures(filename):
    """Read CPU headers directly; updating must not require Xcode or lipo."""
    with Path(filename).open('rb') as source:
        header = source.read(8)
        if len(header) != 8:
            raise UpdateError('Invalid Mac executable header.')
        magic = header[:4]
        cpus = []
        if magic in (b'\xcf\xfa\xed\xfe', b'\xce\xfa\xed\xfe', b'\xfe\xed\xfa\xcf', b'\xfe\xed\xfa\xce'):
            endian = '<' if magic[0] in (0xcf, 0xce) else '>'
            cpus.append(struct.unpack(endian+'I', header[4:])[0])
        elif magic in (b'\xca\xfe\xba\xbe', b'\xca\xfe\xba\xbf', b'\xbe\xba\xfe\xca', b'\xbf\xba\xfe\xca'):
            endian = '>' if magic[0] == 0xca else '<'
            count = struct.unpack(endian+'I', header[4:])[0]
            if not 1 <= count <= 16:
                raise UpdateError('Invalid universal Mac executable header.')
            size = 32 if magic in (b'\xca\xfe\xba\xbf', b'\xbf\xba\xfe\xca') else 20
            for _ in range(count):
                entry = source.read(size)
                if len(entry) != size:
                    raise UpdateError('Truncated universal Mac executable.')
                cpus.append(struct.unpack(endian+'I', entry[:4])[0])
        else:
            raise UpdateError('The update is not a Mac executable.')
    return {name for cpu, name in ((0x01000007,'x86_64'),(0x0100000c,'arm64')) if cpu in cpus}

================================================================================
mac_updater.py
================================================================================
"""Network-free signed Mac app replacement, startup confirmation, and rollback."""
from contextlib import contextmanager
import ctypes
import hashlib
import hmac
import os
from pathlib import Path
import plistlib
import re
import secrets
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import zipfile

from app_version import APP_VERSION
from platform_support import user_data_directory
from update_security import (UpdateError, atomic_json, strict_json, validated_job,
                             verify_manifest, verify_file, update_root)
from update_trust import TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS
from mac_update_security import BUNDLE_ID, archive_bundle_digest, bundle_digest, verify_bundle


def app_bundle(executable=None):
    executable = Path(executable or sys.executable).resolve()
    bundle = executable.parent.parent.parent
    if executable != bundle / 'Contents/MacOS/GRAILD' or bundle.suffix != '.app':
        raise UpdateError('Install updates from the packaged GRAILD.app, not a Python development session.')
    return bundle


def validate_target(target, *, require_bundle=True):
    raw = Path(target)
    target = raw.resolve()
    if not raw.is_absolute() or raw.is_symlink() or target.suffix != '.app':
        raise UpdateError('Invalid Mac installation target.')
    if target.is_relative_to(update_root().resolve()) or target.is_relative_to(user_data_directory().resolve()):
        raise UpdateError('An app installation cannot replace update storage or player data.')
    if 'AppTranslocation' in target.parts:
        raise UpdateError('Move GRAILD.app to Applications or a writable folder, then reopen it before updating.')
    if not require_bundle:
        return target
    try:
        info = plistlib.loads((target / 'Contents/Info.plist').read_bytes())
        if info.get('CFBundleIdentifier') != BUNDLE_ID or info.get('CFBundleExecutable') != 'GRAILD':
            raise UpdateError('The target is not a GRAIL\'D app.')
    except (OSError, ValueError) as error:
        raise UpdateError('Could not validate the installed Mac app.') from error
    return target


def copy_bundle(source, destination):
    if Path(destination).exists() or Path(destination).is_symlink():
        raise UpdateError('Update staging destination already exists.')
    subprocess.run(['/usr/bin/ditto', '--rsrc', '--extattr', '--acl', str(source), str(destination)],
                   check=True, capture_output=True, timeout=180)


def independent_environment():
    result = {key: value for key, value in os.environ.items() if not key.startswith('_PYI_')}
    result['PYINSTALLER_RESET_ENVIRONMENT'] = '1'
    return result


def launch(command):
    return subprocess.Popen([str(arg) for arg in command], env=independent_environment(),
                            stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                            start_new_session=True, close_fds=True)


def process_path(pid):
    api = ctypes.CDLL('/usr/lib/libproc.dylib', use_errno=True)
    api.proc_pidpath.argtypes = [ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_uint32]
    api.proc_pidpath.restype = ctypes.c_int
    buffer = ctypes.create_string_buffer(4096)
    return Path(os.fsdecode(buffer.value)).resolve() if api.proc_pidpath(int(pid), buffer, len(buffer)) > 0 else None


def app_processes(target):
    executable = target / 'Contents/MacOS/GRAILD'
    output = subprocess.check_output(['/bin/ps', '-axo', 'pid='], text=True, timeout=20)
    return [int(pid) for pid in output.split() if pid.isdigit() and process_path(int(pid)) == executable]


class ParentProcess:
    def __init__(self, pid, target):
        self.pid, self.target = int(pid), target
        if self.pid <= 1 or process_path(self.pid) != target / 'Contents/MacOS/GRAILD':
            raise UpdateError('The updater parent does not match the installed Mac app.')
        if set(app_processes(target)) != {self.pid}:
            raise UpdateError('Close other GRAIL\'D instances before updating.')

    def wait(self):
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 180
        while process_path(self.pid) == self.target / 'Contents/MacOS/GRAILD':
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                raise UpdateError('GRAIL\'D is still open. No update was installed.')
            time.sleep(0.2)
        if app_processes(self.target):
            raise UpdateError('Another GRAIL\'D instance is running. Close it and retry.')


@contextmanager
def install_lock(target):
    import fcntl
    name = '.graild-update-' + hashlib.sha256(str(target).encode()).hexdigest()[:24] + '.lock'
    descriptor = os.open(target.parent / name, os.O_CREAT | os.O_RDWR | getattr(os, 'O_NOFOLLOW', 0), 0o600)
    try:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(descriptor, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        except BlockingIOError as error:
            raise UpdateError('Another Mac update is already running.') from error
        yield
    finally:
        os.close(descriptor)


def read_request(job, keys, *, recovery=False):
    request = strict_json((job / 'request.json').read_bytes())
    if request.get('current_version') != APP_VERSION or request.get('architecture') not in ('arm64', 'x86_64'):
        raise UpdateError('The helper does not match the running Mac app.')
    for name in ('health_token', 'old_sha256'):
        if not isinstance(request.get(name), str) or not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', request[name]):
            raise UpdateError('Invalid Mac updater handoff.')
    manifest = verify_manifest(strict_json((job / 'manifest.json').read_bytes()), keys,
                               request['repository'], request['channel'], request['current_version'],
                               platform='macos', architecture=request['architecture'])
    target = validate_target(request['target'], require_bundle=not recovery)
    return request, manifest, target


def monitor_startup(target, job, token, version):
    process = launch([target / 'Contents/MacOS/GRAILD', '--post-mac-update', job, token])
    atomic_json(job / 'launched.json', {'pid': process.pid})
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 90
    try:
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            try:
                health = strict_json((job / 'health.json').read_bytes())
                if hmac.compare_digest(str(health.get('token', '')), token) and health.get('version') == version and process.poll() is None:
                    return
            except OSError:
                pass
            if process.poll() is not None:
                raise UpdateError('The new Mac app exited before confirming startup.')
            time.sleep(0.2)
        raise UpdateError('The new Mac app did not confirm startup in time.')
    except Exception:
        if process.poll() is None:
            process.terminate()
            try: process.wait(timeout=10)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired: process.kill(); process.wait(timeout=10)
        raise


def restore_backup(target, backup, old_digest, new_digest, job):
    if not backup.is_dir() or bundle_digest(backup) != old_digest:
        raise UpdateError('The Mac recovery backup did not verify. It was left untouched.')
    if target.exists():
        if bundle_digest(target) != new_digest:
            raise UpdateError('The installation changed independently; recovery will not overwrite it.')
        failed = target.parent / ('.graild-failed-' + job.name + '.app')
        if failed.exists():
            raise UpdateError('A previous failed app is already preserved; manual recovery is required.')
        os.rename(target, failed)
    os.rename(backup, target)
    if bundle_digest(target) != old_digest:
        raise UpdateError('Restored Mac app did not verify.')


def install_job(directory, keys=TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS, parent_factory=ParentProcess,
                startup=monitor_startup, bundle_verifier=verify_bundle, copier=copy_bundle, locker=install_lock):
    job = validated_job(directory)
    request, manifest, target = read_request(job, keys)
    payload = job / 'payload.zip'
    verify_file(payload, manifest)
    if archive_bundle_digest(payload) != manifest['bundle_sha256']:
        raise UpdateError('Mac update archive checksum does not match the signed bundle.')
    incoming = target.parent / ('.graild-new-' + job.name + '.app')
    backup = target.parent / ('.graild-backup-' + job.name + '.app')
    moved_old = False
    with locker(target):
        try:
            if bundle_digest(target) != request['old_sha256'] or backup.exists():
                raise UpdateError('The installed app changed or a backup already exists. No files were replaced.')
            parent = parent_factory(request['parent_pid'], target)
            extract = job / 'extracted'
            extract.mkdir(mode=0o700)
            subprocess.run(['/usr/bin/ditto', '-x', '-k', str(payload), str(extract)],
                           check=True, capture_output=True, timeout=180)
            new_app = extract / 'GRAILD.app'
            bundle_verifier(new_app, manifest)
            copier(new_app, incoming)
            bundle_verifier(incoming, manifest)
            atomic_json(job / 'ready.json', {'ready': True})
            parent.wait()
            verify_file(payload, manifest)
            if bundle_digest(target) != request['old_sha256']:
                raise UpdateError('The installed app changed during shutdown.')
            bundle_verifier(incoming, manifest)
            atomic_json(job / 'journal.json', {'phase':'prepared','version':manifest['version']})
            os.rename(target, backup)
            moved_old = True
            os.rename(incoming, target)
            atomic_json(job / 'journal.json', {'phase':'installed','version':manifest['version']})
            bundle_verifier(target, manifest)
            startup(target, job, request['health_token'], manifest['version'])
            atomic_json(job / 'journal.json', {'phase':'healthy','version':manifest['version']})
            atomic_json(job / 'result.json', {'status':'installed','version':manifest['version'],
                                             'message':'Mac update installed and startup verified.'})
        except Exception as error:
            if moved_old:
                try:
                    restore_backup(target, backup, request['old_sha256'], manifest['bundle_sha256'], job)
                    atomic_json(job / 'journal.json', {'phase':'rolled_back'})
                    atomic_json(job / 'result.json', {'status':'rolled_back','message':str(error)+' The previous Mac app was restored.'})
                    launch([target / 'Contents/MacOS/GRAILD'])
                except Exception as recovery_error:
                    atomic_json(job / 'result.json', {'status':'recovery_required','message':str(recovery_error),'backup':str(backup)})
            else:
                atomic_json(job / 'result.json', {'status':'failed','message':str(error)})
            raise


def prepare_install(manager):
    if sys.platform != 'darwin' or not getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        raise UpdateError('Restart & Install is available in the packaged Mac app.')
    with manager.lock:
        if manager.state['status'] != 'ready' or not manager.ready_job or not manager.candidate:
            raise UpdateError('No verified Mac update is ready to install.')
        job = validated_job(manager.ready_job)
        manifest = manager._verify_manifest(strict_json((job / 'manifest.json').read_bytes()),
                                            manager.config['repository'], manager.config['channel'])
        verify_file(job / 'payload.zip', manifest)
        target = validate_target(app_bundle())
        probe = target.parent / ('.graild-write-test-' + secrets.token_hex(16))
        try:
            probe.write_bytes(b'test')
        except OSError as error:
            raise UpdateError('Move GRAILD.app to a writable Applications folder, reopen it, and retry. The current app was left open.') from error
        finally:
            if probe.exists(): probe.unlink()
        old_digest = bundle_digest(target)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(job / 'payload.zip') as archive:
            expanded = sum(item.file_size for item in archive.infolist())
        old_size = sum(path.stat().st_size for path in target.rglob('*') if path.is_file() and not path.is_symlink())
        margin = 64 * 1024**2
        required = old_size + expanded + margin
        if target.parent.stat().st_dev == job.stat().st_dev:
            required += expanded
        if shutil.disk_usage(job).free < required or shutil.disk_usage(target.parent).free < expanded + margin:
            raise UpdateError('Free disk space before updating. The current Mac app was left open.')
        runner = job / 'runner.app'
        copy_bundle(target, runner)
        if bundle_digest(runner) != old_digest:
            raise UpdateError('Independent Mac helper copy did not verify.')
        subprocess.run(['/usr/bin/codesign','--verify','--deep','--strict',str(runner)], check=True, capture_output=True, timeout=60)
        atomic_json(job / 'request.json', {'target':str(target),'parent_pid':os.getpid(),
                    'old_sha256':old_digest,'current_version':APP_VERSION,'architecture':manager.architecture,
                    'repository':manager.config['repository'],'channel':manager.config['channel'],
                    'health_token':secrets.token_hex(32)})
        manager._state('installing', 'Preparing the Mac updater. GRAIL\'D will restart shortly...')
    process = launch([runner / 'Contents/MacOS/GRAILD', '--mac-update-helper', '--job', job])
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 60
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if (job / 'ready.json').is_file():
            return manager.snapshot()
        if (job / 'result.json').is_file():
            raise UpdateError(strict_json((job / 'result.json').read_bytes()).get('message', 'Mac updater preparation failed.'))
        if process.poll() is not None: break
        time.sleep(0.1)
    if process.poll() is None:
        process.terminate()
        try: process.wait(timeout=10)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired: process.kill(); process.wait(timeout=10)
    atomic_json(job / 'result.json', {'status':'failed','message':'Mac updater preparation timed out; the current app was left open.'})
    raise UpdateError('The Mac updater did not become ready. Your app was left open; retry the download.')


def acknowledge_startup(handoff):
    if sys.platform != 'darwin' or not getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        return
    target = app_bundle()
    directories = [handoff[0]] if handoff else list((update_root() / 'jobs').glob('*'))[-100:]
    for directory in directories:
        try:
            job = validated_job(directory)
            request, manifest, expected = read_request_for_new_app(job)
            if expected != target or manifest['version'] != APP_VERSION:
                continue
            if handoff and not hmac.compare_digest(request['health_token'], handoff[1]):
                continue
            if not handoff and strict_json((job / 'journal.json').read_bytes()).get('phase') not in ('prepared','installed'):
                continue
            verify_file(job / 'payload.zip', manifest)
            verify_bundle(target, manifest)
            atomic_json(job / 'health.json', {'token':request['health_token'],'version':APP_VERSION})
            if not handoff:
                atomic_json(job / 'journal.json', {'phase':'healthy','version':APP_VERSION})
                atomic_json(job / 'result.json', {'status':'installed','version':APP_VERSION,
                                                 'message':'Interrupted Mac update recovered; startup verified.'})
        except (OSError, UpdateError, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            continue


def read_request_for_new_app(job):
    request = strict_json((job / 'request.json').read_bytes())
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', str(request.get('health_token', ''))):
        raise UpdateError('Invalid Mac startup handoff.')
    manifest = verify_manifest(strict_json((job / 'manifest.json').read_bytes()), TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS,
                               request['repository'], request['channel'], request['current_version'],
                               platform='macos', architecture=request['architecture'])
    return request, manifest, validate_target(request['target'])


def recover_job(directory, keys=TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS, locker=install_lock):
    job = validated_job(directory)
    request, manifest, target = read_request(job, keys, recovery=True)
    backup = target.parent / ('.graild-backup-' + job.name + '.app')
    with locker(target):
        phase = strict_json((job / 'journal.json').read_bytes()).get('phase')
        current = bundle_digest(target) if target.exists() else None
        if phase == 'healthy':
            if current != manifest['bundle_sha256']:
                raise UpdateError('Healthy installation changed; recovery will not overwrite it.')
            return
        if current == request['old_sha256']:
            atomic_json(job / 'result.json', {'status':'rolled_back','message':'The previous Mac app is already installed.'})
            return
        if app_processes(target):
            raise UpdateError('Close GRAIL\'D before recovering an interrupted update.')
        restore_backup(target, backup, request['old_sha256'], manifest['bundle_sha256'], job)
        atomic_json(job / 'journal.json', {'phase':'rolled_back'})
        atomic_json(job / 'result.json', {'status':'rolled_back','message':'Interrupted Mac update recovered; the previous app was restored.'})


def main(argv=None):
    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--job', required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--recover', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        recover_job(args.job) if args.recover else install_job(args.job)
        return 0
    except Exception as error:
        try:
            job = validated_job(args.job)
            if not (job / 'result.json').exists():
                atomic_json(job / 'result.json', {'status':'failed','message':str(error)})
        except Exception: pass
        return 1

oauth_config.py
