"""Public desktop OAuth configuration, included by PyInstaller on all platforms.

The production ID comes from the existing Windows development configuration.
Only the public client ID belongs here; credentials and tokens must never be added.
"""
import os


DEFAULT_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID = (
    "[REDACTED_PUBLIC_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID]"
)


def google_client_id():
    """Resolve at login time so development overrides take effect immediately.

    Empty or whitespace-only overrides use the production configuration. A Python
    module avoids depending on the launch environment or bundled resource paths.
    """
    return os.environ.get("GRAILD_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID", "").strip() or DEFAULT_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID

================================================================================
platform_support.py
================================================================================
"""OS-specific storage paths and macOS Keychain-backed session encryption."""
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import threading

_MAC_SESSION_LOCK = threading.RLock()


def user_data_directory():
    if sys.platform == 'darwin':
        return Path.home() / 'Library' / 'Application Support' / 'GRAILD'
    return Path(os.environ.get('APPDATA', os.path.expanduser('~'))) / 'JuiceSpot'


def update_directory():
    if sys.platform == 'darwin':
        return Path.home() / 'Library' / 'Caches' / 'GRAILD' / 'updates'
    return Path(os.environ.get('LOCALAPPDATA') or Path.home() / 'AppData' / 'Local') / 'GRAILD' / 'updates'


def mac_session_data(data, decrypt=False):
    with _MAC_SESSION_LOCK:
        return _mac_session_data(data, decrypt=decrypt)


def _mac_session_data(data, decrypt=False):
    # Instantiate Apple's backend explicitly: never select a plaintext fallback.
    try:
        from keyring.backends.macOS import Keyring
        from cryptography.fernet import Fernet
        backend = Keyring()
        service, account = 'com.graild.desktop', 'session-encryption-v1'
        key = backend.get_password(service, account)
        if key is None:
            if decrypt:
                raise OSError('The saved session key is missing from macOS Keychain. Sign in again.')
            key = Fernet.generate_key().decode('ascii')
            backend.set_password(service, account, key)
            # Use the value committed to Keychain, not an unpersisted key.
            key = backend.get_password(service, account)
            if not key:
                raise OSError('Could not save the session key in macOS Keychain.')
        cipher = Fernet(key.encode('ascii'))
        return cipher.decrypt(data) if decrypt else cipher.encrypt(data)
    except OSError:
        raise
    except Exception:
        # Do not expose key material or backend error details to the UI/logs.
        raise OSError('macOS Keychain could not protect this session. Unlock your login Keychain and try signing in again.') from None

================================================================================
prepare_beta_release.py
================================================================================
"""Build a versioned beta in isolation, then sign it with the existing signer."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import uuid

from update_security import SemVer,sha256_file
from update_trust import DEFAULT_REPOSITORY,TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS

SOURCES = ['JuiceGuesserMain.py','web_app.py','app_version.py','update_trust.py','update_security.py',
           'platform_support.py','auto_update.py','graild_updater.py','update_health.py','achievements.py','audio_files.py',
           'random_snippets.py','canary_client.py','canary_integration.py','canary_smoke.py',
           'beta_update_smoke.py','standalone_smoke.py','index.html','canary_ui.js','update_ui.js',
           'mac_updater.py','mac_update_security.py','tls_support.py']


def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--version',required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--private-key',required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--key-id',default='graild-beta-2026')
    parser.add_argument('--notes',required=True)
    args=parser.parse_args()
    SemVer.parse(args.version)
    if args.key_id not in TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS or not DEFAULT_REPOSITORY:
        parser.error('Configure the publisher repository and public key first.')
    root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    output=root/'dist'/'releases'/('v'+args.version)
    if output.exists():
        parser.error('The release output already exists; it was not overwritten. Move it aside before rebuilding.')
    stage=root/'build'/'beta-releases'/('v'+args.version+'-'+uuid.uuid4().hex)
    source=stage/'source';source.mkdir(parents=True)
    for name in SOURCES:shutil.copyfile(root/name,source/name)
    shutil.copytree(root/'assets', source/'assets')
    (source/'app_version.py').write_text('APP_VERSION = '+repr(args.version)+'\nAPP_ID = "GRAILD"\n',encoding='utf-8')
    binary=stage/'bin'
    def build(script,name,extra):
        print('Building',name,'version',args.version,flush=True)
        with open(stage/(name+'.build.log'),'w',encoding='utf-8') as log:
            subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-m','PyInstaller','--noconfirm','--onefile','--noconsole','--name',name,
                            '--distpath',str(binary),'--workpath',str(stage/'work'),'--specpath',str(stage/'spec'),
                            *extra,str(source/script)],cwd=source,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
    build('graild_updater.py','GRAILDUpdater',['--icon',str(source/'assets/graild.ico')])
    extra=['--hidden-import=pygame','--collect-all','pygame','--collect-all','webview','--collect-all','imageio_ffmpeg']
    extra += ['--icon',str(source/'assets/graild.ico')]
    extra += ['--collect-data','certifi']
    for name in ['index.html','canary_ui.js','update_ui.js']:
        extra += ['--add-data',str(source/name)+';.']
    extra += ['--add-data',str(source/'assets')+';assets']
    extra += ['--add-data',str(binary/'GRAILDUpdater.exe')+';.']
    build('JuiceGuesserMain.py','GRAILD',extra)
    output.mkdir(parents=True)
    for name in ['GRAILD.exe','GRAILDUpdater.exe']:shutil.copyfile(binary/name,output/name)
    shutil.copyfile(args.notes,output/'release-notes.md')
    subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(root/'release_tools.py'),'manifest','--private-key',args.private_key,
                    '--key-id',args.key_id,'--repository',DEFAULT_REPOSITORY,'--channel','beta','--version',args.version,
                    '--asset',str(output/'GRAILD.exe'),'--notes',str(output/'release-notes.md'),
                    '--output',str(output/'graild-update.json')],check=True,cwd=root)
    (output/'build-info.json').write_text(json.dumps({'version':args.version,'repository':DEFAULT_REPOSITORY,
        'channel':'beta','sha256':sha256_file(output/'GRAILD.exe'),'source_stage':str(stage),
        'upload':['GRAILD.exe','graild-update.json']},indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print('Prepared (NOT published):',output,flush=True)


if __name__=='__main__':main()

================================================================================
prepare_github_mac_build.py
================================================================================
"""Package an allowlisted source tree for GitHub Mac builds, without secrets."""
from pathlib import Path
import json
import hashlib
import zipfile
from prepare_mac_release import FILES
from app_version import APP_VERSION


def main():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    target = root / 'dist/releases' / ('v' + APP_VERSION) / 'GRAILD-GitHub-Mac-Build.zip'
    if target.exists():
        raise SystemExit('The upload bundle already exists. Archive it before recreating; nothing was overwritten.')
    files = [root / name for name in FILES]
    files += [root / '.github/workflows/build-macos.yml', root / 'GITHUB_MAC_BUILDS.md', root / '.gitignore']
    files += [root / 'release-notes' / ('v' + APP_VERSION + '.md')]
    files += [path for path in (root / 'assets').rglob('*') if path.is_file()]
    for path in files:
        if not path.is_file(): raise SystemExit('Missing build input: ' + str(path))
        if path.suffix.lower() in {'.pem', '.key', '.dpapi', '.p8', '.pfx', '.p12'}:
            raise SystemExit('Refusing private-key file in source package.')
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    hashes = {}
    with zipfile.ZipFile(target, 'x', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
        for path in sorted(set(files)):
            name = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
            data = path.read_bytes()
            hashes[name] = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
            archive.writestr(name, data)
        archive.writestr('source-checksums.json', json.dumps(hashes, indent=2))
    print('Prepared (not uploaded):', target)


if __name__ == '__main__': main()

================================================================================
prepare_mac_release.py
================================================================================
"""Prepare a secret-free native Mac build handoff without claiming a native build."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import stat
import zipfile

FILES = ['mac_launcher.py', 'web_app.py', 'platform_support.py', 'app_version.py',
         'auto_update.py', 'update_security.py', 'update_health.py', 'update_trust.py',
         'achievements.py', 'random_snippets.py', 'audio_files.py', 'canary_client.py',
         'canary_integration.py', 'index.html', 'canary_ui.js', 'update_ui.js',
         'requirements.txt', 'GRAILD-mac.spec', 'build_macos.py', 'test_mac_support.py',
         'MACOS_SUPPORT.md', 'test_account_tags.py', 'test_achievements.py',
         'test_mac_build_assets.py', 'test_audio_files.py', 'test_game_modes.py', 'test_round_resume.py', 'test_brand_assets.py',
         'mac_updater.py', 'mac_update_security.py', 'test_mac_updater.py', 'MAC_AUTO_UPDATES.md',
         'tls_support.py', 'test_tls_support.py', 'oauth_config.py', 'test_oauth_config.py',
         'GOOGLE_OAUTH.md', 'prepare_mac_release.py']
COMMAND = '''#!/bin/bash
set -euo pipefail
cd -- "$(dirname -- "$0")"
if [[ "$(uname -s)" != "Darwin" || "$(uname -m)" != "x86_64" ]]; then
  echo "Run this Intel build kit on an Intel Mac."; exit 1
fi
if ! command -v python3.13 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "Install native Intel Python 3.13 from python.org, then run this again."; exit 1
fi
python3.13 -m venv .venv-mac
source .venv-mac/bin/activate
python -m pip install --upgrade pip
python -m pip install -r requirements.txt
python -m unittest test_mac_support test_mac_updater test_mac_build_assets test_brand_assets test_tls_support test_oauth_config test_account_tags test_achievements test_audio_files test_game_modes test_round_resume -v
python build_macos.py --expected-arch x86_64 --output-dir ../macOS-x86_64
echo "Built in ../macOS-x86_64. Native testing and Developer ID notarization are still required."
'''
README = '''# GRAIL'D 0.9.0 — Intel Mac build handoff

This folder contains source and build tools, NOT a compiled Mac application.
No Mac app could be built on the Windows development computer.

On the Intel Mac:

1. Extract GRAILD-0.9.0-macOS-x86_64-build-kit.zip into a writable folder.
2. Install native Intel Python 3.13 and Xcode Command Line Tools (`xcode-select --install`).
3. Open Terminal in the extracted GRAILD-macOS-x86_64-build-kit folder.
4. Run `bash Build.command`.
5. Find GRAILD.app and its ZIP in the sibling macOS-x86_64 folder.

The local test app is ad-hoc signed unless GRAILD_MAC_SIGNING_IDENTITY is set
before building. For distribution, follow MACOS_SUPPORT.md for Developer ID
signing, notarization, and testing. Do not publish this build kit as the playable
Mac download. Friends will only need the final notarized app ZIP, not Python.

This build supports signed Mac updates. See MAC_AUTO_UPDATES.md for the per-architecture manifests and release signing procedure. Existing manual-only Mac builds require this build to be installed once.
'''


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--arch', choices=['x86_64', 'arm64'], default='x86_64')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    label = 'Apple Silicon' if args.arch == 'arm64' else 'Intel'
    command = COMMAND.replace('x86_64', args.arch).replace('Intel', label)
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    from app_version import APP_VERSION
    readme = README.replace('0.9.0', APP_VERSION).replace('x86_64', args.arch).replace('Intel', label)
    release = root / 'dist/releases' / ('v' + APP_VERSION)
    kit = release / ('GRAILD-macOS-' + args.arch + '-build-kit')
    archive = release / ('GRAILD-' + APP_VERSION + '-macOS-' + args.arch + '-build-kit.zip')
    if kit.exists() or archive.exists():
        raise SystemExit('Build handoff already exists; archive it first. Nothing was overwritten.')
    for filename in FILES:
        if not (root / filename).is_file():
            raise SystemExit('Missing required source: ' + filename)
    kit.mkdir(parents=True)
    for filename in FILES:
        shutil.copyfile(root / filename, kit / filename)
    shutil.copytree(root / 'assets', kit / 'assets')
    (kit / 'Build.command').write_text(command, encoding='utf-8', newline='\n')
    (kit / 'README.md').write_text(readme, encoding='utf-8')
    hashes = {str(p.relative_to(kit)).replace('\\', '/'): hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
              for p in sorted(kit.rglob('*')) if p.is_file()}
    (kit / 'source-checksums.json').write_text(json.dumps(hashes, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as output:
        for path in sorted(kit.rglob('*')):
            if not path.is_file(): continue
            name = path.relative_to(release).as_posix()
            info = zipfile.ZipInfo(name)
            info.create_system = 3
            mode = 0o755 if path.name == 'Build.command' else 0o644
            info.external_attr = (stat.S_IFREG | mode) << 16
            info.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED
            output.writestr(info, path.read_bytes())
    (release / ('MACOS-' + args.arch + '-STATUS.md')).write_text(readme, encoding='utf-8')
    shutil.copyfile(root / 'release-notes' / ('v' + APP_VERSION + '.md'), release / 'release-notes.md')
    print('Prepared ' + label + ' Mac source handoff:', archive)
    print('Native GRAILD.app status: pending build and testing on the ' + label + ' Mac.')


if __name__ == '__main__': main()

================================================================================
publish_build.py
================================================================================
"""Publish a finished Windows build without overwriting a running executable."""
import argparse
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import tempfile
from datetime import datetime


def publish_build(source, target):
    source, target = Path(source).resolve(), Path(target).resolve()
    if not source.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Built executable was not found: {source}")
    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    descriptor, staging_name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".juicespot-build-", suffix=".exe", dir=target.parent)
    os.close(descriptor)
    staging = Path(staging_name)
    try:
        shutil.copy2(source, staging)
        try:
            os.replace(staging, target)
            return target, False
        except PermissionError:
            # A running Windows .exe cannot be replaced. Keep it intact and
            # publish the completed build under a fresh name instead.
            stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S-%f")
            alternate = target.with_name(f"{target.stem}-updated-{stamp}{target.suffix}")
            os.replace(staging, alternate)
            return alternate, True
    finally:
        if staging.exists():
            staging.unlink()


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("source")
    parser.add_argument("target")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        path, alternate = publish_build(args.source, args.target)
    except OSError as error:
        print(f"Could not save the completed build: {error}")
        return 1
    if alternate:
        print("The existing executable is in use or cannot be replaced.")
        print("Your running app was left open. Close it, then launch the new file below.")
    print(f"Build finished! Updated executable: {path}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

================================================================================
publisher_keys.py
================================================================================
"""Publisher-only key storage. Never imported by the distributed application."""
import csv
import ctypes
from ctypes import wintypes
import getpass
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess

from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import Ed25519PrivateKey

HEADER = b"GRAILD-DPAPI-ED25519-v1\n"


class Blob(ctypes.Structure):
    _fields_ = [("size", wintypes.DWORD), ("data", ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_byte))]


def protect(data, decrypt=False):
    if os.name != "nt":
        raise OSError("Windows-protected publisher keys require Windows.")
    crypt = ctypes.WinDLL("crypt32", use_last_error=True)
    kernel = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True)
    buffer = ctypes.create_string_buffer(data)
    incoming = Blob(len(data), ctypes.cast(buffer, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_byte)))
    outgoing = Blob()
    if decrypt:
        function = crypt.CryptUnprotectData
        function.argtypes = [ctypes.POINTER(Blob), ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, wintypes.DWORD, ctypes.POINTER(Blob)]
        ok = function(ctypes.byref(incoming), None, None, None, None, 1, ctypes.byref(outgoing))
    else:
        function = crypt.CryptProtectData
        function.argtypes = [ctypes.POINTER(Blob), wintypes.LPCWSTR, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, wintypes.DWORD, ctypes.POINTER(Blob)]
        ok = function(ctypes.byref(incoming), "GRAILD publisher signing key", None, None, None, 1, ctypes.byref(outgoing))
    if not ok:
        raise OSError("Windows could not protect or unlock the publisher key for this account.")
    try:
        return ctypes.string_at(outgoing.data, outgoing.size)
    finally:
        kernel.LocalFree.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p]
        kernel.LocalFree.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
        kernel.LocalFree(ctypes.cast(outgoing.data, ctypes.c_void_p))


def outside_repository(path):
    path = Path(path).expanduser().resolve()
    if path.is_relative_to(Path(__file__).resolve().parent):
        raise ValueError("Private signing keys must be stored outside the project repository.")
    return path


def restrict_permissions(path):
    if os.name != "nt":
        os.chmod(path, 0o700 if Path(path).is_dir() else 0o600)
        return
    who = subprocess.check_output(['whoami', '/user', '/fo', 'csv', '/nh'], text=True, encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
    sid = next(csv.reader([who.strip()]))[1]
    if not sid.startswith('S-1-'):
        raise OSError('Could not identify the Windows publisher account.')
    inheritance = '(OI)(CI)' if Path(path).is_dir() else ''
    subprocess.run(['icacls', str(path), '/inheritance:r', '/grant:r', '*'+sid+':'+inheritance+'F', '*S-1-5-18:'+inheritance+'F'],
                   check=True, capture_output=True, creationflags=getattr(subprocess,'CREATE_NO_WINDOW',0))


def new_password():
    password = getpass.getpass('Private key password (at least 12 characters): ')
    confirm = getpass.getpass('Confirm password: ')
    if len(password) < 12 or password != confirm:
        raise ValueError('Passwords must match and contain at least 12 characters.')
    return password.encode('utf-8')


def generate_key(filename, windows_protected=False):
    path = outside_repository(filename)
    if path.exists():
        raise FileExistsError('A signing key already exists at that path; it was not replaced.')
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    restrict_permissions(path.parent)
    private = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
    if windows_protected:
        secret = private.private_bytes(serialization.Encoding.Raw, serialization.PrivateFormat.Raw, serialization.NoEncryption())
        content = HEADER + protect(secret)
    else:
        content = private.private_bytes(serialization.Encoding.PEM, serialization.PrivateFormat.PKCS8, serialization.BestAvailableEncryption(new_password()))
    with open(path, 'xb') as file:
        file.write(content)
        file.flush(); os.fsync(file.fileno())
    restrict_permissions(path)
    return private


def load_key(filename):
    content = outside_repository(filename).read_bytes()
    if content.startswith(HEADER):
        return Ed25519PrivateKey.from_private_bytes(protect(content[len(HEADER):], decrypt=True))
    password = getpass.getpass('Release key password: ').encode('utf-8')
    private = serialization.load_pem_private_key(content, password=password)
    if not isinstance(private, Ed25519PrivateKey):
        raise ValueError('The signing key must be Ed25519.')
    return private


def export_encrypted(filename, destination):
    destination = outside_repository(destination)
    destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    restrict_permissions(destination.parent)
    content = load_key(filename).private_bytes(serialization.Encoding.PEM, serialization.PrivateFormat.PKCS8, serialization.BestAvailableEncryption(new_password()))
    with open(destination, 'xb') as file:
        file.write(content)
    restrict_permissions(destination)

================================================================================
random_snippets.py
================================================================================
"""Decode random snippets to WAV so seeking works for MP3, OGG, and WAV."""
import io
import random
import wave

import pygame


class RandomSnippet:
    def __init__(self, filename, longest_preview, *, start_frame=None):
        frequency, sample_format, channels = pygame.mixer.get_init()
        self.frequency = frequency
        self.channels = channels
        self.sample_width = abs(sample_format) // 8
        self.frame_width = self.sample_width * channels
        self.pcm = pygame.mixer.Sound(filename).get_raw()
        if sample_format == -8:
            self.pcm = bytes((sample + 128) % 256 for sample in self.pcm)
        self.frames = len(self.pcm) // self.frame_width
        if not self.frames:
            raise ValueError("The song has no playable audio.")
        reserved = min(self.frames, int(longest_preview * frequency))
        if start_frame is not None and (type(start_frame) is not int or not 0 <= start_frame <= self.frames - reserved):
            raise ValueError("The saved snippet no longer fits this audio file.")
        self.start_frame = random.randint(0, self.frames - reserved) if start_frame is None else start_frame

    def clip(self, duration):
        end_frame = min(self.frames, self.start_frame + max(1, int(duration * self.frequency)))
        audio = self.pcm[self.start_frame * self.frame_width:end_frame * self.frame_width]
        buffer = io.BytesIO()
        with wave.open(buffer, "wb") as output:
            output.setnchannels(self.channels)
            output.setsampwidth(self.sample_width)
            output.setframerate(self.frequency)
            output.writeframes(audio)
        buffer.seek(0)
        return buffer, (end_frame - self.start_frame) / self.frequency

================================================================================
release_tools.py
================================================================================
"""Offline publisher key generation and signed GitHub release manifests."""
import argparse
import base64
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import getpass
import json
from pathlib import Path

from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import Ed25519PrivateKey

from app_version import APP_VERSION
from update_trust import TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS
from update_security import SemVer, canonical, repository, sha256_file
from publisher_keys import generate_key, load_key, export_encrypted


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    key = commands.add_parser("keygen")
    key.add_argument("--private-key", required=True)
    key.add_argument("--key-id", required=True)
    key.add_argument("--windows-protected", action="store_true")
    key.add_argument("--public-output")
    public = commands.add_parser("public-key")
    public.add_argument("--private-key", required=True)
    public.add_argument("--key-id", required=True)
    public.add_argument("--public-output")
    export = commands.add_parser("export-key")
    export.add_argument("--private-key", required=True)
    export.add_argument("--output", required=True)
    sign = commands.add_parser("manifest")
    sign.add_argument("--private-key", required=True)
    sign.add_argument("--key-id", required=True)
    sign.add_argument("--repository", required=True)
    sign.add_argument("--channel", choices=("beta", "stable"), default="beta")
    sign.add_argument("--version", default=APP_VERSION)
    sign.add_argument("--asset", required=True)
    sign.add_argument("--notes", required=True)
    sign.add_argument("--output", required=True)
    sign.add_argument('--platform', choices=('windows', 'macos'), default='windows')
    sign.add_argument('--architecture', choices=('arm64', 'x86_64'))
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.command == "export-key":
        export_encrypted(args.private_key, args.output)
        print("Encrypted backup created outside the repository.")
        return
    if args.command in ("keygen", "public-key"):
        private = generate_key(args.private_key, args.windows_protected) if args.command == "keygen" else load_key(args.private_key)
        public = base64.b64encode(private.public_key().public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.Raw, serialization.PublicFormat.Raw)).decode("ascii")
        record = {"key_id": args.key_id, "public_key": public}
        if args.public_output:
            Path(args.public_output).write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        print("Public verification key:", json.dumps(record))
        return
    private = load_key(args.private_key)
    public = base64.b64encode(private.public_key().public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.Raw, serialization.PublicFormat.Raw)).decode("ascii")
    if TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS.get(args.key_id) != public:
        parser.error("Configure this public key in update_trust.py and rebuild both executables before signing a release.")
    version = SemVer.parse(args.version)
    if args.channel == "stable" and version.prerelease:
        parser.error("Stable releases cannot use prerelease versions.")
    asset = Path(args.asset)
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    manifest = {"schema": 1, "app": "GRAILD", "repository": repository(args.repository),
                "channel": args.channel, "version": args.version, "tag": "v" + args.version,
                "asset": asset.name, "size": asset.stat().st_size, "sha256": sha256_file(asset),
                "issued_at": now.isoformat(), "expires_at": (now + timedelta(days=90)).isoformat(),
                "notes": Path(args.notes).read_text(encoding="utf-8")}
    if args.platform == 'macos':
        if not args.architecture or asset.name != f'GRAILD-{args.version}-macOS-{args.architecture}.zip':
            parser.error('Choose the matching Mac app ZIP and --architecture.')
        from mac_update_security import archive_bundle_digest, BUNDLE_ID
        manifest.update(platform='macos', architecture=args.architecture, bundle_id=BUNDLE_ID,
                        bundle_sha256=archive_bundle_digest(asset))
    envelope = {"manifest": manifest, "key_id": args.key_id,
                "signature": base64.b64encode(private.sign(canonical(manifest))).decode("ascii")}
    Path(args.output).write_bytes(canonical(envelope))
    print("Signed manifest written. Upload " + Path(args.output).name + " alongside " + asset.name + ".")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

render-broker/discord_oauth_broker/app.py
================================================================================
"""Discord OAuth broker for the GRAIL'D Windows desktop client."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import secrets
import time
import urllib.parse
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

import httpx
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, RedirectResponse, Response
from redis.asyncio import Redis
from tag_rules import ACHIEVEMENT_IDS, TAGS

DISCORD_ISSUER = "https://discord.com"
DISCORD_AUTHORIZE_URL = "https://discord.com/oauth2/authorize"
DISCORD_TOKEN_URL = "https://discord.com/api/v10/oauth2/token"
DISCORD_USER_URL = "https://discord.com/api/v10/users/@me"
CALLBACK_PATH = "/oauth/callback"
STATE_TTL_SECONDS = 600
TICKET_TTL_SECONDS = 120
REDIS_PREFIX = "graild-discord-oauth:v1:"
GLOBAL_SESSION_TTL_SECONDS = 90 * 24 * 60 * 60
GLOBAL_SESSION_PREFIX = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}global-session:"
GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_SCORES = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}leaderboard:scores"
GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_PROFILES = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}leaderboard:profiles"
GLOBAL_PLAYERS = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}players"
GLOBAL_TAG_PREFIX = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}tag:"
GLOBAL_CUSTOM_TAGS = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}custom-tags"
STATE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32,128}$")

CONSUME_TICKET_SCRIPT = """
local raw = redis.call('GET', KEYS[1])
if not raw then
  return 0
end
local ticket = cjson.decode(raw)
if ticket.desktop_state ~= ARGV[1] or ticket.redirect_uri ~= ARGV[2] then
  return -1
end
redis.call('DEL', KEYS[1])
return raw
"""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Settings:
  discord_client_id: str
  discord_client_secret: str
  discord_redirect_uri: str
  redis_url: str
  owner_discord_ids: str = "[REDACTED_DISCORD_ACCOUNT_ID]"

  @classmethod
  def from_env(cls) -> "Settings":
    return cls(
        discord_client_id=os.environ.get("DISCORD_CLIENT_ID", "").strip(),
        discord_client_secret=os.environ.get("DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET", ""),
        discord_redirect_uri=os.environ.get("DISCORD_REDIRECT_URI", "").strip(),
        redis_url=os.environ.get("REDIS_URL", "redis://redis:6379/0").strip(),
        owner_discord_ids=os.environ.get("GRAILD_OWNER_DISCORD_IDS", "[REDACTED_DISCORD_ACCOUNT_ID]").strip(),
    )

  def validate(self) -> None:
    if not self.discord_client_id or not self.discord_client_secret:
      raise RuntimeError("DISCORD_CLIENT_ID and DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET must be set on the broker.")
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(self.discord_redirect_uri)
    if (
        parsed.scheme != "https"
        or not parsed.hostname
        or parsed.path != CALLBACK_PATH
        or parsed.query
        or parsed.fragment
        or parsed.username
        or parsed.password
    ):
      raise RuntimeError(
          "DISCORD_REDIRECT_URI must be the public HTTPS callback ending in /oauth/callback."
      )
    if not self.redis_url:
      raise RuntimeError("REDIS_URL must point to a Redis 7 service.")
    if any(not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]{15,22}', value.strip()) for value in self.owner_discord_ids.split(',') if value.strip()):
      raise RuntimeError('GRAILD_OWNER_DISCORD_IDS must contain numeric Discord user IDs separated by commas.')


def desktop_redirect_is_valid(value: str) -> bool:
  """Only permit the exact temporary IPv4 loopback redirect used by web_app.py."""
  try:
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(value)
    port = parsed.port
  except ValueError:
    return False
  return bool(
      parsed.scheme == "http"
      and parsed.hostname == "127.0.0.1"
      and port is not None
      and 1 <= port <= 65535
      and parsed.path == "/"
      and not parsed.query
      and not parsed.fragment
      and not parsed.username
      and not parsed.password
  )


def parse_form(body: bytes, content_type: str, required: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict[str, str]:
  if content_type.split(";", 1)[0].strip().lower() != "application/x-www-form-urlencoded":
    raise HTTPException(status_code=415, detail="Expected application/x-www-form-urlencoded.")
  if len(body) > 8192:
    raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail="Request is too large.")
  try:
    values = urllib.parse.parse_qs(body.decode("utf-8"), keep_blank_values=True, strict_parsing=True)
  except (UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
    raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Malformed form data.") from None
  result: dict[str, str] = {}
  for name in required:
    items = values.get(name, [])
    if len(items) != 1 or not items[0]:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Missing or repeated field: {name}.")
    result[name] = items[0]
  return result


async def issue_global_session(redis: Any, identity: dict[str, str | None]) -> str:
  token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
  session_key = GLOBAL_SESSION_PREFIX + hashlib.sha256(token.encode("ascii")).hexdigest()
  session_value = json.dumps({
      "provider": identity["provider"],
      "subject": identity["subject"],
      "display_name": identity.get("display_name") or "",
  }, separators=(",", ":"))
  stored = await redis.set(
      session_key,
      session_value,
      ex=GLOBAL_SESSION_TTL_SECONDS,
      nx=True,
  )
  if not stored:
    raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Could not create leaderboard session.")
  await register_global_player(redis, identity)
  return token


def global_player_id(identity):
  return hashlib.sha256(f"{identity['provider']}:{identity['subject']}".encode()).hexdigest()


async def register_global_player(redis, identity):
  player_id = global_player_id(identity)
  await redis.hset(GLOBAL_PLAYERS, player_id, json.dumps({
      'provider': identity['provider'], 'subject': identity['subject'],
      'display_name': identity.get('display_name') or identity['provider'].title() + ' player',
  }))
  return player_id


async def authenticated_global_identity(request: Request) -> dict[str, str]:
  authorization = request.headers.get("authorization", "")
  scheme, separator, token = authorization.partition(" ")
  if not separator or scheme.casefold() != "bearer" or not token:
    raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="A valid leaderboard bearer token is required.")
  key = GLOBAL_SESSION_PREFIX + hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
  value = await request.app.state.redis.get(key)
  if not value:
    raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Leaderboard session expired or was revoked.")
  try:
    identity = json.loads(value)
  except (TypeError, ValueError):
    await request.app.state.redis.delete(key)
    raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Leaderboard session is invalid.") from None
  if identity.get("provider") not in ("google", "discord") or not identity.get("subject"):
    await request.app.state.redis.delete(key)
    raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Leaderboard session is invalid.")
  return identity


def make_app(
    settings: Settings | None = None,
    redis_client: Any | None = None,
    http_client: Any | None = None,
) -> FastAPI:
  config = settings or Settings.from_env()
  owns_redis = redis_client is None
  owns_http = http_client is None

  @asynccontextmanager
  async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    config.validate()
    if owns_redis:
      app.state.redis = Redis.from_url(config.redis_url, decode_responses=True)
    else:
      app.state.redis = redis_client
    if owns_http:
      app.state.http = httpx.AsyncClient(
          timeout=httpx.Timeout(15.0, connect=5.0),
          follow_redirects=False,
          headers={"User-Agent": "GRAILD-Discord-OAuth-Broker/1.0"},
      )
    else:
      app.state.http = http_client
    await app.state.redis.ping()
    try:
      yield
    finally:
      if owns_http:
        await app.state.http.aclose()
      if owns_redis:
        await app.state.redis.aclose()

  app = FastAPI(
      title="GRAIL'D Discord OAuth Broker",
      docs_url=None,
      redoc_url=None,
      openapi_url=None,
      lifespan=lifespan,
  )
  owner_ids = {value.strip() for value in config.owner_discord_ids.split(',') if value.strip()}

  def is_owner(identity):
    return identity['provider'] == 'discord' and identity['subject'] in owner_ids

  async def tag_definitions():
    custom = await app.state.redis.hgetall(GLOBAL_CUSTOM_TAGS)
    return {**TAGS, **{tag_id: json.loads(raw) for tag_id, raw in custom.items()}}

  async def global_tags(identity, definitions=None):
    player_id = global_player_id(identity)
    tags = [{**TAGS['owner'], 'awarded_at': None}] if is_owner(identity) else []
    definitions = definitions if definitions is not None else await tag_definitions()
    for tag_id, definition in definitions.items():
      if tag_id == 'owner':
        continue
      raw = await app.state.redis.get(GLOBAL_TAG_PREFIX + player_id + ':' + tag_id)
      if raw:
        tags.append({**definition, 'awarded_at': json.loads(raw)['awarded_at']})
    return {'player_id': player_id, 'provider': identity['provider'], 'tags': tags,
            'can_manage_tags': is_owner(identity)}

  @app.post('/account/tags/sync')
  async def sync_account_tags(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get('content-type', ''), ('achievement_ids',))
    try:
      ids = json.loads(form['achievement_ids'])
      if not isinstance(ids, list) or len(ids) > 50 or any(not isinstance(item, str) for item in ids):
        raise ValueError()
      if not set(ids) <= ACHIEVEMENT_IDS or len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
        raise ValueError()
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail='Expected up to 50 distinct, recognized achievement IDs.') from None
    player_id = await register_global_player(app.state.redis, identity)
    if set(ids) == ACHIEVEMENT_IDS:
      await app.state.redis.set(GLOBAL_TAG_PREFIX + player_id + ':top_earner',
                                json.dumps({'awarded_at': int(time.time())}), nx=True)
    return {'success': True, **await global_tags(identity)}

  @app.get('/account/tags')
  async def get_account_tags(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    await register_global_player(app.state.redis, identity)
    return {'success': True, **await global_tags(identity)}

  @app.get('/account/tag-players')
  async def tag_players(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    if not is_owner(identity):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='Only the verified owner can manage global tags.')
    players = await app.state.redis.hgetall(GLOBAL_PLAYERS)
    accounts = []
    definitions = await tag_definitions()
    for player_id, raw in players.items():
      player = json.loads(raw)
      tags = await global_tags(player, definitions)
      accounts.append({'player_id': player_id, 'display_name': player['display_name'],
                       'provider': player['provider'], 'tag_ids': [tag['id'] for tag in tags['tags']],
                       'beta_tester': any(t['id'] == 'beta_tester' for t in tags['tags'])})
    return {'success': True, 'accounts': sorted(accounts, key=lambda p: (p['display_name'].casefold(), p['player_id']))}

  @app.post('/account/tags/assign')
  async def assign_account_tag(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    if not is_owner(identity):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='Only the verified owner can manage global tags.')
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get('content-type', ''), ('player_id', 'tag_id', 'assigned'))
    await check_assignable_tag(form['tag_id'], form['assigned'])
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[a-f0-9]{64}', form['player_id']):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail='Invalid global player ID.')
    if not await app.state.redis.hget(GLOBAL_PLAYERS, form['player_id']):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail='This player must sign in before a tag can be assigned.')
    return await store_assigned_tag(identity, form['player_id'], form['assigned'] == 'true', form['tag_id'])

  async def check_assignable_tag(tag_id, assigned):
    definition = (await tag_definitions()).get(tag_id)
    if not definition or tag_id == 'owner' or definition['kind'] != 'assigned' or assigned not in ('true', 'false'):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail='Choose an assignable tag. Owner and earned tags cannot be assigned manually.')
    return definition

  async def store_assigned_tag(identity, player_id, assigned, tag_id):
    key = GLOBAL_TAG_PREFIX + player_id + ':' + tag_id
    if assigned:
      await app.state.redis.set(key, json.dumps({'awarded_at': int(time.time()),
                                               'assigned_by': global_player_id(identity)}), nx=True)
    else:
      await app.state.redis.delete(key)
    definition = (await tag_definitions())[tag_id]
    return {'success': True, 'message': definition['name'] + ' tag ' + ('assigned.' if assigned else 'removed.')}

  @app.get('/account/tags/catalog')
  async def custom_tag_catalog(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    if not is_owner(identity):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='Only the verified owner can manage custom tags.')
    return {'success': True, 'entries': [tag for tag_id, tag in (await tag_definitions()).items()
                                       if tag_id != 'owner' and tag['kind'] == 'assigned']}

  @app.post('/account/tags/create')
  async def create_custom_tag(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    if not is_owner(identity):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='Only the verified owner can create custom tags.')
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get('content-type', ''), ('name', 'description'))
    name, description = ' '.join(form['name'].split()), form['description'].strip()
    reserved = {'owner', 'creator', 'owner / creator', 'owner/creator'} | {tag['name'].casefold() for tag in TAGS.values()}
    if not 1 <= len(name) <= 32 or not 1 <= len(description) <= 240 or name.casefold() in reserved or any(ord(c) < 32 or ord(c) == 127 for c in name + description):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail='Use a unique tag name (1–32 characters) and hover description (1–240 characters). Built-in names are reserved.')
    tag_id = 'custom_' + hashlib.sha256(name.casefold().encode()).hexdigest()[:24]
    definition = {'id': tag_id, 'name': name, 'description': description, 'kind': 'assigned'}
    if not await app.state.redis.hsetnx(GLOBAL_CUSTOM_TAGS, tag_id, json.dumps(definition)):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail='A tag with that name already exists. Choose it from the tag list.')
    return {'success': True, 'message': 'Custom tag created.', 'tag': definition}

  @app.post('/account/tags/discord')
  async def assign_discord_tag(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    if not is_owner(identity):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail='Only the verified owner can manage global tags.')
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get('content-type', ''), ('discord_id', 'tag_id', 'assigned'))
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]{15,22}', form['discord_id']):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail='Enter a numeric Discord user ID.')
    await check_assignable_tag(form['tag_id'], form['assigned'])
    player_id = global_player_id({'provider': 'discord', 'subject': form['discord_id']})
    result = await store_assigned_tag(identity, player_id, form['assigned'] == 'true', form['tag_id'])
    result['message'] += ' The change applies when that Discord account signs in on any device.'
    return result

  @app.middleware("http")
  async def no_store_and_security_headers(request: Request, call_next):
    response = await call_next(request)
    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    response.headers["Pragma"] = "no-cache"
    response.headers["X-Content-Type-Options"] = "nosniff"
    response.headers["Referrer-Policy"] = "no-referrer"
    response.headers["X-Frame-Options"] = "DENY"
    return response

  @app.get("/healthz")
  async def health(request: Request):
    try:
      await request.app.state.redis.ping()
    except Exception:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Broker storage unavailable.") from None
    return {"status": "ok"}

  @app.post("/oauth/google/verify")
  async def google_verify(request: Request):
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get("content-type", ""),
                      ("access_token",))
    if len(form["access_token"]) > 8192:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid Google access token.")
    try:
      response = await request.app.state.http.get(
          "https://openidconnect.googleapis.com/v1/userinfo",
          headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {form['access_token']}", "Accept": "application/json"},
      )
      response.raise_for_status()
      user = response.json()
    except (httpx.HTTPError, ValueError) as error:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Google identity could not be verified.") from error
    subject = str(user.get("sub") or "")
    if not subject or len(subject) > 256 or user.get("email_verified") is not True:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Google account email is not verified.")
    identity = {
        "provider": "google",
        "subject": subject,
        "display_name": str(user.get("name") or user.get("email") or "")[:100],
    }
    global_token = await issue_global_session(request.app.state.redis, identity)
    return {
        **identity,
        "email": user.get("email"),
        "global_token": global_token,
    }

  @app.post("/oauth/start")
  async def oauth_start(request: Request):
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get("content-type", ""),
                      ("provider", "state", "redirect_uri"))
    if form["provider"] != "discord":
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unsupported provider.")
    if not STATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(form["state"]):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid desktop state value.")
    if not desktop_redirect_is_valid(form["redirect_uri"]):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Desktop redirect must be an IPv4 loopback URI.")

    broker_state = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    verifier = secrets.token_urlsafe(64)
    challenge = __import__("base64").urlsafe_b64encode(
        hashlib.sha256(verifier.encode("ascii")).digest()
    ).rstrip(b"=").decode("ascii")
    state_record = json.dumps({
        "desktop_state": form["state"],
        "redirect_uri": form["redirect_uri"],
        "verifier": verifier,
        "created_at": int(time.time()),
    }, separators=(",", ":"))
    stored = await request.app.state.redis.set(
        f"{REDIS_PREFIX}state:{hashlib.sha256(broker_state.encode()).hexdigest()}",
        state_record,
        ex=STATE_TTL_SECONDS,
        nx=True,
    )
    if not stored:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Could not create an OAuth state record.")

    authorization_url = DISCORD_AUTHORIZE_URL + "?" + urllib.parse.urlencode({
        "client_id": config.discord_client_id,
        "response_type": "code",
        "redirect_uri": config.discord_redirect_uri,
        "scope": "identify email",
        "state": broker_state,
        "code_challenge": challenge,
        "code_challenge_method": "S256",
    })
    return {"authorization_url": authorization_url}

  @app.get(CALLBACK_PATH, include_in_schema=False)
  async def discord_callback(request: Request):
    query = request.query_params
    provider_state = query.get("state", "")
    if not STATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(provider_state):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid OAuth state.")
    if query.get("iss") != DISCORD_ISSUER:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unexpected OAuth issuer.")

    state_key = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}state:{hashlib.sha256(provider_state.encode()).hexdigest()}"
    raw_state = await request.app.state.redis.getdel(state_key)
    if not raw_state:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="OAuth state expired or was already used.")
    state_record = json.loads(raw_state)

    error = query.get("error")
    if error:
      redirect_query = urllib.parse.urlencode({
          "error": "access_denied",
          "state": state_record["desktop_state"],
          "iss": DISCORD_ISSUER,
      })
      return RedirectResponse(
          f"{state_record['redirect_uri']}?{redirect_query}",
          status_code=303,
          headers={"Cache-Control": "no-store", "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer"},
      )

    code = query.get("code", "")
    if not code or len(code) > 4096:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Missing or invalid authorization code.")

    try:
      token_response = await request.app.state.http.post(
          DISCORD_TOKEN_URL,
          data={
              "grant_type": "authorization_code",
              "code": code,
              "redirect_uri": config.discord_redirect_uri,
              "code_verifier": state_record["verifier"],
          },
          auth=(config.discord_client_id, config.discord_client_secret),
          headers={"Accept": "application/json"},
      )
      token_response.raise_for_status()
      tokens = token_response.json()
      access_token = tokens.get("access_token")
      scopes = set(str(tokens.get("scope", "")).split())
      if not access_token or "identify" not in scopes:
        raise ValueError("Discord did not grant the identify scope.")
      user_response = await request.app.state.http.get(
          DISCORD_USER_URL,
          headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {access_token}", "Accept": "application/json"},
      )
      user_response.raise_for_status()
      user = user_response.json()
    except (httpx.HTTPError, ValueError, KeyError) as error:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Discord authorization could not be verified.") from error

    subject = str(user.get("id", ""))
    if not subject.isdigit() or len(subject) > 24:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=502, detail="Discord returned an invalid user identity.")
    display_name = user.get("global_name") or user.get("username") or ""
    ticket = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    ticket_record = json.dumps({
        "desktop_state": state_record["desktop_state"],
        "redirect_uri": state_record["redirect_uri"],
        "subject": subject,
        "email": user.get("email") if user.get("verified") else None,
        "display_name": str(display_name)[:100],
        "username": str(user.get("username", ""))[:64],
        "issuer": DISCORD_ISSUER,
        "issued_at": int(time.time()),
    }, separators=(",", ":"))
    stored = await request.app.state.redis.set(
        f"{REDIS_PREFIX}ticket:{hashlib.sha256(ticket.encode()).hexdigest()}",
        ticket_record,
        ex=TICKET_TTL_SECONDS,
        nx=True,
    )
    if not stored:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=503, detail="Could not issue a sign-in ticket.")

    redirect_query = urllib.parse.urlencode({
        "ticket": ticket,
        "state": state_record["desktop_state"],
        "iss": DISCORD_ISSUER,
    })
    return RedirectResponse(
        f"{state_record['redirect_uri']}?{redirect_query}",
        status_code=303,
        headers={"Cache-Control": "no-store", "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer"},
    )

  @app.post("/oauth/complete")
  async def oauth_complete(request: Request):
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get("content-type", ""),
                      ("provider", "state", "ticket", "redirect_uri"))
    if form["provider"] != "discord":
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Unsupported provider.")
    if not STATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(form["state"]):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid desktop state value.")
    if not desktop_redirect_is_valid(form["redirect_uri"]):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Desktop redirect must be an IPv4 loopback URI.")
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32,128}", form["ticket"]):
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid sign-in ticket.")

    ticket_key = f"{REDIS_PREFIX}ticket:{hashlib.sha256(form['ticket'].encode()).hexdigest()}"
    result = await request.app.state.redis.eval(
        CONSUME_TICKET_SCRIPT,
        1,
        ticket_key,
        form["state"],
        form["redirect_uri"],
    )
    if result == 0:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=410, detail="Ticket expired or already used.")
    if result == -1:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Ticket does not match this authorization.")
    record = json.loads(result)
    if record.get("issuer") != DISCORD_ISSUER or not record.get("subject", "").isdigit():
      raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Ticket identity validation failed.")
    identity = {
        "provider": "discord",
        "subject": record["subject"],
        "email": record.get("email"),
        "display_name": record.get("display_name", ""),
        "username": record.get("username", ""),
    }
    global_token = await issue_global_session(request.app.state.redis, identity)
    return {**identity, "global_token": global_token}

  @app.post("/leaderboard/update")
  async def leaderboard_update(request: Request):
    identity = await authenticated_global_identity(request)
    form = parse_form(await request.body(), request.headers.get("content-type", ""),
                      ("display_name", "points", "correct", "wrong"))
    display_name = form["display_name"].strip()
    if not display_name or len(display_name) > 64:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Display name must be 1–64 characters.")
    try:
      points = int(form["points"])
      correct = int(form["correct"])
      wrong = int(form["wrong"])
    except ValueError:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Leaderboard totals must be integers.") from None
    if min(points, correct, wrong) < 0 or max(points, correct, wrong) > 2_000_000_000:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Leaderboard totals are out of range.")
    member = f"{identity['provider']}:{identity['subject']}"
    script = """
    local old_raw = redis.call('HGET', KEYS[1], ARGV[1])
    local old = old_raw and cjson.decode(old_raw) or {}
    local points = math.max(tonumber(old.points or 0), tonumber(ARGV[4]))
    local correct = math.max(tonumber(old.correct or 0), tonumber(ARGV[5]))
    local wrong = math.max(tonumber(old.wrong or 0), tonumber(ARGV[6]))
    local record = cjson.encode({
      provider = ARGV[2], display_name = ARGV[3],
      points = points, correct = correct, wrong = wrong
    })
    redis.call('HSET', KEYS[1], ARGV[1], record)
    redis.call('ZADD', KEYS[2], points, ARGV[1])
    return record
    """
    raw_record = await request.app.state.redis.eval(
        script,
        2,
        GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_PROFILES,
        GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_SCORES,
        member,
        identity["provider"],
        display_name,
        points,
        correct,
        wrong,
    )
    return {"success": True, "entry": json.loads(raw_record)}

  @app.get("/leaderboard")
  async def global_leaderboard(request: Request):
    members = await request.app.state.redis.zrevrange(
        GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_SCORES, 0, 49
    )
    entries = []
    definitions = await tag_definitions()
    for member in members:
      raw = await request.app.state.redis.hget(GLOBAL_LEADERBOARD_PROFILES, member)
      if raw:
        entry = json.loads(raw)
        provider, subject = member.split(':', 1)
        await register_global_player(app.state.redis, {'provider': provider, 'subject': subject,
                                                      'display_name': entry['display_name']})
        entry.update({key: value for key, value in (await global_tags({'provider': provider, 'subject': subject}, definitions)).items()
                      if key in ('tags', 'player_id')})
        entry["rank"] = len(entries) + 1
        entries.append(entry)
    return {"entries": entries}

  @app.post("/leaderboard/revoke")
  async def revoke_global_session(request: Request):
    authorization = request.headers.get("authorization", "")
    token = authorization.partition(" ")[2]
    if not token:
      raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="A leaderboard bearer token is required.")
    key = GLOBAL_SESSION_PREFIX + hashlib.sha256(token.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    removed = await request.app.state.redis.delete(key)
    return {"revoked": bool(removed)}

  return app


app = make_app()

"""Global profile-tag rules; keep achievement IDs aligned with the desktop catalog."""
TARGETS = {
    'correct': [1, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250, 500, 999],
    'points': [100, 500, 1000, 2500, 5000, 10000, 25000, 99999],
    'streak': [3, 5, 10, 15, 25, 50, 99],
    'grails': [1, 5, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250],
    'rounds': [10, 50, 100, 250, 500, 1000],
    'first_try': [1, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250],
    'hard': [1, 10, 25, 50, 100, 250],
}
ACHIEVEMENT_IDS = {f'{metric}_{target}' for metric, targets in TARGETS.items() for target in targets}
ACHIEVEMENT_IDS |= {'linked_google', 'linked_discord'}
TAGS = {
    'owner': {'id': 'owner', 'name': 'Owner / Creator', 'kind': 'assigned',
              'description': "Verified owner / creator of GRAIL'D."},
    'beta_tester': {'id': 'beta_tester', 'name': 'Beta Tester', 'kind': 'assigned',
                    'description': "Awarded by the owner for helping beta test GRAIL'D."},
    'top_earner': {'id': 'top_earner', 'name': 'Top earner', 'kind': 'earned',
                   'description': 'Unlocked all 50 achievements.'},
}

import hashlib
import json
import time
import urllib.parse

import httpx
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app import Settings, make_app


class MemoryRedis:
  def __init__(self):
    self.values = {}
    self.hashes = {}
    self.sorted_sets = {}

  def _live(self, key):
    record = self.values.get(key)
    if record and record[1] is not None and record[1] <= time.time():
      self.values.pop(key, None)
      return None
    return record

  async def ping(self):
    return True

  async def aclose(self):
    return None

  async def set(self, key, value, ex=None, nx=False):
    if self._live(key) and nx:
      return None
    expires = time.time() + ex if ex else None
    self.values[key] = (value, expires)
    return True

  async def get(self, key):
    record = self._live(key)
    return record[0] if record else None

  async def getdel(self, key):
    record = self._live(key)
    self.values.pop(key, None)
    return record[0] if record else None

  def expire_now(self, key):
    value, _expires = self.values[key]
    self.values[key] = (value, time.time() - 1)

  async def eval(self, script, _number_of_keys, *args):
    if "local old_raw" in script:
      profiles_key, scores_key, member, provider, display_name, points, correct, wrong = args
      previous = json.loads(self.hashes[profiles_key][member]) if member in self.hashes.get(profiles_key, {}) else {}
      entry = {
          "provider": provider,
          "display_name": display_name,
          "points": max(previous.get("points", 0), int(points)),
          "correct": max(previous.get("correct", 0), int(correct)),
          "wrong": max(previous.get("wrong", 0), int(wrong)),
      }
      encoded = json.dumps(entry)
      self.hashes.setdefault(profiles_key, {})[member] = encoded
      self.sorted_sets.setdefault(scores_key, {})[member] = entry["points"]
      return encoded
    key, desktop_state, redirect_uri = args
    record = await self.get(key)
    if record is None:
      return 0
    ticket = json.loads(record)
    if ticket["desktop_state"] != desktop_state or ticket["redirect_uri"] != redirect_uri:
      return -1
    self.values.pop(key, None)
    return record

  async def hget(self, key, field):
    return self.hashes.get(key, {}).get(field)

  async def hset(self, key, field, value):
    self.hashes.setdefault(key, {})[field] = value
    return 1

  async def hgetall(self, key):
    return dict(self.hashes.get(key, {}))

  async def hsetnx(self, key, field, value):
    records = self.hashes.setdefault(key, {})
    if field in records:
      return 0
    records[field] = value
    return 1

  async def zrevrange(self, key, start, stop):
    members = sorted(
        self.sorted_sets.get(key, {}),
        key=lambda member: (-self.sorted_sets[key][member], member),
    )
    return members[start:stop + 1]

  async def delete(self, key):
    return int(self.values.pop(key, None) is not None)


def make_test_client():
  redis = MemoryRedis()

  async def discord_mock(request):
    if request.url.path.endswith("/oauth2/token"):
      form = urllib.parse.parse_qs(request.content.decode())
      assert form["grant_type"] == ["authorization_code"]
      assert form["code_verifier"]
      assert form["redirect_uri"] == ["https://broker.example.test/oauth/callback"]
      assert request.headers.get("authorization", "").startswith("Basic ")
      return httpx.Response(200, json={
          "access_token": "[REDACTED_TEST_ACCESS_TOKEN]",
          "scope": "identify email",
      })
    if request.url.path.endswith("/users/@me"):
      assert request.headers["authorization"] == "Bearer [REDACTED_TEST_ACCESS_TOKEN]"
      return httpx.Response(200, json={
          "id": "123456789012345678",
          "username": "test_user",
          "global_name": "Test User",
          "email": "test@example.test",
          "verified": True,
      })
    if request.url.path.endswith("/v1/userinfo"):
      assert request.headers["authorization"] == "Bearer [REDACTED_TEST_ACCESS_TOKEN]"
      return httpx.Response(200, json={
          "sub": "google-subject-987",
          "name": "Google User",
          "email": "google@example.test",
          "email_verified": True,
      })
    return httpx.Response(404)

  http_client = httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.MockTransport(discord_mock))
  settings = Settings(
      discord_client_id="test-client-id",
      discord_client_secret="[REDACTED_TEST_CLIENT_SECRET]",
      discord_redirect_uri="https://broker.example.test/oauth/callback",
      redis_url="redis://unused",
  )
  app = make_app(settings, redis_client=redis, http_client=http_client)
  app.state.redis = redis
  app.state.http = http_client
  return TestClient(app), redis


def test_desktop_redirect_accepts_only_exact_loopback_root():
  from app import desktop_redirect_is_valid

  assert desktop_redirect_is_valid("http://127.0.0.1:49123/")
  assert not desktop_redirect_is_valid("https://127.0.0.1:49123/")
  assert not desktop_redirect_is_valid("http://localhost:49123/")
  assert not desktop_redirect_is_valid("http://127.0.0.1:49123.evil.example/")
  assert not desktop_redirect_is_valid("http://127.0.0.1:49123/other")
  assert not desktop_redirect_is_valid("http://127.0.0.1:49123/?next=https://evil.example")


def test_discord_flow_contract_pkce_and_single_use_ticket():
  client, _redis = make_test_client()
  desktop_state = "d" * 43
  desktop_redirect = "http://127.0.0.1:49123/"
  start = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord",
      "state": desktop_state,
      "redirect_uri": desktop_redirect,
  })
  assert start.status_code == 200
  authorization = urllib.parse.urlsplit(start.json()["authorization_url"])
  assert authorization.hostname == "discord.com"
  query = urllib.parse.parse_qs(authorization.query)
  assert query["scope"] == ["identify email"]
  assert query["code_challenge_method"] == ["S256"]
  assert query["redirect_uri"] == ["https://broker.example.test/oauth/callback"]

  callback = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "one-time-code",
      "state": query["state"][0],
      "iss": "https://discord.com",
  }, follow_redirects=False)
  assert callback.status_code == 303
  returned = urllib.parse.urlsplit(callback.headers["location"])
  assert returned.netloc == "127.0.0.1:49123"
  callback_values = urllib.parse.parse_qs(returned.query)
  assert callback_values["state"] == [desktop_state]
  assert callback_values["iss"] == ["https://discord.com"]
  ticket = callback_values["ticket"][0]

  complete = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord",
      "state": desktop_state,
      "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": desktop_redirect,
  })
  assert complete.status_code == 200
  assert complete.json() == {
      "provider": "discord",
      "subject": "123456789012345678",
      "email": "test@example.test",
      "display_name": "Test User",
      "username": "test_user",
      "global_token": complete.json()["global_token"],
  }
  global_token = complete.json()["global_token"]
  replay = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord",
      "state": desktop_state,
      "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": desktop_redirect,
  })
  assert replay.status_code == 410
  assert global_token


def test_mismatched_ticket_binding_does_not_consume_ticket():
  client, _redis = make_test_client()
  state = "s" * 43
  redirect_uri = "http://127.0.0.1:49000/"
  start = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": state, "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
  })
  broker_state = urllib.parse.parse_qs(
      urllib.parse.urlsplit(start.json()["authorization_url"]).query
  )["state"][0]
  callback = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://discord.com",
  }, follow_redirects=False)
  ticket = urllib.parse.parse_qs(urllib.parse.urlsplit(callback.headers["location"]).query)["ticket"][0]
  mismatch = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": "x" * 43, "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
  })
  assert mismatch.status_code == 403
  redirect_mismatch = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": state, "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": "http://127.0.0.1:49001/",
  })
  assert redirect_mismatch.status_code == 403
  valid = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": state, "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
  })
  assert valid.status_code == 200


def test_expired_ticket_cannot_be_redeemed():
  client, redis = make_test_client()
  state = "e" * 43
  redirect_uri = "http://127.0.0.1:49200/"
  start = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": state, "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
  })
  broker_state = urllib.parse.parse_qs(
      urllib.parse.urlsplit(start.json()["authorization_url"]).query
  )["state"][0]
  callback = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://discord.com",
  }, follow_redirects=False)
  ticket = urllib.parse.parse_qs(urllib.parse.urlsplit(callback.headers["location"]).query)["ticket"][0]
  key = "graild-discord-oauth:v1:ticket:" + hashlib.sha256(ticket.encode()).hexdigest()
  redis.expire_now(key)
  complete = client.post("/oauth/complete", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": state, "ticket": ticket,
      "redirect_uri": redirect_uri,
  })
  assert complete.status_code == 410


def test_expired_oauth_state_cannot_complete_callback():
  client, redis = make_test_client()
  start = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord",
      "state": "t" * 43,
      "redirect_uri": "http://127.0.0.1:49201/",
  })
  broker_state = urllib.parse.parse_qs(
      urllib.parse.urlsplit(start.json()["authorization_url"]).query
  )["state"][0]
  state_key = "graild-discord-oauth:v1:state:" + hashlib.sha256(broker_state.encode()).hexdigest()
  redis.expire_now(state_key)
  callback = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://discord.com",
  })
  assert callback.status_code == 400


def test_google_verification_issues_a_global_session():

  assert response.json()["global_token"]


def test_global_leaderboard_requires_token_and_ranks_verified_users():
  client, _redis = make_test_client()
  unauthorized = client.post("/leaderboard/update", data={
    "display_name": "Player", "points": 100, "correct": 3, "wrong": 1,
  })
  assert unauthorized.status_code == 401

  verified = client.post("/oauth/google/verify", data={"access_token": "[REDACTED_TEST_ACCESS_TOKEN]"})
  token = verified.json()["global_token"]
  headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"}
  update = client.post("/leaderboard/update", headers=headers, data={
    "display_name": "Player", "points": 100, "correct": 3, "wrong": 1,
  })
  assert update.status_code == 200
  stale_update = client.post("/leaderboard/update", headers=headers, data={
    "display_name": "Player", "points": 50, "correct": 1, "wrong": 1,
  })
  assert stale_update.json()["entry"]["points"] == 100
  board = client.get("/leaderboard")
  assert board.json()["entries"][0]["display_name"] == "Player"
  assert board.json()["entries"][0]["provider"] == "google"
  assert client.post("/leaderboard/revoke", headers=headers).json() == {"revoked": True}
  expired_session = client.post("/leaderboard/update", headers=headers, data={
    "display_name": "Player", "points": 110, "correct": 4, "wrong": 1,
  })
  assert expired_session.status_code == 401

      "provider": "discord", "state": "z" * 43,
      "redirect_uri": "https://attacker.example/callback",

  short_state = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": "short",
      "redirect_uri": "http://127.0.0.1:49123/",
  })
  assert short_state.status_code == 400


def test_callback_rejects_wrong_issuer_and_replayed_state():
  client, _redis = make_test_client()
  start = client.post("/oauth/start", data={
      "provider": "discord", "state": "q" * 43,
      "redirect_uri": "http://127.0.0.1:49123/",
  })
  broker_state = urllib.parse.parse_qs(
      urllib.parse.urlsplit(start.json()["authorization_url"]).query
  )["state"][0]
  wrong_issuer = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://attacker.example",
  })
  assert wrong_issuer.status_code == 400
  valid = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://discord.com",
  }, follow_redirects=False)
  assert valid.status_code == 303
  replay = client.get("/oauth/callback", params={
      "code": "code", "state": broker_state, "iss": "https://discord.com",
  })
  assert replay.status_code == 400

render-broker/discord_oauth_broker/tests/test_tags.py
