import asyncio
import json
import runpy
from pathlib import Path

from app import issue_global_session, global_player_id
from tag_rules import ACHIEVEMENT_IDS
from tests.test_app import make_test_client

OWNER = '[REDACTED_DISCORD_ACCOUNT_ID]'


def session(redis, subject, provider='discord'):
    identity = {'provider': provider, 'subject': subject, 'display_name': 'Player ' + subject[-3:]}
    token = asyncio.run(issue_global_session(redis, identity))
    return {'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token}, global_player_id(identity)


def test_catalog_matches_desktop():
    desktop_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / 'achievements.py'
    if desktop_path.is_file():
        desktop = runpy.run_path(str(desktop_path))
        assert ACHIEVEMENT_IDS == {item['id'] for item in desktop['ACHIEVEMENTS']}
    assert len(ACHIEVEMENT_IDS) == 50


def test_owner_assigns_and_second_installation_recognizes_same_identity():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    owner, _ = session(redis, OWNER)
    tester, player_id = session(redis, '123456789012345678')
    assert client.post('/account/tags/assign', headers=owner, data={
        'player_id': player_id, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'true'}).status_code == 200
    before = client.get('/account/tags', headers=tester).json()
    second_installation, second_id = session(redis, '123456789012345678')
    after = client.get('/account/tags', headers=second_installation).json()
    assert player_id == second_id == after['player_id']
    assert before['tags'] == after['tags']
    assert after['tags'][0]['id'] == 'beta_tester'
    players = client.get('/account/tag-players', headers=owner).json()['accounts']
    assert next(p for p in players if p['player_id'] == player_id)['beta_tester']
    assert '123456789012345678' not in json.dumps(players)
    client.post('/account/tags/assign', headers=owner, data={
        'player_id': player_id, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'false'})
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=second_installation).json()['tags'] == []


def test_local_flags_and_other_providers_do_not_grant_ownership():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    tester, player_id = session(redis, '123456789012345678')
    google, _ = session(redis, OWNER, 'google')
    for headers in ({}, tester, google):
        assert client.post('/account/tags/assign', headers=headers, data={
            'player_id': player_id, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'true', 'is_creator': '1'}).status_code in (401, 403)
        assert client.get('/account/tag-players', headers=headers).status_code in (401, 403)
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=google).json()['can_manage_tags'] is False
    owner, _ = session(redis, OWNER)
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=owner).json()['tags'][0]['id'] == 'owner'


def test_top_earner_requires_all_real_ids_and_is_permanent():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    player, _ = session(redis, '123456789012345678')
    ids = sorted(ACHIEVEMENT_IDS)
    assert client.post('/account/tags/sync', headers=player, data={'achievement_ids': json.dumps(ids[:-1])}).json()['tags'] == []
    for invalid in (ids[:-1] + ['invented'], ids[:-1] + [ids[0]], 'all', [None]):
        assert client.post('/account/tags/sync', headers=player, data={'achievement_ids': json.dumps(invalid)}).status_code == 400
    earned = client.post('/account/tags/sync', headers=player, data={'achievement_ids': json.dumps(ids)}).json()
    assert earned['tags'][0]['id'] == 'top_earner'
    assert client.post('/account/tags/sync', headers=player, data={'achievement_ids': '[]'}).json()['tags'] == earned['tags']
    other, _ = session(redis, '987654321012345678')
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=other).json()['tags'] == []


def test_owner_cannot_assign_earned_owner_or_unknown_tags():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    owner, _ = session(redis, OWNER)
    _, player_id = session(redis, '123456789012345678')
    for tag in ('top_earner', 'owner', 'unknown'):
        assert client.post('/account/tags/assign', headers=owner, data={
            'player_id': player_id, 'tag_id': tag, 'assigned': 'true'}).status_code == 400
    assert client.post('/account/tags/assign', headers=owner, data={
        'player_id': 'a' * 64, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'true'}).status_code == 404


def test_leaderboard_uses_server_tags():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    owner, _ = session(redis, OWNER)
    tester, player_id = session(redis, '123456789012345678')
    client.post('/leaderboard/update', headers=tester, data={
        'display_name': 'Tester', 'points': 100, 'correct': 1, 'wrong': 0, 'tags': '["owner"]'})
    client.post('/account/tags/assign', headers=owner, data={
        'player_id': player_id, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'true'})
    entry = client.get('/leaderboard').json()['entries'][0]
    assert entry['player_id'] == player_id
    assert [tag['id'] for tag in entry['tags']] == ['beta_tester']


def test_assign_by_discord_before_first_sign_in():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    owner, _ = session(redis, OWNER)
    subject = '456789012345678901'
    result = client.post('/account/tags/discord', headers=owner, data={
        'discord_id': subject, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'true'})
    assert result.status_code == 200
    player, _ = session(redis, subject)
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=player).json()['tags'][0]['id'] == 'beta_tester'
    assert client.post('/account/tags/discord', headers=player, data={
        'discord_id': subject, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'false'}).status_code == 403
    client.post('/account/tags/discord', headers=owner, data={
        'discord_id': subject, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'false'})
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=player).json()['tags'] == []


def test_direct_discord_assignment_validates_id_and_cannot_grant_earned_tags():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    owner, _ = session(redis, OWNER)
    for discord_id, tag in [('username', 'beta_tester'), ('123', 'beta_tester'), ('456789012345678901', 'owner'), ('456789012345678901', 'top_earner')]:
        assert client.post('/account/tags/discord', headers=owner, data={
            'discord_id': discord_id, 'tag_id': tag, 'assigned': 'true'}).status_code == 400


def test_owner_creates_custom_tag_assigns_globally_and_removes_it():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    owner, _ = session(redis, OWNER)
    player, player_id = session(redis, '456789012345678901')
    response = client.post('/account/tags/create', headers=owner, data={
        'name': 'Contributor', 'description': 'Helped improve the game.'})
    assert response.status_code == 200
    tag = response.json()['tag']
    assert tag['kind'] == 'assigned' and tag['id'].startswith('custom_')
    assert tag in client.get('/account/tags/catalog', headers=owner).json()['entries']
    assert client.post('/account/tags/create', headers=owner, data={
        'name': 'CONTRIBUTOR', 'description': 'Duplicate'}).status_code == 409
    assert client.post('/account/tags/discord', headers=owner, data={
        'discord_id': '456789012345678901', 'tag_id': tag['id'], 'assigned': 'true'}).status_code == 200
    second_device, _ = session(redis, '456789012345678901')
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=second_device).json()['tags'][0]['description'] == tag['description']
    client.post('/leaderboard/update', headers=player, data={'display_name':'Contributor','points':10,'correct':1,'wrong':0})
    assert client.get('/leaderboard').json()['entries'][0]['tags'][0]['name'] == 'Contributor'
    client.post('/account/tags/assign', headers=owner, data={
        'player_id': player_id, 'tag_id': tag['id'], 'assigned': 'false'})
    assert client.get('/account/tags', headers=second_device).json()['tags'] == []


def test_custom_tag_creation_is_owner_only_and_protects_reserved_tags():
    client, redis = make_test_client()
    owner, _ = session(redis, OWNER)
    player, _ = session(redis, '456789012345678901')
    for headers in ({}, player):
        assert client.post('/account/tags/create', headers=headers, data={
            'name':'VIP','description':'Special member'}).status_code in (401,403)
        assert client.get('/account/tags/catalog', headers=headers).status_code in (401,403)
    for name, description in [('Owner','Fake'),('Top earner','Fake'),('Beta Tester','Fake'),('x'*33,'Too long'),('VIP','x'*241),('VIP','\x01')]:
        assert client.post('/account/tags/create', headers=owner, data={
            'name':name,'description':description}).status_code == 400

standalone_smoke.py

"""Opt-in packaged offline resource and local playback verification."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import traceback
import wave


def run(output_filename):
    output = Path(output_filename).resolve()
    report = {"passed": False, "frozen": bool(getattr(sys, "frozen", False)), "checks": []}
    try:
        # The external runner supplies the project path solely as a deny rule.
        forbidden = os.environ.get("GRAILD_STANDALONE_DENY_PROJECT")
        if forbidden:
            forbidden = Path(forbidden).resolve()
            def audit(event, args):
                if event in ("open", "os.listdir", "os.scandir") and args and isinstance(args[0], (str, bytes, os.PathLike)):
                    path = Path(os.fsdecode(args[0])).resolve()
                    if path.is_relative_to(forbidden):
                        raise PermissionError("Standalone test blocked a project-folder read")
            sys.addaudithook(audit)
        import pygame
        import webview
        import imageio_ffmpeg
        import requests
        import mutagen
        import cryptography
        import sqlite3
        import ssl
        from web_app import JuiceSpotApp, resource_path
        from app_version import APP_VERSION
        from audio_files import get_playback_path
        from tls_support import ca_bundle_path, verified_ssl_context
        import ssl
        assert Path(ca_bundle_path()).is_file()
        tls = verified_ssl_context()
        assert tls.check_hostname and tls.verify_mode == ssl.CERT_REQUIRED and tls.get_ca_certs()
        report['checks'].append('bundled CA certificates and strict TLS verification')
        report["version"] = APP_VERSION
        # Fail network calls in this process: UI and local gameplay must still work.
        def offline(*args, **kwargs):
            raise requests.ConnectionError("Offline packaging check")
        requests.sessions.Session.request = offline
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="GRAILD-standalone-profile-") as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            os.environ["APPDATA"] = str(root / "roaming")
            os.environ["LOCALAPPDATA"] = str(root / "local")
            profile = root / "roaming" / "JuiceSpot"
            profile.mkdir(parents=True)
            music = root / "External music with spaces"
            music.mkdir()
            with wave.open(str(music / "Standalone test.wav"), "wb") as sound:
                sound.setnchannels(1); sound.setsampwidth(2); sound.setframerate(44100)
                sound.writeframes(b"\0\0" * 88200)
            (profile / "juicespot_config.json").write_text(json.dumps({"music_folder": str(music)}))
            app = JuiceSpotApp()
            assert not app.audio_error, app.audio_error
            report["audio_driver"] = pygame.mixer.get_init()
            for name in ("index.html", "canary_ui.js", "update_ui.js", "assets/tailwind.css", "assets/fonts.css", "assets/inter-400.ttf", "assets/inter-600.ttf", "assets/inter-700.ttf", "assets/inter-900.ttf", "GRAILDUpdater.exe"):
                assert Path(resource_path(name)).is_file(), name
            assert Path(imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe()).is_file()
            for name in ('assets/graild-logo.png', 'assets/graild.ico', 'assets/graild.icns'):
                assert Path(resource_path(name)).is_file(), name
            assert app.register_account("standalone_test", "isolated-smoke-password")["success"]
            assert len(app.tracks) == 1 and app.music_folder == str(music)
            assert Path(get_playback_path(music / "Standalone test.wav")).is_file()
            for mode in ("classic", "random"):
                app.set_game_mode(mode)
                assert app.play_snippet()["ok"]
                app.stop_audio()
            report["checks"].extend(["bundled dependencies, FFmpeg, UI resources and updater helper", "external music folder and both local gameplay modes", "real pygame mixer initialization and decoded playback"])
            html = Path(resource_path("index.html")).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            assert 'cdn.tailwindcss.com' not in html and 'fonts.googleapis.com' not in html
            window = webview.create_window("GRAILD standalone check", resource_path("index.html"), js_api=app, hidden=True)
            app._window = window
            ui = {"passed": False}
            def loaded():
                try:
                    assert window.evaluate_js("typeof renderState === 'function' && typeof canaryRequest === 'function' && typeof updateNow === 'function'")
                    assert window.evaluate_js("(() => { const n = document.createElement('div'); n.className='hidden bg-purple-600'; document.body.append(n); const s=getComputedStyle(n); const ok=s.display==='none' && s.backgroundColor==='rgb(147, 51, 234)'; n.remove(); return ok && !document.querySelector('script[src^=https],link[href^=https]'); })()")
                    deadline = time.monotonic() + 20
                    while not window.evaluate_js("document.fonts.check('400 16px Inter') && document.fonts.status === 'loaded'"):
                        assert time.monotonic() < deadline, "Bundled font did not load"
                        time.sleep(0.1)
                    bridge = threading.Event()
                    def checked(value):
                        ui["bridge"] = bool(value); bridge.set()
                    window.evaluate_js("pywebview.api.get_account_state().then(s => s.authenticated)", callback=checked)
                    assert bridge.wait(15) and ui.get("bridge"), "Native Python bridge failed"
                    ui["passed"] = True
                except Exception:
                    ui["error"] = traceback.format_exc()
                finally:
                    window.destroy()
            window.events.loaded += loaded
            timer=threading.Timer(60, window.destroy);timer.daemon=True;timer.start()
            try: webview.start(gui="edgechromium", icon=resource_path('assets/graild.ico'))
            finally: timer.cancel()
            assert ui["passed"], ui
            report["checks"].append("native WebView2, Python bridge, local CSS and Inter font without CDN")
            report["project_reads_denied"] = bool(forbidden)
            app.stop_audio(); pygame.mixer.music.unload(); pygame.mixer.quit()
        report["passed"] = True
    except Exception:
        report["error"] = traceback.format_exc()
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return 0 if report["passed"] else 1

test_account_tags.py

import json
import tempfile
import unittest
import urllib.error
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import Mock
from achievements import ACHIEVEMENTS
from web_app import JuiceSpotApp


class AccountTagTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.app = JuiceSpotApp.__new__(JuiceSpotApp)
        self.app.account_db_path = str(Path(temporary.name) / 'accounts.sqlite3')
        self.app.initialize_account_store()
        self.app.current_user_id = 'local-owner'
        self.app.get_discord_broker_url = lambda: 'https://broker.example.test'
        self.app.protect_session_data = lambda data, decrypt=False: data[::-1]
        with self.app.account_connection() as db:
            for user in ('local-owner', 'other'):
                db.execute('INSERT INTO accounts (user_id, username, username_key, password_salt, password_hash, '
                           'password_iterations, display_name, avatar, created_at, is_creator) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
                           (user, user, user, b'salt', b'hash', 1, user, '*', '2026-11-02', 1))
        self.response = {'success': True, 'provider': 'discord', 'player_id': 'a' * 64,
                         'tags': [{'id': 'beta_tester', 'awarded_at': 123}], 'can_manage_tags': False}
        self.app.oauth_json_request = Mock(side_effect=lambda *args, **kwargs: dict(self.response))

    def connect(self, user='local-owner'):
        self.app.save_global_identity(user, {'global_token': 'verified-session'})

    def profile(self):
        return self.app.get_account_state()['profile']

    def test_local_owner_and_local_tags_are_not_global_grants(self):
        with self.app.account_connection() as db:
            db.execute("INSERT INTO account_tags (user_id, tag_id, awarded_at) VALUES ('local-owner', 'beta_tester', 'old')")
        self.assertFalse(self.profile()['is_creator'])
        self.assertEqual(self.profile()['tags'], [])
        self.assertFalse(self.app.get_tag_accounts()['success'])
        self.assertFalse(self.app.set_account_tag('a' * 64, 'beta_tester', True)['success'])
        self.app.oauth_json_request.assert_not_called()

    def test_verified_tags_cache_for_offline_display_without_tokens(self):
        self.connect()
        result = self.app.sync_global_tags()
        self.assertTrue(result['success'])
        self.assertEqual(result['tags'][0]['id'], 'beta_tester')
        self.assertIn('beta test', result['tags'][0]['description'])
        self.assertNotIn('verified-session', json.dumps(result))
        self.assertEqual(self.app.oauth_json_request.call_args.kwargs['headers']['Authorization'], 'Bearer verified-session')
        self.app.oauth_json_request.side_effect = OSError('offline')
        self.assertFalse(self.app.sync_global_tags()['success'])
        self.assertEqual(self.profile()['tags'][0]['id'], 'beta_tester')

    def test_cache_is_bound_to_local_account_broker_and_provider_session(self):
        self.connect()
        self.app.sync_global_tags()
        self.app.current_user_id = 'other'
        self.assertEqual(self.profile()['tags'], [])
        self.app.current_user_id = 'local-owner'
        self.app.save_global_identity('local-owner', {'global_token': 'different-provider-session'})
        self.assertEqual(self.profile()['tags'], [])
        self.app.sync_global_tags()
        self.app.get_discord_broker_url = lambda: 'https://different.example.test'
        self.assertEqual(self.profile()['tags'], [])
        self.assertFalse(self.app.sync_global_tags()['success'])

    def test_only_server_owner_permissions_enable_management(self):
        self.connect()
        self.response.update(can_manage_tags=True, tags=[{'id': 'owner'}])
        self.app.sync_global_tags()
        self.assertTrue(self.profile()['is_creator'])
        self.app.oauth_json_request.side_effect = urllib.error.HTTPError('https://broker.example.test', 403, 'denied', {}, None)
        self.assertFalse(self.app.set_account_tag('b' * 64, 'beta_tester', True)['success'])
        self.assertFalse(self.app.get_tag_accounts()['success'])

    def test_assignment_uses_global_id_and_validates_inputs(self):
        self.connect()
        self.assertTrue(self.app.set_account_tag('b' * 64, 'beta_tester', True)['success'])
        self.assertEqual(self.app.oauth_json_request.call_args.args[1],
                         {'player_id': 'b' * 64, 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'true'})
        for tag in ('owner', 'top_earner', 'unknown', []):
            self.assertFalse(self.app.set_account_tag('b' * 64, tag, True)['success'])
        self.assertFalse(self.app.set_account_tag('local-username', 'beta_tester', True)['success'])

    def test_discord_assignment_accepts_numeric_id_and_mentions(self):
        self.connect()
        for value in ('456789012345678901', '<@456789012345678901>', '<@!456789012345678901>'):
            self.assertTrue(self.app.set_discord_account_tag(value, 'beta_tester', True)['success'])
            self.assertEqual(self.app.oauth_json_request.call_args.args[1], {
                'discord_id': '456789012345678901', 'tag_id': 'beta_tester', 'assigned': 'true'})
        for value in ('username', '123', '<@456789012345678901', None):
            self.assertFalse(self.app.set_discord_account_tag(value, 'beta_tester', True)['success'])
        self.assertFalse(self.app.set_discord_account_tag('456789012345678901', 'top_earner', True)['success'])

    def test_custom_tags_are_created_assigned_and_cached_with_hover_descriptions(self):
        self.connect()
        tag_id = 'custom_' + 'a' * 24
        self.assertTrue(self.app.create_custom_tag('Contributor', 'Helped build the game')['success'])
        self.assertTrue(self.app.set_account_tag('b' * 64, tag_id, True)['success'])
        self.assertTrue(self.app.set_discord_account_tag('456789012345678901', tag_id, True)['success'])
        self.response['tags'] = [{'id':tag_id,'kind':'assigned','name':'Contributor',
                                  'description':'Helped build the game','awarded_at':123}]
        self.app.sync_global_tags()
        self.assertEqual(self.profile()['tags'][0]['description'], 'Helped build the game')
        self.assertFalse(self.app.create_custom_tag('', 'description')['success'])
        self.assertFalse(self.app.create_custom_tag('x'*33, 'description')['success'])

    def test_earned_sync_uses_real_unlocked_ids(self):
        self.connect()
        with self.app.account_connection() as db:
            for item in ACHIEVEMENTS:
                db.execute('INSERT INTO account_achievements VALUES (?, ?, ?)', ('local-owner', item['id'], 'earlier'))
        self.app.sync_global_tags()
        ids = json.loads(self.app.oauth_json_request.call_args.args[1]['achievement_ids'])
        self.assertEqual(set(ids), {item['id'] for item in ACHIEVEMENTS})
        self.assertEqual(len(ids), 50)

    def test_switch_during_sync_does_not_apply_another_accounts_tags(self):
        self.connect()
        def switch(*args, **kwargs):
            self.app.current_user_id = 'other'
            return self.response
        self.app.oauth_json_request.side_effect = switch
        self.assertFalse(self.app.sync_global_tags()['success'])
        self.assertEqual(self.profile()['tags'], [])


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_account_tags_ui.py
================================================================================
"""Exercise tag rendering and owner controls in the actual desktop HTML."""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest


class AccountTagUITests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_profile_tags_tooltips_and_owner_controls(self):
        edge = Path(r'C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe')
        if not edge.is_file():
            self.skipTest('Microsoft Edge is required for the browser check')
        fixture = r'''<script>
        (async () => { try {
            const assert = (value, message) => { if (!value) throw Error(message); };
            const logo = document.getElementById('app-logo');
            await logo.decode();
            assert(logo.naturalWidth===256 && logo.alt.includes('logo'),'App logo failed to load');
            const profile = {username:'owner', display_name:'Owner', avatar:'*',
                created_at:'2026-11-02', linked_providers:[], is_creator:true,
                tags:[{id:'beta_tester',name:'Beta Tester',kind:'assigned',description:'Awarded for beta testing.'}]};
            const account = {authenticated:true, profile,
                achievements:{entries:[],unlocked:49,total:50}};
            let assigned = false;
            let customTag = null;
            window.pywebview = {api:{
                get_tag_catalog:async()=>({success:true,entries:[{id:'beta_tester',name:'Beta Tester'},...(customTag?[customTag]:[])]}),
                create_custom_tag:async(name,description)=>{
                    customTag={id:'custom_'+'a'.repeat(24),name,description,kind:'assigned'};
                    return {success:true,message:'Custom tag created',tag:customTag};
                },
                sync_global_tags:async()=>({success:true,player_id:'a'.repeat(64),tags:profile.tags,can_manage_tags:profile.is_creator,message:'Tags synced'}),
                get_global_leaderboard:async()=>({success:true,message:'Global scores',entries:[{rank:1,display_name:'Tester',player_id:'b'.repeat(64),points:100,tags:assigned?[{id:'beta_tester',name:'Beta Tester',description:'Beta testing'}]:[]}]}),
                set_discord_account_tag:async(id,tag,value)=>{
                    assert(id==='456789012345678901' && (tag==='beta_tester'||tag===customTag?.id),'Discord assignment arguments');
                    assigned=value;return {success:true,message:'Discord tag saved'};
                },
                get_tag_accounts:async()=>({success:true,accounts:[{player_id:'b'.repeat(64),provider:'discord',display_name:'<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>',beta_tester:assigned}]}),
                set_account_tag:async(user, tag, value)=>{
                    assert(user==='b'.repeat(64) && (tag==='beta_tester'||tag===customTag?.id),'Assignment arguments');
                    assigned=value;return {success:true,message:value?'Tag assigned':'Tag removed'};
                },
                get_account_state:async()=>account
            }};
            renderAccountState(account);
            switchTab('account');
            await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
            const badge = document.querySelector('#profile-tags .profile-tag');
            assert(badge && badge.textContent.includes('Beta Tester'),'Assigned tag missing');
            const tooltip = document.getElementById(badge.getAttribute('aria-describedby'));
            assert(tooltip.textContent==='Awarded for beta testing.','Tooltip description');
            assert(getComputedStyle(tooltip).display==='none','Tooltip should start hidden');
            badge.focus();
            assert(getComputedStyle(tooltip).display!=='none','Keyboard focus should reveal tooltip');
            assert(!document.querySelector('#tag-account-select img'),'Account name rendered as HTML');
            assert(document.getElementById('top-earner-progress').textContent.includes('49/50'),'Locked progress');
            assert(!document.getElementById('tag-management').classList.contains('hidden'),'Owner controls missing');
            document.getElementById('guess-input').value = 'unfinished guess';
            document.getElementById('btn-game').click();
            assert(!document.getElementById('tab-game').classList.contains('hidden'),'Game navigation missing');
            assert(document.getElementById('btn-game').classList.contains('bg-purple-600'),'Game tab not active');
            assert(!document.getElementById('btn-hub').classList.contains('bg-purple-600'),'Hub should not remain active');
            switchTab('catalog');
            document.getElementById('btn-game').click();
            assert(document.getElementById('guess-input').value==='unfinished guess','Navigation reset current input');
            const gameState = {authenticated:true,has_track:true,game_mode:'classic',difficulty:'Easy',
                durations:[1,2,4,7,11,16],attempt:2,score:0,streak:0,correct_guesses:0,wrong_guesses:0,
                game_over:false,track_count:2,eligible_track_count:2,recording_eras:[],recording_era:'',
                library_source:'local',folder:'Test',volume:70};
            let modeChanges = 0;
            window.pywebview.api.set_game_mode = async mode => {
                modeChanges++; gameState.game_mode=mode; gameState.attempt=0; return gameState;
            };
            renderState(gameState);
            assert(!document.getElementById('game-mode-select'),'Mode picker should not be inside game');
            assert(document.getElementById('nav-mode-classic').getAttribute('aria-checked')==='true','Menu initial mode');

            assert(!document.getElementById('game-mode-menu').hidden,'Hover should open Game menu');

            assert(document.getElementById('game-mode-title').textContent==='Random Drop','Mode heading');
            assert(document.getElementById('guess-input').value==='','Old guess not cleared');
            await chooseNavGameMode('random');
            assert(modeChanges===1,'Same mode restarted game');
            await chooseNavGameMode('classic');
            assert(modeChanges===2 && document.getElementById('current-attempt').textContent==='1','Switch back did not reset');
            window.pywebview.api.set_game_mode = async () => { throw Error('Test failure'); };
            await chooseNavGameMode('random');

            assert(document.activeElement.id==='nav-mode-classic','Keyboard should enter menu');
            document.activeElement.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown',{key:'Escape',bubbles:true}));
            assert(document.getElementById('game-mode-menu').hidden,'Escape should close menu');
            switchTab('account');
            await assignTesterTag(true);
            await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
            assert(assigned,'Assign action');
            assert(document.getElementById('tag-account-select').textContent.includes('Beta Tester'),'Assignment label');
            await assignTesterTag(false);
            await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
            assert(!assigned,'Remove action');
            document.getElementById('tag-discord-id').value='456789012345678901';
            await assignDiscordTesterTag(true);
            assert(assigned,'Assign by Discord action');
            await refreshGlobalLeaderboard();
            let tagButton=document.querySelector('#global-leaderboard button');
            assert(tagButton && tagButton.textContent==='Remove Beta Tester','Owner leaderboard controls missing');
            await assignLeaderboardTag(tagButton,'b'.repeat(64),false);
            assert(!assigned,'Leaderboard removal');
            tagButton=document.querySelector('#global-leaderboard button');
            assert(tagButton.textContent==='Assign Beta Tester','Leaderboard assignment control missing');
            document.getElementById('custom-tag-name').value='Contributor';
            document.getElementById('custom-tag-description').value='Helped improve the game';
            await createCustomTag();
            assert(document.getElementById('tag-definition-select').value===customTag.id,'New custom tag not selected');
            await assignDiscordTesterTag(true);
            await refreshGlobalLeaderboard();
            const selector=document.querySelector('#global-leaderboard select');
            assert(selector.value===customTag.id,'Custom leaderboard tag missing');
            await assignLeaderboardTag(document.querySelector('#global-leaderboard button'),'b'.repeat(64),false,customTag.id);
            renderProfileTags([customTag]);
            assert(document.querySelector('#profile-tags .tag-tooltip').textContent==='Helped improve the game','Custom hover description');
            profile.is_creator=false;
            await refreshGlobalLeaderboard();
            assert(!document.querySelector('#global-leaderboard button'),'Non-owner leaderboard controls visible');
            profile.tags=[{id:'top_earner',name:'Top earner',kind:'earned',description:'Unlocked all 50 achievements.'}];
            account.achievements.unlocked=50;
            renderAccountState(account);
            assert(document.getElementById('tag-management').classList.contains('hidden'),'Non-owner controls visible');
            assert(document.getElementById('top-earner-progress').textContent.includes('unlocked'),'Earned state');
            assert(document.querySelector('#profile-tags .profile-tag').textContent.includes('Top earner'),'Earned badge');
            renderAccountState({authenticated:false});
            assert(!document.getElementById('profile-tags').children.length,'Tags leaked on sign-out');
            assert(!document.getElementById('tag-account-select').children.length,'Accounts leaked on sign-out');
            document.body.setAttribute('data-tag-test','passed');
        } catch(error) { document.body.setAttribute('data-tag-test','failed: '+error.message); }})();
        </script>'''
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='graild-tag-ui-') as temporary:
            folder = Path(temporary)
            html = Path('index.html').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
            html = html.replace('<head>', '<head><base href="' + Path.cwd().as_uri() + '/">', 1)
            page = folder / 'preview.html'
            page.write_text(html.replace('</body>', fixture + '</body>'), encoding='utf-8')
            result = subprocess.run([
                str(edge), '--headless', '--disable-gpu', '--in-process-gpu', '--no-first-run',
                '--user-data-dir=' + str(folder / 'profile'), '--virtual-time-budget=3000',
                '--dump-dom', page.as_uri()], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding='utf-8',
                errors='replace', timeout=40)
            match = re.search(r'data-tag-test="([^"]*)"', result.stdout)
            self.assertTrue(match and match[1] == 'passed', match[1] if match else result.stderr[-1000:])


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_achievements.py
================================================================================
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

from achievements import ACHIEVEMENTS
from web_app import JuiceSpotApp


class AchievementTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.app = JuiceSpotApp.__new__(JuiceSpotApp)
        self.app.account_db_path = str(Path(temporary.name) / "accounts.sqlite3")
        self.app.initialize_account_store()
        self.app.current_user_id = "a"
        self.app.current_track = "Test Song.mp3"
        self.app.current_difficulty = "Hard"
        self.app.attempt = 0
        self.app.streak = 1
        for user in ("a", "b"):
            with self.app.account_connection() as db:
                db.execute("INSERT INTO accounts (user_id, username, username_key, password_salt, password_hash, password_iterations, display_name, avatar, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                           (user, user, user, b"salt", b"hash", 1, user, "*", "2026-10-30T00:00:00+00:00"))

    def entries(self):
        return {item["id"]: item for item in self.app.get_achievements()["entries"]}

    def test_catalog_has_fifty_unique_achievements(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(ACHIEVEMENTS), 50)
        self.assertEqual(len({item["id"] for item in ACHIEVEMENTS}), 50)
        self.assertTrue(all(item["description"] and item["target"] > 0 for item in ACHIEVEMENTS))

    def test_first_hard_win_unlocks_correct_collection_and_special_badges(self):
        self.assertIsNone(self.app.record_song_guess(True, 30))
        entries = self.entries()
        for key in ("correct_1", "grails_1", "first_try_1", "hard_1"):
            self.assertTrue(entries[key]["unlocked"], key)
        self.assertFalse(entries["points_100"]["unlocked"])
        self.assertEqual(entries["points_100"]["progress"], 30)

    def test_wrong_guess_and_later_attempt_do_not_count_as_first_try(self):
        self.app.record_song_guess(False)
        self.app.attempt = 1
        self.app.record_song_guess(True, 20)
        self.assertFalse(self.entries()["first_try_1"]["unlocked"])
        self.assertTrue(self.entries()["hard_1"]["unlocked"])

    def test_easy_win_does_not_count_as_hard_and_grails_are_unique(self):
        self.app.current_difficulty = "Easy"
        self.app.record_song_guess(True, 60)
        self.app.record_song_guess(True, 60)
        entries = self.entries()
        self.assertFalse(entries["hard_1"]["unlocked"])
        self.assertEqual(entries["grails_5"]["progress"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(entries["first_try_10"]["progress"], 2)

    def test_existing_stats_unlock_and_dates_are_permanent(self):
        with self.app.account_connection() as db:
            db.execute("UPDATE accounts SET total_correct = 100, total_points = 1000, best_streak = 10, songs_played = 100 WHERE user_id = ?", ("a",))
        with patch.object(self.app, "utc_now", return_value="2026-10-30T12:00:00+00:00"):
            before = self.entries()
        self.assertTrue(before["correct_100"]["unlocked"])
        self.assertTrue(before["streak_10"]["unlocked"])
        self.assertFalse(before["first_try_1"]["unlocked"])
        with self.app.account_connection() as db:
            db.execute("UPDATE accounts SET best_streak = 0 WHERE user_id = ?", ("a",))
        after = self.entries()
        self.assertTrue(after["streak_10"]["unlocked"])
        self.assertEqual(after["streak_10"]["unlocked_at"], before["streak_10"]["unlocked_at"])
        self.assertEqual(after["streak_10"]["progress"], 10)

    def test_accounts_and_signed_out_progress_are_isolated(self):
        self.app.record_song_guess(True, 30)
        self.assertGreater(self.app.get_achievements()["unlocked"], 0)
        self.app.current_user_id = "b"
        self.assertEqual(self.app.get_achievements()["unlocked"], 0)
        self.app.current_user_id = None
        self.assertEqual(self.app.get_achievements()["entries"], [])

    def test_provider_badges_require_linked_identity(self):
        with self.app.account_connection() as db:
            db.execute("INSERT INTO external_identities VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                       ("discord", "123", "a", None, "2026-10-30"))
        entries = self.entries()
        self.assertTrue(entries["linked_discord"]["unlocked"])
        self.assertFalse(entries["linked_google"]["unlocked"])

    def test_schema_upgrade_preserves_existing_progress(self):
        self.app.record_song_guess(True, 30)
        self.app.initialize_account_store()
        self.assertTrue(self.entries()["first_try_1"]["unlocked"])
        with self.app.account_connection() as db:
            self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT total_points FROM accounts WHERE user_id = 'a'").fetchone()[0], 30)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_audio_files.py
================================================================================
import os
os.environ["SDL_AUDIODRIVER"] = "dummy"

from pathlib import Path
import struct
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
import wave
from unittest.mock import patch

import imageio_ffmpeg
import pygame
from audio_files import get_playback_path, is_audio_file
from web_app import JuiceSpotApp


class AudioFormatTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        cls.addClassCleanup(cls.temporary.cleanup)
        cls.folder = Path(cls.temporary.name)
        cls.source = cls.folder / "source.wav"
        with wave.open(str(cls.source), "wb") as audio:
            audio.setnchannels(1)
            audio.setsampwidth(2)
            audio.setframerate(44100)
            audio.writeframes(b"".join(struct.pack("<h", (i % 100) * 200) for i in range(22050)))
        cls.formats = {"mp3": "libmp3lame", "m4a": "aac", "aac": "aac", "flac": "flac",
                       "opus": "libopus", "wma": "wmav2", "aiff": "pcm_s16be", "ogg": "libvorbis"}
        for extension, codec in cls.formats.items():
            subprocess.run([imageio_ffmpeg.get_ffmpeg_exe(), "-nostdin", "-v", "error", "-y",
                            "-i", str(cls.source), "-c:a", codec, str(cls.folder / ("song." + extension))],
                           check=True, capture_output=True, creationflags=getattr(subprocess, "CREATE_NO_WINDOW", 0))

    def setUp(self):
        self.cache = self.folder / self.id().split(".")[-1]

    def test_real_audio_formats_decode_to_playable_pcm(self):
        for extension in ["wav", *self.formats]:
            with self.subTest(extension=extension):
                source = self.source if extension == "wav" else self.folder / ("song." + extension)
                self.assertTrue(is_audio_file(source))
                normalized = get_playback_path(source, self.cache)
                with wave.open(normalized) as audio:
                    self.assertEqual(audio.getnchannels(), 2)
                    self.assertEqual(audio.getframerate(), 44100)
                    self.assertEqual(audio.getsampwidth(), 2)
                    self.assertGreater(audio.getnframes(), 0)

    def test_repeat_playback_uses_cache_and_source_changes_invalidate_it(self):
        source = self.folder / "change.wav"
        source.write_bytes(self.source.read_bytes())
        first = get_playback_path(source, self.cache)
        with patch("audio_files.subprocess.run") as decoder:
            self.assertEqual(get_playback_path(source, self.cache), first)
            decoder.assert_not_called()
        stamp = source.stat().st_mtime_ns
        os.utime(source, ns=(stamp + 1000000000, stamp + 1000000000))
        self.assertNotEqual(get_playback_path(source, self.cache), first)

    def test_invalid_files_fail_without_leaving_partial_cache(self):
        corrupt = self.folder / "broken.m4a"
        corrupt.write_text("not audio", encoding="utf-8")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "could not be decoded"):
            get_playback_path(corrupt, self.cache)
        self.assertEqual(list(self.cache.iterdir()), [])
        self.assertFalse(is_audio_file(self.folder))
        self.assertFalse(is_audio_file(self.folder / "notes.txt"))

    def test_known_audio_extensions_skip_mime_lookup(self):
        with patch("audio_files.mimetypes.guess_type", side_effect=AssertionError("MIME lookup")):
            self.assertTrue(is_audio_file(self.source))
            self.assertFalse(is_audio_file(self.folder / "missing.mp3"))
            self.assertFalse(is_audio_file(self.folder))

    def test_directory_scan_uses_entry_metadata_and_preserves_mime_fallback(self):
        with os.scandir(self.folder) as entries:
            entry = next(item for item in entries if item.name == self.source.name)
            with patch("audio_files.Path.is_file", side_effect=AssertionError("Extra stat")):
                self.assertTrue(is_audio_file(entry.path, entry=entry))
        unusual = self.folder / "song.customaudio"
        unusual.write_bytes(b"audio")
        with patch("audio_files.mimetypes.guess_type", return_value=("audio/custom", None)):
            self.assertTrue(is_audio_file(unusual))

    def test_m4a_plays_in_both_modes_and_answer_reveal(self):
        pygame.mixer.init(frequency=44100, size=-16, channels=2)
        self.addCleanup(pygame.mixer.quit)
        app = JuiceSpotApp.__new__(JuiceSpotApp)
        app.current_user_id = "player"
        app.current_track = "song.m4a"
        app.music_folder = str(self.folder)
        app.is_game_over = False
        app.audio_error = None
        app.preview_durations = [1, 2, 4]
        app.attempt = 0
        app.random_snippet = None
        app.snippet_buffer = None
        app.stop_timer = None
        with patch("web_app.get_playback_path", side_effect=lambda source: get_playback_path(source, self.cache)), patch("web_app.threading.Timer"):
            app.game_mode = "classic"
            self.assertTrue(app.play_snippet()["ok"])
            app.game_mode = "random"
            self.assertTrue(app.play_snippet()["ok"])
            self.assertIsNone(app.play_correct_answer())
        pygame.mixer.music.unload()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_brand_assets.py
================================================================================
"""Check generated icon containers without a runtime image-library dependency."""
from pathlib import Path
import struct
import unittest


class BrandAssetsTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_header_png_dimensions(self):
        data = Path('assets/graild-logo.png').read_bytes()
        self.assertEqual(data[:8], b'\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n')
        self.assertEqual(struct.unpack('>II', data[16:24]), (256, 256))

    def test_windows_icon_has_small_and_high_resolution_sizes(self):
        data = Path('assets/graild.ico').read_bytes()
        reserved, kind, count = struct.unpack('<HHH', data[:6])
        self.assertEqual((reserved, kind), (0, 1))
        sizes = set()
        for index in range(count):
            width, height, _, _, _, _, length, offset = struct.unpack('<BBBBHHII', data[6+16*index:22+16*index])
            sizes.add((width or 256, height or 256))
            self.assertLessEqual(offset + length, len(data))
        self.assertTrue({(16,16),(32,32),(48,48),(256,256)} <= sizes)

    def test_mac_icon_contains_retina_payloads(self):
        data = Path('assets/graild.icns').read_bytes()
        self.assertEqual(data[:4], b'icns')
        self.assertEqual(struct.unpack('>I', data[4:8])[0], len(data))
        chunks = set()
        offset = 8
        while offset < len(data):
            kind = data[offset:offset+4]
            size = struct.unpack('>I', data[offset+4:offset+8])[0]
            self.assertGreaterEqual(size, 8)
            chunks.add(kind)
            offset += size
        self.assertEqual(offset, len(data))
        self.assertIn(b'ic10', chunks)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_canary_auto_eras.py
================================================================================
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock

from canary_client import CanaryError
from canary_integration import CanaryIntegration, automatic_era, build_title_index
from web_app import JuiceSpotApp


class AutomaticEraTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.folder = Path(temporary.name)
        self.client = Mock()
        self.client.cached_recording_eras.return_value = []
        self.songs = [
            {"id": "rental", "title": "Rental", "era": "DRFL", "tracker": {"alt_names": ["Rent"]}},
            {"id": "rental-master", "title": "Rental (Master)", "era": "DRFL", "tracker": {"alt_names": ["Rental"]}},
            {"id": "session", "title": "Rental (Session Edit)", "era": "WOD"},
            {"id": "legends", "title": "Legends (Sessions)", "era": "WOD"},
            {"id": "conflict1", "title": "Shared Name", "era": "WOD"},
            {"id": "conflict2", "title": "Shared Name", "era": "DRFL"},
        ]
        self.client.get_song_index.return_value = {"songs": self.songs}
        self.service = CanaryIntegration(self.folder / "metadata", self.client)

    def files(self, *names):
        for name in names:
            (self.folder / name).write_bytes(b"local audio")
        return list(names)

    def scan(self, files, force=False):
        self.service.start_era_scan(self.folder, files, force=force)
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 3
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            status = self.service.era_scan_status(self.folder)
            if status["status"] not in ("queued", "running"):
                return status
            time.sleep(0.01)
        self.fail("Era scan did not finish")

    def test_exact_title_alias_and_version_preservation(self):
        index = build_title_index(self.songs)
        self.assertEqual(automatic_era("Juice WRLD - Rental.mp3", index)["era"], "DRFL")
        self.assertEqual(automatic_era("Rent.flac", index)["era"], "DRFL")
        self.assertEqual(automatic_era("Legends (Session Edit).mp3", index)["era"], "WOD")
        self.assertEqual(automatic_era("Rental (Session Edit).mp3", index)["era"], "WOD")
        self.assertEqual(automatic_era("Rental (Alt Version).mp3", index)["era"], "")
        self.assertEqual(automatic_era("Rental [WOD era].mp3", index)["era"], "")
        self.assertEqual(automatic_era("Shared Name.mp3", index)["status"], "needs_review")

    def test_numeric_song_titles_are_not_treated_as_track_numbers(self):
        index = build_title_index(self.songs + [{"id": "ten", "title": "10 Feet", "era": "DRFL"},
                                              {"id": "feet", "title": "Feet", "era": "WOD"}])
        self.assertEqual(automatic_era("10 Feet.mp3", index)["era"], "DRFL")
        self.assertEqual(automatic_era("Feet.mp3", index)["era"], "WOD")
        self.assertEqual(automatic_era("01 - Rental.mp3", index)["era"], "DRFL")

    def test_auto_check_does_not_link_recordings_and_drives_era_filter(self):
        files = self.files("Rental.mp3", "Shared Name.mp3", "Unknown.mp3")
        status = self.scan(files)
        self.assertEqual(status["status"], "complete")
        self.assertEqual(status["resolved"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(status["review"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.service.library_matches(self.folder, files), {})
        app = JuiceSpotApp.__new__(JuiceSpotApp)
        app._canary = self.service
        app.tracks = files
        app.music_folder = str(self.folder)
        app.recording_era = "DRFL"
        self.assertEqual(app.get_eligible_tracks(), ["Rental.mp3"])
        self.assertEqual(app.get_recording_eras(), ["DRFL"])

    def test_unchanged_files_use_saved_checks_and_replacements_are_rechecked(self):
        files = self.files("Rental.mp3")
        self.scan(files)
        self.scan(files)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get_song_index.call_count, 1)
        (self.folder / files[0]).write_bytes(b"changed audio file")
        self.assertEqual(self.service.library_era_checks(self.folder, files), {})
        self.scan(files)
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get_song_index.call_count, 2)

    def test_manual_confirmation_has_priority_and_offline_does_not_erase_era(self):
        files = self.files("Rental.mp3")
        self.scan(files)
        self.client.get_song_index.side_effect = CanaryError("offline")
        self.assertEqual(self.scan(files, force=True)["status"], "failed")
        self.assertEqual(self.service.library_era_checks(self.folder, files)["Rental.mp3"]["era"], "DRFL")
        self.client.get_song.return_value = {"id": "manual", "title": "Different recording", "era": "WOD"}
        self.client.get_cover.return_value = {"image": ""}
        self.service.confirm(self.folder / "Rental.mp3", "manual")
        self.assertEqual(self.scan(files, force=True)["status"], "complete")
        self.assertEqual(self.service.library_era_checks(self.folder, files)["Rental.mp3"]["era"], "WOD")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_canary_client.py
================================================================================
import json
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock

import requests
from canary_client import CanaryClient, CanaryError


class CanaryClientTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.session = Mock()
        self.client = CanaryClient(Path(temporary.name) / "cache.sqlite3", session=self.session, interval=0)

    def response(self, data, status=200, headers=None):
        response = Mock(status_code=status, headers=headers or {})
        response.json.return_value = data
        self.session.get.return_value = response
        return response

    def test_search_uses_documented_filters_and_caches(self):
        self.response({"songs": [{"id": "rental", "title": "Rental", "audio_url": "forbidden"}], "total": 1})
        first = self.client.search_songs("Rental", era="DRFL", category="Songs")
        params = self.session.get.call_args.kwargs["params"]
        self.assertEqual(params["q"], "Rental")
        self.assertEqual(params["era"], "DRFL")
        self.assertEqual(params["category"], "Songs")
        self.assertNotIn("audio_url", first["songs"][0])
        self.assertTrue(self.client.search_songs("Rental", era="DRFL", category="Songs")["_cached"])
        self.assertEqual(self.session.get.call_count, 1)

    def test_http_errors_are_friendly(self):
        for status in (400, 404, 429, 503):
            with self.subTest(status=status):
                self.client.cooldown = 0
                self.response({}, status=status)
                with self.assertRaises(CanaryError) as result:
                    self.client.get_song("song" + str(status))
                self.assertEqual(result.exception.status, status)

    def test_offline_expired_cache_is_available(self):
        self.response({"songs": [], "total": 0})
        self.client.search_songs("saved")
        with self.client._db() as db:
            db.execute("UPDATE api_cache SET saved = 0")
        self.session.get.side_effect = requests.ConnectionError("offline")
        self.assertTrue(self.client.search_songs("saved")["_stale"])

    def test_timeout_without_cache_and_rate_limit_cooldown(self):
        self.session.get.side_effect = requests.Timeout()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CanaryError, "timed out"):
            self.client.get_catalog()
        self.session.get.side_effect = None
        self.response({}, 429, {"Retry-After": "30"})
        with self.assertRaises(CanaryError):
            self.client.get_catalog()
        count = self.session.get.call_count
        with self.assertRaises(CanaryError):
            self.client.get_catalog()
        self.assertEqual(self.session.get.call_count, count)

    def test_random_song_uses_documented_metadata_pages_and_era(self):
        from unittest.mock import patch
        self.response({"songs": [{"id": "rental", "title": "Rental", "era": "DRFL", "page_url": "https://canary.best/song/rental"}], "total": 1})
        with patch("canary_client.random.randrange", return_value=0):
            result = self.client.random_song("DRFL")
        self.assertEqual(result["song"]["id"], "rental")
        self.assertEqual(self.session.get.call_args.kwargs["params"]["era"], "DRFL")
        self.assertTrue(self.session.get.call_args.args[0].endswith("/songs"))
        self.response({"songs": [], "total": 0})
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(CanaryError, "no songs"):
            self.client.random_song("EMPTY")

    def test_eras_are_derived_from_canary_metadata_and_cached(self):
        self.response({"songs": [{"id": "a", "title": "Title containing WOD", "era": "DRFL"},
                                  {"id": "b", "title": "Another", "era": "JUTE"}], "total": 2})
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get_recording_eras()["eras"], ["DRFL", "JUTE"])
        self.assertEqual(self.client.cached_recording_eras(), ["DRFL", "JUTE"])
        self.assertTrue(self.client.get_recording_eras()["_cached"])
        self.assertEqual(self.session.get.call_count, 1)

    def test_detail_and_cover_routes_are_metadata_only(self):
        self.response({"id": "rental", "title": "Rental", "tracker": {"alt_names": ["Alias"]}})
        self.assertEqual(self.client.get_song("rental")["tracker"]["alt_names"], ["Alias"])
        response = self.response({}, headers={"Content-Type": "image/png"})
        response.iter_content.return_value = [b"image"]
        self.assertTrue(self.client.get_cover("rental")["image"].startswith("data:image/png"))
        self.assertTrue(self.session.get.call_args.args[0].endswith("/songs/rental/cover"))
        with self.assertRaises(CanaryError):
            self.client.get_song("../private/audio")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_canary_matching.py
================================================================================
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock

from canary_integration import CanaryIntegration, rank_candidates, normalized_title


class CanaryMatchingTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.folder = Path(temporary.name)
        self.song = self.folder / "Rental (Session Edit).mp3"
        self.song.write_bytes(b"local audio")
        self.client = Mock()
        self.client.cached_recording_eras.return_value = []
        self.service = CanaryIntegration(self.folder / "metadata", self.client)
        self.records = [{"id": "rental", "title": "Rental", "era": "DRFL", "category": "Songs"},
                        {"id": "rental-session", "title": "Rental (Session Edit)", "era": "DRFL", "category": "Sessions"},
                        {"id": "alias", "title": "Other Title", "tracker": {"alt_names": ["Rental (Session Edit)"]}}]
        self.client.search_songs.return_value = {"songs": self.records}
        self.client.get_song.return_value = self.records[1]
        self.client.get_cover.return_value = {"image": "data:image/png;base64,aW1hZ2U="}

    def test_versions_stay_distinct_and_aliases_rank(self):
        ranked = rank_candidates(self.song.name, self.records)
        self.assertEqual(ranked[0]["id"], "rental-session")
        self.assertEqual(ranked[1]["id"], "alias")
        self.assertNotEqual(normalized_title("Rental"), normalized_title("Rental (Session Edit)"))

    def test_search_never_automatically_saves_a_match(self):
        result = self.service.local_info(self.song)
        self.assertFalse(result["confirmed"])
        self.assertIsNone(self.service.local_match(self.song))
        self.assertEqual(self.client.search_songs.call_args.args[0], "Rental")

    def test_confirmation_is_per_file_and_invalidated_by_replacement(self):
        self.service.confirm(self.song, "rental-session")
        self.assertEqual(self.service.local_match(self.song)["song"]["id"], "rental-session")
        other = self.folder / "Rental.mp3"
        other.write_bytes(b"another recording")
        self.assertIsNone(self.service.local_match(other))
        self.song.write_bytes(b"replacement recording")
        self.assertIsNone(self.service.local_match(self.song))

    def test_era_filter_uses_only_confirmed_local_files(self):
        from web_app import JuiceSpotApp
        app = JuiceSpotApp.__new__(JuiceSpotApp)
        app._canary = self.service
        app.account_db_path = str(self.folder / "accounts.sqlite3")
        app.initialize_account_store()
        with app.account_connection() as db:
            db.execute("INSERT INTO accounts (user_id, username, username_key, password_salt, password_hash, password_iterations, display_name, avatar, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)", ("player", "player", "player", b"s", b"h", 1, "Player", "*", "2026-10-30"))
        app.music_folder = str(self.folder)
        other = self.folder / "Other.mp3"
        other.write_bytes(b"another local song")
        app.tracks = [self.song.name, other.name]
        app.current_user_id = "player"
        app.current_track = other.name
        app.game_mode = "classic"
        app.current_difficulty = "Easy"
        app.recording_era = ""
        app.stop_audio = Mock()
        app.state = lambda: {"eligible": app.get_eligible_tracks()}
        self.service.confirm(self.song, "rental-session")
        self.assertFalse(app.set_recording_era("UNKNOWN")["success"])
        self.assertTrue(app.set_recording_era("DRFL")["success"])
        self.assertEqual(app.get_eligible_tracks(), [self.song.name])
        self.assertEqual(app.current_track, self.song.name)
        app.recording_era = ""
        app.load_game_preferences()
        self.assertEqual(app.recording_era, "DRFL")
        with app.account_connection() as db:
            db.execute("INSERT INTO accounts (user_id, username, username_key, password_salt, password_hash, password_iterations, display_name, avatar, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)", ("other", "other", "other", b"s", b"h", 1, "Other", "*", "2026-10-30"))
        app.current_user_id = "other"
        app.load_game_preferences()
        self.assertEqual(app.recording_era, "")
        app.current_user_id = "player"
        app.load_game_preferences()
        self.assertEqual(app.recording_era, "DRFL")
        self.assertFalse(app.reroll_song()["changed"])
        self.assertTrue(app.set_library_source("canary")["success"])
        self.assertEqual(app.get_eligible_tracks(), [])
        app.library_source = "local"
        app.load_game_preferences()
        self.assertEqual(app.library_source, "canary")
        self.assertEqual(app.recording_era, "DRFL")
        self.assertTrue(app.set_library_source("local")["success"])
        self.service.forget(self.song)
        app.next_round()
        self.assertIsNone(app.current_track)
        self.assertEqual(app.get_eligible_tracks(), [])
        app.set_recording_era("")
        self.assertEqual(len(app.get_eligible_tracks()), 2)

    def test_era_never_comes_from_local_filename(self):
        from web_app import JuiceSpotApp
        filename = self.folder / "Rental [WOD era].mp3"
        filename.write_bytes(b"local audio")
        self.service.confirm(filename, "rental-session")
        app = JuiceSpotApp.__new__(JuiceSpotApp)
        app._canary = self.service
        app.music_folder = str(self.folder)
        app.tracks = [filename.name]
        app.recording_era = "WOD"
        self.assertEqual(app.get_recording_eras(), ["DRFL"])
        self.assertEqual(app.get_eligible_tracks(), [])
        app.recording_era = "DRFL"
        self.assertEqual(app.get_eligible_tracks(), [filename.name])

    def test_network_failure_keeps_confirmed_metadata(self):
        from canary_client import CanaryError
        self.service.confirm(self.song, "rental-session")
        self.client.get_song.side_effect = CanaryError("offline")
        saved = self.service.local_info(self.song)
        self.assertTrue(saved["confirmed"])
        self.assertEqual(saved["song"]["id"], "rental-session")
        self.assertIn("offline", saved["notice"])

    def test_background_job_returns_immediately(self):
        def delayed(*args, **kwargs):
            time.sleep(0.2)
            return {"songs": []}
        self.client.search_songs.side_effect = delayed
        start = time.monotonic()
        job = self.service.start("search", query="Rental")
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - start, 0.1)
        while self.service.poll(job["job_id"])["status"] == "running":
            time.sleep(0.02)
        self.assertEqual(self.service.poll(job["job_id"])["status"], "complete")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
