"""Headless browser checks for the actual desktop HTML and Canary UI script."""
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import unittest


class CanaryUITests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_catalogue_matching_information_and_error_states(self):
        edge = Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe")
        if not edge.exists():
            self.skipTest("Microsoft Edge is required for the browser check")
        folder = Path("build/canary-ui-tests").resolve()
        folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copyfile("canary_ui.js", folder / "canary_ui.js")
        fixture = r"""<script>
(async () => {
try {
    const filename = 'Rental (Session Edit).mp3';
    const song = {id:'rental',title:'Rental',artist:'Juice WRLD',era:'DRFL',category:'Songs',duration_str:'4:13',page_url:'https://canary.best/song/rental',tracker:{producers:'Seezyn',record_date:'November 10, 2018',alt_names:['Alias']}};
    let confirmations = 0;
    let unavailable = false;
    const calls = [];
    const state = {authenticated:true,has_track:true,durations:[1,2,4,7,11,16],attempt:0,score:0,streak:0,correct_guesses:0,wrong_guesses:0,game_over:false,track_count:1,eligible_track_count:1,recording_eras:['DRFL'],recording_era:'',folder:'Test',difficulty:'Easy',game_mode:'classic',volume:70};
    window.pywebview = {api:{get_catalog:async()=>[{filename,title:'Rental (Session Edit)',format:'MP3',canary:confirmations ? song : null}],get_state:async()=>state,get_canary_era_scan:async()=>({status:'complete',resolved:1,total:1,review:0}),set_library_source:async source=>{state.library_source=source;return {success:true,state};},open_canary_page:async url=>{window.openedCanaryPage=url;return {success:true};}}};
    canaryRequest = async (operation, options={}) => {
        calls.push({operation, options});
        if (unavailable && operation === 'search') throw Error('Canary is offline');
        if (operation === 'eras') return {eras:['DRFL','WOD']};
        if (operation === 'random') return {song};
        if (operation === 'catalog') return {categories:['Songs','Sessions','Masters']};
        if (operation === 'search') return {songs:options.page ? [{...song,title:'Second page'}] : [song],total:21};
        if (operation === 'local') return {confirmed:false,candidates:[{...song,match_reason:'Verify this recording version.'}],message:'Choose a recording explicitly.'};
        if (operation === 'confirm') { confirmations++; return {song,artwork:'',confirmed:true}; }
        if (operation === 'info') return {song,artwork:''};
    };
    renderState(state);
    await loadCanaryManifest();
    document.getElementById('canary-search').value = 'Rental';
    document.getElementById('canary-era').value = 'DRFL';
    document.getElementById('canary-category').value = 'Sessions';
    await searchCanary(0);
    const query = calls.filter(call=>call.operation==='search').at(-1).options;
    if (query.query !== 'Rental' || query.era !== 'DRFL' || query.category !== 'Sessions') throw Error('Search filters');
    if (document.getElementById('canary-next').disabled) throw Error('Next page disabled');
    await searchCanary(1);
    if (!document.getElementById('canary-results').textContent.includes('Second page')) throw Error('Pagination');
    await openLocalCanary(filename);
    if (confirmations !== 0) throw Error('Automatic matching');
    if (!document.getElementById('song-info-content').textContent.includes('Verify this recording')) throw Error('Uncertain match warning');
    await openCanarySong('rental',filename);
    const details = document.getElementById('song-info-content').textContent;
    if (!details.includes('Seezyn') || !details.includes('November 10, 2018') || !details.includes('4:13')) throw Error('Information fields');
    const button = [...document.querySelectorAll('#song-info-content button')].find(item=>item.textContent==='Confirm this recording');
    await confirmLocalCanary(button,filename,'rental');
    if (confirmations !== 1) throw Error('Explicit confirmation');
    if (!document.getElementById('catalog-grid').textContent.includes('Canary: Rental')) throw Error('Confirmed metadata card');
    if (!document.querySelector('#recording-era-select option[value="DRFL"]')) throw Error('Local era filter');
    await refreshCanaryEraList();
    await changeLibrarySource('canary');
    if (document.getElementById('hub-library-source').value !== 'canary' || document.getElementById('account-library-source').value !== 'canary') throw Error('Saved source synchronization');
    await playRandomCanarySong();
    if (window.openedCanaryPage !== 'https://canary.best/song/rental') throw Error('Canary public page launcher');
    if (!document.getElementById('canary-random-status').textContent.includes('Press Play there')) throw Error('Website playback instructions');
    await changeLibrarySource('local');
    await watchAutomaticEraScan();
    if (!document.getElementById('automatic-era-status').textContent.includes('1 / 1 songs have Canary eras')) throw Error('Automatic scan progress');
    unavailable = true;
    await searchCanary(0);
    if (!document.getElementById('canary-browser-status').textContent.includes('offline')) throw Error('Offline message');
    document.body.setAttribute('data-canary-test','passed');
} catch(error) { document.body.setAttribute('data-canary-test','failed: '+error.message); }
})();
</script>"""
        html = Path("index.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        html = re.sub(r'<script src="https://[^"]+"></script>', '', html)
        html = re.sub(r'<link href="https://[^>]+>', '', html)
        preview = folder / "preview.html"
        preview.write_text(html.replace("</body>", fixture + "</body>"), encoding="utf-8")
        result = subprocess.run([str(edge), "--headless", "--disable-gpu", "--no-first-run", "--user-data-dir=" + str(folder / "browser-profile"), "--virtual-time-budget=1000", "--dump-dom", preview.as_uri()], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace", timeout=40)
        (folder / "rendered.html").write_text(result.stdout, encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertIn('data-canary-test="passed"', result.stdout)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_desktop_entry.py
================================================================================
import ast
from pathlib import Path
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch


ENTRY = Path(__file__).with_name("JuiceGuesserMain.py")


class DesktopEntryTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_entry_contains_only_runtime_dispatch(self):
        source = ENTRY.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        tree = ast.parse(source)
        imports = [node for node in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(node, (ast.Import, ast.ImportFrom))]
        imported = {alias.name for node in imports for alias in node.names}

        self.assertNotIn("tkinter", imported)
        self.assertNotIn("pygame", imported)
        self.assertNotIn("Spotify local Files", source)
        self.assertFalse(any(isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef) for node in tree.body))

    def test_explicit_beta_diagnostic_dispatches_without_test_environment(self):
        import JuiceGuesserMain

        with patch.object(JuiceGuesserMain.sys, "argv", [str(ENTRY), "--beta-update-smoke-test", "context.json"]), \
             patch.dict("os.environ", {}, clear=True), \
             patch("beta_update_smoke.run", return_value=7) as run:
            self.assertEqual(JuiceGuesserMain.main(), 7)
        run.assert_called_once_with("context.json")

    def test_post_update_sets_health_handoff_then_starts_app(self):
        import JuiceGuesserMain

        with patch.object(JuiceGuesserMain.sys, "argv", [str(ENTRY), "--post-update", "job", "token"]), \
             patch("update_health.set_handoff") as set_handoff, \
             patch("web_app.main") as run_app:
            self.assertEqual(JuiceGuesserMain.main(), 0)
        set_handoff.assert_called_once_with("job", "token")
        run_app.assert_called_once_with()

    def test_beta_environment_alone_does_not_switch_startup_mode(self):
        import JuiceGuesserMain

        with patch.object(JuiceGuesserMain.sys, "argv", [str(ENTRY)]), \
             patch.dict("os.environ", {"GRAILD_BETA_TEST_CONTEXT": "context.json"}), \
             patch("web_app.main") as run_app:
            self.assertEqual(JuiceGuesserMain.main(), 0)
        run_app.assert_called_once_with()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_game_modes.py
================================================================================
import os
os.environ["SDL_AUDIODRIVER"] = "dummy"

import struct
import tempfile
import unittest
import wave
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import pygame
from random_snippets import RandomSnippet
from web_app import JuiceSpotApp


class GameModeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        pygame.mixer.init(frequency=8000, size=-16, channels=1)

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls):
        pygame.mixer.quit()

    def setUp(self):
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.addCleanup(pygame.mixer.music.unload)
        playback = patch("web_app.get_playback_path", side_effect=lambda path: path)
        playback.start()
        self.addCleanup(playback.stop)
        self.folder = Path(temporary.name)
        self.song = self.folder / "song.wav"
        with wave.open(str(self.song), "wb") as audio:
            audio.setnchannels(1)
            audio.setsampwidth(2)
            audio.setframerate(8000)
            audio.writeframes(b"".join(struct.pack("<h", i % 30000) for i in range(30 * 8000)))

    def app(self):
        app = JuiceSpotApp.__new__(JuiceSpotApp)
        app.current_user_id = "player"
        app.current_track = "song.wav"
        app.tracks = ["song.wav", "other.wav"]
        app.game_mode = "classic"
        app.music_folder = str(self.folder)
        app.preview_durations = [1, 2, 4, 7, 11, 16]
        app.attempt = 0
        app.is_game_over = False
        app.audio_error = None
        app.random_snippet = None
        app.snippet_buffer = None
        app.stop_timer = None
        app.state = lambda: {"game_mode": app.game_mode}
        return app

    def test_random_clip_starts_at_chosen_offset_and_expands_from_same_point(self):
        with patch("random_snippets.random.randint", return_value=16000):
            snippet = RandomSnippet(str(self.song), 16)
        short, duration = snippet.clip(1)
        long, _ = snippet.clip(4)
        with wave.open(short) as audio:
            self.assertEqual(audio.getnframes(), 8000)
            short_pcm = audio.readframes(8000)
        with wave.open(long) as audio:
            self.assertEqual(audio.getnframes(), 32000)
            self.assertEqual(audio.readframes(8000), short_pcm)
        self.assertEqual(short_pcm, snippet.pcm[16000 * 2:24000 * 2])
        self.assertEqual(duration, 1)

    def test_short_song_clips_at_end_without_empty_audio(self):
        snippet = RandomSnippet(str(self.song), 60)
        buffer, duration = snippet.clip(60)
        self.assertEqual(duration, 30)
        self.assertEqual(snippet.start_frame, 0)
        with wave.open(buffer) as audio:
            self.assertEqual(audio.getnframes(), 30 * 8000)

    def test_saved_random_start_replays_identical_audio(self):
        original = RandomSnippet(str(self.song), 16, start_frame=16000)
        restored = RandomSnippet(str(self.song), 16, start_frame=original.start_frame)
        before, _ = original.clip(2)
        after, _ = restored.clip(2)
        self.assertEqual(before.read(), after.read())
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            RandomSnippet(str(self.song), 16, start_frame=999999999)

    def test_mode_change_and_reroll_reset_random_start(self):
        app = self.app()
        app.random_snippet = object()
        self.assertEqual(app.set_game_mode("random")["game_mode"], "random")
        self.assertIsNone(app.random_snippet)
        app.random_snippet = object()
        previous = app.current_track
        app.reroll_song()
        self.assertIsNone(app.random_snippet)
        self.assertNotEqual(app.current_track, previous)

    def test_invalid_or_signed_out_mode_changes_are_ignored(self):
        app = self.app()
        app.set_game_mode("unknown")
        self.assertEqual(app.game_mode, "classic")
        app.current_user_id = None
        app.set_game_mode("random")
        self.assertEqual(app.game_mode, "classic")

    def test_random_mode_replay_reuses_decoded_audio_and_valid_wav_playback(self):
        app = self.app()
        app.game_mode = "random"
        with patch("web_app.threading.Timer") as timer:
            self.assertTrue(app.play_snippet()["ok"])
            decoded = app.random_snippet
            app.attempt = 1
            self.assertEqual(app.play_snippet()["duration"], 2)
            self.assertIs(app.random_snippet, decoded)
            self.assertEqual(timer.call_args.args[0], 2)
        pygame.mixer.music.stop()

    def test_classic_mode_keeps_intro_playback(self):
        app = self.app()
        with patch("web_app.RandomSnippet") as decoder, patch("web_app.threading.Timer"):
            self.assertTrue(app.play_snippet()["ok"])
            decoder.assert_not_called()
        pygame.mixer.music.stop()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_global_leaderboard.py
================================================================================
import ast
import base64
import json
import sqlite3
import urllib.error
import unittest
from contextlib import contextmanager
from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory

# Exercise bridge methods without starting pygame, a webview, or real account storage.
source = ast.parse(Path('web_app.py').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
cls = next(n for n in source.body if isinstance(n, ast.ClassDef) and n.name == 'JuiceSpotApp')
methods = [n for n in cls.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name in ('save_global_identity', 'get_global_leaderboard')]
scope = dict(base64=base64, json=json, sqlite3=sqlite3, urllib=__import__('urllib'))
exec(compile(ast.fix_missing_locations(ast.Module(body=[ast.ClassDef(name='Bridge', bases=[], keywords=[], body=methods, decorator_list=[])], type_ignores=[])), 'web_app.py', 'exec'), scope)
Bridge = scope['Bridge']

class LeaderboardTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.path = str(Path(self.temp.name) / 'accounts.sqlite3')
        self.app = Bridge()
        self.app.current_user_id = 'player'
        @contextmanager
        def connection():
            db = sqlite3.connect(self.path)
            db.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            try:
                yield db
                db.commit()
            finally:
                db.close()
        self.app.account_connection = connection
        self.app.get_discord_broker_url = lambda: 'https://example.test'
        self.app.protect_session_data = lambda data, decrypt=False: data[::-1]
        with connection() as db:
            db.executescript("CREATE TABLE app_meta (key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT); CREATE TABLE accounts (user_id TEXT, display_name TEXT, total_points INTEGER, total_correct INTEGER, total_wrong INTEGER); INSERT INTO accounts VALUES ('player', 'Player', 120, 3, 2);")
        self.calls = []
        def request(url, payload=None, headers=None):
            self.calls.append((url, payload, headers))
            return {'entries': [{'rank': 1, 'display_name': 'Player', 'points': 120}]}
        self.app.oauth_json_request = request

    def test_verified_score_sync_and_private_credentials(self):
        self.app.save_global_identity('player', {'global_token': 'secret'})
        result = self.app.get_global_leaderboard()
        self.assertTrue(result['synced'])
        self.assertEqual(self.calls[0][1]['points'], 120)
        self.assertEqual(self.calls[0][2]['Authorization'], 'Bearer secret')
        self.assertNotIn('secret', json.dumps(result))

    def test_guest_only_reads_public_board(self):
        self.app.current_user_id = None
        self.assertTrue(self.app.get_global_leaderboard()['success'])
        self.assertEqual(len(self.calls), 1)
        self.assertIsNone(self.calls[0][1])

    def test_expired_token_still_loads_board(self):
        self.app.save_global_identity('player', {'global_token': 'expired'})
        original = self.app.oauth_json_request
        def request(url, payload=None, headers=None):
            if payload:
                raise urllib.error.HTTPError(url, 401, 'expired', {}, None)
            return original(url)
        self.app.oauth_json_request = request
        result = self.app.get_global_leaderboard()
        self.assertTrue(result['success'])
        self.assertFalse(result['synced'])
        self.assertIn('Reconnect', result['message'])

    def test_other_account_cannot_use_saved_token(self):
        self.app.save_global_identity('other-player', {'global_token': 'secret'})
        self.assertFalse(self.app.get_global_leaderboard()['synced'])
        self.assertEqual(len(self.calls), 1)

    def test_damaged_credential_does_not_hide_public_board(self):
        with self.app.account_connection() as db:
            db.execute("INSERT INTO app_meta VALUES (?, ?)",
                       ("global_identity:player", '{"broker":"https://example.test"}'))
        result = self.app.get_global_leaderboard()
        self.assertTrue(result['success'])
        self.assertFalse(result['synced'])
        self.assertIn('Reconnect', result['message'])

    def test_old_server_reports_missing_deployment(self):
        def request(*args, **kwargs):
            raise urllib.error.HTTPError(args[0], 404, 'not found', {}, None)
        self.app.oauth_json_request = request
        result = self.app.get_global_leaderboard()
        self.assertFalse(result['success'])
        self.assertIn('deploy', result['message'])

    def test_service_failure_is_recoverable(self):
        self.app.oauth_json_request = lambda *args, **kwargs: (_ for _ in ()).throw(OSError('offline'))
        self.assertFalse(self.app.get_global_leaderboard()['success'])

if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_hotfix_builds.py
================================================================================
"""Build metadata must not weaken legacy updater version or signature checks."""
import unittest

from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import Ed25519PrivateKey

from app_version import APP_VERSION
from test_updater import public_keys, resign, signed_release
from update_security import UpdateError, verify_manifest


class HotfixBuildTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.private = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
        self.keys = public_keys(self.private)

    def envelope(self, version, architecture):
        manifest = signed_release(self.private, version=version)['manifest']
        if architecture:
            manifest.update(platform='macos', architecture=architecture,
                            asset=f'GRAILD-{version}-macOS-{architecture}.zip',
                            bundle_id='com.graild.desktop', bundle_sha256='0' * 64)
        return resign(self.private, manifest)

    def verify(self, envelope, current, architecture):
        return verify_manifest(envelope, self.keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', current,
                               platform='macos' if architecture else 'windows',
                               architecture=architecture)

    def test_public_and_internal_identity(self):
        self.assertEqual(APP_VERSION, '0.9.2')

    def test_upgrade_matrix_on_all_platforms(self):
        for architecture in (None, 'x86_64', 'arm64'):
            newer = self.envelope('0.9.2', architecture)
            self.assertEqual(self.verify(newer, '0.9.1', architecture)['version'], '0.9.2')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(UpdateError, 'not newer'):
                self.verify(self.envelope('0.9.1', architecture), '0.9.2', architecture)

    def test_build_metadata_does_not_change_semantic_precedence(self):
        for architecture in (None, 'x86_64', 'arm64'):
            envelope = self.envelope('0.9.2+9011', architecture)
            self.assertEqual(self.verify(envelope, '0.9.1', architecture)['version'], '0.9.2+9011')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(UpdateError, 'not newer'):
                self.verify(self.envelope('0.9.1+9011', architecture), '0.9.1', architecture)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_mac_build_assets.py
================================================================================
"""Regression coverage for Mac resources flattened by browser uploads."""
from contextlib import redirect_stdout
import io
from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory
import unittest
from build_macos import ensure_ui_assets, UI_ASSET_FILES


class MacBuildAssetsTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_flattened_upload_is_restored(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root=Path(temp)
            for name in UI_ASSET_FILES:
                (root/name).write_bytes(('test-'+name).encode())
            with redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
                folder=ensure_ui_assets(root)
            for name in UI_ASSET_FILES:
                self.assertEqual((folder/name).read_bytes(),(root/name).read_bytes())
                self.assertTrue((root/name).is_file())
            self.assertEqual(ensure_ui_assets(root),folder)

    def test_existing_resources_are_never_overwritten(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root=Path(temp);assets=root/'assets';assets.mkdir()
            for name in UI_ASSET_FILES:
                (assets/name).write_bytes(b'original')
                (root/name).write_bytes(b'flat-copy')
            ensure_ui_assets(root)
            for name in UI_ASSET_FILES:self.assertEqual((assets/name).read_bytes(),b'original')

    def test_incomplete_upload_fails_before_copying(self):
        with TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root=Path(temp);(root/'tailwind.css').write_text('test')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit,'Missing UI resources:'):
                ensure_ui_assets(root)
            self.assertFalse((root/'assets').exists())


if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_mac_support.py
================================================================================
import sys
import tempfile
import types
from pathlib import Path
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch, Mock
import platform_support


class MacSupportTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_windows_paths_remain_unchanged(self):
        with patch('platform_support.sys.platform', 'win32'), patch.dict('os.environ', {'APPDATA':'C:/Roaming', 'LOCALAPPDATA':'C:/Local'}):
            self.assertEqual(platform_support.user_data_directory(), Path('C:/Roaming/JuiceSpot'))
            self.assertEqual(platform_support.update_directory(), Path('C:/Local/GRAILD/updates'))

    def test_mac_uses_library_directories(self):
        with patch('platform_support.sys.platform', 'darwin'), patch('platform_support.Path.home', return_value=Path('/Users/test')):
            self.assertEqual(platform_support.user_data_directory(), Path('/Users/test/Library/Application Support/GRAILD'))
            self.assertEqual(platform_support.update_directory(), Path('/Users/test/Library/Caches/GRAILD/updates'))

    def test_mac_encryption_roundtrip_and_tampering(self):
        values = {}
        backend = Mock()
        backend.get_password.side_effect=lambda service, account: values.get((service, account))
        backend.set_password.side_effect=lambda service, account, value: values.__setitem__((service, account),value)
        module = types.ModuleType('keyring.backends.macOS');module.Keyring=lambda:backend
        with patch.dict(sys.modules, {'keyring.backends.macOS':module}):
            encrypted=platform_support.mac_session_data(b'session-token')
            self.assertNotIn(b'session-token', encrypted)
            self.assertEqual(platform_support.mac_session_data(encrypted, decrypt=True), b'session-token')
            with self.assertRaises(OSError):platform_support.mac_session_data(encrypted[:-3]+b'bad', decrypt=True)
            backend.set_password.assert_called_once()

    def test_missing_key_does_not_create_replacement_on_decrypt(self):
        backend=Mock();backend.get_password.return_value=None
        module=types.ModuleType('keyring.backends.macOS');module.Keyring=lambda:backend
        with patch.dict(sys.modules, {'keyring.backends.macOS':module}):
            with self.assertRaises(OSError):platform_support.mac_session_data(b'old-ciphertext', decrypt=True)
        backend.set_password.assert_not_called()

    def test_mac_uses_native_update_platform(self):
        from auto_update import UpdateManager
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            session=Mock()
            with patch('auto_update.sys.platform','darwin'), patch('auto_update.platform.machine',return_value='arm64'):
                manager=UpdateManager(root=temp,session=session)
            self.assertEqual(manager.platform,'macos')
            self.assertEqual(manager.architecture,'arm64')


if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_mac_updater.py
================================================================================
"""Mac update signatures, archive safety, and native replacement/rollback."""
import base64
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import hashlib
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import plistlib
import shutil
import stat
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch
import uuid
import zipfile

from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import Ed25519PrivateKey
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.serialization import Encoding, PublicFormat
from app_version import APP_VERSION
from auto_update import UpdateManager
import mac_updater
from mac_update_security import archive_bundle_digest, bundle_digest, verify_bundle, macho_architectures, BUNDLE_ID
from update_security import UpdateError, atomic_json, canonical, verify_manifest


def app_zip(version='99.0.0', extras=()):
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(buffer, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
        entries = [('GRAILD.app/Contents/Info.plist', plistlib.dumps({
            'CFBundleIdentifier': BUNDLE_ID, 'CFBundleExecutable': 'GRAILD',
            'CFBundleShortVersionString': version}), stat.S_IFREG | 0o644),
            ('GRAILD.app/Contents/MacOS/GRAILD', b'#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n', stat.S_IFREG | 0o755)]
        for name, data, mode in [*entries, *extras]:
            item = zipfile.ZipInfo(name)
            item.create_system = 3
            item.external_attr = mode << 16
            archive.writestr(item, data)
    return buffer.getvalue()


def signed_mac(path, arch='arm64'):
    private = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
    keys = {'test': base64.b64encode(private.public_key().public_bytes(Encoding.Raw, PublicFormat.Raw)).decode()}
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    manifest = {'schema':1,'app':'GRAILD','repository':'example/graild','channel':'beta',
                'version':'99.0.0','tag':'v99.0.0','platform':'macos','architecture':arch,
                'asset':f'GRAILD-99.0.0-macOS-{arch}.zip','bundle_id':BUNDLE_ID,
                'bundle_sha256':archive_bundle_digest(path),'size':path.stat().st_size,
                'sha256':hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
                'issued_at':now.isoformat(),'expires_at':(now+timedelta(days=30)).isoformat(),'notes':'Mac update'}
    envelope = {'manifest':manifest,'key_id':'test','signature':base64.b64encode(private.sign(canonical(manifest))).decode()}
    return envelope, keys


class MacArchiveTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(temp.name)
        self.archive = self.root / 'app.zip'
        self.archive.write_bytes(app_zip())

    def test_mac_manifest_is_bound_to_platform_architecture_and_bytes(self):
        envelope, keys = signed_mac(self.archive)
        verify_manifest(envelope, keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', APP_VERSION, platform='macos', architecture='arm64')
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError):
            verify_manifest(envelope, keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', APP_VERSION, platform='macos', architecture='x86_64')
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError):
            verify_manifest(envelope, keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', APP_VERSION)
        envelope['manifest']['bundle_sha256'] = '0' * 64
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError):
            verify_manifest(envelope, keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', APP_VERSION, platform='macos', architecture='arm64')

    def test_architecture_checks_do_not_require_developer_tools(self):
        import struct
        binary=self.root/'GRAILD'
        binary.write_bytes(b'\xcf\xfa\xed\xfe'+struct.pack('<I',0x0100000c))
        self.assertEqual(macho_architectures(binary),{'arm64'})
        binary.write_bytes(b'\xcf\xfa\xed\xfe'+struct.pack('<I',0x01000007))
        self.assertEqual(macho_architectures(binary),{'x86_64'})
        binary.write_bytes(b'not a MachO executable')
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): macho_architectures(binary)

    def test_universal_macho_architectures_are_bounded(self):
        import struct
        binary=self.root/'GRAILD'
        binary.write_bytes(b'\xca\xfe\xba\xbe'+struct.pack('>I',2)+
                           struct.pack('>IIIII',0x01000007,0,0,0,0)+struct.pack('>IIIII',0x0100000c,0,0,0,0))
        self.assertEqual(macho_architectures(binary),{'arm64','x86_64'})
        binary.write_bytes(b'\xca\xfe\xba\xbe'+struct.pack('>I',100))
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): macho_architectures(binary)

    def test_traversal_external_links_special_files_and_writes_through_links_rejected(self):
        cases = [[('../escape', b'x', stat.S_IFREG | 0o644)],
                 [('GRAILD.app/../../escape', b'x', stat.S_IFREG | 0o644)],
                 [('GRAILD.app/Contents/link', b'/tmp/escape', stat.S_IFLNK | 0o777)],
                 [('GRAILD.app/Contents/link', b'../../../escape', stat.S_IFLNK | 0o777)],
                 [('GRAILD.app/Contents/socket', b'x', stat.S_IFIFO | 0o644)],
                 [('GRAILD.app/Contents/link', b'MacOS', stat.S_IFLNK | 0o777),
                  ('GRAILD.app/Contents/link/attack', b'x', stat.S_IFREG | 0o644)],
                 [('GRAILD.app/Contents//link', b'MacOS', stat.S_IFLNK | 0o777)],
                 [('GRAILD.app/Contents/deep/link', b'../../Contents/shallow/../../outside', stat.S_IFLNK | 0o777),
                  ('GRAILD.app/Contents/shallow', b'../Contents', stat.S_IFLNK | 0o777)],
                 [('GRAILD.app/Contents/link', b'MacOS', stat.S_IFLNK | 0o777),
                  ('GRAILD.app/Contents/LINK/attack', b'x', stat.S_IFREG | 0o644)]]
        for extras in cases:
            self.archive.write_bytes(app_zip(extras=extras))
            with self.subTest(extras=extras), self.assertRaises(UpdateError):
                archive_bundle_digest(self.archive)

    def test_duplicate_and_case_colliding_entries_rejected(self):
        self.archive.write_bytes(app_zip(extras=[('GRAILD.app/Contents/info.plist', b'x', stat.S_IFREG | 0o644)]))
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): archive_bundle_digest(self.archive)

    def test_mac_manager_selects_only_its_signed_architecture(self):
        envelope, keys = signed_mac(self.archive)
        session = Mock()
        with patch('auto_update.sys.platform','darwin'), patch('auto_update.platform.machine',return_value='arm64'):
            manager = UpdateManager(root=self.root/'updates',keys=keys,session=session)
        manager.config.update(repository='example/graild',channel='beta')
        url = 'https://github.com/example/graild/releases/download/v99.0.0/'
        release = {'tag_name':'v99.0.0','assets':[
            {'name':'graild-update.json','browser_download_url':url+'graild-update.json'},
            {'name':'graild-update-macos-arm64.json','browser_download_url':url+'graild-update-macos-arm64.json'},
            {'name':envelope['manifest']['asset'],'browser_download_url':url+envelope['manifest']['asset']}]}
        manager._json = Mock(side_effect=[[release],envelope])
        manager._check_worker()
        self.assertEqual(manager.snapshot()['status'],'available')
        self.assertTrue(manager._json.call_args.args[0].endswith('graild-update-macos-arm64.json'))
        self.assertTrue(manager.candidate['url'].endswith('-macOS-arm64.zip'))

    def test_unsigned_zip_never_becomes_installable(self):
        envelope, keys = signed_mac(self.archive)
        with patch('auto_update.sys.platform','darwin'), patch('auto_update.platform.machine',return_value='arm64'):
            manager = UpdateManager(root=self.root/'updates',keys=keys,session=Mock())
        manager.config.update(repository='example/graild',channel='beta')
        manager._response = Mock()
        manager._response.return_value.iter_content.return_value = [b'corrupted archive']
        manager._download_worker({'envelope':envelope,'repository':'example/graild','channel':'beta','url':'https://github.com/test'})
        self.assertEqual(manager.snapshot()['status'],'error')
        self.assertIsNone(manager.ready_job)

    def test_cancel_during_mac_bundle_verification_never_marks_download_ready(self):
        envelope, keys = signed_mac(self.archive)
        with patch('auto_update.sys.platform','darwin'), patch('auto_update.platform.machine',return_value='arm64'):
            manager = UpdateManager(root=self.root/'cancel-updates',keys=keys,session=Mock())
        manager._response = Mock()
        manager._response.return_value.iter_content.return_value = [self.archive.read_bytes()]
        def cancel(_path):
            manager.cancel.set()
            return envelope['manifest']['bundle_sha256']
        with patch('mac_update_security.archive_bundle_digest',side_effect=cancel):
            manager._download_worker({'envelope':envelope,'repository':'example/graild','channel':'beta','url':'https://github.com/test'})
        self.assertEqual(manager.snapshot()['status'],'cancelled')
        self.assertIsNone(manager.ready_job)

    def test_preparation_timeout_stops_helper_before_parent_can_close(self):
        envelope, keys = signed_mac(self.archive)
        with patch('auto_update.sys.platform','darwin'), patch('auto_update.platform.machine',return_value='arm64'):
            manager = UpdateManager(root=self.root/'timeout-updates',keys=keys,session=Mock())
        manager.config.update(repository='example/graild',channel='beta')
        job=manager.root/'jobs'/uuid.uuid4().hex; job.mkdir()
        (job/'payload.zip').write_bytes(self.archive.read_bytes()); atomic_json(job/'manifest.json',envelope)
        manager.ready_job=job; manager.candidate={'manifest':envelope['manifest']}; manager.state['status']='ready'
        target=self.root/'GRAILD.app'; target.mkdir(); (target/'original').write_bytes(b'old app')
        process=Mock(); process.poll.return_value=None
        with patch('update_security.update_root',return_value=manager.root), \
             patch('mac_updater.sys.platform','darwin'), patch('mac_updater.sys.frozen',True,create=True), \
             patch('mac_updater.app_bundle',return_value=target), patch('mac_updater.validate_target',return_value=target), \
             patch('mac_updater.bundle_digest',return_value='0'*64), patch('mac_updater.copy_bundle'), \
             patch('mac_updater.subprocess.run'), patch('mac_updater.launch',return_value=process), \
             patch('mac_updater.time.monotonic',side_effect=[0,61]), self.assertRaises(UpdateError):
            mac_updater.prepare_install(manager)
        process.terminate.assert_called_once()
        self.assertEqual((target/'original').read_bytes(),b'old app')


@unittest.skipUnless(sys.platform == 'darwin', 'Native ditto, file modes and bundle transactions require macOS')
class NativeMacTransactionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(temp.name)
        self.updates = self.root/'cache'
        self.job = self.updates/'jobs'/uuid.uuid4().hex
        self.job.mkdir(parents=True)
        self.target = self.root/'Applications'/'GRAILD.app'
        (self.target/'Contents/MacOS').mkdir(parents=True)
        (self.target/'Contents/Info.plist').write_bytes(plistlib.dumps({
            'CFBundleIdentifier':BUNDLE_ID,'CFBundleExecutable':'GRAILD','CFBundleShortVersionString':APP_VERSION}))
        binary=self.target/'Contents/MacOS/GRAILD'; binary.write_text('#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n'); binary.chmod(0o755)
        payload=self.job/'payload.zip'; payload.write_bytes(app_zip())
        self.envelope,self.keys=signed_mac(payload,platform.machine())
        atomic_json(self.job/'manifest.json',self.envelope)
        self.old=bundle_digest(self.target)
        self.request={'target':str(self.target),'parent_pid':123,'current_version':APP_VERSION,
                      'old_sha256':self.old,'health_token':'a'*64,'repository':'example/graild',
                      'channel':'beta','architecture':platform.machine()}
        atomic_json(self.job/'request.json',self.request)
        for attribute,value in [('update_security.update_root',lambda:self.updates),('mac_updater.update_root',lambda:self.updates),
                                ('mac_updater.user_data_directory',lambda:self.root/'profile')]:
            patcher=patch(attribute,value); patcher.start(); self.addCleanup(patcher.stop)
        self.parent=Mock()
        self.verifier=lambda bundle,manifest:verify_bundle(bundle,manifest,codesign=False)

    def install(self,startup):
        mac_updater.install_job(self.job,keys=self.keys,parent_factory=lambda *args:self.parent,
                               startup=startup,bundle_verifier=self.verifier)

    def test_native_process_identity_resolves_native_child(self):
        import subprocess, time
        process = subprocess.Popen(['/bin/sleep','10'])
        try:
            deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
            actual = None
            while not actual and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                actual = mac_updater.process_path(process.pid)
                time.sleep(0.02)
            self.assertTrue(actual and actual.samefile('/bin/sleep'))
        finally:
            process.terminate(); process.wait(timeout=10)

    def test_native_bundle_replacement_preserves_backup_and_file_modes(self):
        def startup(target,job,token,version):
            self.assertTrue((job/'ready.json').is_file())
            self.assertEqual(version,'99.0.0')
            self.assertEqual(bundle_digest(target),self.envelope['manifest']['bundle_sha256'])
        self.install(startup)
        self.parent.wait.assert_called_once()
        backup=self.target.parent/('.graild-backup-'+self.job.name+'.app')
        self.assertEqual(bundle_digest(backup),self.old)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads((self.job/'result.json').read_bytes())['status'],'installed')

    def test_failed_startup_rolls_back_and_relaunches_previous_app(self):
        with patch('mac_updater.launch') as launch, self.assertRaises(UpdateError):
            self.install(Mock(side_effect=UpdateError('failed startup')))
        self.assertEqual(bundle_digest(self.target),self.old)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads((self.job/'result.json').read_bytes())['status'],'rolled_back')
        launch.assert_called_once()

    def test_changed_target_is_never_replaced(self):
        (self.target/'Contents/extra').write_bytes(b'changed')
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): self.install(Mock())
        self.assertTrue((self.target/'Contents/extra').is_file())

    def test_interrupted_swap_recovers_old_bundle(self):
        backup=self.target.parent/('.graild-backup-'+self.job.name+'.app')
        self.target.rename(backup)
        atomic_json(self.job/'journal.json',{'phase':'prepared'})
        with patch('mac_updater.app_processes',return_value=[]):
            mac_updater.recover_job(self.job,keys=self.keys)
        self.assertEqual(bundle_digest(self.target),self.old)


if __name__ == '__main__': unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_oauth_config.py
================================================================================
"""Google public configuration works without a developer launch environment."""
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import threading
import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

import oauth_config
from prepare_mac_release import FILES
from web_app import JuiceSpotApp


class GoogleOAuthConfigurationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_development_without_environment_uses_production_id(self):
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=True):
            self.assertEqual(oauth_config.google_client_id(), oauth_config.DEFAULT_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID)
        self.assertRegex(oauth_config.DEFAULT_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID,
                         r'^\d+-[a-z0-9]+\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$')

    def test_development_override_is_trimmed_and_resolved_at_call_time(self):
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {'GRAILD_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID': '  development-id  '}):
            self.assertEqual(oauth_config.google_client_id(), 'development-id')
            os.environ['GRAILD_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID'] = 'another-id'
            self.assertEqual(oauth_config.google_client_id(), 'another-id')

    def test_blank_overrides_use_production_id(self):
        for value in ('', '   '):
            with self.subTest(value=value), patch.dict(os.environ, {'GRAILD_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID': value}):
                self.assertEqual(oauth_config.google_client_id(), oauth_config.DEFAULT_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID)

    def test_packaged_login_without_environment_or_resource_files(self):
        for platform, arch in (('win32', 'AMD64'), ('darwin', 'x86_64'), ('darwin', 'arm64')):
            with self.subTest(platform=platform, arch=arch), \
                    patch.dict(os.environ, {}, clear=True), \
                    patch.object(sys, 'frozen', True, create=True), \
                    patch.object(sys, '_MEIPASS', '/nonexistent/bundle', create=True), \
                    patch.object(sys, 'platform', platform), \
                    patch('platform.machine', return_value=arch):
                self.assert_login_client_id(oauth_config.DEFAULT_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID)

    def test_packaged_login_honors_environment_override(self):
        with patch.object(sys, 'frozen', True, create=True), \
                patch.dict(os.environ, {'GRAILD_GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID': ' override-id '}):
            self.assert_login_client_id('override-id')

    def assert_login_client_id(self, expected):
        app = JuiceSpotApp.__new__(JuiceSpotApp)
        app.social_flow_lock = threading.Lock()
        app.social_flow = None
        server = Mock(server_port=43210)
        with patch('web_app.http.server.HTTPServer', return_value=server) as listener, \
                patch('web_app.threading.Thread'):
            result = app.start_social_auth('google')
        self.assertTrue(result['success'], result)
        self.assertEqual(app.social_flow['client_id'], expected)
        self.assertEqual(app.social_flow['redirect_uri'], 'http://127.0.0.1:43210/')
        self.assertTrue(app.social_flow['state'])
        self.assertTrue(app.social_flow['verifier'])
        self.assertEqual(listener.call_args.args[0], ('127.0.0.1', 0))

    def test_mac_source_handoffs_include_configuration_and_regressions(self):
        for name in ('oauth_config.py', 'test_oauth_config.py'):
            self.assertIn(name, FILES)
            self.assertTrue(Path(__file__).with_name(name).is_file())


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest
from publisher_keys import generate_key,load_key,HEADER


class PublisherKeyTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_windows_protected_key_roundtrip_without_plaintext_storage(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            path=Path(directory)/'key.dpapi'
            first=generate_key(path,windows_protected=True)
            self.assertTrue(path.read_bytes().startswith(HEADER))
            second=load_key(path)
            first.public_key().verify(second.sign(b'publisher key test'),b'publisher key test')
            with self.assertRaises(FileExistsError):generate_key(path,windows_protected=True)

    def test_private_key_generation_inside_project_is_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):generate_key(Path(__file__).parent/'do-not-create.dpapi',windows_protected=True)

================================================================================
test_round_resume.py
================================================================================
import json
import os
os.environ['SDL_AUDIODRIVER'] = 'dummy'
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch
import pygame

from web_app import DIFFICULTIES, JuiceSpotApp


class RoundResumeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.addCleanup(pygame.mixer.quit)
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(temporary.name)
        self.music = self.root / 'Music'
        self.music.mkdir()
        for name in ('First.mp3', 'Second.mp3'):
            (self.music / name).write_bytes(b'audio fixture')
        (self.root / 'juicespot_config.json').write_text(json.dumps({'music_folder': str(self.music)}))
        setup = JuiceSpotApp.__new__(JuiceSpotApp)
        setup.account_db_path = str(self.root / 'accounts.sqlite3')
        setup.initialize_account_store()
        with setup.account_connection() as db:
            for user in ('a', 'b'):
                db.execute('INSERT INTO accounts (user_id, username, username_key, password_salt, password_hash, '
                           'password_iterations, display_name, avatar, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)',
                           (user, user, user, b'salt', b'hash', 1, user, '*', '2026-11-02'))

    def open_app(self, user='a'):
        canary = Mock()
        canary.client.cached_recording_eras.return_value = []
        canary.library_era_checks.return_value = {}
        def restore(app):
            app.current_user_id = user
            if user:
                app.load_account_stats()
        with patch('web_app.user_data_directory', return_value=self.root), \
             patch('web_app.UpdateManager'), patch('web_app.CanaryIntegration', return_value=canary), \
             patch.object(JuiceSpotApp, 'get_legacy_config_files', return_value=[]), \
             patch.object(JuiceSpotApp, 'restore_persistent_session', autospec=True, side_effect=restore):
            app = JuiceSpotApp()
        return app

    def test_restart_restores_song_attempt_mode_difficulty_and_random_start(self):
        app = self.open_app()
        app.current_track = 'Second.mp3'
        app.set_game_mode('random')
        app.set_difficulty('Hard')
        track = app.current_track
        app._random_start_frame = 12345
        app.skip_attempt()
        reopened = self.open_app()
        self.assertEqual(reopened.current_track, track)
        self.assertEqual(reopened.game_mode, 'random')
        self.assertEqual(reopened.current_difficulty, 'Hard')
        self.assertEqual(reopened.preview_durations, DIFFICULTIES['Hard'])
        self.assertEqual(reopened.attempt, 1)
        self.assertEqual(reopened._random_start_frame, 12345)
        self.assertIsNone(reopened.random_snippet)
        self.assertFalse(reopened.is_game_over)

    def test_wrong_guess_is_restored_without_resetting_attempts_or_stats(self):
        app = self.open_app()
        app.submit_guess('not the title')
        reopened = self.open_app()
        self.assertEqual(reopened.attempt, 1)
        self.assertEqual(reopened.wrong_guesses, 1)
        self.assertEqual(reopened.current_track, app.current_track)

    def test_completed_round_stays_completed_and_cannot_score_again(self):
        app = self.open_app()
        with patch.object(app, 'play_correct_answer', return_value=None), patch.object(app, 'get_cover_data', return_value=''):
            self.assertTrue(app.submit_guess(Path(app.current_track).stem)['success'])
        reopened = self.open_app()
        self.assertTrue(reopened.is_game_over)
        score = reopened.score
        reopened.submit_guess(Path(reopened.current_track).stem)
        self.assertEqual(reopened.score, score)
        self.assertEqual(reopened.correct_guesses, 1)
        reopened.next_round()
        self.assertFalse(self.open_app().is_game_over)

    def test_last_skip_restores_finished_round(self):
        app = self.open_app()
        with patch.object(app, 'get_cover_data', return_value=''):
            for _ in app.preview_durations:
                app.skip_attempt()
        reopened = self.open_app()
        self.assertTrue(reopened.is_game_over)
        self.assertEqual(reopened.attempt, len(app.preview_durations))
        self.assertEqual(reopened.get_account_state()['profile']['songs_played'], 1)

    def test_saves_are_isolated_and_survive_sign_out(self):
        app = self.open_app()
        app.skip_attempt()
        track = app.current_track
        app.sign_out()
        other = self.open_app('b')
        self.assertEqual(other.attempt, 0)
        other.set_game_mode('random')
        reopened = self.open_app('a')
        self.assertEqual(reopened.attempt, 1)
        self.assertEqual(reopened.current_track, track)
        self.assertEqual(reopened.game_mode, 'classic')

    def test_missing_song_or_invalid_save_starts_valid_round(self):
        app = self.open_app()
        missing = app.current_track
        (self.music / missing).unlink()
        reopened = self.open_app()
        self.assertNotEqual(reopened.current_track, missing)
        self.assertEqual(reopened.attempt, 0)
        with reopened.account_connection() as db:
            db.execute("UPDATE saved_rounds SET attempt = 999 WHERE user_id = 'a'")
        self.assertEqual(self.open_app().attempt, 0)

    def test_reroll_and_mode_change_replace_saved_round(self):
        app = self.open_app()
        app.skip_attempt()
        previous = app.current_track
        app.reroll_song()
        reopened = self.open_app()
        self.assertNotEqual(reopened.current_track, previous)
        self.assertEqual(reopened.attempt, 0)
        reopened.set_game_mode('random')
        self.assertEqual(self.open_app().game_mode, 'random')

    def test_mode_switch_discards_previous_round_and_overwrites_single_save(self):
        app = self.open_app()
        original_track = app.current_track
        app.skip_attempt()
        app.set_game_mode('random')
        self.assertNotEqual(app.current_track, original_track)
        self.assertEqual(app.attempt, 0)
        app._random_start_frame = 12345
        app.skip_attempt()
        app.set_game_mode('classic')
        self.assertEqual(app.attempt, 0)
        self.assertIsNone(app._random_start_frame)
        reopened = self.open_app()
        self.assertEqual(reopened.game_mode, 'classic')
        self.assertEqual(reopened.attempt, 0)
        with app.account_connection() as db:
            self.assertEqual(db.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM saved_rounds WHERE user_id = 'a'").fetchone()[0], 1)

    def test_selecting_current_mode_keeps_round(self):
        app = self.open_app()
        app.skip_attempt()
        track = app.current_track
        app.set_game_mode('classic')
        self.assertEqual(app.current_track, track)
        self.assertEqual(app.attempt, 1)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_tls_support.py
================================================================================
import json
from pathlib import Path
import ssl
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch

import certifi
import tls_support
from web_app import JuiceSpotApp


class CertificateBundleTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(temporary.name)

    def test_development_uses_certifi(self):
        with patch('tls_support.sys.frozen',False,create=True):
            self.assertEqual(Path(tls_support.ca_bundle_path()),Path(certifi.where()))

    def test_frozen_onefile_or_onedir_bundle_takes_precedence(self):
        bundle = self.root/'certifi/cacert.pem'; bundle.parent.mkdir(); bundle.write_bytes(b'bundled CA')
        with patch('tls_support.sys.frozen',True,create=True), patch('tls_support.sys._MEIPASS',str(self.root),create=True), patch('tls_support.certifi.where',return_value=str(self.root/'missing.pem')):
            self.assertEqual(Path(tls_support.ca_bundle_path()),bundle)

    def test_mac_resources_layout_is_resolved(self):
        contents=self.root/'GRAILD.app/Contents'
        bundle=contents/'Resources/certifi/cacert.pem'; bundle.parent.mkdir(parents=True); bundle.write_bytes(b'bundled CA')
        with patch('tls_support.sys.frozen',True,create=True), patch('tls_support.sys._MEIPASS',str(contents/'Frameworks'),create=True), patch('tls_support.certifi.where',return_value=str(self.root/'missing.pem')):
            self.assertEqual(Path(tls_support.ca_bundle_path()),bundle)

    def test_frozen_certifi_resource_fallback(self):
        with patch('tls_support.sys.frozen',True,create=True), patch('tls_support.sys._MEIPASS',str(self.root),create=True):
            self.assertEqual(Path(tls_support.ca_bundle_path()),Path(certifi.where()))

    def test_missing_bundle_fails_closed(self):
        with patch('tls_support.sys.frozen',True,create=True), patch('tls_support.sys._MEIPASS',str(self.root),create=True), patch('tls_support.certifi.where',return_value=str(self.root/'missing.pem')):
            with self.assertRaises(FileNotFoundError): tls_support.verified_ssl_context()

    def test_context_keeps_verification_and_adds_certifi_to_system_roots(self):
        context=tls_support.verified_ssl_context()
        self.assertEqual(context.verify_mode,ssl.CERT_REQUIRED)
        self.assertTrue(context.check_hostname)
        self.assertGreater(len(context.get_ca_certs()),0)
        default=Mock()
        with patch('tls_support.ssl.create_default_context',return_value=default):
            self.assertIs(tls_support.verified_ssl_context(),default)
        default.load_verify_locations.assert_called_once_with(cafile=certifi.where())

    def test_bad_pem_does_not_fall_back_to_unverified_tls(self):
        invalid=self.root/'invalid.pem'; invalid.write_text('not a CA certificate')
        with patch('tls_support.ca_bundle_path',return_value=str(invalid)):
            with self.assertRaises(ssl.SSLError): tls_support.verified_ssl_context()

    def test_oauth_client_passes_verified_context_without_changing_request(self):
        context=tls_support.verified_ssl_context()
        response=Mock(); response.__enter__=Mock(return_value=response); response.__exit__=Mock(return_value=False)
        response.read.return_value=b'{"authorization_url":"https://discord.com/oauth2/authorize"}'
        with patch('web_app.verified_ssl_context',return_value=context), patch('web_app.urllib.request.urlopen',return_value=response) as urlopen:
            JuiceSpotApp.oauth_json_request('https://broker.example/oauth/start',{'provider':'discord','state':'state-token'},headers={'X-Test':'unchanged'})
        request=urlopen.call_args.args[0]
        self.assertEqual(request.get_method(),'POST')
        self.assertIn(b'state=state-token',request.data)
        self.assertEqual(request.get_header('X-test'),'unchanged')
        self.assertIs(urlopen.call_args.kwargs['context'],context)
        self.assertEqual(urlopen.call_args.kwargs['timeout'],15)


if __name__ == '__main__': unittest.main()

test_update_ui.py
