from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import unittest


class UpdateUITests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_notification_progress_later_and_manual_check(self):
        edge=Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Microsoft\Edge\Application\msedge.exe")
        if not edge.exists(): self.skipTest("Microsoft Edge is required")
        folder=Path('build/update-ui-tests').resolve();folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        for name in ('canary_ui.js','update_ui.js'): shutil.copyfile(name,folder/name)
        html=Path('index.html').read_text(encoding='utf-8')
        html=re.sub(r'<script src="https://[^"]+"></script>','',html)
        html=re.sub(r'<link href="https://[^>]+>','',html)
        fixture=r"""<script>
(async()=>{try{
 const base={current_version:'0.8.0',repository:'example/graild',channel:'beta',automatic:true,signing_configured:true,status:'available',version:'0.8.1-beta.1',message:'Verified release',notes:'Fix <img src=x onerror=alert(1)>',total:100,downloaded:0};
 let cancelled=0,installs=0;
 window.pywebview={api:{download_update:async()=>({...base,status:'downloading',downloaded:25,success:true}),cancel_update_download:async()=>{cancelled++;return {...base,status:'cancelled'};},check_for_updates:async()=>base,install_update:async()=>{installs++;return {success:false,message:'Isolated test: installation prevented'};},save_update_settings:async()=>({...base,success:true})}};
 renderUpdateState(base);
 if(document.getElementById('update-notification').classList.contains('hidden'))throw Error('Notification missing');
 if(document.getElementById('update-version').textContent!=='0.8.1-beta.1')throw Error('Version display');
 if(document.querySelector('#update-notes img'))throw Error('Release notes interpreted as HTML');
 await updateNow();
 if(document.getElementById('update-progress').value!==25)throw Error('Download progress');
 await cancelUpdateDownload();
 if(cancelled!==1)throw Error('Cancellation');
 renderUpdateState(base);updateLater();
 if(!document.getElementById('update-notification').classList.contains('hidden'))throw Error('Later');
 await manualUpdateCheck();
 if(document.getElementById('update-notification').classList.contains('hidden'))throw Error('Manual update check');
 updateDownloadArmed=true;renderUpdateState({...base,status:'ready'});
 await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));
 if(installs!==1)throw Error('Verified install handoff');
 if(document.getElementById('settings-current-version').textContent!=='0.8.0')throw Error('App version');
 document.body.setAttribute('data-update-test','passed');
}catch(error){document.body.setAttribute('data-update-test','failed: '+error.message);}})();
</script>"""
        page=folder/'preview.html';page.write_text(html.replace('</body>',fixture+'</body>'),encoding='utf-8')
        result=subprocess.run([str(edge),'--headless','--disable-gpu','--no-first-run','--user-data-dir='+str(folder/'profile'),'--virtual-time-budget=1000','--dump-dom',page.as_uri()],capture_output=True,text=True,encoding='utf-8',errors='replace',timeout=40)
        (folder/'rendered.html').write_text(result.stdout,encoding='utf-8')
        match=re.search(r'data-update-test="([^"]*)"',result.stdout)
        self.assertTrue(match and match[1]=='passed',match[1] if match else result.stderr[-500:])

if __name__=='__main__': unittest.main()

================================================================================
test_updater.py
================================================================================
import base64
from contextlib import nullcontext
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import Mock, patch
import uuid

from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import Ed25519PrivateKey

from auto_update import UpdateManager, validate_asset_url
import graild_updater
from update_security import (UpdateError, SemVer, canonical, strict_json, verify_manifest,
                             sha256_file, atomic_json, update_root)


def signed_release(private, data=b"MZverified-build", version="0.8.1-beta.1", channel="beta"):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    manifest = {"schema": 1, "app": "GRAILD", "repository": "example/graild", "channel": channel,
                "version": version, "tag": "v" + version, "asset": "GRAILD.exe", "size": len(data),
                "sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), "issued_at": now.isoformat(),
                "expires_at": (now + timedelta(days=30)).isoformat(), "notes": "A signed beta update."}
    return resign(private, manifest)


def resign(private, manifest):
    return {"manifest": manifest, "key_id": "test", "signature": base64.b64encode(private.sign(canonical(manifest))).decode()}


def public_keys(private):
    public = private.public_key().public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.Raw, serialization.PublicFormat.Raw)
    return {"test": base64.b64encode(public).decode()}


class Response:
    def __init__(self, data, status=200, headers=None, chunks=None):
        self.data, self.status_code, self.headers = data, status, headers or {}
        self.chunks = chunks
        self.closed = False
    def iter_content(self, size):
        yield from (self.chunks() if self.chunks else [self.data])
    def close(self):
        self.closed = True


class UpdaterSecurityTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.private = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
        self.keys = public_keys(self.private)
        self.envelope = signed_release(self.private)

    def test_semantic_version_order(self):
        versions = ['0.8.0-alpha', '0.8.0-alpha.1', '0.8.0-alpha.beta', '0.8.0-beta', '0.8.0-beta.2', '0.8.0-beta.11', '0.8.0-rc.1', '0.8.0', '0.8.1', '0.8.10']
        self.assertEqual(sorted(versions, key=SemVer.parse), versions)
        self.assertEqual(SemVer.parse('0.8.0+one'), SemVer.parse('0.8.0+two'))
        for invalid in ('0.8', '01.8.0', '0.8.0-beta.01', 'latest', '../1.2.3', '1\u0662.0.0'):
            with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): SemVer.parse(invalid)

    def test_signed_beta_and_channel_policy(self):
        self.assertEqual(verify_manifest(self.envelope, self.keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', '0.8.0')['version'], '0.8.1-beta.1')
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): verify_manifest(self.envelope, self.keys, 'example/graild', 'stable', '0.8.0')
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): verify_manifest(self.envelope, self.keys, 'other/repo', 'beta', '0.8.0')
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): verify_manifest(self.envelope, self.keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', '0.9.0')
        stable = signed_release(self.private, version='0.8.1', channel='stable')
        verify_manifest(stable, self.keys, 'example/graild', 'stable', '0.8.0')
        verify_manifest(stable, self.keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', '0.8.0')

    def test_tampering_unknown_keys_and_expiry_are_rejected(self):
        changed = json.loads(json.dumps(self.envelope))
        changed['manifest']['sha256'] = '0' * 64
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): verify_manifest(changed, self.keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', '0.8.0')
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): verify_manifest(self.envelope, {}, 'example/graild', 'beta', '0.8.0')
        old = dict(self.envelope['manifest'], expires_at=(datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=1)).isoformat())
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): verify_manifest(resign(self.private, old), self.keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', '0.8.0')

    def test_manifest_paths_urls_and_duplicate_json_are_rejected(self):
        bad = dict(self.envelope['manifest'], asset='../GRAILD.exe')
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): verify_manifest(resign(self.private, bad), self.keys, 'example/graild', 'beta', '0.8.0')
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): strict_json(b'{"manifest":{},"manifest":{}}')
        for url in ('http://github.com/example/graild/releases/download/v1/GRAILD.exe',
                    'https://evil.example/GRAILD.exe', 'https://github.com/other/repo/releases/download/v1/GRAILD.exe'):
            with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): validate_asset_url(url, 'example/graild', 'v1', 'GRAILD.exe')


class DownloadTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.private = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
        self.envelope = signed_release(self.private)
        self.data = b'MZverified-build'
        self.session = Mock()
        self.session.headers = {}
        version = patch('auto_update.APP_VERSION', '0.8.0')
        version.start(); self.addCleanup(version.stop)
        self.manager = UpdateManager(Path(temporary.name) / 'updates', public_keys(self.private), self.session)
        self.manager.save_settings('example/graild', 'beta')
        self.url = 'https://github.com/example/graild/releases/download/v0.8.1-beta.1/'
        self.release = {'tag_name': 'v0.8.1-beta.1', 'prerelease': True, 'draft': False,
                        'assets': [{'name': name, 'browser_download_url': self.url + name} for name in ('graild-update.json', 'GRAILD.exe')]}
        def response(url, **kwargs):
            if url.startswith('https://api.github.com/'):
                return Response(json.dumps([self.release]).encode())
            if url.endswith('graild-update.json'):
                return Response(canonical(self.envelope))
            return Response(self.data)
        self.session.get.side_effect = response

    def test_check_signed_download_and_dev_install_rejection(self):
        self.manager._check_worker()
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.snapshot()['status'], 'available')
        self.manager._download_worker(self.manager.candidate)
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.snapshot()['status'], 'ready')
        self.assertEqual((self.manager.ready_job / 'payload.exe').read_bytes(), self.data)
        with patch('sys.frozen', False, create=True), self.assertRaises(UpdateError):
            self.manager.install('not-used')

    def test_bad_signature_never_downloads_payload(self):
        self.envelope['signature'] = base64.b64encode(b'x' * 64).decode()
        self.manager._check_worker()
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.snapshot()['status'], 'error')
        self.assertFalse(any(call.args[0].endswith('GRAILD.exe') for call in self.session.get.call_args_list))

    def test_corrupt_payload_and_interruption_leave_no_installable_file(self):
        self.manager._check_worker()
        self.data = b'wrong bytes'
        self.manager._download_worker(self.manager.candidate)
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.snapshot()['status'], 'error')
        self.assertIsNone(self.manager.ready_job)
        self.assertFalse(list((self.manager.root / 'jobs').glob('*/download.part')))
        self.assertFalse(list((self.manager.root / 'jobs').glob('*/payload.exe')))

    def test_cancel_cleans_partial_download(self):
        self.manager._check_worker()
        def chunks():
            yield self.data[:2]
            self.manager.cancel.set()
            yield self.data[2:]
        self.session.get.side_effect = lambda *args, **kwargs: Response(self.data, chunks=chunks)
        self.manager._download_worker(self.manager.candidate)
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.snapshot()['status'], 'cancelled')
        self.assertFalse(list((self.manager.root / 'jobs').glob('*/download.part')))
        self.assertIsNone(self.manager.ready_job)

    def test_stable_skips_prereleases_and_unconfigured_check_has_no_network(self):
        self.manager.save_settings('example/graild', 'stable')
        self.manager._check_worker()
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.snapshot()['status'], 'up_to_date')
        self.manager.save_settings('', 'beta')
        self.session.get.reset_mock()
        self.assertEqual(self.manager.check()['status'], 'unconfigured')
        self.session.get.assert_not_called()

    def test_inaccessible_update_storage_is_fail_closed(self):
        from auto_update import UnavailableUpdateManager
        manager = UnavailableUpdateManager(PermissionError('update folder is read only'))
        self.assertEqual(manager.check()['status'], 'error')
        self.assertFalse(manager.snapshot()['can_download'])
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): manager.download()
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): manager.install('unused')

    def test_unsafe_redirect_is_rejected(self):
        self.session.get.side_effect = None
        self.session.get.return_value = Response(b'', 302, {'Location': 'http://evil.example/payload.exe'})
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): self.manager._response(self.url + 'GRAILD.exe')


class InstallerTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        temporary = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(temporary.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(temporary.name)
        version = patch('graild_updater.APP_VERSION', '0.8.0')
        version.start(); self.addCleanup(version.stop)
        environment = patch.dict(os.environ, {'LOCALAPPDATA': str(self.root / 'local'), 'APPDATA': str(self.root / 'roaming')})
        environment.start()
        self.addCleanup(environment.stop)
        self.private = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
        self.keys = public_keys(self.private)
        self.data = b'MZverified-build'
        self.job = update_root() / 'jobs' / uuid.uuid4().hex
        self.job.mkdir(parents=True)
        self.target = self.root / 'install' / 'GRAILD.exe'
        self.target.parent.mkdir()
        self.target.write_bytes(b'old local app')
        self.old_digest = sha256_file(self.target)
        self.envelope = signed_release(self.private, self.data)
        atomic_json(self.job / 'manifest.json', self.envelope)
        (self.job / 'payload.exe').write_bytes(self.data)
        atomic_json(self.job / 'request.json', {'target': str(self.target), 'parent_pid': os.getpid(), 'old_sha256': self.old_digest,
                    'current_version': '0.8.0', 'repository': 'example/graild', 'channel': 'beta', 'health_token': 'a' * 64})
        self.user_data = self.root / 'roaming' / 'JuiceSpot' / 'session.dat'
        self.user_data.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        self.user_data.write_bytes(b'persistent discord login')
        lock = patch('graild_updater.install_lock', side_effect=lambda target: nullcontext())
        lock.start(); self.addCleanup(lock.stop)
        self.parent = Mock()
        self.factory = Mock(return_value=self.parent)

    def test_install_waits_for_parent_and_preserves_user_data(self):
        self.parent.wait.side_effect = lambda: self.assertEqual(sha256_file(self.target), self.old_digest)
        startup = Mock(side_effect=lambda target, *args: self.assertEqual(target.read_bytes(), self.data))
        graild_updater.install_job(self.job, self.keys, self.factory, startup)
        self.parent.wait.assert_called_once()
        startup.assert_called_once()
        self.assertEqual(strict_json((self.job / 'result.json').read_bytes())['status'], 'installed')
        self.assertEqual(self.user_data.read_bytes(), b'persistent discord login')

    def test_payload_changed_during_shutdown_is_never_executed(self):
        self.parent.wait.side_effect = lambda: (self.job / 'payload.exe').write_bytes(b'tampered')
        startup = Mock()
        with self.assertRaises(UpdateError): graild_updater.install_job(self.job, self.keys, self.factory, startup)
        startup.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(sha256_file(self.target), self.old_digest)

    def test_failed_startup_rolls_back(self):
        with patch('graild_updater.launch') as launch:
            with self.assertRaises(UpdateError):
                graild_updater.install_job(self.job, self.keys, self.factory, Mock(side_effect=UpdateError('startup failed')))
        self.assertEqual(sha256_file(self.target), self.old_digest)
        self.assertEqual(strict_json((self.job / 'result.json').read_bytes())['status'], 'rolled_back')
        launch.assert_called_once_with([str(self.target)])
        self.assertEqual(self.user_data.read_bytes(), b'persistent discord login')

    def test_new_app_health_requires_trusted_manifest_and_matching_version(self):
        import update_health
        self.target.write_bytes(self.data)
        with patch('sys.frozen', True, create=True), patch('sys.executable', str(self.target)), patch('update_health.APP_VERSION', '0.8.1-beta.1'), patch('update_health.TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS', self.keys), patch('update_health._HANDOFF', (str(self.job), 'a' * 64)):
            update_health.acknowledge_startup()
        health = strict_json((self.job / 'health.json').read_bytes())
        self.assertEqual(health['version'], '0.8.1-beta.1')
        (self.job / 'health.json').unlink()
        with patch('sys.frozen', True, create=True), patch('sys.executable', str(self.target)), patch('update_health.APP_VERSION', '0.8.1-beta.1'), patch('update_health.TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS', {}), patch('update_health._HANDOFF', (str(self.job), 'a' * 64)):
            update_health.acknowledge_startup()
        self.assertFalse((self.job / 'health.json').exists())

    def test_ui_failure_never_acknowledges_update_health(self):
        from web_app import JuiceSpotApp
        app = JuiceSpotApp.__new__(JuiceSpotApp)
        app._window = Mock()
        app._window.evaluate_js.return_value = False
        app._updates = Mock()
        with patch('update_health.acknowledge_startup') as acknowledge:
            app._on_window_loaded()
            acknowledge.assert_not_called()
        app._updates.check.assert_not_called()

    def test_interrupted_commit_recovery(self):
        backup = self.target.parent / ('.graild-backup-' + self.job.name + '.exe')
        backup.write_bytes(self.target.read_bytes())
        self.target.write_bytes(self.data)
        atomic_json(self.job / 'journal.json', {'phase': 'installed'})
        graild_updater.recover_job(self.job, self.keys)
        self.assertEqual(sha256_file(self.target), self.old_digest)
        self.assertEqual(strict_json((self.job / 'result.json').read_bytes())['status'], 'rolled_back')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

================================================================================
tls_support.py
================================================================================
"""Explicit CA trust for urllib in source and frozen desktop builds."""
from pathlib import Path
import ssl
import sys

import certifi


def ca_bundle_path():
    candidates = []
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False) and getattr(sys, '_MEIPASS', None):
        root = Path(sys._MEIPASS)
        candidates.extend((root / 'certifi/cacert.pem', root.parent / 'Resources/certifi/cacert.pem'))
    candidates.append(Path(certifi.where()))
    for path in candidates:
        if path.is_file():
            return str(path)
    raise FileNotFoundError('The trusted CA certificate bundle is missing. Reinstall the complete GRAIL\'D app.')


def verified_ssl_context():
    # Keep system/administrator roots (including Windows trust), then add the
    # bundled Mozilla roots. Never alter global SSL defaults or disable checks.
    context = ssl.create_default_context()
    context.load_verify_locations(cafile=ca_bundle_path())
    return context


def write_diagnostic_report(destination):
    """Opt-in packaged TLS check; never opens a browser or touches accounts."""
    import json
    import urllib.error
    import urllib.request
    from web_app import JuiceSpotApp
    report = {'passed':False,'frozen':bool(getattr(sys,'frozen',False)),
              'default_verify_paths':ssl.get_default_verify_paths()._asdict()}
    try:
        report['ca_bundle'] = ca_bundle_path()
        context = verified_ssl_context()
        report.update(check_hostname=context.check_hostname, verify_mode=int(context.verify_mode),
                      trusted_roots=len(context.get_ca_certs()))
        assert context.check_hostname and context.verify_mode == ssl.CERT_REQUIRED
        broker = JuiceSpotApp.get_discord_broker_url()
        # A context with no roots must reject this otherwise-valid server.
        empty = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLS_CLIENT)
        try:
            urllib.request.urlopen(broker+'/healthz', context=empty, timeout=15)
            raise AssertionError('An empty trust store unexpectedly accepted the broker.')
        except urllib.error.URLError as error:
            assert isinstance(error.reason, ssl.SSLCertVerificationError), str(error)
            report['empty_trust_store_rejected'] = True
        statuses = {}
        for endpoint in ('/healthz','/oauth/start','/oauth/complete'):
            try:
                payload = None if endpoint == '/healthz' else {'provider':'discord','state':'tls-check-invalid','redirect_uri':'http://127.0.0.1:49123/'}
                JuiceSpotApp.oauth_json_request(broker+endpoint,payload)
                statuses[endpoint] = 200
            except urllib.error.HTTPError as error:
                statuses[endpoint] = error.code
                assert error.code == 400, str(error)
                error.close()
        report['verified_https_statuses'] = statuses
        assert statuses['/healthz'] == 200
        report['passed'] = True
    except Exception as error:
        report['error'] = str(error)
    Path(destination).write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    return 0 if report['passed'] else 1

================================================================================
update_health.py
================================================================================
"""Acknowledge a verified new build only after its native window loads."""
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys

from app_version import APP_VERSION
from update_trust import TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS
from update_security import (UpdateError, atomic_json, strict_json, validated_job,
                             verify_manifest, verify_file, update_root)

_HANDOFF = None


def set_handoff(directory, token):
    global _HANDOFF
    _HANDOFF = (str(directory), str(token))


def acknowledge_startup():
    if sys.platform == 'darwin':
        from mac_updater import acknowledge_startup as mac_acknowledge
        return mac_acknowledge(_HANDOFF)
    if sys.platform != "win32" or not getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
        return
    target = Path(sys.executable).resolve()
    directories = [_HANDOFF[0]] if _HANDOFF else list((update_root() / "jobs").glob("*"))[-100:]
    for directory in directories:
        try:
            job = validated_job(directory)
            request = strict_json((job / "request.json").read_bytes())
            if Path(request["target"]).resolve() != target:
                continue
            manifest = verify_manifest(strict_json((job / "manifest.json").read_bytes()), TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS,
                                       request["repository"], request["channel"], request["current_version"])
            if manifest["version"] != APP_VERSION:
                continue
            verify_file(target, manifest)
            token = request["health_token"]
            if _HANDOFF and token != _HANDOFF[1]:
                continue
            if not _HANDOFF:
                journal = strict_json((job / "journal.json").read_bytes())
                if journal.get("phase") not in ("prepared", "installed"):
                    continue
            atomic_json(job / "health.json", {"token": token, "version": APP_VERSION})
            if not _HANDOFF:
                atomic_json(job / "journal.json", {"phase": "healthy", "version": APP_VERSION})
                atomic_json(job / "result.json", {"status": "installed", "version": APP_VERSION,
                            "message": "Interrupted installation recovered; this build started successfully."})
        except (OSError, UpdateError, KeyError, TypeError):
            continue

================================================================================
update_security.py
================================================================================
"""Shared fail-closed signature, semantic-version and filesystem checks."""
import base64
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from functools import total_ordering
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import uuid

from platform_support import update_directory

from cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import Ed25519PublicKey


class UpdateError(Exception):
    pass


@total_ordering
@dataclass(frozen=True, eq=False)
class SemVer:
    major: int
    minor: int
    patch: int
    prerelease: tuple = ()

    @classmethod
    def parse(cls, value):
        if not isinstance(value, str):
            raise UpdateError("Invalid semantic version.")
        match = re.fullmatch(r"(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)\.(0|[1-9][0-9]*)(?:-([0-9A-Za-z-]+(?:\.[0-9A-Za-z-]+)*))?(?:\+([0-9A-Za-z-]+(?:\.[0-9A-Za-z-]+)*))?", value)
        if not match:
            raise UpdateError("Invalid semantic version: " + value[:80])
        pre = tuple(match[4].split(".")) if match[4] else ()
        if any(part.isdigit() and len(part) > 1 and part.startswith("0") for part in pre):
            raise UpdateError("Numeric prerelease identifiers cannot have leading zeroes.")
        return cls(int(match[1]), int(match[2]), int(match[3]), pre)

    def __eq__(self, other):
        return isinstance(other, SemVer) and (self.major, self.minor, self.patch, self.prerelease) == (other.major, other.minor, other.patch, other.prerelease)

    def __lt__(self, other):
        if not isinstance(other, SemVer):
            return NotImplemented
        left, right = (self.major, self.minor, self.patch), (other.major, other.minor, other.patch)
        if left != right:
            return left < right
        if not self.prerelease or not other.prerelease:
            return bool(self.prerelease) and not other.prerelease
        for a, b in zip(self.prerelease, other.prerelease):
            if a == b:
                continue
            if a.isdigit() and b.isdigit():
                return int(a) < int(b)
            if a.isdigit() != b.isdigit():
                return a.isdigit()
            return a < b
        return len(self.prerelease) < len(other.prerelease)


def strict_json(data):
    if isinstance(data, bytes):
        if len(data) > 131072:
            raise UpdateError("Update metadata is too large.")
        data = data.decode("utf-8")
    def pairs(items):
        result = {}
        for key, value in items:
            if key in result:
                raise UpdateError("Duplicate update metadata fields.")
            result[key] = value
        return result
    def invalid(value):
        raise UpdateError("Non-finite metadata number.")
    try:
        return json.loads(data, object_pairs_hook=pairs, parse_constant=invalid)
    except (ValueError, UnicodeError) as error:
        raise UpdateError("Unreadable update metadata.") from error


def canonical(data):
    return json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False).encode("utf-8")


def repository(value):
    value = str(value or "").strip()
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9-]{0,38}/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,100}", value) or value.split("/")[1] in (".", ".."):
        raise UpdateError("Enter a GitHub repository as owner/repository.")
    return value


def verify_manifest(envelope, keys, repo, channel, current_version, expected_tag=None, *, platform="windows", architecture=None):
    try:
        if not isinstance(envelope, dict) or set(envelope) != {"manifest", "signature", "key_id"}:
            raise UpdateError("Invalid signed release manifest.")
        key = keys.get(envelope["key_id"])
        if not key:
            raise UpdateError("Release was not signed by a trusted publisher key.")
        manifest = envelope["manifest"]
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise UpdateError("Invalid release manifest.")
        Ed25519PublicKey.from_public_bytes(base64.b64decode(key, validate=True)).verify(
            base64.b64decode(envelope["signature"], validate=True), canonical(manifest))
        if type(manifest.get("schema")) is not int or manifest.get("schema") != 1 or manifest.get("app") != "GRAILD":
            raise UpdateError("Release is not a supported GRAIL'D update.")
        if repository(manifest.get("repository")).casefold() != repository(repo).casefold():
            raise UpdateError("Release repository does not match your configuration.")
        version = SemVer.parse(manifest.get("version"))
        if version <= SemVer.parse(current_version):
            raise UpdateError("Release is not newer than this build.")
        if channel not in ("stable", "beta") or manifest.get("channel") not in ("stable", "beta"):
            raise UpdateError("Invalid release channel.")
        if channel == "stable" and (version.prerelease or manifest["channel"] != "stable"):
            raise UpdateError("This release is not on the stable channel.")
        tag = manifest.get("tag")
        if not isinstance(tag, str) or tag not in (manifest["version"], "v" + manifest["version"]):
            raise UpdateError("Release tag and version do not match.")
        if expected_tag and tag != expected_tag:
            raise UpdateError("Manifest does not belong to this release tag.")
        if platform == 'macos':
            if architecture not in ('arm64', 'x86_64') or manifest.get('platform') != 'macos' or manifest.get('architecture') != architecture:
                raise UpdateError('This Mac update is for a different platform or architecture.')
            if manifest.get('asset') != f"GRAILD-{manifest['version']}-macOS-{architecture}.zip" or manifest.get('bundle_id') != 'com.graild.desktop':
                raise UpdateError('Invalid Mac application asset.')
            if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-f]{64}', manifest.get('bundle_sha256', '')):
                raise UpdateError('Missing signed Mac app bundle checksum.')
        elif platform != 'windows' or manifest.get('platform', 'windows') != 'windows' or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_.+-]{0,120}\.exe", manifest.get("asset", "")):
            raise UpdateError("Invalid executable asset name.")
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", manifest.get("sha256", "")):
            raise UpdateError("Invalid signed checksum.")
        size = manifest.get("size")
        if type(size) is not int or not 1 <= size <= 2 * 1024**3:
            raise UpdateError("Invalid signed executable size.")
        if not isinstance(manifest.get("issued_at"), str) or not isinstance(manifest.get("expires_at"), str):
            raise UpdateError("Invalid manifest validity dates.")
        issued = datetime.fromisoformat(manifest["issued_at"].replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        expires = datetime.fromisoformat(manifest["expires_at"].replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if issued.tzinfo is None or expires.tzinfo is None or issued > now + timedelta(minutes=5) or expires <= now or not timedelta(0) < expires - issued <= timedelta(days=180):
            raise UpdateError("The signed update manifest has expired or has invalid dates.")
        if not isinstance(manifest.get("notes", ""), str) or len(manifest.get("notes", "")) > 50000:
            raise UpdateError("Invalid signed release notes.")
        return manifest
    except InvalidSignature as error:
        raise UpdateError("Release signature verification failed. Nothing will be installed.") from error
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, UnicodeError) as error:
        raise UpdateError("Invalid signed release manifest.") from error


def sha256_file(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as file:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: file.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def verify_file(path, manifest):
    path = Path(path)
    if path.is_symlink() or path.stat().st_size != manifest["size"] or not hmac.compare_digest(sha256_file(path), manifest["sha256"]):
        raise UpdateError("Executable verification failed. Nothing will be installed.")


def atomic_json(path, data):
    path = Path(path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    temporary = path.with_name(path.name + "." + uuid.uuid4().hex + ".tmp")
    try:
        with open(temporary, "xb") as file:
            file.write(canonical(data))
            file.flush()
            os.fsync(file.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, path)
    finally:
        if temporary.exists():
            temporary.unlink()


def update_root():
    return update_directory()


def validated_job(path):
    path = Path(path).resolve()
    root = (update_root() / "jobs").resolve()
    if path.parent != root or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{32}", path.name):
        raise UpdateError("Invalid updater staging directory.")
    return path

update_trust.py

"""Publisher configuration compiled into both the app and its updater.

Add only public Ed25519 keys here. Keep private release-signing keys offline.
Never load trust keys from a downloaded manifest or user-editable settings.
"""
DEFAULT_REPOSITORY = "[REDACTED_GITHUB_OWNER]/GRAILD-Releases"
DEFAULT_CHANNEL = "beta"
TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS = {'graild-beta-2026': 'K6XHKB3+iANUKw8KRTrC0L3eT3Z4Eed2OcciGpy0F0o='}

================================================================================
update_ui.js
================================================================================
let updateSettingsLoaded = false;
let updateFormError = "";
let updateDismissed = false;
let updateDownloadArmed = false;
let updateInstallStarted = false;
let updatePollPending = false;
let currentUpdateState = {status: 'idle'};

function renderUpdateState(state) {
    currentUpdateState = state;
    document.getElementById('settings-current-version').textContent = state.current_version || '0.8.0';
    const automaticOption = document.getElementById('update-automatic').closest('label');
    automaticOption.classList.toggle('hidden', state.automatic_checks_supported === false);
    if (state.automatic_checks_supported === false) {
        document.getElementById('update-settings-status').textContent = 'Automatic update checks are disabled on this Mac. Use Check for Updates to check manually.';
    }
    if (!updateSettingsLoaded) {
        document.getElementById('update-repository').value = state.repository || '';
        document.getElementById('update-channel').value = state.channel || 'beta';
        document.getElementById('update-automatic').checked = state.automatic !== false;
        updateSettingsLoaded = true;
    }
    const downloading = ['downloading', 'verifying'].includes(state.status);
    const installing = state.status === 'installing';
    document.getElementById('update-settings-status').textContent = updateFormError || (state.automatic_checks_supported === false ? 'Automatic update checks are disabled on this Mac. Use Check for Updates to check manually.' : state.message || '');
    document.getElementById('manual-update-check').disabled = downloading || installing || state.status === 'checking';
    document.getElementById('update-last-result').textContent = state.last_result ? `Last update: ${state.last_result.message || state.last_result.status}` : '';
    const failedDownload = ['error', 'cancelled'].includes(state.status) && state.version && state.version !== state.current_version;
    const visible = !updateDismissed && (failedDownload || ['available', 'downloading', 'verifying', 'ready', 'installing'].includes(state.status));
    document.getElementById('update-notification').classList.toggle('hidden', !visible);
    document.getElementById('update-version').textContent = state.version || '';
    document.getElementById('update-notes').textContent = state.notes || '';
    const percent = state.total ? Math.min(100, Math.floor((state.downloaded || 0) * 100 / state.total)) : 0;
    document.getElementById('update-status').textContent = (state.message || '') + (state.status === 'downloading' ? ` ${percent}%` : '');
    document.getElementById('update-progress').classList.toggle('hidden', !downloading);
    document.getElementById('update-progress').value = percent;
    document.getElementById('update-cancel').classList.toggle('hidden', !downloading);
    document.getElementById('update-now').disabled = downloading || installing || state.can_download === false;
    document.getElementById('update-now').textContent = state.status === 'ready' ? 'Restart & Install' : 'Update Now';
    document.getElementById('update-later').disabled = installing;
    if (state.status === 'ready' && updateDownloadArmed && !updateInstallStarted) installVerifiedUpdate();
    if (['error', 'cancelled'].includes(state.status)) updateDownloadArmed = false;
}

async function pollUpdateState() {
    if (updatePollPending) return;
    updatePollPending = true;
    try { renderUpdateState(await window.pywebview.api.get_update_state()); }
    catch (error) { document.getElementById('update-settings-status').textContent = 'Could not read update status.'; }
    finally { updatePollPending = false; }
}

async function saveUpdateSettings() {
    try {
        const result = await window.pywebview.api.save_update_settings(document.getElementById('update-repository').value.trim(), document.getElementById('update-channel').value, document.getElementById('update-automatic').checked);
        if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.message);
        updateFormError = "";
        updateSettingsLoaded = false;
        renderUpdateState(result);
    } catch (error) { updateFormError = error.message; document.getElementById('update-settings-status').textContent = error.message; }
}

async function manualUpdateCheck() {
    updateFormError = "";
    updateDismissed = false;
    updateInstallStarted = false;
    try { renderUpdateState(await window.pywebview.api.check_for_updates()); }
    catch (error) { document.getElementById('update-settings-status').textContent = 'Could not start the update check.'; }
}

async function updateNow() {
    updateDownloadArmed = true;
    updateDismissed = false;
    if (currentUpdateState.status === 'ready') { installVerifiedUpdate(); return; }
    try {
        const result = await window.pywebview.api.download_update();
        if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.message);
        renderUpdateState(result);
    } catch (error) {
        updateDownloadArmed = false;
        document.getElementById('update-status').textContent = error.message;
    }
}

async function installVerifiedUpdate() {
    updateInstallStarted = true;
    try {
        const result = await window.pywebview.api.install_update();
        if (!result.success) throw new Error(result.message);
    } catch (error) {
        updateInstallStarted = false;
        updateDownloadArmed = false;
        document.getElementById('update-status').textContent = error.message;
        document.getElementById('update-settings-status').textContent = error.message;
    }
}

async function cancelUpdateDownload() {
    updateDownloadArmed = false;
    try { renderUpdateState(await window.pywebview.api.cancel_update_download()); }
    catch (error) { document.getElementById('update-status').textContent = 'Could not cancel the download. Please retry.'; }
}

function updateLater() {
    updateDownloadArmed = false;
    updateDismissed = true;
    if (['downloading', 'verifying'].includes(currentUpdateState.status)) cancelUpdateDownload();
    document.getElementById('update-notification').classList.add('hidden');
}

window.addEventListener('pywebviewready', () => {
    pollUpdateState();
    window.setInterval(pollUpdateState, 1000);
});

================================================================================
updater_windows_acceptance.py
================================================================================
"""Build TEST-ONLY signed Windows fixtures and exercise the real updater EXE."""
import base64
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import uuid

from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import Ed25519PrivateKey
from update_security import canonical, sha256_file, atomic_json


def main():
    project = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    root = project / 'build' / 'update-acceptance-TEST-ONLY'
    source = root / 'source'; source.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    private = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
    public = base64.b64encode(private.public_key().public_bytes(serialization.Encoding.Raw, serialization.PublicFormat.Raw)).decode()
    for name in ('graild_updater.py', 'update_security.py', 'app_version.py'):
        shutil.copyfile(project / name, source / name)
    (source / 'update_trust.py').write_text('TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS = ' + repr({'test-only': public}) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
    # This ephemeral key is deliberately NOT written to the app's production trust file.
    def build(script, name):
        print('Building test fixture:', name, flush=True)
        with open(root / (name + '.build.log'), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as log:
            subprocess.run([sys.executable, '-m', 'PyInstaller', '--noconfirm', '--onefile', '--noconsole',
                            '--name', name, '--distpath', str(root / 'bin'), '--workpath', str(root / 'work'),
                            '--specpath', str(root / 'spec'), str(script)], cwd=source, check=True, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
        return root / 'bin' / (name + '.exe')
    helper = build(source / 'graild_updater.py', 'GRAILDUpdater-TEST-ONLY')
    fixtures = {}
    for version, bad in [('0.8.0', False), ('0.8.1', False), ('0.8.2', True)]:
        script = source / ('fixture_' + version.replace('.', '_') + '.py')
        script.write_text("import json,sys,time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nVERSION = VERSION_LITERAL\nFAIL = FAIL_LITERAL\nif len(sys.argv) == 3 and sys.argv[1] == '--wait':\n    while not Path(sys.argv[2]).exists(): time.sleep(0.05)\nelif len(sys.argv) == 4 and sys.argv[1] == '--post-update':\n    if FAIL: raise SystemExit(2)\n    (Path(sys.argv[2]) / 'health.json').write_text(json.dumps({'token':sys.argv[3],'version':VERSION}),encoding='utf-8')\n    time.sleep(0.5)\n".replace('VERSION_LITERAL',repr(version)).replace('FAIL_LITERAL',repr(bad)), encoding='utf-8')
        existing = root / 'bin' / ('Fixture-' + version + '.exe')
        fixtures[version] = existing if existing.exists() else build(script, 'Fixture-' + version)
    runtime = root / 'runtime'; runtime.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    environment = dict(os.environ, LOCALAPPDATA=str(runtime / 'local'), APPDATA=str(runtime / 'roaming'))
    user_file = runtime / 'roaming' / 'JuiceSpot' / 'session.dat'
    user_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    user_file.write_bytes(b'preserved-user-authentication')
    results = []
    def prepare(label, version):
        folder = runtime / label; folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        target = folder / 'GRAILD.exe'
        shutil.copyfile(fixtures['0.8.0'], target)
        job = runtime / 'local' / 'GRAILD' / 'updates' / 'jobs' / uuid.uuid4().hex
        job.mkdir(parents=True)
        data = fixtures[version].read_bytes()
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        manifest = {'schema':1,'app':'GRAILD','repository':'example/graild','channel':'beta','version':version,
                    'tag':'v'+version,'asset':'GRAILD.exe','size':len(data),'sha256':hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),
                    'issued_at':now.isoformat(),'expires_at':(now+timedelta(days=30)).isoformat(),'notes':'TEST ONLY'}
        envelope = {'manifest':manifest,'key_id':'test-only','signature':base64.b64encode(private.sign(canonical(manifest))).decode()}
        atomic_json(job / 'manifest.json', envelope)
        (job / 'payload.exe').write_bytes(data)
        request = {'target':str(target.resolve()),'parent_pid':0,'old_sha256':sha256_file(target),
                   'repository':'example/graild','channel':'beta','current_version':'0.8.0','health_token':uuid.uuid4().hex+uuid.uuid4().hex}
        return target, job, request
    for label, version, expected in [('success','0.8.1','installed'),('failed-startup','0.8.2','rolled_back'),('tampered','0.8.1','failed')]:
        target, job, request = prepare(label, version)
        marker = target.parent / 'close-parent'
        if marker.exists(): marker.unlink()
        parent = subprocess.Popen([str(target),'--wait',str(marker)],env=environment)
        request['parent_pid'] = parent.pid
        atomic_json(job / 'request.json', request)
        if label == 'tampered':
            with open(job / 'payload.exe','ab') as output: output.write(b'tampered')
        installer = subprocess.Popen([str(helper),'--job',str(job)],env=environment)
        try:
            deadline=time.monotonic()+45
            while time.monotonic()<deadline and not (job/'ready.json').exists() and not (job/'result.json').exists(): time.sleep(.1)
            assert sha256_file(target)==request['old_sha256'], 'Running executable was modified'
            if label != 'tampered': assert (job/'ready.json').exists(), 'Updater never became ready'
            marker.write_text('exit',encoding='utf-8')
            parent.wait(timeout=20)
            installer.wait(timeout=120)
            result=json.loads((job/'result.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            assert result['status']==expected, result
            expected_hash=sha256_file(fixtures[version]) if expected=='installed' else request['old_sha256']
            assert sha256_file(target)==expected_hash
            assert user_file.read_bytes()==b'preserved-user-authentication'
            results.append({'case':label,'passed':True,'status':result['status']})
            print('Passed packaged updater case:',label,flush=True)
        finally:
            marker.write_text('exit',encoding='utf-8')
            if parent.poll() is None: parent.wait(timeout=20)
            if installer.poll() is None: installer.wait(timeout=120)
    target, job, request=prepare('interrupted','0.8.1')
    atomic_json(job/'request.json',request)
    backup=target.parent/('.graild-backup-'+job.name+'.exe')
    shutil.copyfile(target,backup)
    shutil.copyfile(fixtures['0.8.1'],target)
    atomic_json(job/'journal.json',{'phase':'installed'})
    subprocess.run([str(helper),'--job',str(job),'--recover'],env=environment,check=True,timeout=45)
    assert sha256_file(target)==request['old_sha256']
    results.append({'case':'interrupted installation recovery','passed':True})
    report={'passed':True,'production_trust_modified':False,'checks':results}
    (root/'results.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps(report,indent=2),flush=True)


if __name__=='__main__':main()

================================================================================
validate_beta_releases.py
================================================================================
"""Validate real signed beta builds in fresh per-user test installations."""
import argparse
import base64
from datetime import datetime,timezone,timedelta
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
import time
import uuid

from publisher_keys import load_key,restrict_permissions
from update_security import atomic_json,canonical,sha256_file,verify_manifest,strict_json
from update_trust import DEFAULT_REPOSITORY,TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS


def clone_profile(destination):
    original=Path(os.environ['APPDATA'])/'JuiceSpot'
    destination.mkdir(parents=True)
    if not (original/'session.dat').is_file() or not (original/'accounts.sqlite3').is_file():
        raise RuntimeError('No saved profile/session is available; sign into the existing app before validating login persistence.')
    for path in original.iterdir():
        if not path.is_file() or path.name.endswith(('-wal','-shm','.tmp')):continue
        target=destination/path.name
        if path.suffix=='.sqlite3':
            incoming=sqlite3.connect(path.as_uri()+'?mode=ro',uri=True)
            outgoing=sqlite3.connect(target)
            try:incoming.backup(outgoing)
            finally:outgoing.close();incoming.close()
        else:shutil.copyfile(path,target)


def wait_for(path,seconds=180,process=None):
    deadline=time.monotonic()+seconds
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        if path.is_file():return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        error_file=path.parent/'diagnostic-error.json'
        if error_file.exists():raise RuntimeError(json.loads(error_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['error'])
        if process is not None and process.poll() is not None:
            raise RuntimeError('The isolated app exited before writing its test report.')
        time.sleep(.2)
    raise RuntimeError('Timed out waiting for '+path.name)


def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--private-key',required=True)
    args=parser.parse_args()
    project=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    first=project/'dist/releases/v0.8.0/GRAILD.exe'
    second=project/'dist/releases/v0.8.1/GRAILD.exe'
    manifest=project/'dist/releases/v0.8.1/graild-update.json'
    for file in (first,second,manifest):
        if not file.is_file():raise RuntimeError('Prepare both release builds before testing.')
    verify_manifest(strict_json(manifest.read_bytes()),TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS,DEFAULT_REPOSITORY,'beta','0.8.0')
    original_hashes={str(path):sha256_file(path) for path in (first,second)}
    base=Path(os.environ['LOCALAPPDATA'])/'GRAILD-BetaValidation'
    base.mkdir(exist_ok=True);restrict_permissions(base)
    results=[]
    for failure in (False,True):
        root=base/uuid.uuid4().hex;root.mkdir();restrict_permissions(root)
        nonce=uuid.uuid4().hex
        (root/'ISOLATED_TEST_ONLY').write_text(nonce,encoding='utf-8')
        install=root/'install';install.mkdir()
        target=install/'GRAILD.exe'
        if target.exists():raise RuntimeError('Refusing to overwrite an existing test installation.')
        shutil.copyfile(first,target)
        clone_profile(root/'roaming'/'JuiceSpot')
        (root/'local').mkdir()
        selected_manifest=manifest;selected_asset=second
        if failure:
            selected_asset=root/'BadGRAILD.exe';selected_asset.write_bytes(b'MZ - deliberately invalid signed test executable')
            original=json.loads(manifest.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
            now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            data=dict(original['manifest'],version='0.8.2',tag='v0.8.2',asset=selected_asset.name,
                      size=selected_asset.stat().st_size,sha256=sha256_file(selected_asset),issued_at=now.isoformat(),expires_at=(now+timedelta(days=10)).isoformat(),notes='LOCAL FAILURE TEST ONLY - DO NOT PUBLISH')
            private=load_key(args.private_key)
            envelope={'manifest':data,'key_id':original['key_id'],'signature':base64.b64encode(private.sign(canonical(data))).decode()}
            selected_manifest=root/'failure-manifest.json';atomic_json(selected_manifest,envelope)
            del private
        context={'nonce':nonce,'asset':str(selected_asset),'manifest':str(selected_manifest),'expected_failure':failure}
        atomic_json(root/'context.json',context)
        environment=dict(os.environ,APPDATA=str(root/'roaming'),LOCALAPPDATA=str(root/'local'),
                         SDL_AUDIODRIVER='dummy')
        print('Testing isolated '+('rollback' if failure else '0.8.0 -> 0.8.1')+' case.',flush=True)
        process=subprocess.Popen([str(target),'--beta-update-smoke-test',str(root/'context.json')],env=environment)
        initial=wait_for(root/'initial-report.json',process=process)
        if not initial.get('passed'):raise RuntimeError('Initial update check failed.')
        process.wait(timeout=100)
        job=Path(initial['job'])
        outcome=wait_for(job/'result.json')
        expected='rolled_back' if failure else 'installed'
        if outcome['status']!=expected:raise RuntimeError(outcome['message'])
        final=wait_for(root/('rollback-report.json' if failure else 'post-update-report.json'))
        if not final.get('passed'):raise RuntimeError('Persistent data check failed.')
        digest=sha256_file(first if failure else second)
        if sha256_file(target)!=digest:raise RuntimeError('Final installed executable does not match the expected build.')
        result={'case':'rollback' if failure else '0.8.0-to-0.8.1','passed':True,'release_feed':'local simulation of GitHub Releases',
                'notification':initial['notification'],'download_progress':initial['progress_observed'],'signed_download_verified':initial['verified_download'],
                'installer_status':outcome['status'],'new_version':final['version'],
                'persistent_login_retained':final['persistent_login_retained'],'discord_link_retained':final['discord_session_retained'],
                'settings_and_cache_retained':final['settings_and_cache_retained'],'test_directory':str(root)}
        # The test profile contains a copy of real user data. Remove only that
        # protected copy after the hidden test app has closed; keep binaries/logs.
        time.sleep(5)
        copied_profile=(root/'roaming').resolve()
        if root.parent.resolve()!=base.resolve() or not copied_profile.is_relative_to(base.resolve()):
            raise RuntimeError('Unsafe test-profile cleanup path.')
        try:
            shutil.rmtree(copied_profile)
            result['copied_profile_removed']=True
        except OSError:
            result['copied_profile_removed']=False
        results.append(result)
        print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in result.items() if k!='test_directory'}),flush=True)
    for filename,digest in original_hashes.items():
        if sha256_file(filename)!=digest:raise RuntimeError('A release artifact changed during testing.')
    report={'passed':True,'repository':DEFAULT_REPOSITORY,'published':False,'original_installations_overwritten':False,'checks':results}
    output=project/'build/beta-validation-results.json';atomic_json(output,report)
    print('Validation report:',output,flush=True)


if __name__=='__main__':main()

================================================================================
verify_standalone_release.py
================================================================================
"""Audit a signed release and exercise its actual EXE outside the project."""
import argparse
import hashlib
from html.parser import HTMLParser
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import uuid
import pefile

from PyInstaller.archive.readers import CArchiveReader
from update_security import verify_manifest, verify_file, sha256_file
from update_trust import TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS, DEFAULT_REPOSITORY


class References(HTMLParser):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__(); self.local = []; self.remote = []
    def handle_starttag(self, tag, attrs):
        attrs = dict(attrs)
        if tag not in ('script', 'link', 'img'): return
        value = attrs.get('src') or attrs.get('href')
        if not value or value.startswith(('data:', '#')): return
        (self.remote if value.startswith(('https:', 'http:', '//')) else self.local).append(value)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--release', default='dist/releases/v0.8.0')
    parser.add_argument('--live', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    project = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    release = Path(args.release).resolve()
    source = release / 'GRAILD.exe'
    digest = sha256_file(source)
    envelope = json.loads((release / 'graild-update.json').read_bytes())
    manifest = verify_manifest(envelope, TRUSTED_PUBLIC_KEYS, DEFAULT_REPOSITORY, 'beta', '0.0.0', 'v' + envelope['manifest']['version'])
    verify_file(source, manifest)
    archive = CArchiveReader(str(source))
    members = {name.replace('\\', '/'): name for name in archive.toc}
    pyz = archive.open_embedded_archive(next(name for name in archive.toc if name.endswith('.pyz')))
    expected = ['web_app', 'auto_update', 'update_security', 'update_health', 'canary_client', 'canary_integration', 'audio_files',
                'pygame', 'webview', 'requests', 'mutagen', 'cryptography', 'imageio_ffmpeg', 'sqlite3', 'ssl', 'pythonnet', 'clr_loader']
    for name in expected: assert name in pyz.toc, name
    assert 'tkinter' not in pyz.toc, 'Legacy Tkinter frontend was included in the production executable'
    assert 'JuiceGuesserMain' in archive.scripts, 'Production entry-point script was not found in the executable archive'
    assert any(name.endswith('python313.dll') for name in members)
    assert any('ffmpeg' in name and name.endswith('.exe') for name in members)
    assert any('WebView2Loader.dll' in name for name in members)
    assert 'GRAILDUpdater.exe' in members
    assert not any(name.startswith(('discord_oauth_broker', 'publisher_keys', 'release_tools')) for name in pyz.toc)
    for name in members:
        assert not name.endswith(('.dpapi', '.private', '.key', 'session.dat', '.sqlite3')), name
        assert Path(name).name not in ('.env', '.env.local'), name
        if name.endswith('.pem'):
            assert b'PRIVATE KEY' not in archive.extract(members[name]), 'Private key in PEM asset'
    references = References()
    references.feed(archive.extract(members['index.html']).decode('utf-8'))
    assert not references.remote, 'Remote UI resources: ' + repr(references.remote)
    for name in references.local: assert name in members, name
    for name in ['assets/fonts.css', 'assets/tailwind.css']:
        css = archive.extract(members[name]).decode('utf-8')
        for url in re.findall(r'url\([\'\"]?([^\)\'\"]+)', css):
            if url.startswith('data:'): continue
            assert not url.startswith(('https:', 'http:')), url
            assert 'assets/' + url in members, url
    bundled_names = {name.rsplit('/', 1)[-1].lower() for name in members}
    native_count = 0
    unresolved = {}
    for name in members:
        if not name.lower().endswith(('.dll', '.pyd')) or '/win-arm64/' in name or '/win-x86/' in name:
            continue
        try:
            pe = pefile.PE(data=archive.extract(members[name]), fast_load=True)
            pe.parse_data_directories(directories=[pefile.DIRECTORY_ENTRY['IMAGE_DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IMPORT']])
        except pefile.PEFormatError:
            continue
        native_count += 1
        for entry in getattr(pe, 'DIRECTORY_ENTRY_IMPORT', []):
            dll = entry.dll.decode().lower()
            if dll in bundled_names or dll.startswith(('api-ms-', 'ext-ms-')) or (Path(os.environ['SystemRoot']) / 'System32' / dll).is_file():
                continue
            unresolved.setdefault(dll, []).append(name)
    assert not unresolved, unresolved
    root = Path(os.environ['LOCALAPPDATA']) / 'GRAILD-StandaloneValidation' / uuid.uuid4().hex
    root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    target = root / 'GRAILD.exe'
    shutil.copy2(source, target)
    env = dict(os.environ)
    for key in list(env):
        if key.startswith(('PYTHON', 'GRAILD_', '_PYI')): env.pop(key, None)
    env['PATH'] = str(Path(os.environ['SystemRoot']) / 'System32') + ';' + os.environ['SystemRoot']
    env['APPDATA'] = str(root / 'roaming')
    env['LOCALAPPDATA'] = str(root / 'local')
    env['GRAILD_STANDALONE_DENY_PROJECT'] = str(project)
    env['GRAILD_SMOKE_REAL_AUDIO'] = '1'
    # Avoid parent development/frozen loader settings leaking into the new EXE.
    env['PYINSTALLER_RESET_ENVIRONMENT'] = '1'
    reports = []
    for flag, filename in [('--standalone-smoke-test', 'offline.json')] + ([('--canary-smoke-test', 'live.json')] if args.live else []):
        output = root / filename
        result = subprocess.run([str(target), flag, str(output)], cwd=root, env=env,
                                capture_output=True, timeout=360)
        assert output.is_file(), 'Packaged diagnostic produced no report; exit ' + str(result.returncode)
        report = json.loads(output.read_text())
        reports.append(report)
        assert result.returncode == 0 and report['passed'], report
        print(json.dumps({'diagnostic': flag, 'report': report}), flush=True)
    assert sha256_file(source) == digest == sha256_file(target), 'Signed executable changed during verification'
    summary = {'passed': True, 'version': manifest['version'], 'sha256': digest,
               'test_directory': str(root), 'bundled_modules': expected,
               'remote_ui_assets': references.remote, 'signature_and_checksum': True,
               'native_binaries_audited': native_count, 'unresolved_non_system_dlls': unresolved,
               'project_folder_read_denial': True, 'python_removed_from_child_path': True,
               'existing_installations_modified': False, 'reports': reports}
    output = project / 'build' / 'standalone-audit' / ('v' + manifest['version'] + '-results.json')
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    output.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
    print('Verification report:', output, flush=True)


if __name__ == '__main__': main()
